### [1] [Architectural and Regularization Components in Deep Learning Medical Image Registration: Systematic Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05484)
*Nabira Rashid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过受控消融实验分离正则化损失与仿射预对齐架构对深度学习配准方法的贡献，发现正则化损失是精度的主要驱动因素，在不增加推理成本的情况下实现了大部分形变控制，而仿射架构提供了较小但互补的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习配准方法通常同时叠加两类增强：架构增强（如仿射预对齐）和训练目标增强（如正则化损失），但现有文献常常同时采用两者，无法厘清各自的实际贡献。

Method: 在OASIS脑MRI数据集上训练同一配准流程的四种变体：基线3D U-Net+基础相似度损失、U-Net+完整正则化套件、仿射+可变形架构+基础损失、仿射架构+完整套件，分别评估配准精度、形变质量和计算成本。

Result: 正则化单独贡献了MSE改善指标21.3%的相对增益和NCC改善21.8%的相对增益，并将最大形变削减99.0%，推理时间几乎无增加（-0.06%）。组合模型获得最大精度增益25.8%，解剖合理性从0.596提升至0.930，推理时间成本增加9.8%。所有增强变体均在合理形变约束下达到亚体素精度。

Conclusion: 在该设置中正则化损失是主要驱动因素，推理阶段免费提供精度增益和几乎全部的形变控制；仿射架构以可接受的成本提供互补的较小增益。99%的不真实形变削减解决了临床部署中的一个已知障碍。

Abstract: Deep learning registration methods routinely stack two kinds of enhancement on a base network: architectural additions such as affine pre-alignment stages, and training-objective additions such as regularization losses. Papers tend to adopt both at once, so it is unclear which is doing the work. I ran a controlled ablation to separate them. Using the OASIS brain MRI dataset (394 training subjects, 20 test subjects), I trained four variants of the same registration pipeline: a baseline 3D U-Net with basic similarity losses, the same U-Net with a full regularization suite, an affine-plus-deformable architecture with basic losses, and the affine architecture with the full suite. I evaluated registration accuracy (MSE, NCC, SSIM), deformation quality (Jacobian determinant preservation, displacement statistics, an anatomical plausibility score), and computational cost. Regularization alone accounted for most of the gain: a 21.3% relative gain on the MSE-improvement metric (1.78% to 2.16%, P<.001) and a 21.8% relative gain in NCC improvement, while cutting maximum deformation from 53.1 to 0.51 units, a 99.0% reduction, at essentially no computational cost (-0.06% inference time). The combined model produced the largest accuracy gain, 25.8% (1.78% to 2.24%), and raised anatomical plausibility from 0.596 to 0.930, at a moderate +9.8% inference-time cost. Gradient correlation rose from 0.742 at baseline to 0.980 for the fully enhanced model. All enhanced variants reached sub-voxel accuracy under plausible deformation constraints. Regularization losses are the primary driver in this setting, delivering the accuracy gains and almost all of the deformation control for free at inference time, while the affine architecture adds a smaller complementary benefit at acceptable cost. The 99% reduction in unrealistic deformations addresses a known barrier to clinical deployment.

</details>


### [2] [A Survey on Adversarial Attacks and Defenses for Diffusion Models Across Multiple Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05503)
*Ozgur Kara,Tarik Can Ozden,Furkan Horoz,Zeqian Long,Haotian Xue,Yipu Chen,Oguzhan Akcin,Yongxin Chen,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是首个统一综述扩散模型对抗攻击与防御的文献，涵盖了图像、视频和3D三种视觉模态，并提出了以任务为中心的全面分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为视觉领域的主流生成模型，但其公开可获取性导致大规模滥用问题，促使对抗攻击与防御研究快速增长，亟需一份统一的综述来整理和总结该领域的文献。

Method: 提出了一个以任务为中心的全面分类体系：首先按模态（图像、视频、3D）划分文献；在每个模态内，将方法分为攻击和防御两类；再按生成任务分组，并在每个任务内按时间顺序排列。同时深入分析了评估设置，整理了数据集、指标和基准。

Result: 提供了首个覆盖图像、视频和3D三种视觉模态的扩散模型对抗攻击与防御统一综述，建立了系统化的任务中心分类框架，并整合了评估数据集、指标和基准。

Conclusion: 识别了若干开放挑战，并提出了具体未来研究方向，为该领域后续研究提供了指导。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the dominant family of generative models in the visual domain. However, their widespread public availability enables misuse at scale, motivating a rapidly growing body of research on adversarial attacks and defenses. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified review of this literature across three visual modalities: image, video, and 3D. We introduce a comprehensive, task-centric taxonomy: we first divide the literature by modality; within each modality, we separate methods into attacks and defenses, and then group them by the generative task they target, presenting them chronologically within each task. Moreover, we provide an in-depth analysis of their evaluation settings, consolidating the datasets, metrics, and benchmarks used to assess them. We conclude by identifying several open challenges and outlining concrete future research directions. Project Webpage: https://github.com/ozgurkara99/awesome-adv-attack-defense-on-diffusion

</details>


### [3] [An Exploratory Study of Frequency-Aware Task Weighting for YOLOv8-Based Unified Driving Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05516)
*Zhiyuan Nie,Zixi Zhou,Xianbin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出频域感知任务加权（FTW）方法，通过分析近期损失历史中的低频能量占比来动态调整多任务感知中各任务的权重，并在统一的YOLOv8感知框架（检测、可行驶区域分割、车道分割）中进行验证。实验表明该方法可行，但尚未能确立其相对于基线的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一感知网络中多任务联合优化面临任务收敛速度、损失尺度和优化稳定性不同的挑战。现有任务加权方法基于损失幅度、学习不确定性、短期损失变化或梯度统计，本文探索利用近期损失历史的频率结构作为补充信号。

Method: 提出Frequency-aware Task Weighting (FTW)方法，从近期损失历史窗口中估计低频频能占比作为损失轨迹稳定性代理。该规则为均值中心化损失轨迹中低频功率占比更大的任务分配更大权重。在统一的YOLOv8感知框架（含三个任务头）下，分别在全网络静态训练和渐进冻结两种配置中与固定加权及不确定性加权基线进行比较。

Result: 在Mapillary Vistas数据集上的六组单次运行配置中，静态FTW取得最大的综合得分和车道mIoU，渐进FTW取得最大的检测mAP，静态不确定性加权取得最大的可行驶区域mIoU。但缺乏重复种子估计、单任务基线和FTW消融实验，排名仅为描述性结果。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持基于损失频率的任务加权在该pipeline中的可行性，但尚不能证明其优于基线方法，也不能证明其可推广到所报告运行之外的情境。

Abstract: Unified perception enables autonomous driving systems to perform object detection, drivable-area segmentation, and lane segmentation within a single network, improving efficiency and reducing deployment complexity. Jointly optimizing multiple perception tasks remains challenging because tasks exhibit different convergence rates, loss scales, and optimization stability. Existing task-weighting methods use loss magnitude, learned uncertainty, short-term loss changes, or gradient statistics; here, we explore the frequency structure of a recent loss-history window as a complementary signal.
  We implement and examine Frequency-aware Task Weighting (FTW), a dynamic task-balancing rule that estimates a loss-trajectory stability proxy from the low-frequency energy ratio of recent loss histories. FTW assigns larger weights to tasks whose mean-centered loss trajectories contain a larger proportion of low-frequency power. We document FTW and two baselines under full-network static training and progressive freezing using a unified YOLOv8-based perception framework with three task-specific heads.
  Experiments on Mapillary Vistas compare FTW with fixed and uncertainty-based weighting under both configurations. Final holdout metrics are reported for the checkpoint with the lowest per-epoch validation loss in each run. Across six single-run configurations, static FTW has the largest derived overall score and lane mIoU, progressive FTW has the largest detection mAP, and static uncertainty weighting has the largest drivable-area mIoU. Without repeated-seed estimates, single-task baselines, or FTW ablations, these rankings are descriptive. The evidence supports the feasibility of loss-frequency-based weighting in this pipeline, but does not establish improvement over the baselines or generalization beyond the reported runs.

</details>


### [4] [Emergent Goal-Directed Attention in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05517)
*Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了通用视觉语言模型（VLMs）能否在没有眼动监督的情况下产生与人类目标导向注意力一致的注视预测。Qwen3-VL和Gemma两个模型在视觉搜索和自由观看任务中，其注视预测在目标匹配条件下与人类注视对齐更紧密，表明任务目标可以在无眼动训练的情况下自然涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数自然场景注视预测模型都是针对自由观看进行眼动监督训练的，对于目标导向（任务驱动）的注意力是否能在无眼动监督的系统中涌现这一问题尚未得到解答。

Method: 在两个大规模视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking和Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it）上，对4,887个自然场景分别施加视觉搜索和自由观看指令，将模型预测与相同图像上的人类注视数据进行对比，并在目标缺失场景及模型解码器层读出了进行交叉验证分析。

Result: 两个模型在与任务目标匹配的条件下与人类注视对齐程度显著高于目标不匹配的条件；这种交叉效应在目标缺失场景中依然存在（排除了简单视觉接地的解释），并在模型的解码器层读出了得到体现。模型思维轨迹在搜索任务中锚定于目标语义，在自由观看中锚定于视觉显著性。

Conclusion: 通用目的的视觉语言模型无需眼动专项训练即可生成与人类一致的目标导向空间优先性，为理解目标导向注意机制提供了新见解，也为跨任务预测人类注视位置提供了可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Human observers prioritize visual information according to task goals. Most computational models of naturalistic viewing are gaze-trained for free viewing, leaving open whether goal-directed attention can emerge in systems without gaze supervision. We tested two off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs), Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking and Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it, on 4,887 naturalistic scenes under visual-search and free-viewing instructions. Model predictions were compared with human fixations on the same images under corresponding tasks. Both models aligned more closely with human fixations under matching goals than under mismatched goals. This crossover persisted in target-absent scenes, where alignment could not be explained by simple visual grounding, and appeared in decoder-layer readouts. Furthermore, model-thinking traces were grounded in target semantics during search and in visual prominence during free viewing. These findings show that general-purpose VLMs can generate human-aligned, goal-directed spatial priorities without gaze-specific training, informing theories of goal-directed attention and offering scalable tools for predicting where people look across tasks.

</details>


### [5] [CrossModalQA: A Cross-modal and Multi-hop Benchmark for Multimodal Retrieval-augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05518)
*Jiacheng Cai,Zijin Hong,Zheng Yuan,Huachi Zhou,Qinggang Zhang,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossModalQA，一个开放域多模态检索增强生成基准，包含1,863个问答对，覆盖五种跨模态推理路径，用于评估多模态RAG系统在异构语料上的检索与推理能力。实验发现现有系统难以恢复完整证据链，且完整检索比模型规模对准确率提升更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM参数知识不完整且难以更新，需要多模态RAG来弥补。但已有基准存在两个局限：(i)多注重单跳检索或局限于小规模上下文的推理，缺乏开放域证据发现；(ii)对跨模态推理路径覆盖零散，多跳和多图像推理未得到充分探索。

Method: 通过多模态知识图谱引导的子图采样构建基准，结合规则一致性检查和LLM验证确保多模态依赖性和证据可追溯性。构建了1,863个问答对，来源于4,987篇Wikipedia文章和4,431张Wikimedia Commons图片，涵盖五种推理路径：vision-to-text、text-to-vision、vision-to-text-to-vision、multi-image intersection和image-set reasoning，平均推理深度为3.50跳。

Result: 实验表明现有多模态RAG系统难以恢复完整证据链，在不完整检索引入干扰上下文时可能不如封闭式模型。进一步分析显示完整跨模态检索对答案准确率的贡献大于生成器规模扩展，而多图像检索与推理仍是端到端性能的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: CrossModalQA填补了多模态开放域检索与推理评估的空白，揭示了当前多模态RAG系统在证据链恢复和多图像推理方面的不足，并为该领域未来的改进方向提供了指导。

Abstract: Despite the strong capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), their parametric knowledge remains incomplete and difficult to update, motivating multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to ground responses in external text and images. However, existing benchmarks face two major limitations: (i) they typically emphasize single-hop retrieval or reasoning over a small set of provided contexts rather than open-domain evidence discovery; and (ii) they provide fragmented coverage of cross-modal reasoning paths, leaving complex multi-hop and multi-image reasoning underexplored. In this paper, we introduce CrossModalQA, an open-domain benchmark for evaluating multimodal retrieval and reasoning over heterogeneous corpora. CrossModalQA contains 1,863 question-answer pairs constructed from 4,987 Wikipedia articles and 4,431 Wikimedia Commons images. It covers five complementary reasoning paths: vision-to-text, text-to-vision, vision-to-text-to-vision, multi-image intersection, and image-set reasoning. Every question requires retrieving and composing distributed textual and visual evidence, with an average reasoning depth of 3.50 hops. We construct the benchmark through multimodal knowledge graph-guided subgraph sampling and apply rule-based consistency checking and LLM verification to ensure multimodal dependence and traceable evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that existing multimodal RAG systems struggle to recover complete evidence chains and can underperform closed-book models when incomplete retrieval introduces distracting context. Further analysis reveals that complete cross-modal retrieval contributes more to answer accuracy than generator scaling, while multi-image retrieval and reasoning remain the primary bottlenecks limiting end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [6] [Contrastive Knowledge Distillation for Anomaly Detection in Multi-Illumination/Focus Display Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05520)
*Jihyun Lee,Hangil Park,Yongmin Seo,Taewon Min,Joodong Yun,Jaewon Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比蒸馏学习（MCD）的多光照多对焦显示图像自动异常检测方法，通过拉近/推开教师与学生网络特征距离实现无锚点的对比学习，并引入混合模块处理多通道信息，在自制MMdAD数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显示表面的微小缺陷在RGB图像中难以被发现，且仅使用正常数据训练的模型难以检测出此类细微异常，需要更有效的异常检测方案。

Method: 以多分辨率知识蒸馏（MKD）为基线，提出多分辨率对比蒸馏（MCD）——无需锚点和正负样本对，通过拉近/推远教师与学生特征距离实现对比学习；同时设计混合模块将多通道显示图像信息转换并聚合为MCD所需的三通道输入。

Result: 在收集的多光照多对焦显示图像异常检测数据集（MMdAD）上，AUROC和准确率指标均显著优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: MCD方法有效解决了多光照多对焦条件下显示表面微小缺陷的自动检测难题，验证了无锚点对比蒸馏在知识蒸馏型异常检测中的优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle automatic anomaly detection in multi-illumination and multi-focus display images. The minute defects on the display surface are hard to spot out in RGB images and by a model trained with only normal data. To address this, we propose a novel contrastive learning scheme for knowledge distillation-based anomaly detection. In our framework, Multiresolution Knowledge Distillation (MKD) is adopted as a baseline, which operates by measuring feature similarities between the teacher and student networks. Based on MKD, we propose a novel contrastive learning method, namely Multiresolution Contrastive Distillation (MCD), which does not require positive/negative pairs with an anchor but operates by pulling/pushing the distance between the teacher and student features. Furthermore, we propose the blending module that transforms and aggregate multi-channel information to the three-channel input layer of MCD. Our proposed method significantly outperforms competitive state-of-the-art methods in both AUROC and accuracy metrics on the collected Multi-illumination and Multi-focus display image dataset for Anomaly Detection (MMdAD).

</details>


### [7] [Diffusion models for eye-gaze trajectory generation using position and velocity representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05522)
*Laxman Basnet,Alexander Szorkovszky,Pedro G. Lind,Anis Yazidi,Shailendra Bhandari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的去噪扩散概率模型（DDPMs），用于从视觉搜索数据中无条件生成眼动轨迹。一种在二维注视位置空间生成，另一种在速度空间生成，两者均采用FiLM条件化的1D U-Net自注意力架构。位置空间模型在九个运动学特征上平均JS散度为0.016±0.004，远超统计和马尔可夫基线；合成数据训练的模型达成真实数据R²的82.7%。速度空间模型在速度分量和转向角上平均JS散度为0.0065。两种模型都能较好地捕捉局部注视运动学和短程时间/方向结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动追踪数据的采集成本高昂（需要专业硬件和受控实验室条件），且因隐私约束难以共享。这限制了眼动相关研究的数据可得性和可复现性，因此需要一种能够生成逼真合成眼动数据的方法来替代真实数据的使用。

Method: 采用两种互补的去噪扩散概率模型（DDPMs）进行眼动轨迹的无条件生成。两种模型使用相同的FiLM条件化一维U-Net自注意力架构（19.35M参数），在8秒滑动窗口序列上（来自28名参与者）训练。位置空间模型生成原始二维注视位置序列，速度空间模型生成二分量速度序列，两者使用各自特定的预处理、训练设置、数据划分和评估协议。每种模型在三个独立训练种子上评估。

Result: 位置空间模型在九个运动学特征上平均JS散度为0.016±0.004，最高特征均值低于0.030，注视持续时间与真实数据的差异在2%以内，Frechet Gaze Distance比统计和马尔可夫基线低一个数量级以上。在Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real协议下，合成数据训练达到R²=0.66±0.02（真实数据R²的82.7%）。速度空间模型在速度分量、速度、对数速度和转向角上平均JS散度为0.0065，最大为0.015±0.005。重建路径长度准确性较低（0.21±0.02 vs 位置空间的0.03±0.01）。

Conclusion: 无条件扩散模型能有效捕捉局部注视运动学以及短程的时间和方向结构，但在长程特性（如扫视次数和累积路径几何）方面仍有不足，这些可通过未来的条件生成模型来弥补。

Abstract: Eye-tracking data are expensive to collect, requiring specialized hardware and controlled laboratory conditions, and difficult to share because of privacy constraints. We address this using two complementary denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs) for unconditional generation of eye-gaze dynamics from visual-search data. Both use an identical FiLM-conditioned one-dimensional U-Net with self-attention (19.35,M parameters), trained on 8,s sliding-window sequences from 28 participants. One model generates raw two-dimensional gaze-position sequences, while the other generates two-component velocity sequences; each uses representation-specific preprocessing, training settings, data partitions, and evaluation protocols. Both are evaluated across three independent training seeds, with aggregated metrics reported as mean,$\pm$,SD. The position-space model achieves a mean Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence of $0.016\pm0.004$ across nine kinematic features, with the highest feature-wise mean below $0.030$, fixation duration within 2% of real data, and a Fr'echet Gaze Distance more than an order of magnitude below statistical and Markovian baselines. Under a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real protocol, synthetic-only training achieves $R^2=0.66\pm0.02$, or 82.7% of the real-data $R^2$ point estimate. The velocity-space model achieves a mean JS divergence of $0.0065$ across velocity components, speed, log-speed, and turning angle, with a maximum of $0.015\pm0.005$. Reconstructed path length is less accurate ($0.21\pm0.02$ versus $0.03\pm0.01$ in position space), although the protocols differ. Overall, unconditional diffusion captures local gaze kinematics and short-range temporal and directional structure, while long-range properties such as saccade counts and cumulative path geometry remain targets for future conditioned models.

</details>


### [8] [Infrastructure-based Monocular 3D Vehicle Localization Framework with Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05523)
*Akos T. Kopeczi-Bocz,Tian Mi,Gabor Orosz,Denes Takacs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单阶段学习框架，直接从路侧单目相机图像中估计地面固定坐标系下的车辆状态（位置、尺寸和偏航角），无需传统方法中先进行图像平面车辆检测再几何后处理的流程。论文还构建了基于路侧相机与无人机同步视频的数据采集和标签生成流程，并在Mcity试验场进行了多轮实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路侧感知方法通常先在图像平面检测车辆，再通过几何后处理来推断地面坐标下的车辆状态，这种分阶段处理方式繁琐且容易累积误差。论文旨在探索一种直接从视觉特征端到端估计车辆空间状态的方法，以提升城市路口基础设施感知的效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出一个单阶段学习框架，利用预训练目标检测器的视觉特征，同时估计每辆车的地面平面位置、尺寸和偏航角。同时开发了一套基于路侧相机与无人机同步视频的数据采集与标签生成管道：无人机作为临时俯视传感平台提供车辆轨迹、尺寸和朝向，转换到地面固定坐标系并与路侧图像时间对齐，生成真值标签用于模型训练和评估。

Result: 在Mcity试验场的多次实验中，该框架能够从单目路侧影像中恢复车辆轨迹和朝向，无需单独的几何后处理阶段，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该单阶段框架展示了作为城市路口基于基础设施感知的可扩展方法的潜力，可以摆脱对分离式几何后处理的依赖，直接实现从单目影像到车辆空间状态的映射。

Abstract: This paper presents a one-stage learning framework that maps monocular roadside-camera images directly to vehicle states in a ground-fixed coordinate frame. Unlike conventional approaches that first detect vehicles in the image plane and subsequently apply geometric post-processing, the proposed method leverages features from a pretrained object detector to jointly estimate each vehicle's ground-plane position, dimensions, and yaw angle. The framework therefore uses visual features not only for vehicle detection but also for direct spatial and orientation estimation. To support model training and evaluation, we develop a data-collection and label-generation pipeline based on synchronized video from a roadside camera and an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Acting as a temporary top-view sensing platform, the UAV provides vehicle trajectories, dimensions, and orientations, which are transformed into the ground-fixed coordinate frame and temporally aligned with the roadside-camera images to generate ground-truth labels. The framework is evaluated using data collected during multiple experiments at the Mcity Test Facility. Results show that the proposed method can recover vehicle trajectories and orientations from monocular roadside imagery without a separate geometric post-processing stage, demonstrating its potential as a scalable approach to infrastructure-based perception at urban intersections.

</details>


### [9] [DIVA: Exploiting Cross-Step Conditional Propagation for Visual Jailbreaks in Discrete Diffusion Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05525)
*Guorui Song,Runqing Tang,Jingye Zhang,Luyuan Zhang,Feice Huang,Cong Ray,Guocun Wang,Dake Zhong,Choo Sin Wai,Bingquan Dai,Chuming Wang,Tongxu Lin,Wanyu Guo,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究多模态离散扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）的视觉越狱漏洞，提出一种仅针对扩散架构特有的攻击框架DIVA，并在三个模型上实现远超现有基线（基于自回归模型设计）的越狱成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉越狱研究几乎全部集中在自回归架构的VLM上，而新兴的多模态离散扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）的安全漏洞尚未被研究。由于扩散模型独特的去噪生成机制与自回归模型的逐token前缀生成机制本质不同，已有攻击方法无法直接迁移，亟需针对该架构家族的系统性安全分析。

Method: 作者首先识别出扩散生成特有的漏洞机制——"跨步条件传播"（cross-step conditional propagation），即视觉嵌入在每一步反向去噪中都作为条件参与而非一次性前缀，导致对抗性视觉语义沿生成轨迹反复传播与放大。随后提出了DIVA框架，包含两个核心组件：跨模态意图混淆（cross-modal intent obfuscation）和扩散感知的多时间步对抗优化（diffusion-aware multi-timestep adversarial optimization）。方法验证包括阶段敏感性分析、提示级切换率、成对去噪bin不一致指标，并通过bootstrap重采样确认统计显著性。

Result: 在三个dVLMs上，DIVA在Beaver奖励模型度量下分别达到58.8%、67.7%和69.1%的HADES攻击成功率（ASR），显著优于为自回归模型设计的视觉越狱基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散式VLM因其生成过程中的条件传播特性存在不同于自回归模型的安全漏洞，需要专门的攻击与防御策略来应对，DIVA为这一方向的研究提供了新的框架与基准。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical settings, yet existing visual jailbreak research has focused almost exclusively on autoregressive architectures, leaving an important emerging family unstudied: multimodal discrete diffusion vision-language models (dVLMs). We identify a vulnerability specific to diffusion generation: because the visual embedding conditions every reverse denoising step rather than acting as a one-time prefix, adversarial visual semantics are repeatedly propagated and amplified across the generation trajectory, a phenomenon we term cross-step conditional propagation. We provide empirical evidence through stage-sensitivity analysis, prompt-level switch rates, and pairwise denoising-bin disagreement metrics, confirmed by bootstrap resampling. We propose DIVA (Discrete-diffusion Vision-language model Attack), a white-box visual jailbreak framework using cross-modal intent obfuscation and diffusion-aware multi-timestep adversarial optimization. Across three dVLMs, DIVA reaches 58.8%, 67.7%, and 69.1% HADES ASR under the Beaver reward-model metric, outperforming visual jailbreak baselines designed for autoregressive models. Code: https://github.com/loststars2002/DIVA

</details>


### [10] [A Specialized Large Multimodal Model for Interpreting PET/CT in Head and Neck Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05532)
*Haengbok Chung,SunGyu Kim,Joo hyun Lee,Sangjin Bae,Min Jeong Cho,Minseok Suh,Jae Sung Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过两阶段课程微调LLaVA-NeXT，开发了专门用于头颈癌PET/CT影像解读的大语言多模态模型（LMM），在多机构外部验证中显著超越ChatGPT和基座模型，展示出自动化辅助诊断的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头颈癌PET/CT诊断因解剖结构复杂而具有挑战性和耗时性，需要计算机辅助诊断支持；通用大语言多模态模型因缺乏领域知识、隐私安全隐患和冗长输出而在医学应用中受限，因此需要开发专业化的独立LMM。

Method: 基于LLaVA-NeXT进行微调，采用两级课程学习：第一级使用28,000个图像对话对学习基础信息（模态类型、高代谢）；第二级使用12,975个对话对学习原发肿瘤存在性及颈部淋巴结转移的解剖位置。数据集由两名放射科医师从公共数据中筛选标注，并在包含四种不同成像设备的四个机构上进行外部验证。

Result: 专业化LMM在外部验证中显著优于ChatGPT和未微调的LLaVA-NeXT（通用模型得分均低于0.1，专化模型ROUGE-L、ROUGE-S、余弦相似度、精确率、召回率和F1值分别达0.8751、0.8794、0.8324、0.8794、0.8711和0.8751）。原发肿瘤分类内部准确率83.14%±1.15%，外部69.03%±0.81%；淋巴结定位各项指标在0.5287至0.6648之间。

Conclusion: 专门化的LMM在PET/CT辅助诊断和医学教育方面展现出良好前景，具备快速、准确诊断支持的潜力，为临床转化提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Background: Diagnosing head and neck cancer using PET/CT is clinically challenging and time-consuming due to the anatomical complexity of the region, motivating computer-aided diagnosis (CAD). Generalist Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) remain limited in medical contexts by insufficient domain-specific knowledge, privacy and security concerns, and verbosity, motivating specialized standalone LMMs. Purpose: We evaluated the feasibility of a specialized LMM for automated PET/CT interpretation in head and neck cancer using a large-scale multi-institutional PET/CT dataset, a tailored training curriculum, and autoregressive training. Methods: LLaVA-NeXT was fine-tuned using a two-level curriculum with image-conversation pairs curated by two radiologists from public data. The dataset included clinically important annotations such as primary tumor presence and metastatic lymph node location. Level 1 used 28,000 image-conversation pairs to learn basic information, including modality type and hypermetabolism. Level 2 used 12,975 pairs to learn primary tumor presence and the existence and anatomical location of cervical lymph node metastases. External validation included four institutions with diverse imaging devices. Results: The specialized LMM substantially outperformed ChatGPT and LLaVA-NeXT. In Level-2 external validation, ROUGE-L, ROUGE-S, Cosine Similarity, Precision, Recall, and F1 were 0.8751, 0.8794, 0.8324, 0.8794, 0.8711, and 0.8751, while generalist models consistently scored below 0.1. Primary tumor classification accuracy was 83.14 +/- 1.15% internally and 69.03 +/- 0.81% externally. For lymph node localization, the corresponding scores were 0.6389, 0.6257, 0.5287, 0.5782, 0.6371, and 0.6648. Conclusion: Specialized LMMs show promising results for fast, accurate PET/CT-based diagnostic support and medical education, highlighting their potential for clinical translation.

</details>


### [11] [SimpleMemVLA: A Simple but Effective Native-Video Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05533)
*Cheng Yin,Wang Xu,Junpeng Yang,Sikyuen Tam,Hanyu Liu,Yuan Yao,Xiangrui Zeng,Junbo Cui,Yequan Wang,Zhouping Yin,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimpleMemVLA提出一种无需专用记忆模块的VLA方法，通过将完整时序历史以时间戳视频格式直接传入主干网络，并利用子任务生成的隐状态作为唯一历史通道，在四个记忆基准上刷新SOTA，且通过前缀预填充保持接近单帧VLA的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界操控任务本质上是部分可观测的，决策所需信息可能出现在较早时刻的观测中。传统记忆机制（检索库、学习压缩器、循环状态）必须在知晓未来决策需求之前决定保留哪些过去信息，这存在根本性的信息损失风险。作者认为"分钟级历史过大无法直接处理"这一假设在现代VLM骨干下已不成立，因此无需专门记忆模块。

Method: SimpleMemVLA不使用专用记忆模块，而是将采样的完整历史直接传递给预训练骨干网络，并采用骨干在预训练时就处理的时间戳视频格式。生成子任务的隐状态成为连接历史与标准流匹配动作头的唯一通道。为降低延迟，利用连续决策共享大部分历史的特点，在执行动作时预填充共享前缀。

Result: SimpleMemVLA在四个记忆基准上取得了新的最先进结果，且不牺牲通用控制性能。在固定骨干网络和训练设置下，其性能大幅超越检索、压缩和循环状态等记忆机制。因果干预实验证实策略确实有效读取了历史信息。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在具备现代VLM骨干的条件下，取消专用记忆模块而直接处理完整历史是更优的选择，为长视界操控中的记忆设计提供了新的范式参考，同时开源了代码便于复现。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation is partially observable: the information needed to choose the next action may appear only in observations from minutes earlier. Existing memory mechanisms: retrieval banks, learned compressors, recurrent states must decide what to keep from the past before knowing what a future decision will require. This was motivated by the assumption that minute-scale history is too large to process directly, which modern VLM backbones no longer make true. In this work, we introduce SimpleMemVLA, a VLA without a dedicated memory module. It keeps the sampled history intact and passes it to the backbone in the timestamped video format the backbone was pretrained to process; the hidden states of a generated sub-task then form the only channel from history to a standard flow-matching action head. Since consecutive decisions share most of their history, prefilling the shared prefix during action execution keeps latency close to a single-frame VLA. SimpleMemVLA sets a new state of the art on four memory benchmarks without cost on general-purpose control. Holding the backbone and training setup fixed, it outperforms retrieval, compression and recurrent-state mechanisms by a wide margin, and causal interventions confirm that the policy genuinely reads its history. Code available at https://github.com/wadeKeith/SimpleMemVLA

</details>


### [12] [Beyond the Verdict: Evidence-Aligned Evaluation of Visual Prompt-Injection Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05535)
*Suyoung Lee,Myungsub Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超越二元判断的VLM评估方法。作者创建了Mind2Web-Injection基准（9,954个指令-截图对），包含像素级证据框和图像侧反事实。研究表明，平均精度相近的模型在证据对齐检测（EAD）上可相差九倍；当指令被替换为支持攻击命令的指令时，Qwen3-VL-32B仅有58.7%保持对齐判断，而GPT-5.6-luna达到99.9%。作者还提出了ReadGate和CmdCompare两种无需训练的干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对视觉语言模型（VLM）的评估仅关注最终判断结果（verdict），无法揭示模型是否真正基于支撑其决策的视觉证据。在网络代理防护场景中，VLM需要判断屏幕上文本是否与用户指令冲突，仅仅评估判断正确性是不够的，还需要验证模型是否使用了正确的视觉证据。

Method: 构建Mind2Web-Injection基准，包含9,954个指令-截图对，标注了指令相对标签和像素精确的证据框，并设计了图像侧匹配反事实。提出了Evidence-Aligned Detection（EAD）指标衡量攻击检测与定位的一致性。设计了两个无需训练的干预方法：ReadGate（在不改变判断的前提下改进视觉基础定位）和CmdCompare（测试显式指令-命令比较能否解决指令侧不一致问题）。

Result: 在六个VLM上的实验表明，两个平均精度几乎相同的模型在EAD上相差九倍。通过将指令替换为支持被引为证据的命令来测试判断是否真正依赖证据时，Qwen3-VL-32B仅有58.7%返回对齐，而GPT-5.6-luna达到99.9%。ReadGate和CmdCompare两种干预为诊断证据基础失败提供了工具。

Conclusion: 论文主张在评估VLM时应分别报告判断正确性、证据定位和反事实响应性三个维度，而不仅仅是最终结论。这为更全面地评估VLM的视觉推理能力提供了方法论框架，并揭示了仅看平均精度等宏观指标可能产生的严重误导。

Abstract: Verdict-only evaluation does not reveal whether a vision-language model (VLM) used the visual evidence that should support its decision. We study this problem in web-agent guardrails, where a VLM judges whether on-screen text conflicts with a user instruction. We introduce Mind2Web-Injection, a benchmark of 9,954 instruction-screenshot pairs with instruction-relative labels, pixel-exact evidence boxes, and matched image-side counterfactuals. Across six VLMs, two models with nearly identical average precision differ ninefold in Evidence-Aligned Detection (EAD), the fraction of attacks both detected and correctly localized. To test whether a verdict depends on the command cited as evidence, we replace the instruction with one that endorses that command. Qwen3-VL-32B, the strongest open-weight localizer, returns aligned in only 58.7% of cases, whereas GPT-5.6-luna does so in 99.9%. To diagnose these failures, we propose two training-free interventions. ReadGate improves grounding without changing verdicts, while CmdCompare tests whether explicit instruction-command comparison resolves instruction-side inconsistency. These results motivate reporting verdict correctness, evidence localization, and counterfactual responsiveness separately.

</details>


### [13] [VIS-DICT: A Visual Dictionary for Missing Modality Imputation in Social Network Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05537)
*Hamed Marvi,Mohammad Mehdi Keikha,Abolfazl Nadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vis-Dict，一种基于词典的方法，通过将词语与完整训练帖子中的平均图像向量关联来构建缺失的视觉特征，用于社交媒体抑郁症检测。该方法无需额外训练图像生成模块，在用户时间线数据上达到F1-score为0.9454、ROC-AUC为0.9890的性能，与生成式网络相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体帖子中很多没有图像，导致多模态抑郁症检测模型难以应用。现有的缺失图像填补方法依赖检索或生成式模型，需要额外训练，成本高且复杂。

Method: Vis-Dict采用基于词典的策略：从包含完整图像-文本对的训练帖子中，为每个词语计算对应的平均图像向量作为视觉词典；对缺失图像的帖子，根据其文本中出现的词语查找对应的平均图像向量，从而估计缺失的视觉特征；然后将估计的视觉特征与文本结合，用于追踪用户行为随时间的变化。

Result: 在社交媒体数据集上使用最多512条帖子构成的用户时间线进行测试，Vis-Dict达到F1-score 0.9454和ROC-AUC 0.9890，性能与生成式网络相当，且图像生成部分零可训练参数。

Conclusion: 将词语直接映射到视觉特征是一种有效且实用的缺失图像处理方案，为抑郁症检测系统提供了一种轻量级、无需额外训练的多模态替代方法。

Abstract: Tracking social media posts can help spot early signs of depression. Recent studies show that combining text and images works better for detecting depression than using text alone. However, many social media posts do not have images, which makes it hard to use multimodal models. Most existing methods fill in missing images using retrieval or generative models that need extra training. In this paper, we introduce Vis-Dict, a dictionary-based method that builds missing visual features by linking words to average image vectors from complete training posts. These estimated visual features are then combined with text to track changes in user behavior over time. We tested Vis-Dict on a social media dataset using user timelines of up to 512 posts and compared it with other missing-data methods. The results show that Vis-Dict performs on par with generative networks, reaching an F1-score of 0.9454 and an ROC-AUC of 0.9890. Most importantly, Vis-Dict achieves this strong performance with zero trainable parameters for image generation. These findings show that directly connecting words to visual features is an effective and practical way to handle missing images in depression detection systems.

</details>


### [14] [Dual-Latent Memory Routing for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05539)
*Hao-Xuan Ma,Jin-Fei Qi,Yicheng Xiao,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DLMR是一种参数高效的机制，为多模态大语言模型（MLLM）配备双向潜在记忆——视觉记忆压缩图像证据、推理记忆追踪中间结论和约束，并通过路由器动态选择重用哪些记忆，从而在冻结基础模型的情况下，以少量可训练参数显著提升长序列视觉语言推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉语言推理中取得了显著进展，但随着生成序列变长，其性能往往下降。关键原因在于模型在单一不断增长的上下文中容易丢失早期的视觉证据和中间约束，导致长距离推理能力不足。

Method: DLMR通过三个训练阶段实现：1）潜在记忆构建（压缩图像证据形成视觉记忆、追踪中间结论形成推理记忆）；2）选择性路由器学习；3）在冻结基础MLLM的前提下进行参数高效训练。路由器在推理过程中动态决定使用哪种记忆以及重用的程度。

Result: DLMR在通用基准和推理基准上均取得了显著增益，仅需少量额外可训练参数。分析还显示其具备可解释的状态依赖路由、专门的记忆角色，并在长序列生成中减少了解码token数量。

Conclusion: DLMR验证了将视觉记忆与推理记忆分离并动态路由的思路能有效解决MLLM长序列推理中视觉接地保持和推理连贯性下降的问题，是一种参数高效且具有可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently made strong progress in vision-language reasoning, yet their performance often degrades as generations grow longer. A key factor is that they frequently lose track of earlier visual evidence and intermediate constraints under a monolithic growing context. Inspired by how humans separately recall what they see and what they infer when solving complex tasks, we propose DLMR, a parameter-efficient mechanism that equips MLLMs with Dual Latent Memories: a visual memory that compresses image evidence and a reasoning memory that tracks intermediate conclusions and constraints. A Router then dynamically decides which memory and how much to reuse during inference, preserving visual grounding while maintaining coherent long-horizon reasoning. DLMR is trained in three stages, from latent memory construction to selective router learning, while keeping the base MLLM frozen, yielding substantial gains on both general and reasoning benchmarks with only a small number of additional trainable parameters. Analyses further show interpretable, state-dependent routing with specialized memory roles and reduced decoding tokens over long generations. Code is available at https://github.com/Hunter-Wrynn/DLMR.

</details>


### [15] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Abstention and Refusal Reasoning in Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05540)
*Karan Dua,Amit Agarwal,Hitesh Laxmichand Patel,Hansa Meghwani,Jyotika Singh,Ranjeet Gupta,Graham Horwood,Tao Sheng,Avi Sil,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PARITY基准，用于审计视觉语言模型（VLMs）在面对无法从静态面部照片回答的自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）配对图像查询时是否会主动拒答。研究发现模型可分为"拒答优先"和"猜测型"两类，其中猜测型模型在特定表情下更容易产生有害的归因判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多医疗诊断需要多维度临床评估而非仅凭视觉外观。然而VLMs越来越多地被用于涉及医疗或诊断判断的图像解读，这可能产生安全隐患。ASD诊断需要行为和发展证据，而非静态面部照片，因此需要审计VLM在此类无法回答的查询上的表现。

Method: 作者构建了PARITY（配对面部评估）数据集，这是一个合成的、人口统计均衡的、身份受控的中性/表情肖像配对集合，包含中性-中性对照。所有身份均为合成且无ASD状态。通过跨多个当代VLM的测试，评估模型是否拒答此类无法从图像回答的配对查询。

Result: 研究发现当代VLM明显分裂为拒答优先型和猜测型两类；在猜测型模型中，某些表情（如特定面部表达）会不成比例地触发有害的选择。此外，临床护栏和单图像框架能显著提高拒答率，表明提示词和界面设计方面存在可行的缓解措施。

Conclusion: VLMs在涉及医疗诊断判断的图像查询中存在严重的安全隐患，部分模型会进行无根据的推测性归因。通过临床护栏和界面设计的改进可以有效提升拒答行为，为减少VLM在敏感医疗场景中的滥用提供了可行的缓解方向。

Abstract: Many medical conditions require diagnosis through detailed, multi-context clinical assessment rather than from visual appearance alone. Despite this, vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly queried to interpret images in ways that touch on medical or diagnostic judgments, raising safety concerns when such inferences are unsupported. ASD diagnosis requires behavioral and developmental evidence, not static facial photographs. We audit whether VLMs abstain from this unanswerable paired-image query, and whether expressions sway non-abstaining choices.
  We introduce PARITY (Paired Assessment with Reused Identity), a synthetic, demographically balanced set of identity-controlled neutral/expression portrait pairs with neutral-neutral controls. All identities are synthetic and have no ASD status; because the query is unanswerable from images, any non-abstaining selection is treated as a harmful attribution. Across contemporary VLMs, we find a clear split between refusal-first models and speculative models; in the latter, certain expressions disproportionately trigger harmful selections. Clinical guardrails and single-image framing substantially increase abstention, suggesting actionable mitigations in both prompting and interface design

</details>


### [16] [Video Compression with Graph-inspired Neural Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05541)
*Changqi Wang,Ge Gao,Fan Zhang,Yue Li,Kai Zhang,Li Zhang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G-NeRV，一种图启发的隐式神经表示方法用于视频压缩。该方法在隐式潜在空间显式利用时间冗余，通过构建帧嵌入的时间邻域并进行消息传递，结合记忆银行机制实现高效的时间邻域检索，最终在UVG数据集上以BD-rate衡量，比最优的INR编解码器NVRC和最新标准编解码器VVC VTM分别节省8.86%和14.68%的码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于隐式神经表示的视频压缩方法，如INR，通常通过共享网络参数和帧索引嵌入隐式地利用视频中的时间冗余，这种方式可能导致压缩性能不够理想。作者希望显式地增强时间冗余的利用，以提升压缩效率。

Method: 该方法受到信息论中全相关（total correlation）原理的启发，为帧嵌入构建时间邻域，并进行消息传递以从相邻帧聚合可重用信息，通过自适应门控控制邻域信息的注入量。同时，受传统视频编码中参考帧缓冲区的启发，设计了记忆银行机制，用于在INR训练的随机帧索引采样下实现高效的时间邻域检索。整个模型被集成到一个先进的表示压缩框架中。

Result: 在UVG数据集上以PSNR和BD-rate为指标，G-NeRV相比于最先进的基于INR的编解码器NVRC提升8.86%，相比于最新标准视频编解码器VVC VTM提升14.68%。

Conclusion: 通过图结构启发的显式时间冗余建模，G-NeRV显著超越现有INR编解码器和标准视频编解码器的性能，验证了在隐式神经表示中显式利用时间相关性的有效性。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INR) provide a compact and content-adaptive paradigm for video compression, typically representing a video through shared network parameters and frame-indexed embeddings. Compared to conventional or autoencoder-based codecs, these approaches exploit temporal redundancy within videos in an implicit manner, which potentially results in sub-optimal compression performance. In this paper, we propose G-NeRV, a graph-inspired INR that explicitly improves temporal redundancy exploitation in the implicit latent space. Motivated by the total correlation principles in information theory, we construct a temporal neighborhood over frame embeddings and perform message passing to aggregate reusable information from neighboring frames through an adaptive gate controlling the injection of neighboring information. Inspired by the reference frame buffer in conventional video coding, a memory bank mechanism has been further designed to enable efficient temporal-neighbor retrieval under random frame-index sampling in INR training. This new representation model has been integrated into an advanced representation compression framework and compared with existing conventional and neural video codecs. The results show that the G-NeRV codec outperforms the state-of-the-art INR-based codec, NVRC, and the latest standard video codec, VVC VTM, by 8.86\% and 14.68\% (in BD-rate), respectively, measured by PSNR on the UVG dataset.

</details>


### [17] [Do Depressive Facial Patterns Transfer Across Cultures and Contexts? Evidence from a German RCT and E-DAIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05543)
*Misha Sadeghi,Robert Richer,Lydia Helene Rupp,Lena Schindler-Gmelch,Marie Keinert,Farnaz Rahimi,Malin Hager,Bernhard Egger,Matthias Berking,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于面部动态的抑郁症自动评估在跨语料库场景下的泛化问题，通过配对两个大型数据集（EmpkinS-EKSpression RCT和E-DAIC）进行双向迁移学习实验，发现二分类任务比连续回归任务更稳健（前向迁移AUC=0.70），且被动观察阶段的模型迁移性最好，功能情境对齐是跨语料库泛化的首要决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症自动评估在可扩展心理健康监测中具有重要潜力，但从面部动态中学到的生物标志物在跨语料库（跨数据源）场景下的泛化能力仍然是一个未解决的开放挑战。

Method: 采用系统性的双向迁移研究设计，配对两个数据集：EmpkinS-EKSpression随机对照试验（N=256，使用SCID-5-CV诊断）与E-DAIC语料库（N=275，半结构化临床访谈）。从面部动作单元、头部姿态和注视方向提取特征，预测抑郁严重程度和二元诊断状态，并比较不同功能情境（被动观察 vs 主动情绪调节）下的迁移效果。

Result: 跨语料库二分类比连续PHQ-8严重程度回归更稳健，前向迁移达到AUC=0.70；回归迁移受功能情境对齐调控，被动观察阶段产生最具迁移性的模型，而主动情绪调节阶段在语料库内信号更强。

Conclusion: 功能情境对齐是决定跨语料库泛化性能的首要因素，被动诱发情境在语料库内敏感性与跨语料库稳健性之间提供了最佳平衡。

Abstract: Automated assessment of depression from facial dynamics holds promise for scalable mental health monitoring, yet cross-corpus generalization of learned biomarkers remains an open challenge. We present a systematic bidirectional transfer study pairing the EmpkinS-EKSpression randomized controlled trial (RCT; N = 256, SCID-5-CV diagnoses) with the Extended Distress Analysis Interview Corpus (E-DAIC; N = 275, semi-structured clinical interviews), predicting depression severity and binary diagnostic status from facial action units, head pose, and gaze. Cross-corpus binary classification proves more robust than continuous PHQ-8 severity regression, with forward transfer achieving AUC = 0.70. Regression transfer is governed by functional context alignment: passive observation phases yield the most transferable models, while active emotion regulation phases elicit stronger within-corpus signals. These findings establish functional context alignment as the primary determinant of cross-corpus generalization, with passive elicitation contexts offering the best trade-off between within-corpus sensitivity and cross-corpus robustness.

</details>


### [18] [Subject-Relative Micro-Motion and Sleep Dynamics for Near-Infrared Video Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05550)
*Kunmin Jang,You Rim Choi,Hun Heo,Heonjun Lee,Suahn Bae,Dongik Park,Hyun-Woo Shin,Hyung-Sin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViNUSS框架，直接从NIR视频推断睡眠分期，无需重建生理代理信号或辅助生理监督。在475晚约3250小时的NIR视频上，四类睡眠分期达到0.80准确率和0.78宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的睡眠分期方法通常将NIR视频作为重建呼吸/心搏代理或跨模态生理表征的中间途径，本文旨在验证NIR视频本身是否可直接提供睡眠分期证据，而非仅作为恢复生理代理信号的输入。

Method: 提出ViNUSS框架，包含三大组件：(1)空间锚定的预空间微运动编码，保留局部时间变化及其空间上下文；(2)被试内阶段对比学习，利用夜间基线学习阶段线索；(3)双尺度睡眠动力学建模，捕捉epoch内运动演化并组织全夜睡眠阶段轨迹。

Result: 在475晚NIR记录（约3250小时）上，ViNUSS四类睡眠分期达到0.80准确率和0.78宏F1。可解释性分析显示模型关注胸腹周期性运动和与觉醒、体位改变相关的大幅身体运动。

Conclusion: 结果表明NIR视频本身可作为PSG定义睡眠分期估计的独立信息源和互补模态，支持视频原生、无媒介的睡眠分期方法的可行性。

Abstract: Near-infrared (NIR) video is a promising modality for contactless sleep monitoring, but recent video-based sleep staging methods often use it as a route to reconstructed respiratory/cardiac proxies or cross-modal physiological representations. We study video-only sleep staging under labels defined by polysomnography (PSG), where the model infers sleep stages from NIR video alone without explicit physiological proxy reconstruction or auxiliary physiological signal supervision. This tests whether NIR video itself can provide informative sleep-stage evidence, rather than only serving as an input for recovering physiological proxies. We propose ViNUSS (Video-Native Unmediated Sleep Staging), a framework that combines subject-relative micro-motion learning with full-night sleep dynamics modeling. Spatially anchored pre-spatial micro-motion encoding preserves localized temporal variation together with its spatial context. Within-subject stage contrast learns stage cues with respect to each subject's night-specific baseline. Two-scale sleep dynamics modeling captures within-epoch motion evolution and organizes epoch-level evidence into a coherent full-night sleep-stage trajectory. On 475 overnight NIR recordings (~3,250 hours), ViNUSS achieves 0.80 accuracy and 0.78 macro-F1 for four-class sleep staging. Interpretability analysis suggests attention to thoraco-abdominal periodic motion and gross body movements associated with arousals and position changes. These results support NIR video as an independently informative and complementary modality for PSG-defined sleep-stage estimation

</details>


### [19] [ViT3Flow: A Test-Time Training Transformer MeanFlow for Postoperative Radiograph Synthesis in Scoliosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05579)
*Rui Tang,Sicheng Yang,Moxin Zhao,Hongqiu Wang,Guankun Wang,Lei Zhu,Hongliang Ren,Menglin Cong,Nan Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于术前X光片生成术后脊柱形态图像的新方法ViT³Flow，用于辅助脊柱侧弯手术规划。作者构建了首个配对的术前-术后全脊柱X光片数据集ScoliSurg（632对），并提出ViT³Flow框架，通过test-time-training token mixers和Diagnosis-Routed Interval Cross-Attention（DRICA）机制，在单次网络评估中实现高效且解剖学上保真的术后放射影像合成，在感知质量、解剖保真度和临床几何准确性方面均优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术前预测术后脊柱形态能为脊柱侧弯手术规划提供重要支持，但手术矫正引起的大范围空间变化与解剖结构保真之间的矛盾使这一任务极具挑战性。现有方法缺乏配对的术前-术后数据且难以处理脊柱形态的复杂空间变换。

Method: 将问题形式化为术后脊柱侧弯放射影像合成。具体方法包括：（1）构建首个配对数据集ScoliSurg（632对全脊柱X光片）；（2）提出ViT³Flow，一个单次NFE的条件MeanFlow框架，将手术矫正建模为有限区间生成传输；（3）用test-time-training token mixers替代传统自注意力，实现样本特定的内适应；（4）设计Spinal Morphology Extraction Agent提取主弯区域和方向的分布；（5）提出DRICA机制，在传输过程中从独立术前token流进行区间相关的垂直、水平、联合和全局检索。

Result: 在ScoliSurg数据集上的大量实验表明，ViT³Flow在感知图像质量、解剖保真度和临床相关几何准确性方面均达到最佳性能，且仅需单次网络评估，显示了其在手术规划中高效且解剖学保真的术后影像合成潜力。

Conclusion: ViT³Flow为脊柱侧弯手术规划中的术后影像合成提供了高效且解剖学可靠的解决方案，通过创新的token mixing和跨注意力机制有效解决了手术矫正中大尺度空间变化与结构保留的矛盾。

Abstract: Predicting postoperative spinal morphology from preoperative radiographs could provide valuable support for scoliosis surgical planning, but remains challenging because surgical correction induces large spatial changes while anatomical structures must be faithfully retained. We formulate this problem as postoperative scoliosis radiograph synthesis and construct ScoliSurg, the first paired dataset for this task, comprising 632 preoperative--postoperative whole-spine radiograph pairs with structured morphology information. We further propose ViT$^{3}$Flow, a single-NFE conditional MeanFlow framework for efficient postoperative radiograph synthesis. ViT$^{3}$Flow models surgical correction as finite-interval generative transport and replaces conventional self-attention with test-time-training token mixers that perform sample-specific inner adaptation to the anatomy and deformity pattern of each case. In addition, a Spinal Morphology Extraction Agent extracts distributions of dominant-curve region and direction from the preoperative radiograph. These distributions guide Diagnosis-Routed Interval Cross-Attention (DRICA), which performs interval-dependent vertical, horizontal, joint, and global retrieval from a separate preoperative token stream. This design enables the evolving postoperative representation to incorporate spatially corresponding anatomical evidence throughout the transport process. Extensive experiments on ScoliSurg demonstrate that ViT$^{3}$Flow achieves the best performance among the compared methods in perceptual image quality, anatomical fidelity, and clinically relevant geometric accuracy, while requiring only a single network evaluation. These results highlight the potential of ViT$^{3}$Flow for efficient and anatomically faithful postoperative radiograph synthesis in scoliosis surgical planning.

</details>


### [20] [An overview of 3D Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05583)
*Márcus Lobo,Vitor Matias,Afonso Paiva,Jeová Farias,Tiago Novello,Moacir Ponti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于3D视觉-语言模型（3D VLMs）的教程综述，涵盖了从3D表示定义、嵌入编码、跨模态对比对齐到现代多模态框架和3D视觉大语言模型（3D VLLMs）的系统性介绍，并展望了语言引导的3D高斯溅射、3D形状生成及具身AI等前沿方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D深度学习模型通常针对特定任务（如分类、分割、检测）训练，无法自然支持以文本或图像为查询的跨模态检索，需要借鉴CLIP方法将3D嵌入与预训练的图文表征对齐，从而构建支持零样本分类和开放词汇识别的3D VLMs。

Method: 该教程系统性地梳理了3D VLMs的技术路线：首先介绍3D表示的定义及其编码为嵌入的方法，其次讲解跨模态对比对齐的原理与对比学习定义，然后介绍现代多模态框架和3D视觉大语言模型（3D VLLMs），最后展望语言引导的3D高斯溅射、3D形状生成和具身AI在机器人领域的应用。

Result: 教程涵盖了3D VLMs从基础概念到最新进展的完整框架，展示了基于CLIP的方法在实现3D形状的零样本分类、跨模态检索和开放词汇识别方面的能力，并总结了近期在3D VLLMs及相关前沿领域的研究进展。

Conclusion: 3D VLMs通过对比学习实现3D嵌入与视觉/文本表征的对齐，正推动3D理解走向多模态统一框架，未来方向包括语言引导的3D生成、3D高斯溅射渲染以及面向机器人应用的具身AI。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are reshaping computer vision by aligning visual and textual embeddings, allowing models to recognize visual concepts and reason about them using natural language. Traditional 3D deep-learning models, however, are typically trained for specific tasks, such as classification, segmentation, or detection, and do not naturally support cross-modal retrieval from their embedding spaces using text or images as queries. To address this issue, Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP)-based methods align 3D embeddings with pretrained image and text representations, giving rise to 3D Vision-Language Models (3D VLMs) that support zero-shot classification, cross-modal retrieval, and open-vocabulary recognition of 3D shapes. This tutorial provides an overview of 3D VLMs, ranging from basic definitions of 3D representations and their encoding into embeddings to cross-modal contrastive alignment, modern multimodal frameworks, and 3D Vision-Large Language Models (3D VLLMs). We present the main definitions of contrastive learning for multimodal embedding alignment and highlight recent advances in language-guided 3D Gaussian splatting, 3D shape generation, and embodied AI for robotics.

</details>


### [21] [SceneMosaic: Efficient and Diverse Simulation-Ready Scene Generation via Hybrid Agentic Layout Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05594)
*Xingjian Ran,Xiaoye Mo,Sihao Liu,Jianyu Zhang,Li Luo,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SceneMosaic框架，结合参数化图像到3D场景模型的高效性与基于VLM的智能体管线的高保真度，通过将场景分解为独立局部单元分别进化再用笛卡尔积组合全局场景，在SceneEval-100基准上达到与最强智能体基线相当的语义布局质量的同时实现24倍加速，并显著减少物理违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内场景生成面临两大范式各自的局限：基于VLM的智能体管线生成保真度高但需要昂贵的迭代式物体放置与精炼过程；参数化图像到3D场景模型生成高效但常产生不精确且物理无效的场景。此外，两种范式都难以针对单一输入输出多样化场景，无法反映真实场景的动态变化特性。

Method: SceneMosaic首先从基于图像先验的学习中获得初始候选场景，随后通过VLM智能体对结果进行进化，兼顾效率与物理有效性。在进化过程中利用自然场景的局部性，将场景分解为独立的局部单元，在每个单元内独立进化，最后通过笛卡尔积组合全局场景以保证输出的多样性。

Result: 在SceneEval-100基准上，SceneMosaic在语义布局质量上与最强智能体基线持平，同时实现了24倍的速度提升，大幅减少了物理违规，并获得了最高的人类评分。

Conclusion: SceneMosaic通过混合两种范式的优势，成功实现了室内场景的高效、物理有效且多样化生成，为交互式娱乐和具身智能应用提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Diverse and simulation-ready indoor scenes are essential for interactive entertainment and embodied AI, yet their scalable generation remains challenging. Recent agentic text-to-3D scene pipelines that rely on vision-language models (VLMs) can generate scenes of high fidelity but require costly iterative object placement and refinement. Another mainstream paradigm, parametric image-to-3D scene models, produces scenes efficiently from strong priors learned from 2D images but often leads to imprecise and physically invalid scenes. More importantly, both paradigms struggle to output diverse scenes for a single input, making it hard for them to reflect the dynamically changing nature of real scenes. In this paper we propose \textbf{SceneMosaic}, a framework that combines the merits of both paradigms. It obtains the initial candidate from the learned image-based prior, and subsequently evolves the result through VLM agents, ensuring both efficiency and physical validity. Within the evolution process, SceneMosaic exploits the locality of natural scenes and decomposes a scene into independent local units, allowing separate evolution within each unit before composing the global scene via Cartesian product. On SceneEval-100, SceneMosaic matches the strongest agentic baseline in semantic layout quality with a 24x speedup, substantially reduces physical violations, and receives the highest human ratings. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/rxjfighting/SceneMosaic.

</details>


### [22] [Time-Aware Assistive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05596)
*Masaki Kuribayashi,Zhongkai Shangguan,Eshed Ohn-Bar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项面向MLLM智能体的多模态基准测试，用于户外复杂环境中的自我中心辅助导航任务，揭示现成MLLM在实时、安全导航指令生成方面的不足，并通过直接监督指令背后的原因预测来显著提升性能，同时指出时间推理、安全关键物体感知等仍存在的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型很少规划是否以及何时向用户提供实时响应，但如引导视障人士穿越城市环境等场景需要精细的实时响应能力——不合时宜的响应会分散用户注意力或增加认知负担。当前缺乏针对这一时间敏感性问题的系统性评估和基准。

Method: 构建了一个大规模多模态基准测试，用于户外复杂环境中的自我中心辅助导航任务，并测试现成MLLM的开环、闭环及sim-to-real泛化性能。提出一种简单但有效的模型改进方案——加入直接监督以预测每条指令背后的原因，以提升时间敏感的指令生成能力。

Result: 现成MLLM即使经过大量数据微调，仍难以提供安全且时间敏感的导航指令；加入直接监督预测指令原因后，模型在开环、闭环和sim-to-real泛化设置下均获得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 尽管改进有效，分析仍揭示时间推理、安全关键物体感知以及关系与距离理解方面的持续性挑战。作者计划发布仿真环境、基准和代码以推动可扩展辅助智能体的发展。

Abstract: Can interactive vision-and-language agents learn not just what to say but also \textbf{\textit{when}} to say it? Current language models rarely plan over whether and when to realize a real-time response to a user. However, providing accurate and timely support for human decision-making, such as when guiding visually impaired individuals through urban environments, requires careful real-time responsiveness--poorly timed responses can distract users or add unnecessary cognitive load. As a machine intelligence challenge for Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based agents, we introduce a large-scale multimodal benchmark for an egocentric, assistive navigation task in complex outdoor environments. Using this benchmark, we uncover a fundamental limitation of off-the-shelf MLLMs in delivering safe and time-sensitive navigation instructions, even with model fine-tuning on substantial amounts of data. We then demonstrate that a simple yet effective modification of the model, including direct supervision to predict the underlying reason for each instruction, yields significant performance gains across open-loop, closed-loop, and sim-to-real generalization settings. However, our analysis highlights persistent challenges in temporal reasoning, safety-critical object awareness, and relational and distance understanding. To advance the development of scalable assistive agents, we will release our simulation, benchmark, and code (available at the project website: https://timeli-icra.github.io/).

</details>


### [23] [Unsupervised Transfer Clustering for Mitigating Cold Start in Active Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05636)
*André Camargo Portella,Samuel Felipe dos Santos,Jurandy Almeida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UTC+SQ框架，利用无监督迁移模型的投影聚类替代传统的基于距离的聚类，以解决主动提示学习中冷启动问题和查询子集代表性不足的缺陷，在8个数据集的6个上取得了精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动提示学习(APL)结合主动学习和提示学习以减少标注成本，但仍面临冷启动问题，即初始查询性能可能差于随机采样。现有SOTA方法虽采用平衡采样和多模态特征，但依赖刚性的基于距离的聚类，难以捕捉VLM特征空间中复杂的高维语义分布，导致查询代表性不佳。无监督迁移能够在不需监督的情况下推断任务的人类标注分布，可作为替代方案。

Method: 提出UTC+SQ（无监督迁移聚类与选择性查询）框架，该方法用当前最优的无监督迁移模型替代传统的基于距离的聚类方式，生成高保真伪标签以建立语义一致的聚类结构，从而选择更具代表性的样本进行标注，增强了现有的主动提示学习方法。

Result: 实验表明，从基于距离的聚类转向基于投影的聚类能够提升查询子集的代表性，在测试的8个数据集中有6个取得了精度提升。

Conclusion: 无监督迁移聚类相比距离基聚类能更好地捕捉VLM语义分布，有效改善主动提示学习中样本选择的质量，为APL方法的特征聚类环节提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are able to achieve impressive zero-shot classification performance by aligning visual and textual representations, but each new task still demands handcrafted prompts. Active Prompt Learning (APL) combines Active Learning (AL) and Prompt Learning (PL) into a single framework, allowing for the usage of the VLM prior knowledge for iteratively querying the most informative images to be labeled. However, the cold-start problem is still relevant for APL methods, where the performance of the initial query can be worse than random sampling. While recent state-of-the-art APL methods mitigate with balanced sampling and multimodal features, they rely on rigid, distance-based clustering to group these features. This simplistic approach can struggle to capture the complex, high-dimensional semantic distributions inherent to VLMs, leading to suboptimal query representativeness. Unsupervised transfer can be applied to these features as a possible alternative, since it is capable of inferring the underlying human labeling of a task without any form of supervision. This way, samples can be grouped in semantically coherent clusters. This paper proposes Unsupervised Transfer Clustering with Selective Querying (UTC+SQ), a framework that enhances a recent APL approach by leveraging state-of-the-art unsupervised transfer model. These models generate high-fidelity pseudo-labels that establish semantically meaningful clusters, allowing for the selection of more relevant samples. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that shifting from distance-based to projection-based clustering improves the representativeness of the queried subset, achieving accuracy gains in 6 of the 8 datasets tested.

</details>


### [24] [Facial Age Estimation for Age Fraud Detection in National ID Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05638)
*Sharib Athar,Arka Koner,Chetan Naik,Barada P. Sabut,Tanusree Deb Barma,Anoop M. Namboodiri,Anil K. Jain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SwinAge，一个基于SwinFace架构的人脸年龄估计系统，用于Aadhaar（全球最大国家身份系统）生物特征注册流程中辅助质检人员识别年龄相关欺诈。该系统在145万张人脸图像上训练，在28.3万张独立测试集上评估，针对5/18/60岁三个操作阈值报告FAR/FRR，MAE为2.94年，优于三个零样本视觉语言模型，并在7个公开基准中的5个上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模国家身份系统中，生物特征注册和更新环节存在严重的身份欺诈问题，尤其是虚报年龄以获取年龄限制服务或福利。需要一种可靠的面部年龄估计系统来辅助人工质检，识别潜在的年龄欺诈行为。

Method: 基于SwinFace架构，采用基于关键点的相似性（warp affine）对齐方法。在包含716K独特个体、145万张面部图像的大型内部数据集上训练，在283K张年龄分层独立测试集上评估。设计了针对Aadhaar特定操作阈值（5、18、60岁）的部署分诊框架，建议标志可疑案件并进行人工复核。按照NIST FATE标准报告FAR/FRR而非聚合精度。

Result: 在1% FAR下，模型在<5岁、>18岁和>60岁阈值分别达到3%、0.4%和11.0%的FRR。MAE为2.94年，优于三个零样本视觉语言模型，并在7个公开基准数据集的5个上改善SOTA。论文还报告了分性别的误差分析。

Conclusion: SwinAge展示了在真实大规模国家身份系统中部署面部年龄估计的可行性，为质检人员提供有效的年龄欺诈筛查工具，并为国家身份计划提供了经验教训。

Abstract: Identity fraud during biometric enrollment and updates remains a major challenge for large-scale national identity systems. A common fraud vector is misrepresenting one's age to access age-restricted services or welfare schemes. In this work, we present SwinAge, a facial age estimation system designed for use within the Aadhaar biometric enrollment pipeline, to assist quality-check (QC) operators to flag potential age-related fraud. This is critical for a system like Aadhaar (the world's largest national identity programme), that holds about 1.5 billion unique identities, with 22.4 million new enrollments and 283 million updates in the last year. Building upon the SwinFace architecture with landmark-based similarity (warp affine) alignment, we train on a large in-house dataset of 1.45 million face images and evaluate on an independent, age-stratified test set of 283K images, both drawn from an ethnically diverse population of 716K unique subjects. We investigate three Aadhaar-specific operational thresholds (5, 18, and 60 years) and propose a deployment triage framework that flags suspected cases for manual review. Following NIST FATE, we report false acceptance/rejection rates (FAR/FRR) at each threshold rather than aggregate accuracy: at 1% FAR the model achieves an FRR of 3% (<5yrs), 0.4% (>18yrs) and 11.0% (>60yrs). SwinAge achieves a mean absolute error (MAE) of 2.94 years on the same test set, outperforming three zero-shot vision language models on all benchmarks, and improving the state-of-the-art on 5 out of 7 public benchmark datasets. We further report per-gender errors and distill lessons for national identity programs.

</details>


### [25] [Srijika: OpenType-Layout-Reusing Font Restyling for Nine Indic Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05661)
*Anil Pai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Srijika是一个用于生成九种天城文（Brahmic）脚本可安装OpenType字体的系统。它通过复用模板字体的cmap/GSUB/GPOS数据、对字形轮廓进行重绘（restyling）来生成完整字体，而非从零构建。系统产出66个TTF，全部通过OpenType Sanitizer验证。评估表明在多数情况下直接复制模板优于生成模型，文章同时提供了详尽的负面结果目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度系（Brahmic）字体生成的核心难题在于：成百上千的连接符（conjuncts）、半字形（half forms）和元音符号（matra variants）必须在OpenType shaping下保持相互一致。从零生成字体会破坏这种一致性，因此需要一种能保留排版逻辑而仅重绘外观的方案。

Method: 采用"布局复用"（layout-reusing）范式：Srijika从具备完整shaping能力的模板字体中提取字形轮廓并重新绘制，保留模板的cmap和GSUB闭包以及GPOS数据（在文档化的度量策略下），确保输出的每个字体都是结构完整的。风格选择使用Lipika检索索引（覆盖约650个开源字体系列），再通过参考条件潜在扩散模型（reference-conditioned latent diffusion model）重绘字形，随后进行内容门控（content gating）、协调（harmonization）和shaped-cluster验证，失败时回退到模板轮廓。

Result: Srijika生成66个TTF（57个精选预设和9个开放词汇展示字体），全部通过OpenType Sanitizer。完整闭包审计覆盖80,915个字形和54,812个锚点以量化度量变化。在扩散训练族留置的SSIM门控测试中，直接复制模板在56个字体中的50个上优于生成方法。风格移动仅在内部同模型嵌入（训练语料包含留置族）下可测，结论需谨慎对待。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：（1）布局复用的字体生成范式和流水线；（2）九种脚本的审计与基准；（3）涵盖失败条件设置、目标函数选择和参考引导重绘数据边界限制的负面结果目录。作者明确指出，独立风格度量、学习型基线和人工研究不在本报告范围内，反映了当前方法的局限。

Abstract: We present Srijika, a system for producing installable OpenType fonts for nine Brahmic scripts: Devanagari, Tamil, Bengali, Telugu, Kannada, Malayalam, Gujarati, Gurmukhi, and Odia. Rather than generating fonts from scratch, Srijika restyles glyph outlines from shaping-complete template fonts. It preserves the template's cmap and GSUB closure and its GPOS data under a documented metric policy, making every output a complete font by construction. This addresses a central challenge of Indic font generation: hundreds to thousands of conjuncts, half forms, and matra variants must remain mutually consistent under OpenType shaping.
  Srijika produces 66 TTFs: 57 curated presets and nine open-vocabulary showcase fonts. All pass the OpenType Sanitizer, while HarfBuzz and CoreText reproduce the template glyph-ID sequences on conjunct-heavy probes. A full-closure audit covering 80,915 glyphs and 54,812 anchors quantifies metric changes. Natural-language style selection uses Lipika, a retrieval index over approximately 650 open-license font families. A reference-conditioned latent diffusion model redraws template glyphs in the selected style, followed by content gating, harmonization, and shaped-cluster verification with fallback to template outlines.
  We evaluate against no-learning baselines. On diffusion-training-family-held-out SSIM gates, template copying outperforms generation on 50 of 56 faces. Style movement is measurable only with an internal same-model embedding whose training corpus includes the held-out families, so these results require caution. A learned baseline, independent style metric, and human study are outside this report's scope. Our contributions are the layout-reusing formulation and pipeline, its nine-script audit and benchmark, and a negative-results catalogue covering failed conditioning, objective choices, and data-hull limits of reference-guided restyling.

</details>


### [26] [Full-Page Optical Music Recognition of Handwritten Monophonic Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05662)
*Adrian Rosello,Antonio Ríos-Vila,David Rizo,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了手写乐谱的整页端到端光学音乐识别（OMR）任务，并分析了合成预训练在此场景中的影响。作者提出了一种能够生成排版和手写风格全页乐谱的生成器，以探究预训练中影响最显著的因素。在三个真实手写数据集上的实验表明，合成预训练的优势主要在于学习版面结构惯例，而非与目标手写风格的视觉相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OMR系统依赖精确的谱表分割，存在局限性；虽然基于Transformer的端到端方案在排版乐谱上表现优异，但其对手写乐谱的适用性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出了一种能够生成视觉连贯的全页乐谱生成器，可生成排版与手写两种风格；系统比较了多种全页处理流程与不同合成预训练策略，并在三个真实手写数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验发现合成预训练的主要收益来自于学习版面布局结构而不是视觉上与目标手写风格的相似性。

Conclusion: 合成数据在手写全页OMR预训练中的作用本质上是教会模型理解版面组织规律，而非模仿目标手写外观，这为未来合成数据设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Full-page end-to-end Optical Music Recognition seeks to transcribe entire music pages directly into symbolic notation, avoiding the limitations of traditional pipelines that rely on accurate staff segmentation. Recent Transformer-based architectures have achieved strong performance on typeset scores, relying on large-scale synthetic data for pretraining. However, their applicability to handwritten music remains largely unexplored. In this work, we study full-page transcription on handwritten monophonic collections and analyze the impact of synthetic pretraining in this setting. To investigate which factors are most relevant during pretraining, we introduce a generator capable of producing visually coherent full-page scores in both typeset and handwritten styles. Experiments on three real handwritten datasets provide a comparative evaluation of several full-page pipelines and different synthetic pretraining strategies. The results suggest that the benefits of synthetic pretraining are primarily associated with learning structural layout conventions rather than with visual similarity to the target handwriting.

</details>


### [27] [Bigger Text Encoders Can Hurt CLIP Zero-Shot Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05730)
*Samir Char,Carles Domingo-Enrich,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了CLIP模型中视觉与文本编码器之间容量分配对下游零样本性能的影响，发现过大的文本编码器会导致性能退化（即使总参数增加），通过优化编码器比例可在匹配ViT-B/16性能的同时减少最多55%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP是众多机器学习应用的基础组件，但现有缩放定律仅将总模型大小作为单一变量研究，忽略了视觉与文本编码器之间容量分配对下游性能的影响。作者旨在探索编码器容量分配的最优策略，以支撑CLIP的可靠高效扩展。

Method: 作者训练了多组具有不同视觉与文本编码器尺寸的CLIP模型，系统性地分析了零样本性能随编码器容量分配的变化。采用模态特异的权重衰减系数来缓解过拟合问题，并通过几何分析（嵌入均匀性与跨模态对齐）来解释性能退化的深层机制。

Result: 发现大多数视觉编码器存在一个最优文本编码器规模，超过该规模后零样本性能下降；利用该行为可在匹配ViT-B/16性能时减少最多55%参数。性能退化源于过大文本编码器的过拟合，模态特异的权重衰减不仅能恢复还能提升性能。几何分析揭示文本编码器缩放改善嵌入均匀性但损害跨模态对齐，这两种指标可预测零样本性能。

Conclusion: 编码器间的容量不平衡会导致CLIP性能退化，采用合理的容量分配与模态特异正则化策略可显著提升训练效率。该发现为设计更可靠的CLIP架构与训练方法提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) is a building block of many machine learning applications. Scaling laws have guided resource allocation for large-scale training, yet prior work treats total CLIP model size as a single variable, without exploring how the capacity split between encoders impacts downstream performance. Here, we train multiple CLIP models with different vision and text encoder sizes, revealing that for most vision encoders, there is an optimal text encoder size beyond which zero-shot performance degrades---even as total parameter count increases. Exploiting this behavior yields efficient configurations that match the zero-shot performance of the standard ViT-B/16 architecture with up to 55% fewer parameters. We further show that this degradation stems from overfitting induced by the oversized text encoder, and that using modality-specific weight decay coefficients not only recovers but improves performance across all degraded configurations. A geometric analysis reveals a trade-off in which scaling the text encoder improves embedding uniformity but worsens cross-modal alignment; we further show that these metrics are predictive of zero-shot performance. We hope these findings motivate CLIP architectures and training methods that counteract this degradation, a prerequisite for scaling CLIP reliably and efficiently.

</details>


### [28] [RenderFormer-V2: Neural Rendering with Heterogeneous Scene Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05738)
*Chong Zeng,Yue Dong,Pieter Peers,Lvmin Zhang,Maneesh Agrawala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenderFormer-V2是一个统一的基于Transformer的神经渲染模型，可作为现代物理渲染系统的补充。它通过序列到序列变换建模全局光传输，采用两阶段架构（视图无关的图元间传输阶段和视图相关的像素生成阶段），无需逐场景训练即可处理焦散、体积散射、环境光照、纹理与置换表面及分布外材质等多种光传输效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法在处理多样化的光传输效果（如焦散、体积散射等）时，通常需要逐场景训练或专门的代码支持，缺乏统一且可扩展的方案。RenderFormer-V2旨在通过改进注意力机制和异构场景图元支持，在保持渲染精度的同时提升模型的可扩展性与通用性。

Method: RenderFormer-V2采用两阶段流程：视图无关阶段解决场景内图元到图元的传输，提出结合窗口注意力和渲染感知注意力汇的新机制以提升可扩展性并保持渲染精度；视图相关阶段将内部神经场景表示转换为图像像素。模型支持包括环境贴图和参与介质在内的异构场景图元，并通过独立于底层表面反射模型的新型神经嵌入来编码材质外观。

Result: 作者在多种场景上展示了RenderFormer-V2的通用性，并对改进的注意力机制进行了广泛的消融实验，验证了其在处理焦散、体积散射、环境照明、纹理与置换表面及分布外材质等效果中的有效性。

Conclusion: RenderFormer-V2作为RenderFormer的扩展版本，通过注意力机制的创新（窗口注意力+渲染感知注意力汇）和异构图元及跨材质模型材质编码的支持，实现了无需逐场景训练或专门代码即可处理多样化光传输效果的统一神经渲染模型，为神经渲染与物理渲染系统的互补结合提供了更通用和可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We present 'RenderFormer-V2', a unified learned transformer-based neural rendering model, complementary to modern physics-based rendering systems, that can handle diverse light-transport effects such as caustics, volumetric scattering, environment lighting, textured and displaced surfaces and out-of-distribution materials without per-scene training or specialized code. RenderFormer-V2 models global light transport as a sequence-to-sequence transformation. Following its predecessor, RenderFormer-V2 also employs a two stage process: a view-independent stage that resolves intra-scene primitive to primitive transport, and a view-dependent stage that transforms the internal neural scene representation into image pixels. Different from RenderFormer, our model employs a novel combined windowed-attention and rendering-informed attention sink in the view-independent stage to improve scalability while maintaining render accuracy. To further improve versatility, RenderFormerV2 supports heterogeneous scene primitives, including environment maps and participating media, and it employs a material encoding independent of the underlying surface reflectance model that encodes material appearance via a novel neural embedding. We demonstrate the versatility of RenderFormer-V2 on a variety of scenes and perform an extensive ablation of the improved attention mechanism.

</details>


### [29] [SeRV: Semantic-Aligned Residual Vector Quantization for American Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05742)
*Hongyu Wu,Xu Wu,Tianhao Wu,Jiawei Yu,Phuc Nguyen,Jian Liu,Yi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeRV（语义对齐残差向量量化）分词器，通过语义对齐解决ASL生成中文本与3D动作之间的语义一致性问题，并构建大规模重建3D ASL动作-文本基准数据集，在两个数据集上取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASL生成方法面临两大挑战：配对文本-ASL动作数据稀缺，以及学习到的动作表征难以同时满足重建精度和文本可预测性。已有方法仅针对重建优化动作分词器，缺乏来自配对文本的显式语义监督，导致生成的ASL动作在语义一致性和细粒度方面存在局限。

Method: 提出SeRV语义对齐RVQ分词器：结合句子级动作-文本对齐与token级文本条件监督，学习语义结构化的残差token空间。在此基础上使用分层GPT按从粗到细的方式预测残差动作token。同时从YouTube-ASL视频中重建配对3D动作，构建大规模ASL动作-文本基准。

Result: 在375小时的ASL视频实验中，SeRV在How2Sign和YouTube-ASL数据集上均达到最先进的姿态精度，并能直接从文本生成语义一致的3D ASL动作。

Conclusion: 通过语义对齐的分词策略和分层粗到细生成框架，SeRV有效弥合了文本与3D动作表征之间的语义鸿沟，为文本驱动的ASL动作生成领域提供了新的技术路径和更丰富的数据资源。

Abstract: American Sign Language (ASL) generation remains challenging due to limited paired text-ASL motion data and the difficulty of learning motion representations both precise for reconstruction and predictable from linguistic input. Existing methods rely on motion tokenizers optimized for reconstruction, without explicit semantic supervision from paired text. As a result, the learned tokens remain limited in supporting semantically consistent and fine-grained ASL motion generation. To address this limitation, we propose SeRV (Semantic-Aligned Residual Vector Quantization), a semantic-aligned RVQ tokenizer for ASL generation. SeRV learns a semantically structured residual token space by combining sentence-level motion-text alignment with token-level text-conditioned supervision. Building on this tokenizer, a Hierarchical GPT predicts residual motion tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner, generating structurally coherent and semantically aligned 3D ASL motion. We further construct a large-scale reconstructed 3D ASL motion-text benchmark by recovering paired 3D motion from YouTube-ASL videos. Experiments across 375 hours of ASL video show that SeRV achieves state-of-the-art pose accuracy on both How2Sign and YouTube-ASL datasets, while producing semantically consistent 3D ASL motion directly from text.

</details>


### [30] [Gaussian Linear Functional Manifold Method for Massive Point Cloud Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05744)
*Hong Zhao,Tonglin Zhang,Baijian Yang,Jin Wei-Kocsis,Songlin Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GLFM（高斯线性函数流形）框架，用于从大规模非结构化机载LiDAR点云中重建连续地形表面。该框架采用确定性线性函数基表示连续地形，并用各向同性高斯过程建模微尺度漫反射激光后向散射。通过SVD秩约简算法将精确约束最大似然估计的二次计算成本降为线性时间，在35.2 km²真实LiDAR数据上实现ARI达0.9933，超越四种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的野火-城市交界（WUI）环境中，从大规模非结构化机载LiDAR点云重建连续地形流形仍具挑战性：深度神经网络需要昂贵的逐点标注，非参数曲面重建方法缺乏结构可解释性。现有方法难以兼顾精度、效率和可解释性。

Method: 提出GLFM（高斯线性函数流形）框架：1）用确定性线性函数基表示连续地表地形；2）用各向同性高斯过程建模微尺度漫反射激光后向散射；3）开发代数SVD秩约简算法，实现线性时间参数估计和闭式二次分类，避免精确约束最大似然估计的二次计算成本。

Result: 在35.2 km²真实机载LiDAR数据上，GLFM自动过滤地面点并提取形态特征，调整兰德指数（ARI）达0.9933（对照实地验证的真值），超越四种主流基线方法，同时保持核外内存占用（out-of-core memory footprint）。

Conclusion: GLFM为大规模点云分析提供了严谨、可解释且可扩展的基础框架，在保证高精度的同时兼顾计算效率和内存友好性，适用于复杂的WUI环境地形重建任务。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous terrain manifolds from massive, unstructured airborne LiDAR point clouds remains challenging in complex Wildland-Urban Interface (WUI) environments, where deep neural networks require costly point-wise annotations and nonparametric surface reconstruction methods often lack structural interpretability. This paper introduces the Gaussian Linear Functional Manifold (GLFM), a physics-informed statistical framework that represents continuous surface topography using deterministic linear functional bases while modeling microscale diffuse laser backscatter as an isotropic Gaussian process. To avoid the quadratic computational cost of exact constrained maximum likelihood estimation, we develop an algebraic singular value decomposition (SVD) rank-reduction algorithm that enables linear-time parameter estimation and closed-form quadric classification. Evaluated on 35.2 km^2 of real-world aerial LiDAR data, GLFM automatically filters ground points and extracts morphological features, achieving an adjusted Rand index (ARI) of 0.9933 against field-verified ground truth and outperforming four leading baselines while maintaining an out-of-core memory footprint. The framework provides a rigorous, interpretable, and scalable foundation for large-scale point cloud analytics.

</details>


### [31] [GeoContext: One Context Ladder, Two Failure Modes in Vision-Language Geolocation: Flat Reliance on User-Provided Location Context and False Confirmation of Location Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05761)
*Yifan Zhang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoContext基准，包含GeoHint（给定粗糙位置线索的开放定位）和GeoVerify（验证图像是否在声明位置150米内）两个任务，通过上下文阶梯分层设计，覆盖30个城市109个地点，对五个视觉语言模型进行了大规模评估。结果显示，提示距离增加时定位误差稳步上升，模型表现高度依赖无上下文基线性能，且所有模型在验证任务中均未达到d'=1的判别阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉地理定位基准忽视了用户常常提供的"位置上下文"信息（如"这张照片拍自巴黎"），仅让模型从零开始猜测拍摄位置。作者希望更真实地反映用户在提供位置线索时的定位和验证能力差异。

Method: 构建GeoContext基准，设计"上下文阶梯"（context ladder），根据距离和可参照性（referenceability）对附近参考点分层，使图像固定而上下文变化。包含GeoHint（开放定位）和GeoVerify（二元验证）两个互补任务，评估五个视觉语言模型，共分析21,933条GeoHint响应和6,270条GeoVerify响应。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 提示重复率在各分层间波动极小（1.5-3个百分点），但定位误差随提示距离稳步增加；(2) 模型行为强依赖无上下文准确率：低准确率地点中定位误差与提示距离比约1.00，高准确率地点则为0.24-0.69，偏差校正后仅一个模型在给定邻近提示时保持负误差估计；(3) GeoVerify任务中无模型达到d'=1，83.8%的错误接受伴随着0.8以上的置信度。

Conclusion: GeoContext揭示了视觉语言模型在带上下文的地理定位中系统性能力不足：模型对提示的利用效果强烈依赖于其基础定位能力，且即使声称高置信度也会产生大量误接受。作者开源了完整的基准、构建流程、审计决策和评分代码，以便社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Visual geolocation benchmarks typically ask a model where an image was captured without accounting for the location context that users often provide. We introduce GeoContext, a resource supporting two complementary tasks: GeoHint, open-ended localization given a true but coarse location hint, and GeoVerify, binary verification of whether an image was taken within 150 m of a claimed place. GeoContext constructs a context ladder by stratifying nearby reference points according to distance and referenceability, allowing the image to remain fixed while the supplied context varies. The benchmark covers 109 sites in 30 cities and evaluates five vision-language models using 21,933 GeoHint responses and 6,270 GeoVerify responses.
  Our evaluation reveals three main patterns. First, hint repetition varies by only 1.5 percentage points across referenceability tiers and by less than 3 points across distance bands, while the resulting localization error increases steadily with hint distance. Second, behavior depends strongly on no-context performance: at sites with low no-context accuracy, the median ratio between localization error and hint distance is approximately 1.00, whereas at higher-accuracy sites it ranges from 0.24 to 0.69. After correcting for bias introduced by the site grouping procedure, only one of the five models retains a negative accuracy estimate when given a nearby hint. Third, in GeoVerify, no model reaches d' = 1 for decoys immediately beyond the 150 m tolerance. Model rankings also change when sensitivity is separated from response bias, and 83.8% of false acceptances are reported with confidence of at least 0.8. We release the benchmark, construction pipeline, audit decisions, and scoring code.

</details>


### [32] [AAMBERS-UAV: Acquisition-Aware Multimodal Backbone Evaluation and Ranking for UAV Weedy Rice Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05762)
*Tarek Rahman,Nazim-E-Alam,Md Kishor Morol,Jannatun Noor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出UAV图像语义分割基准在图像层面切分数据会导致同一采集航次的数据同时出现在训练和测试集中，掩盖模型对新采集区域的真实泛化能力。基于WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB数据集，作者提出"采集感知"的评估协议（目标保留vs目标暴露），比较RGB、四波段MS和七通道RGB+MS三种输入下的SegFormer-B0性能，发现采集暴露会提升所有骨干网络的测量性能，但其对模态排序的影响依赖于网络架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV影像采集具有时空关联性，但现有语义分割基准普遍在图像层面随机切分数据，可能将同一采集航次的样本同时放入训练和测试集，导致模型评估结果虚高，无法真实反映对全新采集区域的泛化能力。

Method: 使用734样本的WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB数据集，固定124张目标采集测试集，在相同训练/验证/测试样本数量下比较两种协议：目标保留（开发集排除目标采集）和目标暴露（允许目标采集其余图像进入开发集）。采用固定两套切分种子，评估SegFormer-B0在RGB、四波段MS及RGB+MS七通道三种输入下的性能，并用U-Net/ResNet18固定切分复现验证暴露增益；同时通过供给切分审计检测序列依赖，并加入RGB-MS位移和辐射缩放的对抗测试。

Result: 完全采集保留下RGB最强（IoU 0.7317±0.0201），而目标暴露后RGB+MS变为最强（0.7822±0.0269）。U-Net/ResNet18复现确认三种输入均获得正面的暴露增益，但RGB仍是两种协议下的最佳模态。采集暴露一致提升两个骨干网络的测量性能，对模态排序的影响则依赖架构。抗扰测试显示早期融合对RGB-MS空间错位比对中等辐射缩放更敏感。

Conclusion: 采集感知的同测试评估应作为多模态UAV基准中普通图像层切分的必要补充，以确保模型评估能真实反映跨采集泛化能力。数据中存在强序列依赖性，提示在设计UAV语义分割基准时应更重视采集航次级别的数据划分。

Abstract: UAV image collections contain spatially and temporally related frames, yet semantic-segmentation benchmarks commonly split them at image level. Such splitting can place samples from one acquisition in both model development and testing, obscuring transfer to a genuinely new survey. Using the 734-sample WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB, we fix a 124-image target-acquisition test set and compare two protocols with identical train, validation, and test counts: target-held-out, which excludes the target acquisition from development, and target-exposed, which admits its remaining images. SegFormer-B0 is evaluated with RGB, four-band multispectral (MS), and seven-channel RGB+MS input over two fixed-split seeds. RGB is strongest under complete acquisition holdout ($0.7317\pm0.0201$ IoU), whereas RGB+MS becomes strongest after target exposure ($0.7822\pm0.0269$). A fixed-split U-Net/ResNet18 replication confirms positive exposure gains for all three inputs, but retains RGB as the best modality under both protocols. Acquisition exposure therefore increases measured performance across both evaluated backbones, while its effect on modality ranking is architecture-dependent. A supplied-split audit reveals strong near-sequential dependence, and corruption tests show that early fusion is substantially more sensitive to RGB--MS displacement than to moderate radiometric scaling. These results support acquisition-aware same-test evaluation as a necessary complement to ordinary image-level splitting in multimodal UAV benchmarks. The code and supporting the findings of this study will be publicly released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [33] [MolParser-Mobile: Ultrafast OCSR System for Large-Scale Chemical Literature Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05807)
*Xi Fang,Haocheng Lu,Han Lyu,Chengxiang Luo,Linfeng Zhang,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MolParser-Mobile，一个AutoML优化的轻量级端到端光学化学结构识别（OCSR）框架，仅含9.98M参数，在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU上吞吐量达每秒1,520个分子，同时保持竞争性甚至优于现有方法的识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCSR是化学文献挖掘的基础组件，尽管近期深度学习方法在识别精度上取得了显著进展，但推理吞吐量仍是制约其大规模网络部署的关键瓶颈。

Method: 采用AutoML优化的轻量级端到端框架MolParser-Mobile，通过自动化机器学习技术进行模型架构搜索与优化，在保持识别性能的同时大幅压缩模型参数规模至9.98M。

Result: 模型仅含9.98M参数，在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU上达到每秒1,520个分子的推理吞吐量，且在多个基准测试上保持竞争性甚至更优的识别精度。

Conclusion: MolParser-Mobile证明了在保持甚至超越识别精度的前提下，通过AutoML优化实现轻量化设计，可以有效解决OCSR推理吞吐瓶颈，满足web级大规模部署需求。

Abstract: Optical Chemical Structure Recognition (OCSR) is a fundamental component of chemical literature mining, enabling molecular database construction, reaction extraction, and AI-driven scientific discovery. Despite substantial progress in recognition accuracy with recent deep learning-based methods, inference throughput remains a critical bottleneck that limits web-scale deployment. To address this challenge, we propose MolParser-Mobile, an AutoML-optimized lightweight end-to-end OCSR framework. MolParser-Mobile contains only 9.98M parameters, while reaching a throughput of 1,520 molecules per second on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU. Despite its compact design, it maintains competitive and, on several benchmarks, superior recognition accuracy.

</details>


### [34] [CoRe-SAM3: Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation for SAM3 Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05816)
*Shipeng Liu,Liang Zhao,Dengfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe（条件语义-视觉调和）方法，通过保留SAM3的语义预测作为主要决策路径，利用轻量级语义校准和零初始化的条件残差修正，在五个裂缝数据集上将Crack IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%，clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%，仅引入18.914K可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂缝分割要求模型同时识别目标语义并准确恢复细薄、低对比度且拓扑连续的局部结构，尽管SAM3提供了强大的开放概念分割能力，但直接应用于裂缝领域仍存在漏检弱裂缝、激活类裂缝背景区域及局部边界错误等问题，需要探究内部表征的功能差异以进行有效的领域适配。

Method: 首先在五个裂缝数据集上诊断SAM3内部提示条件语义表征与原生视觉表征的功能差异，发现语义表征已承载大部分任务信息而视觉表征效用依赖当前语义状态。基于此提出CoRe：保留语义预测为主决策路径，采用轻量级语义校准调整目标域决策映射，并使用空间对齐的原生视觉证据生成零初始化、有界且正则化的条件残差来选择性修正现有预测。

Result: 在五个域上，CoRe-SAM3将平均Crack IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%，clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%，仅引入18.914K可训练参数。预测转换分析显示CoRe平均修正了34.38%的原生错误，对原生SAM3正确分类像素的损坏率仅为0.23%。

Conclusion: 基于内部表征功能差异的约束性预测修正，为具有强任务特定语义先验的视觉基础模型提供了一种有效且参数高效的目标域适配策略。

Abstract: Crack segmentation requires a model to recognize target semantics while accurately recovering thin, low-contrast, and topologically continuous local structures. Although SAM3 provides strong open-concept segmentation, its direct application to the crack domain still misses weak cracks, activates crack-like background regions, and produces local boundary errors.
