This structure motivates Joint-Conditioned Stereo Surface Reasoning (JSSR). A temporal-stereo network jointly predicts 3D joints, a direct interaction field, and per-view endpoint evidence. Calibrated candidate search evaluates endpoint hypotheses using joint-specific image compatibility and cross-view correspondence. A hand-shared candidate support lets joints draw on common surface evidence, and a learned residual gate controls the geometric correction when observations are ambiguous. Our system built on this method ranked third on the SHOW3D Interaction Field Challenge leaderboard.

</details>


### [136] [MSSP: Multi-Scale Spatially-Constrained Partition for Unsupervised Semantic Segmentation of 3D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06959)
*Zhenghao Zhang,Xinjie Wang,Wei Wang,Jun Zhang,Hanyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSSP框架，结合多尺度谱分析与空间约束聚类进行无监督3D点云语义分割，在S3DIS和ScanNet上达到最优mIoU。核心发现：多尺度特征需与空间正则化配合才能发挥效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云语义分割对真实世界空间理解至关重要，但人工标注成本过高，需要无监督方法。现有基于超点的方法通常依赖固定粒度的谱分析，难以捕捉复杂室内场景中固有的层次化语义结构。

Method: 提出MSSP（Multi-Scale Spatially-Constrained Partition）框架：1) 多尺度谱分析，在多个聚类粒度下构建丰富的超点描述符；2) 空间约束聚类，将超点合并且限制在物理相邻区域内，提供空间一致性先验以利用高维多尺度特征空间。

Result: 在S3DIS和ScanNet基准上，MSSP在无监督方法中取得最佳mIoU，尤其在S3DIS上提升显著。消融实验揭示"先正则化后增强"的交互机制：仅靠多尺度特征无法改善最终分割效果，但结合空间正则化后变得非常有效。

Conclusion: 空间一致性是多尺度表示在超点聚类中发挥作用的前提条件。多尺度特征与空间正则化的结合，能够更有效地逼近真实世界空间理解所需的层次化语义结构。

Abstract: 3D point cloud semantic segmentation is essential for real-world spatial understanding, yet the prohibitive cost of human annotations motivates unsupervised approaches that require no labels. Existing superpoint-based methods typically rely on spectral analysis at a fixed granularity, failing to capture the hierarchical semantic structures inherent in complex indoor scenes. To bridge this gap, we present a Multi-Scale Spatially-Constrained Partition (MSSP) framework that combines multi-scale spectral analysis with spatially-constrained clustering. Multi-scale spectral analysis constructs enriched superpoint descriptors across multiple clustering granularities; however, the resulting high-dimensional feature space calls for a structural prior to translate into cleaner segmentation. Spatially-constrained clustering supplies this prior by restricting superpoint merging to physically adjacent regions, imposing the spatial coherence needed for multi-scale features to be effective. Extensive experiments on S3DIS and ScanNet show that MSSP achieves the best mIoU among unsupervised methods on the main benchmarks, with particularly significant gains on S3DIS. Notably, our ablation reveals a regularize-then-enrich interaction: multi-scale features alone do not improve final segmentation, yet become highly effective when coupled with spatial regularization, underscoring that spatial coherence is aprerequisite for multi-scale representations in superpoint clustering.

</details>


### [137] [DPSF-Net: A Dual-Prior Spatial-Frequency Network for Real-World Remote Sensing Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06962)
*Mei Lu,Shangliang Shao,Shanliang Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPSF-Net，一种基于双先验空间-频率网络的真实遥感图像去雾方法。该方法以有雾RGB图像和暗通道先验（DCP）图作为联合输入，通过空间-频率残差交互模块、先验引导特征注意力模块和选择性核互补融合模块，在真实世界RRSHID基准上取得了最先进性能，并在多个合成数据集上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实遥感图像去雾面临大气散射、空间不均匀雾霾和色彩失真共同作用的挑战，现有深度学习方法仅依赖RGB输入和空间域特征提取，难以有效区分全局背景雾霾与局部地表细节，限制了去雾性能。

Method: 该方法基于MCAF-Net构建双先验空间-频率网络（DPSF-Net），具体包含：1）以有雾RGB图像和暗通道先验（DCP）图作为联合输入，利用物理退化线索引导端到端特征学习；2）空间-频率残差交互模块引入傅里叶单元分支到多方向空间交互中，用于建模大尺度雾霾成分；3）先验引导特征注意力模块自适应融合先验与注意力特征，减少色彩偏移和结构失真；4）选择性核互补融合模块通过双向残差互补门控和选择性核融合筛选多尺度跳跃特征。

Result: 在真实世界RRSHID遥感图像去雾基准上取得最先进性能，在多个合成数据集上保持竞争力；同时该方法在恢复质量、参数量和计算复杂度之间取得了良好平衡，验证了双先验空间-频率建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 双先验（物理退化先验）与空间-频率域特征建模的结合能有效提升真实遥感图像去雾性能，且在不显著增加模型复杂度的前提下实现了恢复质量与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Real-world remote sensing image dehazing (RSID) remains challenging because atmospheric scattering, spatially non-uniform haze and colour distortion jointly degrade structural and spectral information. Most deep learning methods rely on RGB inputs and spatial-domain feature extraction, which limits their ability to separate global background haze from local surface details. Here, we propose DPSF-Net, a dual-prior spatial-frequency network built on MCAF-Net for real-world RSID. The network uses hazy RGB images and dark channel prior (DCP) maps as joint inputs, allowing physical degradation cues to guide end-to-end feature learning. A spatial-frequency residual interaction block introduces a FourierUnit branch into multi-directional spatial interaction to model large-scale haze components. A prior-guided feature attention module adaptively fuses prior and attention features to reduce colour shift and structural distortion. A selective kernel complementary fusion module screens multi-scale skip features through bidirectional residual complementary gating and selective kernel fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DPSF-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance on the real-world RRSHID remote sensing image dehazing benchmark and remains competitive across multiple synthetic datasets. Moreover, the proposed method strikes a favourable balance among restoration quality, parameter count and computational complexity, supporting the effectiveness of dual-prior spatial-frequency modelling.

</details>


### [138] [Re-calibrated Contrastive Loss for Transformation-Aware Prompt Conditioning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06967)
*Seungmin Oh,Seunghun Kang,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向视觉-语言模型迁移学习的新方法，通过变换感知的提示条件化和重新校准的对比损失，解决现有方法忽视数据特性、将同类样本视为独立实例以及图像变换与文本提示脱节的问题，在多种实验设置下均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现有迁移学习方法只是简单复用预训练策略，忽视数据特性。具体问题包括：将同类样本视作不同实例处理，以及独立于配对文本提示进行图像变换，这增加了模型学习难度并限制了泛化性能的提升。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心设计：(1)变换感知的提示条件化——使用固定的文本描述符标识应用于配对图像的变换，在不改变类别语义的前提下提供变换级别的一致性，使图像分支与文本分支在变换级别对齐；(2)重新校准的对比损失——缓解软目标交叉熵中当每个锚点对多个有效正样本时产生的正梯度稀释问题，在迁移时将同类样本视为正样本而非独立实例。

Result: 在分布偏移、迁移学习和少样本学习等多种实验设置下，该方法均表现出一致的性能提升，超越了现有的对比方法。

Conclusion: 通过同时考虑变换一致性和同类样本间的正例关系，该方法能够在保持视觉-语言模型泛化能力的同时实现更有效的迁移学习，为VLM迁移任务提供了更优的表示学习策略。

Abstract: Ensuring effective transfer learning for vision-language models without compromising their generalization performance is crucial. However, many existing methods overlook data characteristics and simply reuse the training strategies adopted during pre-training. Specifically, they treat same-class samples as distinct instances and transform images independently of their paired text prompts, which makes model learning more difficult. We address these limitations through transformation-aware prompt conditioning and a re-calibrated contrastive loss. Fixed text descriptors identify the transformations applied to paired images, providing transformation-level consistency without altering class semantics. This design aligns the image and text branches at the transformation level, enabling richer representations while preserving the models' ability to generalize. In addition, our loss function mitigates positive-gradient dilution in soft-target cross-entropy when each anchor has multiple valid positives. During transfer, our approach treats same-class samples as positives rather than distinct instances, enabling the model to learn domain-specific features more effectively. Experiments across distribution shift, transfer learning, and few-shot settings demonstrate consistent improvements over existing approaches. Source code for our method is available at https://github.com/SoongE/ReCalCon.

</details>


### [139] [AnomalyCraft-700K: Component-Level Controllable and Verifiable Synthetic Anomalies for Fine-Grained Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06978)
*Yuzhou Long,Haodong Zhang,Yunpeng Yang,Peng Wu,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AnomalyCraft-700K，一个组件级可控的合成异常数据集，包含超过4万段视频和70万条任务级文本标注，通过渐进式三阶段流水线生成语义丰富、时间结构化的异常事件，并利用组件级验证单元校正视频-文本不一致，支撑从异常检测到细粒度异常推理的六项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界异常视频存在收集困难、内容可控性低等固有缺陷；现有合成异常方法仅在类别或提示层面控制生成，缺乏组件级视频-文本一致性验证，且在正常-异常边界附近缺少足够的硬负样本，制约了视频异常理解（VAU）的发展。

Method: 构建AnomalyCraft-700K数据集：1）从细粒度语义组件出发，采用渐进式三阶段流水线生成细节丰富、语义可控、时间结构化的异常事件；2）构造每类硬负样本以促使模型基于异常语义而非表面视觉线索进行判别；3）以组件作为验证单元，对生成过程中的视频-文本偏差进行组件级校正，确保六项任务的跨模态对齐。

Result: 在传统协议和多模态大语言模型（MLLM）协议下对广泛应用方法的评估表明，AnomalyCraft-700K能够作为有效的监督数据源，从异常检测到细粒度异常理解均带来性能提升。

Conclusion: AnomalyCraft-700K通过组件级可控合成和可靠性验证，有效缓解了真实异常视频数据不足的问题，为视频异常理解提供了可扩展的高质量训练数据来源，推动了从基础检测到细粒度推理的全链路性能提升。

Abstract: Progress in video anomaly understanding (VAU) has long been limited by inherent deficiencies of real-world anomaly videos, which are hard to collect and offer little control over their content. Synthetic anomaly approaches partially alleviate data scarcity, yet their generation remains largely controlled at the category or prompt level. They also lack component-level verification of video-text consistency and provide insufficient hard normal samples near the normal-anomaly boundary. To address this, we present AnomalyCraft-700K, a component-controllable synthetic anomaly dataset for fine-grained VAU, containing over 40K videos and over 700K task-level textual annotations. From fine-grained semantic components and a progressive three-stage pipeline, we craft anomaly events that are richly detailed, semantically controlled, and temporally structured, and additionally construct per-category hard normal samples to prompt the model to discriminate based on anomaly semantics rather than surface visual cues. Moreover, using the components as verification units, AnomalyCraft-700K further performs component-wise correction of video-text discrepancies introduced during generation, providing reliable annotations with verified cross-modal alignment for six tasks that progress from anomaly detection, through anomaly retrieval and captioning, to fine-grained anomaly reasoning. Evaluations of widely used methods under both traditional and MLLM-based protocols demonstrate that AnomalyCraft-700K serves as an effective source of supervision, from anomaly detection to fine-grained anomaly understanding.

</details>


### [140] [AV-SafetyBench: A Safety Benchmark for Text-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06991)
*Suah Choi,Tae-Young Lee,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-SafetyBench，这是首个专门针对文本生成音视频（T2AV）任务的安全基准，包含四轴13类别的分类体系和5,200条人工审核提示词。评估协议从全视听、视频单模态、音频单模态三个视角评判输出，归属四类风险源。对5个开源模型评测发现全视听不安全率在25.1%-49.4%之间，且四个模型中音频单模态和音视频联合风险占总风险的41.6%-48.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本生成音视频模型需要从单一提示中同时生成视频、语音、音效和环境音，但传统安全基准仅分别评估生成的视频或音频，无法捕捉音频轨道中的不安全内容，也无法捕捉需要视觉和音频联合理解才能察觉的风险。

Method: 构建四轴13类别的安全分类体系，创建5,200条人工审阅提示词；设计三视角评估协议（Full-AV、Video-Only、Audio-Only）；利用视频与音频单独判断将全视听不安全输出归因到四种风险来源（Video-Only、Audio-Only、AV-Both、AV-Joint）；评测5个开源T2AV模型并将自动化判断与人工标注进行验证。

Result: 五个模型的Full-AV不安全率介于25.1%至49.4%。四个模型中，仅靠视频评估会遗漏的Audio-Only和AV-Joint风险占可归属风险源的Full-AV不安全输出的41.6%-48.3%；在跨模态危害涌现类别中，AV-Joint占87.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明仅进行视频单模态安全评估会严重低估T2AV模型的真实风险，AV-SafetyBench能够有效评估视觉、音频及其交互产生的多方面安全风险。

Abstract: Recent text-to-audio-video (T2AV) models jointly generate video, speech, sound effects, and ambience from a single text prompt. This capability poses new challenges for safety evaluation, as unsafe content may be conveyed through the audio track or arise only when the visual and audio tracks are interpreted jointly. Existing safety benchmarks largely focus on either generated video or generated audio in isolation and are therefore not designed to capture these risks. To close this gap, we introduce AV-SafetyBench, the first safety benchmark developed specifically for T2AV generation. AV-SafetyBench comprises a four-axis, 13-category taxonomy and 5,200 manually reviewed prompts that specify visual scenes, speech, and non-speech audio. Our evaluation protocol assesses each output under three views: Full-AV, Video-Only, and Audio-Only. It then uses the Video-Only and Audio-Only judgments to assign Full-AV unsafe outputs to one of four risk sources: Video-Only, Audio-Only, AV-Both, or AV-Joint. We evaluate five open-source T2AV models and validate the automated Full-AV judgments against human annotations. Across the five models, Full-AV Unsafe Rates range from 25.1% to 49.4%. Beyond these aggregate rates, risk-source analysis reveals that, for four of the five models, Audio-Only and AV-Joint cases-unsafe outputs missed by video-only evaluation-account for 41.6-48.3% of Full-AV unsafe outputs for which a risk source could be assigned. In the Cross-Modal Harm Emergence category, AV-Joint accounts for 87.5% of unsafe outputs withan assigned risk source. Together, these findings demonstrate the value of AV-SafetyBench for evaluating T2AV safety across the visual and audio modalities and their interaction.

</details>


### [141] [CGSM: Concept-Guided Segmentation Model for Precise Pulmonary Lesion Delineation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07004)
*Changheng Lin,Wenjie Zhang,Yushan Lu,Xinyue Yan,Xiao Jia,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CGSM（概念引导分割模型），通过整合LLM生成并经临床审核的概念到分割流程中，解决现有肺部病灶分割方法缺乏任务特定语义指导的问题。模型包含概念-视觉对齐模块（CVAM）和概念调制解码器（CM-Decoder），在QaTa-COV19等公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割方法缺乏任务特定的语义引导，基于文本的标注通常仅提供病灶的大致位置信息，导致病灶边界描绘不充分且对小尺度病灶分割性能较差。

Method: 提出CGSM概念引导分割模型：1) 设计概念-视觉对齐模块（CVAM），激活与视觉特征对齐的概念中相关token，增强文本与视觉信息交互；2) 引入概念调制解码器（CM-Decoder），利用CVAM输出的概念作为调制信号，促进图像与文本特征的适应性融合。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明CGSM达到SOTA性能，其中在QaTa-COV19数据集上取得91.59%的Dice分数和84.49%的mIoU。

Conclusion: CGSM通过将临床审核的语义概念有效融入分割流程，显著提升了肺部病灶分割的准确性，验证了概念引导方法在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of pulmonary lesions is essential for effective clinical diagnosis and treatment strategies. Existing segmentation approaches often lack task-specific semantic guidance, as text-based annotations typically offer coarse localization of lesions, leading to inadequate delineation of lesion boundaries and poor performance on small-scale lesions. To address this, we propose CGSM, a Concept-Guided Segmentation Model that integrates LLM-generated and clinically reviewed concepts into the segmentation process. Specifically, we design a Concept-Visual Alignment Module (CVAM) to activate relevant tokens within the concepts that align with visual features, enhancing the interaction between textual and visual information. In addition, we introduce a Concept Modulated Decoder (CM-Decoder), which uses concepts from CVAM as modulation signals to facilitate the adaptive fusion of image and text features, improving the segmentation accuracy. Extensive experiments on two public datasets show that CGSM achieves state-of-the-art performance, with results of 91.59% Dice and 84.49% mIoU on the QaTa-COV19 dataset, demonstrating its effectiveness in pulmonary lesion segmentation.

</details>


### [142] [GIFT: Goal-Injected Fine-Tuning for Efficient Manipulation Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07006)
*Xiaoyuan Fang,Shuo Feng,Yuxuan Wang,Enhua Cheng,Peng Zhou,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GIFT（Goal-Injected Fine-Tuning）轻量级微调框架，通过零初始化卷积将生成的目标图像特征注入预训练VLA模型，仅需单轮微调即可在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测目标图像作为高层视觉引导能显著增强VLA模型的鲁棒性，但现有基础模型因高计算训练成本未系统性整合目标图像条件。

Method: 提出GIFT轻量高效微调框架：1）通过零初始化卷积将目标图像特征渐进融入观测（参数从零增长，避免有害噪声破坏预训练策略）；2）采用精细化图像编辑方法从初始观测和任务指令生成语义与视觉一致的目标图像。

Result: 仅需单轮微调，GIFT在SIMPLER两个设定上分别超过基线模型6.0%和13.4%，在LIBERO上超过4.7%。

Conclusion: GIFT以轻量高效的微调方式成功将目标图像条件注入多种预训练VLA模型，兼顾训练效率与任务性能，验证了目标图像引导对VLA模型的有效性。

Abstract: Compared with relying solely on initial observations and language instructions, predicting goal images with generative models as high-level visual guidance can significantly enhance the robustness of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, most existing foundation models have not systematically incorporated goal image conditioning due to the high computational training cost. To this end, we propose Goal-Injected Fine-Tuning (GIFT), a lightweight and efficient fine-tuning framework that seamlessly integrates generated goal images into multiple representative pretrained VLA models. Our approach introduces goal image features into observations via a zero-initialized convolution which progressively grows parameters from zero and prevents harmful noise from disrupting the pretrained policy during fine-tuning. As training proceeds, goal information is gradually incorporated, enabling efficient goal understanding without disrupting model stability. We further introduce a refined image editing method to generate semantically and visually consistent goal images from initial observations and task instructions. Experiments show that goal-aware VLA models achieve substantial performance gains across tasks: with only a single epoch of fine-tuning, GIFT outperforms the base model by 6.0% and 13.4% on two SIMPLER settings, and by 4.7% on LIBERO, demonstrating both efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [143] [Fine-Grained Visual Preprocessing and Dual-Stream Temporal Modeling for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07010)
*Su Li,Yigong Zhang,Lei Xiong,Chune Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对多模态情感分析中视觉模态受噪声干扰和时间建模不足的问题，提出了NAPS噪声抑制流水线、DS-TANet视觉动态网络和DS-TAFNet融合模型三个改进方案，在CH-SIMS v2.0S数据集上分别达到80.98%、82.58%和87.49%的宏F1/准确率，证明了在有限数据条件下提升视觉输入质量和时间表示比增加融合复杂度更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析常以文本为主导，原因是原始视频存在噪声干扰且缺乏充分的时间建模能力，这限制了视觉模态充分发挥其贡献，因此需要改进视觉信息处理以平衡多模态分析效果。

Method: 提出三项改进：(1) NAPS流水线，一个七阶段系统，整合人脸追踪、身份嵌入和归一化嘴唇运动分析来降低视觉噪声；(2) DS-TANet，结合EfficientNetB2静态流、RAFT光流运动流、运动引导注意力及Bi-GRU时间建模；(3) DS-TAFNet，通过拼接融合整合视觉表示与MacBERT-Base文本表示。

Result: 在CH-SIMS v2.0S数据集上，采用NAPS后静态视觉基线宏F1达80.98%（接近文本基线80.55%）；DS-TANet将视觉宏F1提升至82.58%；DS-TAFNet达到87.49%准确率和87.48%宏F1。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在有限数据条件下，改进视觉输入质量和时间表征比增加融合策略的复杂度更为有效，说明从源头治理数据噪声和增强时序建模能力是提升多模态情感分析性能的关键路径。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis often remains text-dominant due to raw-video noise and insufficient temporal modeling. Using CH-SIMS v2.0S, this study proposes three improvements: the NAPS pipeline---a seven-stage system integrating face tracking,identity embedding, and normalized lip-motion analysis to reduce visual noise;DS-TANet, combining an EfficientNetB2 static stream, RAFT optical-flow motion stream, motion-guided attention, and Bi-GRU temporal modeling; and DS-TAFNet, fusing visual and MacBERT-Base textual representations via concatenation fusion. With NAPS, the static visual baseline achieves 80.98\% Macro F1, comparable to the text baseline of 80.55\%; DS-TANet improves visual Macro F1 to 82.58\%;and DS-TAFNet achieves 87.49\% accuracy and 87.48\% Macro F1. These results demonstrate that improving visual input quality and temporal representation is more effective than increasing fusion complexity under limited-data conditions.

</details>


### [144] [ARNAI: Artifact Removal Network based on Autoencoding and Inpainting for Robust Spinal Image Segmentation and Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07013)
*Sang-Jin Park,Jinyoung Choi,Seokwon Kim,Seungeon Song,Insu Park,Dougho Park,Taeyeon Kim,Youjin Lee,Donghoon Yang,Jaeman Cho,Joongwon Yang,Mansu Kim,Heumdai Kwon,Hong Gyu Baek,Dae Chul Cho,Injung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出RSM框架（Restore, Segment, Measure），结合新型伪影去除网络ARNAI，用于术后X光片上自动测量脊柱骨盆参数，对植入物引起的伪影具有鲁棒性。加入ARNAI后分割性能显著提升（DSC从0.814升至0.870），L4-L5节段Cobb角误差平均减少约70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后脊柱X光片上存在植入物伪影，严重影响现有AI框架对脊柱骨盆参数的自动测量准确性和可靠性，需要开发对植入物具有鲁棒性的自动化测量框架。

Method: 开发了Restore, Segment, Measure（RSM）三阶段框架，核心创新是ARNAI（基于自编码和修复的伪影去除网络），用于减轻术后X光片中的植入物相关伪影。使用两个机构的回顾性腰椎侧位X光片数据，通过Wilcoxon符号秩检验和组内相关系数（ICC）评估分割和脊柱骨盆参数（PT、LL、SS、SCA）测量性能。

Result: ARNAI使FCBFormer分割模型平均DSC从0.814提升至0.870，L3-L5改善显著。在91张含植入物的X光片上，L4-L5节段Cobb角平均误差从15.6-16.2°降至4.7°，误差减少约70%。ICC从0.18提升至0.54-0.59，骨盆倾斜、腰椎前凸和骶骨斜率ICC均超过0.70。内部含植入物队列的改善具有统计学显著性（多重比较校正后）。

Conclusion: RSM框架有效改善了含植入物术后X光片的自动脊柱骨盆参数测量。ARNAI通过减轻植入物伪影提高了分割和下游测量精度，对L4-L5节段Cobb角估计的改善最为显著，平均误差降低约70%。

Abstract: Purpose: This study aims to develop an AI framework applicable for postoperative imaging for automated measurement of spinopelvic parameters on radiographs with robustness to the presence of spinal implants.
  Materials and Methods: We retrospectively reviewed lateral lumbar spine radiographs from two institutions (Internal: January 2017--December 2024; External: October 2021--September 2025). We developed the Restore, Segment, and Measure (RSM) framework, incorporating a novel Artifact Removal Network based on Autoencoding and Inpainting (ARNAI) to mitigate implant-related artifacts in postoperative radiographs. Segmentation and spinopelvic parameter (PT, LL, SS, SCA) measurement performance were assessed using Wilcoxon signed-rank tests and intraclass correlation coefficients.
  Results: When ARNAI was added to a recent Transformer-based segmentation model, FCBFormer, the mean DSC increased to 0.870 from 0.814, with marked gains at L3--L5 and smaller improvements at L1--L2. On 91 radiographs with implants, the mean L4--L5 segmental Cobb angle error decreased to 4.7 ° from 15.6--16.2 °, an average error reduction of 70%. The ICC for L4--L5 segmental Cobb angle improved to 0.54 (Rater 1) and 0.59 (Rater 2) from 0.18, and ICCs for pelvic tilt, lumbar lordosis, and sacral slope all exceeded 0.70. The improvement in L4--L5 segmental Cobb angle error was statistically significant in the internal implant-containing cohort after correction for multiple comparisons.
  Conclusion: The proposed RSM framework improved automated spinopelvic parameter measurement in implant-containing postoperative radiographs. By mitigating implant-related artifacts, ARNAI improved segmentation and downstream measurement accuracy, with the greatest benefit observed for L4--L5 segmental Cobb angle estimation, where the mean error was reduced by approximately 70%.

</details>


### [145] [LoGAN: Multilingual Font Localization with Generative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07029)
*Zhuoning Yuan,Ta-Ying Cheng,Benjamin Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoGAN，一个基于VLM代理的多语言字体本地化框架，通过少量字形样本生成其他语言的完整字符集，在跨27种语言（含中日韩）的字体和真实logo数据集上取得了优于现有方法的字形保真度和风格一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将字体本地化为新语言是一项高度复杂的任务，需要精确适配字形设计、颜色/纹理和字距。现有方法大多只关注单个字形生成，缺乏处理多语言字体渲染的能力，因此需要一种能够处理完整多语言字体本地化的框架。

Method: LoGAN由多个组件构成：字形级扩散模型、风格微调模块、字距/字偶距迁移算法、纹理扩展模型，并由VLM代理作为协调器将这些模块整合起来，实现从少量示例字形到其他语言完整字符集的生成。

Result: 在涵盖27种以上语言的字体和真实logo数据集上的评估表明，LoGAN相比专门的字形生成方法和具有强文本渲染能力的SOTA图像编辑模型（如FLUX、Nano-Banana），在字形保真度上更高，同时在风格、纹理和字距一致性上表现更好。

Conclusion: LoGAN通过VLM代理协调多个专业化模块，实现了广泛语言覆盖（包括CJK）的多语言字体本地化，在量化与定性评估中均优于现有的专业字形生成和图像编辑方法，展现了少样本条件下多语言字体渲染的可行性。

Abstract: Localizing a font into new languages is a highly intricate task requiring precise design adaptation of glyphs, color/texture, and spacing/kerning, from source to target languages. Most existing methods focus on single glyph generation with limited capability in handling multilingual font rendering. In this work, we propose LoGAN, a VLM-based agentic framework for few-shot multilingual font localization, which takes in a small number of individual glyphs from a font or letters from a logo and uses them to generate complete character sets in other languages. LoGAN breaks down this task into multiple components: a glyph-level diffusion model, a style finetuning module, a spacing and kerning transfer algorithm, and a texture expansion model, with a VLM agent coordinator. LoGAN achieves broad language coverage for font localization with various styles, including Chinese/Korean/Japanese (CJK). We evaluate our approach on both font and real-world logo datasets spanning more than 27 languages and compare it against both specialized font generation and state-of-the-art image editing models with strong text rendering capabilities (e.g., FLUX, Nano-Banana). Our approach yields higher glyph fidelity while maintaining better style, texture, and kerning consistency according to both quantitative and qualitative evaluations.

</details>


### [146] [FreqDoor: A Hidden Trojan in the Frequency Domain for Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07048)
*Yasir Arafat Prodhan,Sadad Hasan,Mohammed Imamul Hassan Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqDoor，一种在频率域植入后门触发器的新型训练时攻击方法，通过在幅度频谱中混合触发源图像成分同时保留干净图像的相位信息，生成空间分布且视觉上不可感知的触发器，在多个VLM模型上实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLM)对后门攻击具有持续脆弱性，现有后门触发器（空间型、文本型、双模态型）可能产生局部化或可识别的触发器模式，缺乏在频率域探索攻击面的研究。

Method: FreqDoor通过选择性混合触发源图像的幅度频谱分量，同时保留干净图像的相位信息，在频率域植入触发器，生成空间分布且视觉上不可感知的触发器，且无需修改文本输入。

Result: 在Flickr8k上，FreqDoor在BLIP-2、InstructBLIP和LLaVA上分别达到99.6%、99.8%和98.4%的攻击成功率；在VQAv2上，对应成功率为99.6%、92.4%和79.6%，同时保持生成标题的语义质量。

Conclusion: FreqDoor证明了频率域作为VLM后门攻击的新攻击面是有效的，能够在保持图像语义质量的同时实现高攻击成功率，为VLM安全研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently shown excellent progress in open-ended image-to-text generation. However, their multimodal nature makes them persistently vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Existing backdoor triggers for VLMs are either spatial, textual, or bimodal, which may yield localized or recognizable trigger patterns. In this work, we explore a different attack surface and propose \ textsc {FreqDoor}, a training-time backdoor attack that implants triggers in the frequency domain. \ textsc {FreqDoor} mixes amplitude-spectrum components from a trigger-source image selectively while preserving the phase of a clean image to generate a spatially distributed and visually imperceptible trigger without modifying the textual input. We evaluate the attack on BLIP-2, InstructBLIP, and LLaVA for image captioning and visual question answering. On Flickr8k, \ textsc {FreqDoor} achieves attack success rates of $99.6\%$, $99.8\%$, and $98.4\%$ on the three models, respectively, while preserving the semantic quality of the generated captions. On VQAv2, the corresponding attack success rates are $99.6\%$, $92.4\%$, and $79.6\%$.

</details>


### [147] [CNsEMD: An Expert-Annotated Multi-Field-Strength MRI Dataset and a Hyperspherical Manifold Network for Multimodal Cranial Nerve Parcellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07058)
*Lei Xie,Junxiong Huang,Guoqiang Xie,Jiawei Zhang,Jiahao Huang,Tianling Lyu,Ye Wu,Mingchu Li,Shoujun Yu,Shanshan Wang,Qingrun Zeng,Yuanjing Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CNsEMD，一个专家标注的多模态MRI数据集（含202名受试者，覆盖3T/5T/7T扫描仪），用于颅神经（CN）分割；同时提出PHM-Net网络，通过超球面嵌入空间中的跨模态交互实现CN精准分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅神经体积小、图像对比度低、形态细长且解剖轨迹复杂，导致精准分割极其困难；同时缺乏公开的专家标注数据集严重阻碍了基于学习的CN分析方法的发展与公平基准测试。

Method: 1) 构建CNsEMD数据集：202名受试者、3T/5T/7T多场强MRI、专家标注。2) 提出PHM-Net网络，包含三个核心模块：超球面跨模态交互(HCI)模块在单位超球面上实现T1w与DEC表征的双向特征交换；幅值保持投影超球面方向表征(PHOR)捕获DEC方向的轴向特性同时保留扩散幅值；超球面原型分割头(HPSH)利用归一化体素嵌入和可学习类原型将角相似性扩展至体素级分类。

Result: 在CNsEMD数据集上的大量实验表明，PHM-Net相较于现有最先进方法展现了有效性，验证了超球面几何一致性方案在不同MRI采集条件下的CN分割性能。

Conclusion: CNsEMD为多模态CN成像建立了可复现的基准，PHM-Net为跨多种MRI采集的CN分割提供了几何一致性的解决方案。

Abstract: Cranial nerves (CNs) play essential roles in sensory, motor, and autonomic functions. Accurate CN parcellation from multimodal magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is crucial for neuroanatomical analysis and neurosurgical planning. However, accurate CN parcellation remains extremely challenging because CNs are very small, exhibit low image contrast, and have slender tubular morphologies and complex anatomical trajectories. Moreover, the lack of publicly available, expert-annotated datasets has impeded the development and fair benchmarking of learning-based CN analysis methods. In this work, we introduce CNsEMD, an expert-annotated multimodal dataset for CN parcellation. It comprises data from 202 subjects acquired on 3T, 5T, and 7T MRI scanners. We further propose the projective hyperspherical manifold network (PHM-Net), which learns cross-modal representations by capturing angular relationships in a shared hyperspherical embedding space. Rather than performing multimodal fusion in Euclidean space, the proposed Hyperspherical cross-modal interaction (HCI) module enables bidirectional feature exchange between T1-weighted (T1w) and direction-encoded color (DEC) representations on a unit hypersphere. The Magnitude-preserving projective hyperspherical orientation representation (PHOR) captures the axial nature of DEC orientations while preserving diffusion magnitude. The hyperspherical prototype segmentation head (HPSH) further extends angular similarity to voxel-wise classification using normalized voxel embeddings and learnable class prototypes. Extensive experimental results on the CNsEMD dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our PHM-Net against state-of-the-art methods. CNsEMD establishes a reproducible benchmark for multimodal CN imaging, while PHM-Net provides a geometry-consistent solution for CN parcellation across diverse MRI acquisitions.

</details>


### [148] [SpatialBlock: Enhancing Spatial Intelligence in LVLMs via Synthetic Block-Stacking Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07064)
*Soohyun Ryu,Sohee Kim,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialBlock-15k合成数据集，通过积木堆叠任务训练LVLM的空间智能能力，显著提升其在真实场景三维推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在三维场景重建与推理（即空间智能）方面能力有限，现有方法依赖真实场景问答数据，但其密集几何标注成本高、耗时长且常因外部感知模块引入噪声。

Method: 借鉴人类认知发展，提出通过结构化积木操作任务学习基础空间技能。构建SpatialBlock-15k合成数据集，包含15,000个积木堆叠问题，覆盖3D到2D投影、视角变换和结构组合任务，并通过受控颜色调制提供视觉线索以鼓励锚点推理。

Result: 在SpatialBlock-15k上训练的LVLM，无论是直接回答还是基于推理的预测方式，均显著优于基线方法，并能泛化到真实世界的空间任务。

Conclusion: 合成且紧凑的数据集可以有效训练LVLM的空间智能能力并迁移至真实场景，为缓解人工标注昂贵问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved strong performance on diverse visual tasks, yet their ability to reconstruct and reason about the 3D structure of the scene depicted in 2D images -- referred to as spatial intelligence -- remains limited. Existing approaches attempt to address this gap by using real-scene spatial question answering datasets that require dense geometric annotations. However, constructing such labels is costly, time-consuming, and often noisy due to reliance on external perception modules. In this work, we propose a novel paradigm inspired by human cognitive development: learning foundational spatial skills through structured block-manipulation tasks. We introduce SpatialBlock-15k, a synthetic dataset of 15,000 block-stacking problems covering 3D-to-2D projection, viewpoint transformation, and structural combination. The dataset further incorporates controlled color modulation as visual cues to encourage anchor-based reasoning in visually complex conditions. Experiments demonstrate that LVLMs trained on our dataset through either direct answering or reasoning-based prediction significantly outperform baselines and generalize to real-world spatial tasks, despite the dataset's synthetic and compact nature. Code and data are available at https://github.com/rsoohyun/SpatialBlock.

</details>


### [149] [Continuous Token-Level Spatio-Temporal Context Modeling for Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07070)
*Ding Xia,Meiqin Liu,Jing Zhou,Jian Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TLCTrack是一种新型视觉跟踪框架，通过连续更新显著令牌来建模令牌级时空上下文，解决现有方法因离散采样偏离时空连续性而导致的跟踪性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉跟踪方法大多通过离散采样策略提取时空线索，这与时空上下文的连续本质相悖，导致跟踪性能受损。

Method: 提出TLCTrack框架，包含三个核心模块：Masked Unidirectional Attention (MUA)用于提取搜索区域中区分性的目标感知空间特征；Spatial Salient Token Collection (SSTC)逐步抑制背景干扰以增强目标空间表征；Temporal Salient Token Bank (TSTB)通过连续显著令牌更新捕获高质量时空信息。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在性能上超越了现有的最先进跟踪器。

Conclusion: 通过连续更新的显著令牌显式整合时空上下文，TLCTrack能有效克服离散采样的局限，实现更准确的目标表征与跟踪性能。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal context has become increasingly crucial for visual tracking. However, most existing approaches extract spatio-temporal cues via discrete sampling strategies, which inherently deviate from the continuity of spatio-temporal context, thereby deteriorating tracking performance. To address this challenge, we propose TLCTrack, a novel tracking framework that models token-level spatio-temporal context through continuously updated salient tokens, enabling more accurate target representation. Specifically, TLCTrack incorporates three components: Masked Unidirectional Attention (MUA), Spatial Salient Token Collection (SSTC), and Temporal Salient Token Bank (TSTB) modules. By explicitly integrating spatio-temporal context, MUA extracts discriminative targetaware spatial features in the search region. To avoid the negative impact of background on feature learning, SSTC progressively suppresses background interference, thereby enhancing target spatial representation. Finally, TSTB captures high-quality spatio-temporal information through continuous salient token updates. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art trackers. Code and models are available at https://github.com/xiading123/TLCTrack.

</details>


### [150] [Detect Anything in Graphic Design: Element-Level Rewards for Autoregressive Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07072)
*Jiangning Zhu,Bowen Li,Shenyu Qiao,Yima Gu,Zhao Zhang,Yuhui Yuan,Shixia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAD模型，将平面设计元素检测形式化为组合解构任务，按组合顺序解码元素并支持Amodal检测（预测被遮挡元素的完整边界框），同时提出EleRPO优化方法将GRPO从序列级扩展到元素级优化，构建了1000万设计样本数据集，实验证明DAD超越所有基线并达到人类级水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面设计（海报、广告、信息图）中的元素具有明确的组合顺序（层级关系），但现有目标检测模型将这些元素视为无序集合，未利用组合顺序这一关键信息，限制了检测性能。

Method: 1) 提出DAD模型，将图形设计检测重构为组合解构任务，按组合顺序（从底层到高层）解码元素，利用低层元素帮助检测高层元素；2) DAD的核心是基于Amodal检测，预测每个元素的完整边界框（包括被上层元素遮挡的区域）；3) 提出EleRPO（Element Relative Policy Optimization），将GRPO从序列级监督扩展为元素级优化，提供细粒度训练信号；4) 构建包含1000万设计样本的数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: 实验表明DAD优于所有基线方法，在Amodal检测中达到人类水平性能，有效支持图像到图层的分解；EleRPO在九个检测基准上持续优于GRPO。

Conclusion: 利用组合顺序进行元素级检测优化可显著提升平面设计检测性能，Amodal检测结合元素级强化学习为实现图像到图层的自动分解提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Graphic designs, such as posters, advertisements, and infographics, are an important medium for communicating information and shaping understanding. Unlike natural images, they consist of layered elements with explicit compositional order. However, existing object detection models treat these elements as an unordered set, leaving compositional order unexploited. To address this limitation, we present Detect Anything in Graphic Design (DAD), a model that formulates graphic design detection as compositional deconstruction. It decodes elements in compositional order, using lower-layer elements to better detect higher-layer ones. The key feature of DAD is amodal detection, which predicts the full bounding box of each element, including regions occluded by elements placed above it. Building on this formulation, we propose Element Relative Policy Optimization (EleRPO), which extends GRPO from sequence-level supervision to element-level optimization. EleRPO provides fine-grained training signals that capture how each detected element contributes to overall detection quality, and works synergistically with compositional order to improve detection performance. To support training and evaluation, we build a dataset of 10 million graphic designs. Experiments show that DAD outperforms all baselines and achieves human-level performance in amodal detection, supporting effective image-to-layer decomposition. EleRPO consistently improves over GRPO across nine detection benchmarks.

</details>


### [151] [One for All: Generalist Foundation Model for Cross-Sensor Skeleton Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07078)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Yun Chen,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SOfA（Skeleton One for All），这是首个面向通用骨架表示学习的"传感器统一"基础模型。通过引入固定大小的可学习Canonical Joint Slots和基于预训练文本编码器的Semantic Joint Embedding，SOfA解决了不同传感器带来的关节数量、索引协议和拓扑结构差异问题，能够以单一模型在各种下游任务和传感器类型上达到甚至超越数据集专用模型的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法受到骨架数据异质性的严重限制——不同传感器采集的骨架数据在关节数量、索引协议和拓扑结构上各不相同，导致需要为每种传感器甚至每个数据集分别训练专用模型，缺乏真正泛化的通用骨架表示学习能力。

Method: SOfA提出两个核心创新：(1) 引入固定大小的可学习Canonical Joint Slots作为"通用容器"，通过注意力机制动态聚合不同传感器的关节信息，消除维度差异；(2) 提出基于预训练文本编码器的Semantic Joint Embedding替代绝对位置嵌入，解决不同传感器间关节索引不对齐问题。作者还标准化了十个3D骨架数据集进行统一的联合训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，SOfA作为一个通用的编码器，在多种下游任务和传感器类型上取得了SOTA性能，且往往能以单个基础模型超越针对特定数据集训练的专家模型。

Conclusion: SOfA验证了构建真正的通用骨架表示基础模型的可行性，通过统一不同传感器的数据异质性，实现了以单一模型跨传感器、跨数据集的高效泛化能力，为该领域后续研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: For learning generalizable motion representations from large-scale unlabeled data, Self-supervised learning (SSL) has become a widely adopted methodology. However, existing approaches are primarily limited by the inherent heterogeneity of skeleton data---characterized by varying joint counts, indexing protocols, and topological structures across different sensors---which typically necessitates training separate, sensor-specific, or even entirely dataset-specific models. To overcome this, we introduce SOfA (Skeleton One for All), the first generalist foundation model designed to achieve sensor-unified skeleton representation learning across diverse sensors. To accommodate the dimensional gap caused by varying joint counts, we introduce a fixed-size set of learnable Canonical Joint Slots, acting as a universal vessel that seamlessly accommodates arbitrary skeletal topologies. SOfA fills these slots via an attention mechanism that dynamically aggregates skeletal information from sensor-specific inputs. Furthermore, we resolve joint index misalignment between various sensors by introducing a Semantic Joint Embedding derived from a pre-trained text encoder, rather than relying on absolute positional embeddings. To validate our approach, we standardized ten 3D skeleton datasets for unified training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SOfA can serve as a truly universal encoder, achieving state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across a wide range of downstream tasks and sensor types, often outperforming dataset-specific specialist models with a single foundation model.

</details>


### [152] [SupGRPO: Enhancing GRPO with Matching-based Online SFT for Text Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07081)
*Xudong Xie,Yuzhe Li,Jing Shi,Zhifei Zhang,Curtis Wigington,Zhaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SupGRPO，一种结合SFT和GRPO的联合训练策略，用于增强MLLM-based文本检测与识别能力。研究发现SFT和GRPO各自擅长不同任务，SupGRPO通过匹配式在线SFT（仅应用于坐标token）和专门设计的奖励函数，同时优化识别与检测，并在新构建的艺术文本数据集ATS上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专用文本检测器在自然场景中擅长预测紧密边界框，但在复杂或艺术文本上表现不佳；而多模态大语言模型具备强大的识别能力但定位能力薄弱。需要一个方法同时具备强大的识别能力和精确的定位能力。

Method: 探索两种基于MLLM的微调方法：(1) SFT和GRPO的对比研究；(2) 提出SupGRPO联合训练策略，同时使用SFT和GRPO优化模型，包含专门设计的奖励函数和匹配式在线SFT（仅应用于坐标token），以缓解GRPO的奖励稀疏问题和SFT的实例顺序依赖问题；(3) 构建ATS艺术文本数据集用于评估。

Result: 实验表明SFT在检测方面更强而GRPO在识别方面更强（各有专长），SupGRPO联合训练策略在文本识别和检测方面均取得改进，并在ATS数据集上达到优越性能。

Conclusion: SupGRPO通过互补SFT和GRPO的各自优势，成功弥合了MLLM识别能力强而定位弱的鸿沟，特别在艺术文本等挑战性场景中表现出色，为文本检测与识别提供了一个统一且强大的解决框架。

Abstract: Text spotting requires both accurate text recognition and precise spatial localization. Current specialised spotters excel at predicting tight bounding boxes in natural scenes, but falter on complex or artistic text, whereas multimodal large language models (MLLMs) possess strong recognition capabilities yet remain weak at localisation. To equip the text spotter with general and powerful recognition capabilities and to maximize its localization ability, we explore two MLLM-based fine-tuning methods: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning fine-tuning based on Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO). An interesting finding is that SFT is less effective than GRPO at enhancing recognition, while GRPO is less effective than SFT at enhancing detection. To compensate for each other's shortcomings, we introduce a joint training strategy, SupGRPO, which simultaneously optimizes the model using both SFT and GRPO. SupGRPO employs the specially designed reward functions and develops a matching-based online SFT applied solely to coordinate tokens. It both mitigates the reward sparsity problem of GRPO and avoids the instance order dependency problem of SFT. To evaluate particularly challenging cases, we curate ATS, a dataset for artistic text spotting. Experiments demonstrate that SupGRPO improves both text recognition and detection, and attains superior performance. Our code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/Psycho-9/SupGRPO.

</details>


### [153] [Single Image to Textured 3D Object Generation in Frequency Domain: From Theory to Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07085)
*Qisen Wang,Yifan Zhao,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Morpheus3D，从频域角度重新审视2D和3D扩散先验的特性，并设计了一个统一的混合优化框架，通过高频图像提示的2D先验来增强3D先验，实现从任意单张无姿态图像生成高质量3D物体，同时解决视角不一致、低频颜色偏差和高频细节缺失等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图3D重建任务因信息极度缺乏而极具挑战性。现有方法中，基于2D先验的扩散模型存在颜色偏差和视角不一致问题，而基于3D先验的方法则缺乏高频细节。两种先验在空间域直接互补会引入错误的低频2D先验引导，因此需要从频域角度重新审视并设计更优的融合策略。

Method: 首先从频域角度分析不同扩散先验的特性，提出一个在频域中使用多种扩散先验进行混合优化的统一理论框架。基于该框架，提出Morpheus3D流水线：利用3D先验作为基础，通过高通图像提示的2D先验引导来补充高频细节信息，在抑制视角不一致和低频颜色偏差的同时重建高质量的3D物体。

Result: 在公开数据集和自行收集的复杂纹理数据集上进行了定量与定性实验，结果表明该方法在生成质量上显著优于现有方法，有效解决了视角不一致、低频颜色偏差和高频细节缺失三大问题。

Conclusion: 频域视角为融合2D与3D扩散先验提供了新思路，Morpheus3D证明了通过高通2D先验引导增强3D先验，能够在保持几何一致性的同时恢复高频细节，是解决单视图3D重建任务的有效方案。

Abstract: Single-view 3D reconstruction, also known as image-to-3D, is a persistently challenging task due to the extreme lack of information. Recently, diffusion models pre-trained on large-scale datasets served as 2D priors are used to solve the ill-posed task but suffer from color deviation and view inconsistency, which can be curbed by using diffusion models fine-tuned with 3D annotated data served as 3D priors. However, 3D priors lack high-frequency details, which cannot be solved by direct complementation with 2D priors in spatial domain for introducing erroneous low-frequency 2D prior guidance. In this paper, we revisit the characteristics of different diffusion priors from the frequency perspective. Based on our observations, we theoretically present a unified framework of hybrid optimization using multiple diffusion priors in frequency domain. Under this framework, we further propose Morpheus3D, a pipeline of 3D object generation from any single unposed image in the wild. Morpheus3D enhances 3D prior with high-pass image-prompt 2D prior guidance to reconstruct high-quality 3D objects while effectively suppressing view inconsistency, low-frequency color deviation, and high-frequency lacking problems. Both quantitative and qualitative experiments on the public and our collected datasets with complex textures show that our method exhibits significant improvements in generation quality.

</details>


### [154] [CASCADE: A Spatio-Temporal-Causal Reasoning Representation and Dataset for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07094)
*Jenny Schmalfuss,Despoina Paschalidou,Simon Gerstenecker,German Ros,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CASCADE（驾驶环境的因果时空分析），一种用于自动驾驶场景推理的结构化场景表示及基于其上的人工标注数据集。该表示对每个与自车交互的actor逐帧记录其行为、位置及其对其他actor行为和状态的依赖关系，使推理预测可以被机器逐元素验证，无需依赖(M)LLM评判。数据集涵盖PhysicalAI的2,066个驾驶片段，包含34K以上要素、8.6K时间戳动作、3.7K因果链接等，全部为人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理是自动驾驶在长尾场景中实现泛化的有前景途径，但现有方法无法判断模型的结论是否真正遵循场景中的依赖关系。文本推理轨迹缺乏时空基础，时空场景图缺乏因果链接，大规模推理标注多为模型生成且难以验证，因此需要一种使推理预测可机器验证的结构化表示和人工标注数据集。

Method: 提出CASCADE，包含两部分：(1) 结构化场景表示——对每个与自车交互的actor，在其可见期间逐帧记录其动作、发生位置以及如何依赖于其他actor的行为和状态；(2) 基于该表示构建的人工标注数据集——对PhysicalAI数据集的2,066个驾驶片段提供全面人工标注，包括34K以上要素、8.6K带时间戳的自车和agent动作、3.7K因果链接、2.9K潜在影响及6.1K对agent、物体、交通灯和环境的标注。

Result: CASCADE数据集提供了完全人工标注的参考基准，用于：(1) 基准测试Physical AI模型的推理能力；(2) 验证自动生成推理标签的质量。该结构使推理预测可以逐元素机器评分，不依赖(M)LLM评判者。数据集已在HuggingFace上公开。

Conclusion: CASCADE通过引入具有因果关系的时空结构化表示和纯人工标注数据集，弥合了自动驾驶推理验证的缺口，使推理预测可被机器逐元素验证，为Physical AI模型的推理能力提供了可靠基准，也为自动生成标注的质量验证提供了参照。

Abstract: Reasoning is a promising route to the generalization that autonomous driving requires in the long tail, as it can infer how the elements of a scene depend on one another and traverse those dependencies to conclusions beyond what is observed. Yet it is hard to tell whether a model's conclusions follow the scene's dependencies, because no driving representation makes them explicit enough to test against. Text-based reasoning traces lack spatio-temporal grounding, spatio-temporal scene graphs lack causal links, and reasoning annotations at scale are increasingly model-generated and hard to verify. To this end, we introduce CASCADE (Causal Spatio-Temporal Analysis of Driving Environments), which encompasses two components: (1) a structured scene representation for reasoning in driving scenes and (2) a human-annotated dataset built on it. For every actor that interacts with the ego vehicle, the CASCADE representation records frame-by-frame, for as long as the actor is visible, what action is taken, where it occurs, and how it depends on the actions and states of others. The resulting structure makes reasoning predictions machine-verifiable: they can be scored against it element by element, without relying on (M)LLM judges. The CASCADE dataset provides comprehensive human annotations for 2,066 driving clips of the PhysicalAI dataset, with over 34K elements that establish the spatio-temporal and causal context of each scene, including 8.6K time-stamped ego and agent actions, 3.7K causal links and 2.9K potential influences, and 6.1K annotations for agents, objects, traffic lights, and environments. Being entirely human-annotated, CASCADE provides the reference for this comparison: benchmarking the reasoning abilities of Physical AI models, and verifying the quality of automatically generated reasoning labels. The CASCADE dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/nvidia/cascade.

</details>


### [155] [Ambient @ EgoProactive 2026 : Proactive Egocentric Assistance with Visually Grounded Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07099)
*Logesh Kumar Umapathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了可穿戴AI助手的干预时机决策方法，通过将干预生成重构为单token分类任务（预测yes/no而非生成文本），并在缺乏标注数据时使用工具调用视频代理生成额外监督信号。在ECCV 2026可穿戴AI挑战赛EgoProactive赛道中，该方法在大模型组排名第一，在≤2B组排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴AI助手需要在每段8秒的自我中心视频后决定是否介入或保持沉默，但面临两个核心挑战：一是自由文本生成方式对二分类时机判断不够精准；二是标注数据十分有限，仅有官方发布的验证集可用。

Method: 提出两大核心组件：(1) 将干预时机重构为单token分类问题，模型仅预测yes或no，通过对这两个token重归一化的概率做出决策，替代生成interrupt<话术>或silent的自由形式生成；(2) 由于标注数据有限，使用工具调用视频代理逐片段检查并分配干预时间戳以生成额外监督训练数据，并与纯叙述（narration-only）的替代方案进行对比评估。

Result: 单token分类相比自由生成在macro-F1上提升了0.249，G-mean提升了0.30。叙述替代方案虽然规模大4倍且便宜10倍，但迁移效果不如来自无关真实语料的监督，表明视觉接地比标注数量对该任务更重要。

Conclusion: 单token分类是应对干预时机决策任务的有效范式，视觉接地信号的质量比单纯的标注数据规模更具决定性作用，该组合方法在大模型组荣获第一、≤2B组第二。

Abstract: We present our submission to the EgoProactive track of the ECCV 2026 Wearable AI Challenge, which ranked first in the large-model division and second in the <=2B division. The task requires a wearable assistant to decide after each eight-second segment of egocentric video whether to intervene or remain silent.
  Our approach has two main components. First, we reformulate intervention timing as single-token classification. Rather than generating either $interrupt$<utterance> or $silent$, the model predicts yes or no, and we derive the decision from the renormalised probabilities of these two tokens. This formulation improved macro-F1 by 0.249 and G-mean by 0.30 over free-form generation. Second, because labelled data were limited to the released validation set, we generated additional supervision using a tool-calling video agent that inspects each clip and assigns intervention timestamps. A narration-only alternative was four times larger and ten times cheaper, but transferred worse than supervision from an unrelated real corpus, suggesting that visual grounding is more important than annotation volume for this task.

</details>


### [156] [Discovering Natural Transformation Vulnerabilities in Black-Box Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07110)
*Dongsu Song,DaeYun GO,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASA（对抗场景攻击），一种基于查询的黑盒攻击框架，通过多模态语言模型与文本引导生成编辑器，在自然语言编辑场景空间中搜索图像变换（背景、天气、材质/颜色），以高效发现视觉模型在黑盒设定下的攻击漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒设定下生成自然对抗样例（NAE）的方法依赖替代模型、学习到的攻击先验或昂贵的基于查询的优化，且预知哪些自然变换能暴露模型漏洞是不可行的，亟需一种更高效的黑盒NAE生成方法。

Method: ASA框架利用多模态语言模型在自然语言编辑场景空间中搜索，配合文本引导生成编辑器进行图像变换；通过胜者-败者反馈联合探索背景、天气和材质/颜色变换，并使用贪婪探索器只组合能提升攻击成功的场景。

Result: 在多种ImageNet分类器上，ASA相比先前的基于查询的生成式攻击取得了显著更高的攻击成功率，同时需要更少的受害者模型查询，并保持了具有竞争力的感知质量。此外，ASA展现出图像级和提示级两方面的可迁移性：对抗图像跨模型架构有效，发现的编辑场景可复用于同类图像，在部分情况下还能跨架构复用。

Conclusion: 研究表明视觉模型对自然变换模式可能存在可复用的漏洞，ASA能够在黑盒设定下高效识别这些漏洞，为理解与评估视觉模型的鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Natural adversarial examples (NAEs) reveal that vision models can fail under realistic semantic changes beyond norm-bounded perturbations. However, generating NAEs in a black-box setting remains challenging because existing generative attacks often rely on surrogate models, learned attack priors, or costly query-based optimization, whereas the natural transformations that expose model vulnerabilities are unknown a priori. We propose \textbf{Adversarial Scenario Attack (ASA)}, a query-based black-box framework that searches over natural-language editing scenarios using a multimodal language model and a modern text-guided generative editor. ASA jointly explores background, weather, and material/color transformations through winner--loser feedback, and uses a greedy explorer to compose only attack-improving scenarios. Across diverse ImageNet classifiers, ASA achieves substantially higher attack success rates than prior query-based generative attacks while requiring fewer victim-model queries and preserving competitive perceptual quality. Moreover, ASA exhibits both image-level and prompt-level transferability: its adversarial images remain effective across victim-model architectures, while its discovered editing scenarios can be reused across same-class images and, in some cases, across architectures. These findings suggest that vision models possess reusable vulnerabilities to natural transformation patterns, which ASA can efficiently identify in a black-box setting.

</details>


### [157] [A Two-Stage Framework for Ego-Centric Key Object Identification via Object State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07125)
*Shihong Ling,Yue Wan,Xiaowei Jia,Na Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶的关键物体识别新框架，通过引入虚拟自车表示和模块化物体状态预测器，结合时空推理来提升复杂交通环境下关键物体检测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么独立检测物体，要么利用视觉关系，但均未明确考虑自车视角在判断物体重要性中的作用，存在研究空白。

Method: 该框架集成了虚拟自车表示与模块化物体状态预测器，以更准确地估计物体相对自车的行为；随后采用时空推理，基于物体状态和相对空间信息（而非仅依赖视觉关系）来优化关键物体的识别。

Result: 在真实驾驶数据集上的实验结果表明，该框架能够在复杂交通环境中准确检测关键物体。

Conclusion: 通过引入自车视角的显式建模，该框架相比仅依赖视觉关系的方法更能有效识别关键物体，为自动驾驶中的关键物体识别提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework designed to enhance key object identification in autonomous driving. Existing methods primarily focus on either detecting objects independently or leveraging visual relationships, but they do not explicitly consider the ego vehicle's perspective in determining object importance. To address this gap, we propose a structured approach that integrates a virtual ego-vehicle representation and a modular object state predictor, enabling a more accurate estimation of object behaviors relative to the ego-vehicle. Subsequently, our framework employs spatial-temporal reasoning to refine key object identification, prioritizing objects based on their states and relative spatial information rather than relying solely on visual relationships. Experimental results on real-world driving datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in accurately detecting critical objects in complex traffic environments.

</details>


### [158] [NutriBench-Kitchen: Benchmarking Embodied AI for Nutrition Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07135)
*Yulin Wei,Xiangchen Wang,Jianhui Pan,Jinyu Xiao,Zheng Tan,Ruozai Tian,Guanhua Chen,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NutriBench-Kitchen基准测试，包含160个烹饪视频中的1500个问答对，涵盖五类任务家族，用于评估具身厨房助手在营养管理中的连续状态追踪与知识驱动推理能力；同时引入Nutri-Vgent诊断型长视频代理，通过分离的情景、食物状态和食谱记忆证实了显式状态表征与结构化记忆的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估静态食物理解或具身烹饪动作，未能衡量代理在动态厨房环境中持续更新并利用营养相关状态的能力，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 构建NutriBench-Kitchen基准（160个视频、1500个QA对、五个任务家族涵盖食物状态构建/维护/知识检索/决策），并设计Nutri-Vgent代理，采用分离的情景记忆、食物状态记忆和食谱记忆来支持长时间视频理解与营养状态追踪。

Result: 专有和开源大型视觉语言模型与人类表现存在显著差距，特别是在定量成分估计、长期状态追踪和交互约束下的推理方面；Nutri-Vgent在所有任务上呈现一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式的状态表征和结构化记忆对于动态厨房中的营养管理至关重要，NutriBench-Kitchen和Nutri-Vgent为持久状态追踪和知识驱动推理研究提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: An embodied kitchen assistant must do more than recognize food in isolated frames. It must track ingredient states over time and integrate visual observations with recipe and nutritional knowledge to support constraint-aware decision-making. We formalize this capability as \emph{Embodied Nutrition Management}: perceiving nutrition-relevant events, maintaining a persistent food state, and using it for knowledge-grounded planning. Existing benchmarks evaluate static food understanding or embodied cooking actions, but do not measure whether an agent can continuously update and use nutrition-relevant states in dynamic kitchens. To fill this gap, we introduce \textbf{NutriBench-Kitchen}, a benchmark containing 1,500 manually verified question--answer pairs from 160 cooking videos. It covers five task families: Ingredient Entry, Memory Management, Recipe Query, Long-Term Planning, and Short-Term Planning, spanning food-state construction, maintenance, knowledge retrieval, and decision-making across different planning horizons. Evaluations of proprietary and open-source large vision-language models reveal a substantial gap from human performance, particularly in quantitative ingredient estimation, long-term state tracking, and reasoning under interacting constraints. We further introduce \textbf{Nutri-Vgent}, a diagnostic long-video agent with separate episodic, food-state, and recipe memories. Its consistent improvements demonstrate the value of explicit state representations and structured memory for nutrition management. Together, NutriBench-Kitchen and Nutri-Vgent provide a testbed for studying persistent state tracking and knowledge-grounded reasoning in dynamic kitchens. Code is available at https://github.com/V1ol1n/NutriBench-Kitchen.

</details>


### [159] [Flow3D-OPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for 3D Geometry Generation with Flow-Matching Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07137)
*Zhiwei Ning,Zhen Zhou,Puhua Jiang,Xintong Han,Gengming Zhang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Wei Liu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow3D-OPD，一个两阶段后训练框架，将多教师蒸馏引入3D几何生成。第一阶段利用半策略增强基础模型能力并设计智能体验证器评估3D几何质量，通过DPO培养领域专精教师模型；第二阶段通过带硬任务路由采样和梯度累积的在线策略蒸馏整合异构专家到统一学生模型，缓解联合优化中的梯度干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流匹配扩散Transformer的图像到3D生成模型虽能产生高保真网格，但其后训练策略尚未被充分探索。强化学习在该领域面临两个关键瓶颈：定义全面3D几何质量奖励的固有困难，以及联合优化异构目标时的梯度干扰问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1)第一阶段：使用半策略增强预训练模型的基础能力，设计智能体验证器评估3D几何质量，基于验证器通过直接偏好优化(DPO)培养领域专精的教师模型；(2)第二阶段：通过带硬任务路由采样和梯度累积的在线策略蒸馏(OPD)将异构专家知识整合到统一的学生模型中，以缓解梯度干扰。

Result: 该方法在所有几何质量维度上均实现了一致的改进，在平均指标上超越所有教师模型，无需依赖复杂的修改即可取得显著效果。

Conclusion: 大量实验表明，Flow3D-OPD为3D生成中的强化学习提供了一种有效的范式，展示了将多教师在线策略蒸馏应用于3D几何后训练的可行性。

Abstract: Recent image-to-3D generation models built on flow-matching diffusion Transformers (DiT) can produce high-fidelity meshes, yet their post-training strategy remains largely unexplored. There exist several critical bottlenecks in reinforcement learning: the inherent difficulty of defining comprehensive rewards for 3D geometric quality, and the gradient interference that arises when jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives. Inspired by the practicability of on-policy distillation (OPD) in large language models and image generation, we propose \textbf{Flow3D-OPD}, a two-stage post-training framework that introduces multi-teacher distillation into 3D geometry generation. In the first stage, we utilize the semi-policy to enhance the foundational capability of the pretrained model and then design an agentic verifier for 3D geometric quality evaluation. Based on the verifier, we could cultivate domain-specialized teacher models via direct preference optimization (DPO). In the second stage, we consolidate heterogeneous expertise into a unified student model through on-policy distillation with hard task-routing sampling and gradient accumulation, which could mitigate the gradient interference in joint optimization. Without relying on elaborate modifications, our straightforward yet effective design achieves consistent improvements across all geometric quality dimensions and surpasses all teacher models in the average metric. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach provides an effective paradigm for reinforcement learning in 3D generation.

</details>


### [160] [Ambient @ EgoLongQA 2026: Distilling Long-Video perception into a Sub-2B Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07154)
*Logesh Kumar Umapathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个在ECCV 2026可穿戴AI挑战赛EgoLongQA赛道≤2B参数组中排名第一的系统。该系统是一个2B参数的视觉语言模型，通过蒸馏工具使用智能体流水线中的初级感知模块获得，能够以单次前向传播回答关于十分钟自我中心视频的多选题。系统达到大型智能体流水线89%的准确率，仅使用其1.1%的参数，并通过剪枝多语言嵌入表使模型参数从2.2132B降至1.9985B以满足比赛限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ECCV 2026 Wearable-AI挑战赛的EgoLongQA赛道中，需要在≤2B参数限制下回答十分钟自我中心视频的多选题。作者探索如何将强大的工具使用智能体流水线的知识压缩到一个小规模模型中，同时满足比赛参数上限要求，实现高性能与低成本之间的平衡。

Method: 采用蒸馏方法，将工具使用智能体流水线中的初级感知模块（而非智能体本身）蒸馏到一个2B规模的视觉语言模型中。蒸馏时使用经筛选的教师轨迹（仅保留回答正确的轨迹）。为满足参数限制，将多语言嵌入表从248,320行剪枝至143,469行，使总参数从2.2132B降至1.9985B，且在保留的行上产生可证明相同的logits。系统以单次贪婪前向传播直接输出答案。

Result: 在held-out测试集上取得0.8279的成绩，获≤2B参数组第一名。达到大型智能体流水线89%的准确率，仅使用其1.1%的参数。将基础模型准确率从27.1%提升至81.4%。通过嵌入表剪枝，模型参数从2.2132B降至1.9985B，符合比赛2B参数限制。

Conclusion: 蒸馏智能体流水线中的感知模块而非完整智能体，是一种有效的小模型压缩策略，能以极小参数规模获得接近大型流水线的性能。嵌入表剪枝方法可在不损失已保留token预测精度的情况下合法地满足参数限制，为小规模VLM竞赛系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We describe our entry to the EgoLongQA track of the Wearable-AI Challenge in ECCV 2026, which placed first in the <=2B parameter division with 0.8279 on the held-out test set. Our system is a single 2B vision-language model that answers multiple-choice questions about ten-minute egocentric videos in one greedy forward pass; It is obtained by distilling the junior perception module of a tool-using agentic pipeline, not the agent itself into a small student, using teacher traces filtered to those that answered correctly. it reaches 89% of the accuracy of the large agentic pipeline using 1.1% of its parameters. This raises a 27.1% base model to 81.4% on our held-out questions. The 2B backbone has 2.2132B parameters and therefore over the divisional limit, to make the entry admissable we prune the multilingual embedding table from 248,320 to 143,469 rows, reaching 1.9985B with provably identical logits on retained rows.

</details>


### [161] [Mind the Approximation: Fisher-Weighted SVD Compression for ViTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07155)
*Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Groezinger,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Emad Aghajanzadeh,Ryan Pegoud,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Alexander Frickenstein,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FACTS，一种面向Vision Transformer（ViT）的结构化Fisher近似方法，用于Fisher加权SVD压缩。FACTS强制token局部聚合同时保留token内激活-梯度依赖，并引入快速约束秩搜索（CoRS）在固定FLOP约束下优化逐层秩分配。实验表明FACTS在无需微调的情况下一致地改善精度-效率权衡，在Swin-B上较最强SVD基线提升高达+5.8个百分点Top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现对于ViT而言，提高压缩中Fisher近似的保真度并不能很好地预测压缩后的精度，这暗示现有Fisher近似方法未充分考虑ViT的结构特性（如token间的相关性），需要一种针对ViT定制的结构化Fisher近似方案。

Method: 1) 提出FACTS：一种结构化的Fisher近似方法，在Fisher加权SVD压缩中强制token局部聚合（token-local aggregation），同时保留token内部激活-梯度间的依赖关系。2) 提出CoRS：一种快速约束秩搜索方法，在固定的FLOP约束下对各层秩分配进行优化。

Result: 在多种ViT和混合架构上，FACTS无需微调即一致改善精度-效率权衡。在Swin-B上，较最强SVD基线提升达+5.8个百分点Top-1准确率，结合CoRS秩搜索方法可进一步获得性能增益。

Conclusion: 针对ViT结构特性定制的结构化Fisher近似（FACTS）比通用Fisher近似更有效，配合FLOP约束下的秩搜索方法（CoRS），可在无需微调的前提下显著提升ViT的SVD压缩精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Model compression is key to mitigate deployment challenges of ever growing machine learning models. In this area of research, singular value decomposition (SVD)-based compression offers a compelling trade-off between computational efficiency and model accuracy. Fisher-weighted SVD in particular provides principled, loss-aware compression. However, we find that improving the fidelity of Fisher approximation used in the compression is poorly predictive of post-compression accuracy for Vision Transformers (ViTs). Motivated by this observation, we propose FACTS, a structured Fisher Approximation tailored to Compressing ViTs with Fisher-weighted SVD, which enforces token-local aggregation while preserving within-token activation-gradient dependence. Additionally, we introduce a fast Constrained Rank Search (CoRS), that optimizes layer-wise rank allocation while adhering to a fixed floating point operation (FLOP) constraint. Extensive experiments across ViTs and hybrid architectures demonstrate that FACTS consistently improves accuracy-efficiency trade-offs without requiring finetuning. Notably, it outperforms the strongest SVD baseline by up to +5.8 percentage points (p.p.) Top-1 on Swin-B, with further gains driven by our search method. Code is available at https://github.com/MoritzTho/FACTS.

</details>


### [162] [Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Symbol Spotting in Historical Encrypted Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07159)
*Giuseppe De Gregorio,Alicia Fornés,Lei Kang,Beáta Megyesi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线，用于在无标注样本的情况下，从历史加密手稿中识别和定位加密字母表中的符号。通过联合SimCLR+DANN编码器实现域不变的字符表示，并在检索时应用嵌入空间风格自适应机制，无需重新训练即可弥合数字字体与手稿符号之间的域差距。实验表明该方法大幅超越零样本基础模型（如CLIP、DINOv2）和任务特定训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史加密手稿的解密面临根本性挑战：在转写之前必须先识别底层密码字母表的符号库。现有方法受限于带标签目标脚本样本的缺失，以及数字渲染字体查询与退化的手写手稿符号之间存在显著的域差距。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 联合SimCLR+DANN编码器学习域不变的字符表示，弥合字体与手稿之间的域差距；(2) 在嵌入空间中进行风格自适应，应用于检索阶段且无需重新训练；(3) 在无监督设置下计算Raw-Cover指标，提供独特的脚本族指纹以识别未知文档的底层字母表。

Result: 在来自七个加密手稿收藏的十四页上进行实验，该方法在P@1指标上超越零样本基础模型CLIP ViT-L/14达+0.194，超越任务特定的训练基线达+0.138。Raw-Cover指标能有效识别未知文档所属的字母系统。

Conclusion: 该方法无需人工标注即可从加密手稿中定位符号，对古文字学家、历史学家等研究未解密手稿的学者具有直接的实际应用价值。Raw-Cover可作为脚本族指纹工具辅助识别字母表来源。

Abstract: The decipherment of historical encrypted manuscripts poses a fundamental challenge in Digital Humanities: before any transcription can begin, the symbol inventory of the underlying cipher alphabet must first be identified and characterized. We address this challenge through symbol spotting: given a candidate alphabet specified as a set of rendered font glyphs, the task is to determine whether and where its characters appear in an unseen handwritten document, without any labeled examples from the target script. The main difficulty lies in the domain gap between clean, digitally rendered font queries and degraded handwritten manuscript symbols. We propose a three-stage pipeline that bridges this gap without manual annotation, combining a joint SimCLR+DANN encoder for domain-invariant glyph representations with an embedding-space style-adaptation mechanism applied at retrieval time, requiring no re-training. Experiments on fourteen pages from seven encrypted manuscript collections show that our method outperforms zero-shot foundation models, including CLIP and DINOv2, by a large margin ($+0.194$ P@1 over CLIP ViT-L/14), and surpasses task-specific trained baselines by $+0.138$ P@1. We further demonstrate that the Raw-Cover metric, computed in a fully unsupervised setting, provides a meaningful script-family fingerprint that identifies the underlying alphabet of an unknown document. This capability is of direct practical relevance to palaeographers, historians, and other researchers working with undeciphered manuscripts.

</details>


### [163] [Weakly-supervised Kidney Tumor Classification from CT Scans with Multi-Instance Learning and Anatomical Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07178)
*Joonas Ariva,Dmytro Fishman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督CT分类流水线，结合多实例学习（MIL）和解剖过滤方法，仅使用扫描级标签即可实现肾脏肿瘤分类，在内部数据集上达到F1=0.83，并验证了其在外部数据集上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT扫描分析的深度学习模型常受限于像素级标注的稀缺性，而仅使用扫描级标签虽能减少标注成本，但面临低监督比率和大输入体积导致的过拟合与捷径学习问题。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(1) 多实例学习（MIL），将CT体数据划分为2D切片实例，支持高效的2D架构并利用ImageNet预训练；(2) 解剖过滤，利用自监督的Compass体部位回归模型裁剪扫描至病理相关子区域，无需分割掩膜。评估了ABMIL和FocusMIL两种MIL框架。

Result: 在内部测试集上最佳模型仅使用扫描级标签即达到F1=0.83。解剖过滤对基于嵌入的ABMIL的分布外泛化至关重要，而基于实例的FocusMIL对分布偏移表现出更强的内在鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作作为更广泛的弱监督CT分类流水线的概念验证，适用于其他器官和病理类型。

Abstract: Deep learning models for CT scan analysis are often limited by the scarcity of precise pixel-level annotations, which require significant radiologist effort to produce. Training on scan-level labels alone reduces annotation requirements but introduces challenges: low supervision ratios and large input volumes make models prone to overfitting and shortcut learning. In this work, we investigate two complementary methods to address these challenges: multi-instance learning (MIL) and anatomical filtering. MIL divides CT volumes into 2D slice instances, enabling efficient 2D architectures with ImageNet pretraining rather than computationally demanding 3D models. Anatomical filtering uses Compass, our self-supervised body part regression model, to crop scans to pathology-relevant subregions without requiring segmentation masks. We evaluate two MIL frameworks - Attention-based MIL (ABMIL) and FocusMIL - on kidney tumor classification across one internal dataset (TUH) and two external datasets (KiTS23 and TCGA-KiRC). Our best models achieve F1 = 0.83 on the internal test set using only scan-level labels. We further show that anatomical filtering with the Compass model is critical for the out-of-distribution generalization of embedding-based ABMIL, while instance-based FocusMIL demonstrates greater inherent robustness to distribution shift. While evaluated on kidney tumors, we consider this a proof-of-concept for a broader weakly supervised CT classification pipeline applicable to other organs and pathologies.

</details>


### [164] [Deep Learning for Biopsy-Free Subtyping of Basal Cell Carcinoma from Dermatoscopic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07180)
*Alexandros Papadopoulos,Chrysa Episkopou,Ioannis Sarafis,Aimilios Lallas,Anastasios Delopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文初步探索了利用深度学习从单一皮肤镜图像进行基底细胞癌（BCC）亚型分类，采用预训练视觉Transformer（ViT）在1271张皮肤镜图像上实现了AUC 0.784的性能，超过CNN基线以及人类读者表现，为无创BCC亚型分类提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCC是最常见的皮肤癌类型（约占80%），其临床管理依赖于组织病理学亚型，侵袭性变异需要更激进的治疗。目前亚型分类依赖皮肤活检，该过程既昂贵又具有侵入性，因此需要探索一种无创的替代方法。

Method: 利用预训练视觉Transformer（ViT）模型进行BCC亚型分类，仅使用病灶的单张皮肤镜图像作为输入。在有限的小规模数据集条件下，采用重复分层k折交叉验证来评估模型性能，并与基于CNN的基线方法及既往报告的人类读者表现进行比较。

Result: 在包含1271张不同BCC亚型皮肤镜图像的数据集上，ViT模型在区分侵袭性BCC与其他亚型家族的任务中取得了AUC 0.784的优越性能，同时超过了标准CNN基线以及既往人类读者的表现。

Conclusion: 初步研究结果表明，将深度学习与皮肤镜影像相结合具有为BCC亚型分类提供免活检替代方案的潜力，从而有助于改善治疗规划和患者预后。

Abstract: Basal Cell Carcinoma (BCC) is the most common type of skin cancer, accounting for nearly 80% of skin cancer di- agnoses. Its optimal clinical management is guided by the distinct histopathologic subtype, with aggressive variants requiring more drastic measures. In current clinical practice, subtyping relies on skin biopsies, a procedure both costly and invasive. In this paper, we conduct a preliminary investigation into using deep learning for BCC subtyping, solely from a single dermatoscopic image of the lesion. Given the limited data at our disposal, we employ pre-trained vision transformers (ViTs), a state-of-the-art family of models highly effective for challenging downstream tasks with limited labeled data. Through repeated stratified k-fold cross-validation, we demonstrate that ViTs can achieve superior performance (AUC 0.784 on a dataset of 1271 dermatoscopic images of various BCC subtypes) over standard CNN-based baselines as well as previously-reported human reader perfor- mance, on the task of differentiating aggressive BCCs from other subtype families. These initial findings highlight the potential of combining deep learning and dermatoscopy to provide a biopsy- free alternative for BCC subtyping, thus aiding in improving treatment planning and patient outcomes.

</details>


### [165] [CHILD: Human-in-the-Loop OOD Detection for Safe Clinical Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07188)
*Jinlun Ye,Kaiyue Lu,Runhe Lai,Xinhua Lu,Jia-Xin Zhuang,Ruixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHILD是一个无需训练的框架，通过稀疏人类反馈来增强流式医学OOD检测，在严格预算约束下利用自适应风险感知样本选择机制和检索式分数校准模块，仅用5%的反馈预算即可显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应技术虽能提升OOD检测性能，但在安全性至关重要的临床环境中自动调节可能带来安全风险。虽然医生监督可以缓解这些风险，但资源消耗大且需要合理分配，因此需要在安全性与效率之间取得平衡。

Method: CHILD框架包含两个核心组件：1) 自适应风险感知样本选择机制，仅挑选决策最不确定的样本供人类审查；2) 基于检索的分数校准模块，利用紧凑特征缓存改进模型预测，无需任何参数更新。整个框架在严格预算约束下运行，且为训练无关（training-free）方案。

Result: 在四个医学基准数据集上的实验表明，在仅5%的稀疏反馈预算下，CHILD将平均FPR95从72.63%降至60.26%，AUROC从75.53%提高至81.85%，持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: CHILD证明了有限的人类监督可以转化为显著的可靠性提升，为安全关键性临床环境中的OOD检测提供了一种兼顾安全性与效率的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is critical for safe deployment of medical AI systems. Recently, test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a new paradigm for OOD detection, automatically adjusting detector behavior during deployment. However, such automatic adaptation mechanisms may raise safety concerns in safety-critical clinical environments. While physician oversight can mitigate these risks, it is resource-intensive and must be judiciously allocated. To reconcile safety with efficiency, we propose CHILD, a training-free framework designed to enhance streaming OOD detection via sparse human feedback. Operating under strict budget constraints, CHILD employs an adaptive risk-aware sample selection mechanism to pinpoint only the most decision-uncertain samples for review. Crucially, it maximizes the utility of this sparse feedback through a retrieval-based score calibration module, which refines model predictions using a compact feature cache without any parameter updates. Extensive experiments on four medical benchmarks demonstrate that CHILD turns limited supervision into significant reliability gains: with a sparse feedback budget of only 5%, it reduces the average FPR95 from 72.63% to 60.26% and improves AUROC from 75.53% to 81.85%, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/figec/CHILD.

</details>


### [166] [The Price of Consistency: Exploiting Visual Anchors for Multimodal Jailbreaking in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07216)
*Peng Li,Qianqian Xu,Yangbangyan Jiang,Zhipeng Yu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示视频生成中参考图像的"视觉锚定效应"：一致性约束机制会阻止生成内容偏离原始有害意图，消除模型从有害到良性内容的自然安全逃逸路径，从而固有地增加安全风险。作者提出训练免费的DIVA多模态越狱框架，将有害意图解耦为静态视觉锚定图像和动态运动文本提示，并发布首个多条件视频生成安全基准TI2VSafetyBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成已从纯文本驱动转向多条件可控生成，参考图像被广泛用作条件输入以增强时空一致性，但其对安全性的影响尚未被探索。

Method: 提出DIVA框架：将有害意图解耦为静态视觉锚定图像与动态运动文本提示两部分，采用双标准选择机制在攻击隐蔽性和语义保持之间取得平衡，且无需训练。

Result: 在多个主流商业平台和开源视频生成模型上的大量实验表明，DIVA相比纯文本攻击方法实现了显著更高的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 视觉锚定效应使一致性成为安全性的代价；DIVA证明了这一漏洞的有效利用，并贡献了TI2VSafetyBench以推动多条件视频生成安全研究。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of video generation has shifted the paradigm from pure text-driven to multi-conditional controllable generation, with reference images now widely adopted as conditional inputs to achieve superior spatiotemporal consistency. While these reference images serve as powerful visual anchors that significantly enhance controllability, their impact on safety remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reveal the visual anchoring effect: by enforcing consistency, the mechanism prevents the generated content from drifting away from the original harmful intent, thereby eliminating the model's natural safety escape route from harmful to benign content. Consequently, visual anchors inherently increase the safety risk---this is the price of consistency. Building on this insight, we propose Decoupling Intent via Visual Anchors (DIVA), a training-free multimodal jailbreak framework for video generation that exploits this vulnerability. DIVA decouples harmful intent into a static visual anchor image and a dynamic motion text prompt, and employs dual-criteria selection to balance attack stealthiness with semantic preservation. Extensive experiments across various leading commercial platforms and mainstream open-source video generation models demonstrate that DIVA achieves a substantially higher Attack Success Rate than existing text-only methods. To facilitate future research, we additionally contribute TI2VSafetyBench, the first safety benchmark for multi-conditional video generation.

</details>


### [167] [Proximity-CLIP: Text-Guided Semantic Proximity Learning for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07229)
*Manwen Yang,Leqian Ding,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Proximity-CLIP框架，解决视觉语言模型在零样本异常检测中因目标中心偏置导致的文本原型语义重叠问题，通过视觉校准语义边缘和异常查询模块，实现判别性与结构连续性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在零样本异常检测中表现良好，但存在目标中心偏置问题，使得正常与异常文本原型语义高度重叠。虽然强制正交化可提升判别性，但将视觉输入映射到截然正交的原型会破坏预训练模型的结构连续性，形成几何困境。

Method: Proximity-CLIP包含两部分：(1) 视觉校准语义邻近学习机制，通过有界动态正则化学习适当的语义边缘，兼顾判别分离与结构对齐；(2) 异常查询模块(AQM)，以校准后的异常原型作为语义查询，从上下文视觉补丁中主动检索局部缺陷线索，缓解全局池化对细微异常的稀释。

Result: 在多个零样本异常检测基准上，Proximity-CLIP以极小的架构修改超越了当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过视觉校准语义边缘而非强制正交化，Proximity-CLIP有效解决了原型重叠与结构失真之间的矛盾，为零样本异常检测提供了一种轻量而高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models offer a promising approach for zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD). However, due to object-centric bias, normal and anomalous text prototypes exhibit a high semantic overlap. While enforcing strict orthogonality between them improves discriminability, mapping highly contiguous visual inputs onto drastically orthogonal prototypes introduces a geometric dilemma, disrupting the pre-trained structural continuity. To address this problem, we propose Proximity-CLIP, a framework that visually calibrates the semantic margin to guide visual adaptation. First, we introduce a visually-calibrated semantic proximity learning mechanism that uses a bounded dynamic regularization to learn an appropriate semantic margin, ensuring discriminative separation while preserving structural alignment. Second, we design an Anomaly Query Module (AQM) driven by these text priors. Using the calibrated anomalous prototype as a semantic query, the AQM actively retrieves localized defect cues from contextual visual patches, mitigating the dilution of subtle anomalies during global pooling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Proximity-CLIP outperforms current state-of-the-art methods across multiple ZSAD benchmarks with minimal architectural modifications.

</details>


### [168] [MV-STRIDE: Enabling MLLMs to Master Multi-View Spatial Reasoning via Hierarchical Capability Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07258)
*Jin Xu,Xiaojian Huang,Zhuodong Luo,Zhihong Zhang,Xin Liu,Jiansheng Wei,Xinzhi Wang,Jie Zhao,Xuejin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-STRIDE数据集，通过显式建模基础感知、场景理解与复杂语境推理之间的层级依赖关系，为多模态大语言模型提供结构化的3D空间推理学习路径，并配套了多阶段训练框架以实现跨视角一致的空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在2D视觉语言任务上进展迅速，但在多视角空间推理上仍存在根本性瓶颈，主要原因是现有数据集缺乏结构化的3D认知通路，无法有效引导模型建立层级化的空间理解能力。

Method: 构建MV-STRIDE分层空间推理数据集，突破扁平数据结构的局限，显式建模基础感知→场景理解→复杂语境推理之间的依赖关系；开发系统化的QA生成流水线，利用多种3D场景源并强制执行跨视角依赖约束以防止单视角可解性，同时为复杂推理提供基于认知的思维链监督；在此基础上提出多阶段训练框架。

Result: 多阶段训练框架在多个空间推理基准上达到最先进性能，尤其在多视角导向的MMSI-Bench上表现突出，使MLLMs能够在不同视角下维持鲁棒的、3D一致的空间推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过层级化、结构化的空间推理数据集与多阶段训练框架，可以有效增强MLLMs的3D一致空间推理能力，为多模态模型的空间认知提供了可复现的规范化路径，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in 2D vision-language tasks, robust multi-view spatial reasoning remains a fundamental bottleneck due to the lack of structured 3D cognitive pathways in existing datasets. To address this, we introduce MV-STRIDE, a Multi-View hierarchical SpaTial Reasoning dataset with Interdependent and DEcomposed capabilitiEs. Moving beyond flat data structures, MV-STRIDE explicitly models the dependency relationships between foundational perception, scene understanding, and complex contextual reasoning, providing a coherent learning pathway aligned with human spatial cognition. We develop a systematic QA generation pipeline leveraging diverse 3D scene sources that enforces cross-view dependency constraints to prevent single-view solvability, generating multi-level spatial reasoning tasks supported by cognitively grounded chain-of-thought supervision for complex inference. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our multi-stage training framework based on our hierarchical dataset achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple spatial reasoning benchmarks, notably the multi-view oriented MMSI-Bench. Our approach enables MLLMs to maintain robust, 3D-consistent spatial reasoning across diverse viewpoints. The code and dataset are available at https://co1dspring.github.io/MV-STRIDE/.

</details>


### [169] [KODAMA: Multimodal Digital Twin Reconstruction for Urban RF Propagation Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07298)
*Maximiliano Wardle,A. Ryo Koblitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KODAMA是一个自动化pipeline，仅利用现成的地理空间数据（航拍图像、LiDAR和摄影测量）即可在城市尺度重建支持光线追踪的射频数字孪生（RFDT），避免了传统RFDT在手工业和测量校准方面的耗时过程，并在3.6至28 GHz三个场景下实现了与手工校准模型相当的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RFDT构建需要在粗略的自动化场景与手工搭建、需测量校准的模型之间做出选择：前者精度不足，后者每个站点需数周构建时间。研究者希望实现无需现场勘测和校准、仅基于现成地理数据即可自动生成高保真的通信信道预测模型。

Method: KODAMA采用全自动pipeline：利用航拍图像、LiDAR和摄影测量生成地形及封闭的建筑网格；通过曝光加权的多视角融合技术处理街景图像，恢复建筑立面细节、电磁材料属性及杂波信息。整个流程无需实地访问和测量校准。

Result: 在覆盖3.6至28 GHz频率范围的三个站点上，KODAMA无需校准的预测实现了个位数RMSE，将点对点误差相比自动化基线降低了最高5.35 dB，并且与经过测量校准的手工构建RFDT相比仅有0.22 dB的差距。

Conclusion: KODAMA证明全自动、免校准的RFDT重建可以达到接近手工校准模型的精度，为城市尺度的站点特定信道预测提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction typically strives for geometric fidelity or visual plausibility. Radio frequency digital twins (RFDT) are instead judged by whether communication channels behave in them as they do in the real world. RFDTs promise site-specific channel prediction but current practice forces a choice between coarse automated scenes and hand-built, measurement-calibrated models that take weeks to construct per-site. We present KODAMA, an automated pipeline that reconstructs ray tracing-ready RFDTs at city scale from off-the-shelf geospatial data alone: aerial imagery, LiDAR, and photogrammetry yield terrain and watertight building meshes, while exposure-weighted multi-view fusion of street-level imagery recovers façade relief, electromagnetic materials, and clutter---all without site visits or calibration. Across three sites spanning 3.6 to 28 GHz, KODAMA's uncalibrated predictions achieve single-digit RMSE, reducing point-to-point error by up to 5.35 dB over automated baselines and coming within 0.22 dB of a measurement-calibrated, hand-built RFDT.

</details>


### [170] [CRISP: Corneal Confocal Microscopy Real-Time Image Stitching Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07336)
*Qincheng Qiao,Puli Zhang,Jian Zhou,Xinguo Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRISP，一个开源的实时角膜共聚焦显微镜（CCM）视频流角膜上皮下神经丛（SNP）广域拼接框架，通过焦点感知门控、局部配准传播和稀疏锚点图实现低延迟实时拼接，并在时间连续性中断时通过全局外观检索和几何验证完成重定位与子图合并，输出结果可用于离线精细拼接初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流CCM设备单帧视野有限，而SNP空间分布不均匀，离散图像采样对采样位置和帧选择敏感，导致可重复性差、临床采用受限。现有广域拼接方法依赖离线后处理、额外硬件或特定采集协议，缺乏面向常规CCM视频流的开源实时解决方案。

Method: CRISP框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 基于焦点感知门控排除离焦和不连续段；(2) 通过局部成对配准传播位姿；(3) 利用稀疏锚点图维持非冗余空间覆盖。当局部时间连续性中断时，通过全局外观检索加几何验证完成重定位和子图合并。系统在检查过程中提供低延迟覆盖反馈，同时输出已接受的帧、位姿和锚点信息用于离线精细拼接。

Result: CRISP被宣称是首个面向常规CCM视频流公开发布的开源实时SNP广域拼接框架，但摘要中未提供具体的定量评估结果或与其他方法的对比数据。

Conclusion: CRISP通过降低广域拼接技术的采用和复现门槛，有望将SNP广域成像从研究工具转化为常规临床检查流程的一部分，推动CCM作为定量评估工具的临床普及。

Abstract: Morphology of the sub-basal nerve plexus (SNP) reflects peripheral nerve health, and corneal confocal microscopy (CCM) provides an important means for in vivo, real-time, non-invasive observation of the SNP. However, mainstream CCM devices offer a limited field of view per frame, whereas the SNP is spatially non-uniform; discrete image sampling is therefore sensitive to sampling location and frame selection, which limits the reproducibility and clinical adoption of CCM as a quantitative assessment tool.
