Wide-field stitching can reconstruct larger SNP mosaics by integrating sequentially acquired CCM images, but existing methods largely rely on offline post-processing, additional hardware, or specific acquisition protocols, and lack open-source real-time solutions for conventional CCM video streams.
  This paper presents CRISP (Corneal confocal microscopy Real-time Image Stitching Pipeline), an open-source real-time SNP wide-field stitching framework for conventional CCM examination video streams. CRISP excludes defocused and discontinuous segments via focus-aware gating, propagates poses through local pairwise registration, and maintains non-redundant spatial coverage with a sparse anchor map; when local temporal continuity is interrupted, the system completes relocalization and subgraph merging through global appearance retrieval followed by geometric verification. The framework prioritizes low-latency coverage feedback during examination while outputting accepted frames, poses, and anchor information to initialize offline fine stitching.
  To our knowledge, CRISP is the first open-source real-time SNP wide-field stitching framework released for conventional CCM video streams. By lowering the barrier to adoption and reproduction of wide-field stitching, CRISP may help move SNP wide-field imaging from a research tool into routine clinical examination workflows.

</details>


### [171] [From Explicit References to Scene Manifolds: Distributional Fidelity and Realism for Radiance Field Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07346)
*Saeed Mahmoudpour,Gi-Mun Um,Hyon-Gon Choo,Peter Schelkens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCODA，一种轻量级的场景条件式客观质量评估方法，用于评估3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）和新视角合成渲染的图像质量。与需要对齐参考图像的传统全参考指标或需要选择邻近参考的跨参考指标不同，SCODA将质量评估从显式的图像间比较转向场景流形建模——将场景的高质量观测表示为深度特征空间中的多元高斯分布，通过偏离该分布的程度来度量语义保真度；同时利用弱监督的失真感知块判别器提供互补的真实感信号，并通过无监督的有界融合策略将两者结合。实验表明该方法与人类判断高度一致，且在GS和NeRF生成的视图与轨迹上具有鲁棒泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场表示（如3DGS）在重建、渲染和压缩过程中会产生复杂的视图相关伪影，需要可靠的质量评估。然而现有全参考指标需要对齐的参考图像，跨参考指标虽放宽了要求，但在宽基线的辐射场设置下，特别是沿任意轨迹和姿态评估视图时，难以选择可靠的邻近参考。

Method: SCODA通过场景条件化将QA从显式图像对比转变为场景流形建模：(1) 用多元高斯分布表示场景在深度特征空间中的高质量观测，计算语义保真度分数；(2) 采用弱监督的失真感知块判别器提供真实感信号；(3) 通过无监督有界融合策略结合两个线索。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，SCODA与人类判断高度一致，并且在GS和NeRF生成的视图与轨迹上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCODA有效解决了宽基线辐射场设置下参考图像选择困难的问题，提供了一种不依赖对齐参考的、可泛化的场景级质量评估方法，代码已公开。

Abstract: Radiance field representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enable high-quality novel view synthesis but can introduce complex, view-dependent artifacts from reconstruction, rendering, and compression. Reliable perceptual quality assessment (QA) is thus essential for evaluating rendered views and guiding the design of perceptually faithful scene representations. Existing full-reference QA metrics require an aligned reference image, while recent cross-reference metrics relax this requirement by comparing a test view with non-aligned references. However, under wide-baseline radiance field settings, selecting a reliable nearby reference can be difficult, particularly when evaluating views along arbitrary trajectories and poses. We propose SCODA, a lightweight scene-conditioned objective QA method that shifts QA from explicit image-to-image comparison to scene-manifold modeling. High-quality observations of each scene are represented as a multivariate Gaussian distribution in deep feature space, producing a semantic fidelity score that measures deviation from the scene distribution. A weakly-supervised distortion-aware patch discriminator provides a complementary realism signal, and both cues are combined through an unsupervised bounded fusion strategy. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show strong agreement with human judgments and robust generalization across GS- and NeRF-generated views and trajectories. Code is publicly available at https://gitlab.com/saeedmp/scoda.

</details>


### [172] [DF26: We Cannot Tell Fake From Real Anymore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07369)
*Severyn Shykula,Andrii Yermakov,Ivan Samarskyi,Dmytro Mishkin,Jan Cech,Anastasiia Mishchuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DF26基准数据集，用于检测AI生成的视频。该数据集包含271个真实视频和2,420个由七种现代视频模型生成的合成视频，涵盖单人公开演讲场景。研究发现人类和现有最先进的深度伪造检测器在该基准上的表现接近随机猜测，揭示了当前评估协议的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测基准无法适应现代文本到视频和图像到视频模型的分布偏移，需要专门的基准来评估检测器对最新生成模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建DF26基准数据集，包含单人公开演讲场景（直接对镜头录制、官方声明、演播室采访）的真实与AI生成视频，由七种现代视频模型生成2,420个合成视频，并评估人类与SOTA深度伪造检测器的性能。

Result: 人类在检测AI生成视频方面的表现接近随机猜测，最先进的深度伪造检测器同样接近随机水平，表明现有检测方法在现代生成模型面前失效。

Conclusion: 当前评估协议存在显著局限性，亟需设计能够明确衡量对现代生成模型分布偏移鲁棒性的基准。

Abstract: We introduce DF26, a novel benchmark for detecting AI-generated videos containing fully synthetic clips produced by recent text-to-video and image-to-video models. The videos capture single-person public-speaking scenarios, spanning direct-to-camera recordings, official statements, and studio interviews - 271 real and 2,420 synthetic videos generated by seven modern video models. The study on DF26 shows that human performance in detecting AI-generated videos, as well as state-of-the-art deepfake detectors, is close to random chance. Our results highlight the limitations of current evaluation protocols and motivate the need for benchmarks that explicitly measure robustness to modern generative model distribution shifts.

</details>


### [173] [TRAIL: Trajectory-Aware Visual Place Recognition against Unordered Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07373)
*Dominik A. Kloepfer,Patrick Wenzel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAIL框架，利用查询序列中的轨迹上下文信息进行视觉位置识别，通过条件随机场组合视觉相似度与相机运动一致性约束，在特征贫乏环境下相比基线提升最高8.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VPR方法在标准基准上表现优异但在特征贫乏环境中不够鲁棒，因为它们在处理每个查询图像时是孤立的，丢弃了真实轨迹中存在的序列上下文信息。作者希望利用这一被忽视的上下文来提升定位性能。

Method: 论文形式化了一个新任务：给定查询序列，对最后一张图像在无序参考数据库中进行定位（区别于需要数据库有序的序列到序列方法）。提出TRAIL框架，基于条件随机场（CRF）结合视觉相似度和相机运动一致性的学习函数，在查询逐帧到达时精炼候选参考的分布。该框架可作为任何预训练VPR骨干网络的轻量级后处理层。

Result: 在主基准上将最先进基线提升最高8.3个百分点；无需重新训练即可迁移到未见过的数据集；在视觉线索稀缺的环境中增益最大。

Conclusion: 轨迹上下文信息对于提升VPR在困难环境下的鲁棒性至关重要，TRAIL通过轻量级CRF后处理层即可有效利用此类上下文，且具有良好的泛化能力与实用价值。

Abstract: Modern Visual Place Recognition (VPR) methods excel on standard benchmarks yet remain brittle in feature-poor environments. By treating each query image in isolation, they discard the sequential context in any real trajectory. We formalize a task that exploits this context: given a query sequence, localize the final image against an unordered reference database -- which, unlike sequence-to-sequence methods, requires no sequential structure in the database. We propose TRAIL (TRajectory-Aware Image Localization), a principled framework based on Conditional Random Fields (CRF) that combines learned functions for visual similarity and for camera-motion consistency, refining a distribution over candidate references as each query arrives. A lightweight post-processing layer atop any pre-trained VPR backbone, TRAIL improves a state-of-the-art baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points on our primary benchmark, transfers to unseen datasets without retraining, and delivers its largest gains where visual cues are scarce.

</details>


### [174] [Social Intuition vs. Machine Reasoning: Anticipating Human-Robot Interaction from multiple modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07394)
*Raphael Lorenzo-Louis,Bertrand Luvison,Serena Ivaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了从服务机器人视角预测他人互动意图的任务，对比了人类标注者、轻量级姿态模型和视觉语言模型在HUI360数据集上的表现，发现人类在完整视频输入下显著优于VLM，且模型大小与性能并不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一人称视角预测一个人是否会与他人互动对人类来说是直观的任务，但对服务机器人而言具有挑战性。作者希望通过系统性地对比人类、轻量级姿态模型和视觉语言模型的表现，探究当前模型在预测互动意图方面的能力差距。

Method: 在HUI360数据集的100条测试轨迹（25正例、75负例）上进行基准测试。比较了人类标注者在仅有姿态输入和完整视频输入两种条件下的表现，同时评估多个轻量级姿态模型和不同规模的视觉语言模型，并测试了不同输入条件对VLM的影响。

Result: 姿态输入下，人类标注者以+0.08的F1分数小幅优于轻量级姿态模型；但在完整自我中心视频加目标边界框条件下，人类显著优于视觉语言模型（+0.2 F1分数）。此外，VLM的最佳表现与模型大小没有相关性。

Conclusion: 互动预测对社交机器人来说是具有挑战性的任务。具备推理能力的模型是必要的，但仅靠推理能力不足以匹敌人类在社会互动方面的直觉判断。

Abstract: Anticipating whether a person will interact from one's own perspective is a highly intuitive task for humans, that relies on a combination of cues. We investigate how humans perform at predicting a person's intention to interact from a service robot's point of view, using pose-only or full video input, then benchmark different lightweight pose-based models and state-of-the-art vision-language models. We conducted our benchmark on the HUI360 dataset on a fixed pilot subset of 100 test tracks (25 positive, 75 negative). We found that with pose-only input, human annotators outperform lightweight trained pose models but not by large margins (+0.08 in F1-Score). But when given full egocentric video with a target bounding box, human annotators perform substantially better and largely outperform the Vision-Language Models (+0.2 in F1-Score). We also compared VLMs of different size and under different input conditions, and found that the best results do not correlate with model size. Our result confirms that predicting interactions is a challenging task for social robots and that reasoning-capable models are necessary but their actual reasoning capabilities alone do not suffice to match the social intuition of humans.

</details>


### [175] [Federated Binary Gating with Server-Side Vision-Language Inference for Surveillance Anomaly Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07403)
*Côme-Alexis Puech,Sébastien Thuau,Amira Gran,Arthur Mennessier,Siba Haidar,Rachid Chelouah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合两阶段架构，将联邦二元卷积神经网络网关与服务器端零样本VLM结合，用于隐私敏感监控系统中的异常分类。轻量级LiteCNN3D在本地进行异常筛选，仅将标记视频转发给Qwen3-VL-8B进行四类异常分类，在UCF-Crime数据集上评估，实现了集中式系统相当的F1分数同时将传输视频比例降至51.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私敏感的监控系统需要大型视觉语言模型的能力，但VLM通常需要集中访问原始视频。在联邦学习环境中，非独立同分布客户端数据使得直接的多类异常分类不稳定（尤其对稀有类别），因此需要一种能够在联邦场景下兼顾隐私和分类性能的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种混合两阶段架构：(1) 联邦二元CNN网关（LiteCNN3D）在客户端本地进行异常筛选，仅转发标记为异常的短视频；(2) 服务器端使用Qwen3-VL-8B零样本VLM将筛选出的视频分为四类异常元类。该方法将联邦多类分类问题分解为联邦二元筛选+中心化VLM分类的组合。

Result: 直接联邦多类训练在此场景下崩溃，而所提出的分解方法获得了更好的质量-传输权衡。固定阈值路由下，混合流水线几乎保持与集中式CNN+VLM相同的F1-macro，传输视频比例降至51.4%；灵敏度导向路由将macro ROC AUC从0.673提高到0.692，假阴性率从29.3%降至22.9%，但F1-macro从0.503降至0.485且传输率升至57.9%。

Conclusion: 联邦学习更适合粗粒度的本地筛选任务，而路由规则可灵活调整以在服务器端VLM使用量与异常检测灵敏度之间进行权衡。这为隐私敏感监控场景提供了务实的架构指导。

Abstract: Privacy-sensitive surveillance systems could benefit from large vision-language models (VLMs), but such models typically require centralized access to raw video. In federated learning settings, this challenge is amplified by non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) client data, which can make direct multiclass anomaly classification unstable, especially for rare categories. We propose a hybrid two-stage architecture that combines a federated binary convolutional neural network (CNN) gate with server-side zero-shot VLM inference. The lightweight LiteCNN3D gate performs local anomaly screening and forwards only flagged videos to Qwen3-VL-8B, which assigns them to four anomaly metaclasses. We evaluate this design on UCF-Crime grouped into five coarse metaclasses and implement the federated stage in a real three-node heterogeneous deployment.
  In the studied setting, direct federated multiclass training collapses, whereas the proposed decomposition yields a better trade-off between classification quality and raw-video transmission. With fixed-threshold routing, the federated hybrid pipeline preserves nearly the same macro-averaged F1 score (F1-macro) as its centralized CNN+VLM counterpart while reducing the fraction of transmitted videos to 51.4%, although with a lower proxy macro receiver operating characteristic area under the curve (ROC AUC) than the centralized hybrid system. A complementary sensitivity-oriented routing operating point increases macro ROC AUC from 0.673 to 0.692 and reduces the false negative rate from 29.3% to 22.9%, but decreases F1-macro from 0.503 to 0.485 while increasing transmission from 51.4% to 57.9%. These results suggest that federation is better suited to coarse local screening, while routing rules can be adjusted to trade server-side VLM usage for higher anomaly sensitivity.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-label versus multi-class classification of blood cells and their aggregates in microfluidic channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07410)
*Igor Zingman,Shada Abuhattum,Sara Kaliman,Maximilian Schlögel,Paul Müller,Markéta Kubánková,Nadine Ströhlein,Manuela Hauke,Lena Schnörer,Martin Kräter,Jochen Guck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多标签（ML）分类替代传统的多类别（MC）分类来识别变形性细胞计数（DC）中的血细胞及其聚集体，ML分类器能同时为单个成像事件分配多个细胞类型标签，可识别训练数据中未出现的细胞聚集体，并简化标注过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多类别分类需要预先知道区分性特征，无法应用于临床上重要的细胞聚集体识别；而自动血液分析仪无法可靠地分析细胞聚集体，存在临床空白。

Method: 利用变形性细胞计数（DC）数据，对比传统多类别（MC）分类与多标签（ML）分类方法；ML分类器可为单个成像事件同时分配多个细胞类型标签。

Result: 与MC分类不同，ML分类能够识别训练数据中未出现的细胞聚集体，且无需详尽、严格定义的聚集体标签，简化并加速了标注过程。

Conclusion: ML分类方法有望弥补自动血液分析仪在细胞聚集体分析方面的不足，帮助解决这一临床问题。

Abstract: Deformability cytometry (DC) is a type of imaging flow cytometry, which uses a camera-equipped device to measure cellular stiffness in addition to other cellular properties at high throughput. Cellular properties such as area and elongation can identify cell types, but this requires prior knowledge of distinguishing properties and cannot be applied to clinically important cell aggregates. Using DC data, we evaluated conventional multi-class (MC) classification and introduced a multi-label (ML) approach for identifying blood cells and their aggregates. In particular, an ML classifier can simultaneously assign multiple cell-type labels to a single imaged event. We show that, unlike MC classification, ML classification can identify cell aggregates not represented in the training data. It also avoids the need for exhaustive, strictly defined aggregate labels, thereby simplifying and speeding up annotation. Since automated blood analyzers do not reliably analyze cell aggregates, our approach may help address this clinical gap.

</details>


### [177] [RelightFormer: Feed-forward Generative Transformer for Multiview Object Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07414)
*Hejun Wang,Jinxi Li,Junwei Jiang,Shiwei Mao,Hu Cheng,Shouwang Huang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前馈生成式Transformer的图像重光照方法，绕过了传统逆渲染管线中繁杂的内在属性估计。该方法从视频基础模型改编而来，通过潜在光照模块动态注入环境贴图，并采用置换不变的位置编码处理多视图输入，同时构建了包含90K物体和39K光照环境的大规模数据集LOD用于训练。实验在单视图、多视图和新视角重光照任务中均达到了领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像重光照依赖复杂的逆渲染管线，存在病态优化问题；而单图像生成模型忽略了多视角线索，无法充分理解3D几何和材质交互。现有方法在效率和泛化能力上均存在明显不足。

Method: 提出一种前馈生成式Transformer架构，从视频基础模型改编而来，包含三个关键设计：(1) 潜在光照模块，通过交叉注意力将目标环境贴图动态注入空间特征；(2) 置换不变的位置编码，以对称方式处理无序多视图输入；(3) 构建大规模Laval Objaverse数据集（90K物体、39K独特光照）用于训练。整个过程完全绕过显式内在属性（材质、法线等）估计。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在视觉质量和真实感重光照效果上达到最先进水平，并在单视图、多视图和新视角重光照任务中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的生成式方法可以替代传统逆渲染管线实现高效、鲁棒的重光照，通过大规模数据训练和精巧的架构设计，能够有效处理多种重光照场景并具备良好的跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Image relighting is traditionally tackled via complex inverse rendering pipelines, which suffer from ill-posed optimization, or single-image generative models that ignore crucial multi-view cues necessary for understanding 3D geometry and material interactions. To address these limitations, we introduce a feed-forward generative Transformer for direct single- and multi-view image relighting that entirely bypasses explicit intrinsic property estimation. Adapted from a video foundation model, our architecture features a latent illumination module that dynamically injects target environment maps into spatial features via cross-attention. Furthermore, we employ permutation-invariant positional encodings to symmetrically process unordered multi-view inputs without sequential bias. To train this robust data-driven model, we construct the massive Laval Objaverse Dataset (LOD), comprising 90K objects and 39K unique illuminations. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art visual quality, photorealistic relighting quality, and strong zero-shot generalization across single-view, multi-view, and novel-view relighting tasks.

</details>


### [178] [Self-Supervised Multi-View 3D Gaze Target Estimation via Probabilistic Ray Marching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07415)
*Keqi Chen,Vinkle Srivastav,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-MVGTE，一种自监督的多视角3D注视目标估计方法，首次直接从多视角图像预测3D空间中的注视目标，无需目标场景的任何标注，通过概率射线行进框架处理噪声伪标签，在MVGT数据集上超越现有全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多视角设置下是独立地对每个视角估计2D注视目标，缺乏直接进行3D注视目标预测的方法；同时这些方法依赖目标场景的真值标注，泛化能力受限。

Method: 1) 将单目3D凝视向量整合到单目注视目标估计模型中，生成更高质量的伪标签并提升对未见场景的泛化；2) 构建3D凝视锥，从眼球位置沿凝视向量发射一束光线以严格约束解空间；3) 在锥内使用DINOv2和Depth-Anything-3进行深度引导的特征采样，估计注视目标的空间似然分布；4) 将伪注视标签转化为目标分布并进行软优化训练网络。

Result: 在MVGT数据集上的大量实验表明，Self-MVGTE达到了最先进的性能，并超越了现有的全监督基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过自监督方式直接进行3D多视角注视目标估计是可行的，利用概率射线行进和几何先验可以有效处理多视角不一致的噪声伪标签，从而实现无需标注即可超越全监督方法的效果。

Abstract: We present a self-supervised approach, Self-MVGTE, for estimating 3D gaze targets from multiple camera views. Unlike existing methods that independently estimate 2D gaze targets per camera view, Self-MVGTE predicts gaze targets directly in 3D space for the first time. Moreover, it does not require any ground-truth annotations from the target scene and uses only the multi-view input images from a calibrated camera setup, pseudo 2D gaze target labels from a monocular gaze target estimation model, and 3D gaze vectors from a monocular 3D gaze estimation model. A key challenge is that these pseudo labels are inherently noisy and multi-view inconsistent. To address this, we propose a probabilistic ray marching framework, which models the uncertainty of these pseudo labels and exploits 3D gaze vectors as geometric priors. Specifically, these gaze vectors are first integrated into the monocular gaze target estimation model to improve its generalization to unseen scenes, producing higher-quality pseudo labels. Then, for 3D gaze target estimation, we construct a 3D gaze cone by casting a bundle of rays from the eye position around the gaze vector to strictly constrain the solution space. Within this cone, we propose a depth-guided feature sampling strategy using off-the-shelf DINOv2 and Depth-Anything-3 models, and estimate a spatial likelihood distribution of the gaze target. Finally, we convert the pseudo gaze target labels into a target distribution and softly optimize the network. Extensive experiments on the MVGT dataset show that Self-MVGTE achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing existing fully-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Unified Vision-Centric Pedestrian Crossing Action Prediction via Adaptive Patch Projection and Proactive Spatial Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07420)
*Yao Tian,Le Yang,Binglu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViCross，一个以视觉为中心的基于多模态大语言模型的行人过街行为预测框架，无需额外感知模块（仅需首帧目标初始化），通过可变分辨率补丁映射模块和空间约束增强策略解决高分辨率输入计算开销大及时空先验缺失两大挑战，在视觉中心预测设置下表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉线索对行人动作预测有效且信息丰富，但无需帧级外部感知线索的情况下，难以从视频帧中获取稳定的以目标为中心的表示。现有方法多依赖额外感知模块或多源信息融合，视觉中心设置（仅靠视觉）的可靠性仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出ViCross框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 可变分辨率补丁映射模块（Variable Resolution Patch Mapping），用于高效分配视觉token，在保持关键行人细节的同时降低全帧编码的计算开销；(2) 空间约束增强策略（Spatial Constraint Enhancement Strategy），利用历史运动、未来位置和动作语义信息，在训练阶段实现主动的空间校正，弥补缺失的时空先验以实现跨帧一致性推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，ViCross在视觉中心预测设置下取得显著提升，并在多种设置下与多源融合方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ViCross验证了在仅依赖视觉线索（除首帧目标初始化外无额外感知模块）的情况下，利用多模态大语言模型实现可靠的行人过街行为预测是可行的，为视觉中心设置提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Vision cues are available and informative for pedestrian action prediction, but obtaining stable target-centric representations from video frames remains challenging without frame-level external perception cues. Thus, most methods rely on additional perception modules or multi-source information fusion, leaving the reliability of vision-centric setting an open question. To this end, we propose ViCross, a vision-centric pedestrian crossing action prediction framework powered by multimodal large language models, which maintains target-centric reasoning from video frames without additional perception modules beyond first-frame target initialization. While multimodal large language models exhibit strong visual understanding, applying them directly to vision-centric action prediction faces two challenges. First, accurately perceiving target pedestrians often requires high resolution inputs and dense visual tokenization, making full-frame encoding computationally prohibitive. ViCross tackles this with Variable Resolution Patch Mapping module for efficient token allocation while preserving key pedestrian details. Second, missing spatiotemporal priors hinder consistent cross frame reasoning. ViCross mitigates this with a Spatial Constraint Enhancement Strategy that captures past motion, future locations, and action semantics for training-time proactive spatial rectification. Extensive experiments show that ViCross delivers clear gains in vision-centric prediction settings and is competitive with multi-source fusion approaches in several settings. Code is available at https://github.com/2tianyao1/ViCross.git.

</details>


### [180] [AFID: A Unified Open Framework for Automated Fingermark Identification, Quality Assessment and Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07439)
*Tim Oblak,Rudolf Haraksim,Peter Peer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AFID，一个统一的开源自动指纹识别框架，基于单一共享编码器完成识别、质量评估和特征提取三大任务，仅使用公开数据训练，在多个NIST基准上刷新了固定长度指纹识别的最先进水平，并超越商业匹配器，同时开源代码、模型与标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动指纹识别是法医调查的基础，但该领域因碎片化、闭源的解决方案以及依赖私有或已停用数据而进展缓慢，迫切需要统一、开源且基于公开数据的框架。

Method: AFID采用一个共享的编码器，学习用于身份区分的固定长度表征，并在重增强条件下训练。推理时几乎不做预处理（仅缩放和填充），从同一冻结骨干网络分别派生质量评估模块和轻量级解码器，用于恢复细节点、脊线方向和分割。

Result: 在NIST SD 27、SD 302和SD 303上分别达到67.6%、54.9%和67.6%的rank-1识别率，超越商业匹配器；质量评估模块在预测识别效用方面优于所有对比基线且可泛化至独立匹配器；特征提取性能与专门方法相当。

Conclusion: 单一且高效训练的编码器足以支撑完整的指纹处理流程（识别、质量评估到特征提取），并且作者开源代码、模型和标注以加速该领域研究。

Abstract: Automated fingermark identification is the foundation of forensic investigation, yet progress in the field is held back by fragmented, closed-source solutions trained on private or discontinued data. We present AFID, a unified open-source framework for friction ridge image processing that performs recognition, quality assessment, and feature extraction based on a single shared encoder, trained exclusively on publicly available data. At its core is a fixed-length representation learned for identity discrimination, trained under heavy augmentation. Despite applying essentially no preprocessing beyond resizing and padding at inference, AFID sets a new state of the art in fixed-length fingermark recognition, leading identification across NIST SD 27 (67.6% rank-1) , SD 302 (54.9% rank-1), and SD 303 (67.6% rank-1), surpassing a commercial matcher on fingermarks. From the same frozen backbone, a quality assessment module predicts recognition utility more accurately than any compared baseline and generalizes across independent matchers, while lightweight decoders recover minutiae, ridge orientation, and segmentation competitive with dedicated methods. The framework proves that a single, efficiently trained encoder can support the full fingermark processing pipeline, from recognition through quality assessment all the way to feature extraction. To accelerate research on fingermark analysis even further, we release the code, models, and annotations to the community.

</details>


### [181] [Parser-Free VLM Verification for Federated Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07455)
*Sébastien Thuau,Amira Gran,Siba Haidar,Rachid Chelouah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级联邦MIL-VLM级联框架用于视频异常检测（VAD），其中仅在客户端训练紧凑的MIL评分器，而冻结的VLM用于事后验证高评分可疑片段。对比了两种VLM反馈接口：文本生成决策和基于logit的连续异常评分。实验表明logit接口在无需时间后处理的情况下，能持续提升帧级AUC和AP，而文本生成接口虽在时间后处理后有效但对提示、解析器和模型选择敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督VAD方法大多假设集中式训练，近期基于VLM的扩展依赖密集推理、生成解释或额外适配，难以应对监控数据分布化、弱标注和资源受限的实际场景。本文旨在探索在联邦学习框架下如何有效利用VLM提升VAD性能。

Method: 提出联邦MIL-VLM级联架构：客户端仅训练轻量MIL评分器，冻结的VLM事后验证高评分可疑片段。研究了两种VLM反馈接口——基于解析文本生成决策的接口，以及从next-token Yes/No概率提取连续异常分数的logit接口。

Result: 在UCF-Crime数据集上使用InternVL3.5-2B和Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct实验表明：文本生成验证在诊断性时间后处理后能提升帧级AUC，但对提示、解析器、模型选择和平滑敏感；logit接口提供固定的无解析器信号，在两种VLM上均能提升帧级AUC和帧级AP，且主配置无需时间后处理。

Conclusion: next-token logit反馈作为简单的片段局部替代方案，比文本生成验证更稳健和实用，适合联邦且资源受限的VAD场景。

Abstract: How can vision-language models help video anomaly detection (VAD) when surveillance data remain distributed, weakly labeled, and resource-constrained? Most weakly supervised VAD methods assume centralized training; recent VLM-based extensions further rely on dense inference, generated explanations, or additional adaptation. We introduce a lightweight federated MIL-VLM cascade in which only a compact MIL scorer is trained across clients, while a frozen VLM verifies high-scoring suspect segments post hoc. We study two VLM feedback interfaces: parsed text-generation decisions and a logit-based interface that extracts a continuous anomaly score from next-token Yes/No probabilities. Experiments on UCF-Crime with InternVL3.5-2B and Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct show that text-generation verification can improve frame-level AUC after diagnostic temporal post-processing, but remains sensitive to prompts, parsers, model choice, and smoothing. In contrast, the logit interface provides a fixed parser-free signal that improves both frame-level AUC and frame-level AP over the MIL baseline across both VLMs, without temporal post-processing in its main configuration. Since suspect segments are updated independently once available, next-token logit feedback provides a simple segment-local alternative to text-generation verification.

</details>


### [182] [Latent-to-Latent Flow for Volumetric Stochastic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07460)
*Omar Todd,Sooha Kim,Raghav Mehta,Katherine Mackay,David Bernstein,Alexandra Taylor,Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在空间的流匹配（latent-to-latent flow）技术，用于医学体积图像中的随机分割，通过同时对图像和标签空间进行编码表示，显著提升了计算效率（最高14倍）同时保持临床相关性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割中的观察者间变异带来的不确定性对制定治疗方案至关重要，但大规模医学数据集中缺乏多标注数据，尤其是体积数据还面临额外的缩放和计算复杂度挑战，阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 引入一种潜在到潜在（latent-to-latent）的流匹配技术，通过对图像和标签空间进行编码表示，实现对医学体积分割的随机（stochastic）建模，以生成式方式捕获分割不确定性。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的应用（放射治疗计划的勾画不确定性和多器官结构分割）上进行了评估，与全分辨率模型相比实现了最高14倍的效率提升，同时保持了临床相关的性能水平。

Conclusion: 潜在空间的流匹配方法能够有效解决大规模医学体积分割中因缺乏多标注而面临的不确定性建模难题，在显著提升计算效率的同时保证临床可用性。

Abstract: Uncertainty arising from inter-observer variability in medical image segmentation plays an important role in developing treatment plans. Research in this area is inhibited by the lack of multiple annotations for large-scale medical datasets, especially for volumetric data, which suffers from additional scaling and computational complexity challenges. Flow matching has emerged as a powerful framework for generative modelling and has also been demonstrated to maintain strong performance when working with latent representations of images. In this work, we introduce a latent-to-latent flow technique for stochastic segmentation of medical volumes via encoded representations of both the image and label space. We evaluate our method on two challenging applications covering delineation uncertainty for radiotherapy planning and multiple organ structure segmentation, improving efficiency up to 14x compared with full resolution models while maintaining clinically relevant performance.

</details>


### [183] [When Superpixels Fail on Documents: A Study of Segmentation for LIME Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07462)
*Quentin Telnoff,Emanuela Boros,Mickaël Coustaty,Robin Jarry,Fabrice Crohas,Antoine Doucet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了LIME在文档图像分类中的分割步骤对解释可靠性影响，发现文档感知的分割方法（基于OCR边界框和规则网格）比自然图像超像素（Quickshift/SLIC）能产生更稳定、忠实且一致的解释，并暴露了RVL-CDIP数据集中的ID码捷径偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后解释方法（如LIME）的可靠性高度依赖设计选择，其中分割步骤常被视为实现细节而忽略。在文档图像分类中，标准自然图像超像素与文档结构（文本区域、布局块、识别码）对齐不佳，可能导致不可靠的解释。

Method: 在RVL-CDIP数据集上，对比Quickshift和SLIC两种标准分割方法与基于OCR边界框和规则网格的文档感知分割方法，评估LIME解释的一致性、正确性和局部忠实度。

Result: 分割方式强烈影响解释的一致性、正确性和局部忠实度。文档感知分割产生更稳定和忠实的解释，所需扰动次数更少即可收敛，并能暴露RVL-CDIP中基于文档识别码的捷径行为，而基于超像素的LIME常掩盖此偏差。

Conclusion: 可靠的事后解释需要领域感知的可解释表示，分割应被视为解释方法的一部分而非中性预处理步骤。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanation methods are widely used to inspect image classifiers, but their reliability depends on design choices that are often treated as implementation details. We study this issue for LIME on document image classification, focusing on the segmentation step that defines the interpretable units being perturbed. Standard image-based LIME typically relies on natural-image superpixels, which are poorly aligned with document structure such as text regions, layout blocks, and identification codes. Using RVL-CDIP, we compare Quickshift and SLIC with document-aware segmentations based on OCR bounding boxes and regular grids. Our results show that segmentation strongly affects explanation consistency, correctness, and local fidelity. Document-aware segmentations produce more stable and faithful explanations, require fewer perturbations to converge, and expose shortcut behaviour based on document identification codes, a known RVL-CDIP bias that superpixel-based LIME often obscures. These findings show that reliable post-hoc explanation requires domain-aware interpretable representations, and that segmentation should be treated as part of the explanation method rather than as neutral preprocessing.

</details>


### [184] [CrACK: Adversarial Attacks on Cross-Model Consistency in Collaborative Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07499)
*Feifei Liu,Jintao Cheng,Chi Man Vong,Xiaoyu Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现基于视觉基础模型（如CLIP、SAM、DINO）的协作式无训练管线存在一个共同的安全漏洞——模型间接口缺乏语义一致性验证，并提出了CrACK攻击方法来利用该漏洞，无需修改任何输入像素、模型权重或训练数据即可造成灾难性性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全假设认为协作管线的鲁棒性等同于其单个模型的鲁棒性之和，但作者识别出协作管线中每个模型都消费其他模型的中间输出而不验证语义一致性——即语义-空间对齐依赖这一未经验证的前提，而现有对抗攻击只针对单一模型，完全忽视了模型间接口这一安全盲区。

Method: 提出CrACK（跨模型对抗一致性攻击），一种推理时攻击方法，分两个阶段：(1) 对抗亲和矛盾注入——通过反转SAM编码器特征并在CLIP补丁级语义引导下破坏跨模态亲和矩阵；(2) 语义接口投毒——利用CLIP文本嵌入导出的最大距离标签排列来操纵预测。该方法不修改任何像素、权重或训练数据。

Result: 在四个协作管线和八个基准上的实验表明，CrACK造成灾难性的性能退化，且每个独立模型的输出保持完全不变，使得针对单模型的防御在结构上失明；攻击还可级联传播到大视觉语言模型推理中，使LLaVA等模型在视觉输入未受损的情况下产生错误响应。

Conclusion: 协作式AI系统的安全性不能简化为其组件的鲁棒性之和，模型间特征接口必须被视为第一类安全边界加以防护。

Abstract: Training-free collaborative pipelines that integrate Vision Foundation Models such as CLIP, SAM, and DINO achieve strong open-vocabulary dense prediction and are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications. The security of these systems is commonly assumed to follow from the robustness of their individual models. We challenge this assumption. We identify a vulnerability shared by every collaborative pipeline: each model consumes the intermediate output of another without verifying semantic consistency, an unverified premise that we term the semantic-spatial alignment dependency. Existing adversarial attacks target a single model and overlook this premise, leaving the inter-model interface entirely unguarded. We propose CrACK (Cross-model Adversarial Consistency attack), an inference-time attack that exploits this interface without modifying any input pixel, model weight, or training data. CrACK operates in two stages: Adversarial Affinity Contradiction Injection corrupts the cross-modal affinity matrix by inverting SAM encoder features under the guidance of CLIP patch-level semantics, and Semantic Interface Poisoning steers the prediction through a max-distance label permutation derived from CLIP text embeddings. Experiments on four collaborative pipelines across eight benchmarks show that CrACK causes catastrophic degradation while every individual model continues to produce its unchanged standalone output, rendering per-model defenses structurally blind. The corruption further cascades into large vision-language model reasoning, driving models such as LLaVA to produce erroneous responses from visually intact inputs. Our results show that the security of a collaborative AI system cannot be reduced to the robustness of its components, and that inter-model feature interfaces must be treated as first-class security boundaries.

</details>


### [185] [Mitigating Shortcut Learning: Texture-Penalized Prototype Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07504)
*Akshay Anilkumar Girija,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出纹理惩罚原型网络（TPPN），通过惩罚局部纹理提取、在原型超球面上施加几何约束，使CNN从纹理偏置转向形状偏置，将ResNet-50的纹理偏置从55.11%降至29.73%，超越ViT-B/16，且准确率仅下降0.90个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CNN具有强烈的纹理归纳偏置，优先利用局部高频模式而非全局结构形状进行识别，导致在纹理变化或环境干扰下产生自信的错误分类。现有方法依赖资源密集的数据增强，缺乏结构性的解决方案。

Method: TPPN包含两个核心模块：1) 纹理惩罚分支（TPB）通过施加惩罚抑制局部纹理代理的提取，迫使骨干网络丢弃高频线索；2) 基于最终卷积特征的原型超球面相似性评估，施加严格的几何约束，将对象视为关键部件的组合以实现鲁棒分类。

Result: 在纹理-形状线索冲突数据集和合成噪声基准上，TPPN将ResNet-50的纹理偏置从55.11%降至29.73%，超过ViT-B/16的纹理抑制能力；在OOD形状和扰动条件下保持强形状准确率，干净验证数据上仅下降0.90个百分点。

Conclusion: TPPN提供了一种结构性、高效且不依赖增强数据集的CNN纹理偏置解决方案，通过形状-纹理解耦实现更强的形状偏置与稳健泛化。

Abstract: Standard Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) exhibit severe performance degradation due to a strong inductive texture bias that prioritizes local, high-frequency patterns over global structural shapes. This dependency causes confident misclassifications during textural changes or environmental effects. To address this flaw, this study introduces the Texture-Penalized Prototype Network (TPPN), a novel architectural framework that shifts this inherent bias without depending on resource-intensive augmented datasets. Specifically, a Texture-Penalization Branch (TPB) imposes a penalty to suppress the extraction of local texture proxies, forcing the network backbone to discard high-frequency cues and extract purified, shape-biased representations. By evaluating similarities within a prototype-based hypersphere derived from the final convolutional features, the approach enforces strict geometric constraints, treating objects as compositions of essential parts to achieve robust classification. Evaluations on texture-shape cue-conflict datasets and synthetic noise benchmarks demonstrate the stronger shape bias of this structural disentanglement. The proposed framework reduces the inherent texture bias of a baseline ResNet-50 from 55.11% to 29.73%, surpassing the texture-suppression capabilities of an off-the-shelf Vision Transformer (ViT-B/16). Furthermore, the approach demonstrates robust generalization under cue-conflict conditions, resisting textural shortcut learning when encountering Out-of-Distribution (OOD) shapes. The model maintains stronger shape accuracy against elevated perturbations. On clean validation data, the architecture incurs a minimal drop in accuracy of 0.90 percentage points. This provides a structural, efficient solution to CNN texture bias.

</details>


### [186] [PICANet: Physics-Informed Cascaded Asymmetric Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07515)
*Jingjing Liu,Yinchao Han,Xianchao Xiu,Jianhua Zhang,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种即插即用的物理信息级联非对称网络PICANet用于红外小目标检测，通过层次先验解耦模块提取不同层次的物理信息、双先验交互融合模块动态细化目标表征并抑制背景杂波，以及多层级交叉特征注意力模块实现高层语义与低层空间细节的精确对齐，实验表明其在复杂背景下优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法受到严重背景噪声传播和高层语义特征中目标劣化的限制，作者希望解决这些问题以提升检测精度。

Method: 提出PICANet网络，包含三个核心组件：(1)层次先验解耦模块，显式提取低层和高层物理信息以表征不同层级的目标特征；(2)双先验交互融合模块，动态细化目标表征并抑制复杂背景杂波；(3)基于级联非对称机制的多层级交叉特征注意模块，实现高层语义与低层空间细节的精确对齐。整体网络为即插即用设计。

Result: 大量实验表明PICANet在红外小目标检测任务上优于现有最先进方法，即使在复杂背景下也展现出令人满意的检测精度。

Conclusion: PICANet通过引入物理信息先验和级联非对称注意力机制，有效缓解了背景噪声传播与目标特征劣化问题，为红外小目标检测提供了一种新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (ISTD) is an important research direction in image processing. However, existing methods are limited by severe background noise propagation and target degradation in high-level semantic features. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a plug-and-play physics-informed cascaded asymmetric network, named PICANet. Specifically, we construct a hierarchical prior decoupling module to explicitly extract low-level and high-level physical information, thereby characterizing target features at different levels rather than relying solely on convolutional extraction. Furthermore, a dual-prior interactive fusion module is developed to dynamically refine target representations while suppressing complex background clutter. Unlike previous work, a multi-level cross-feature attention module with the cascaded asymmetric mechanism is introduced to achieve precise alignment between high-level semantics and low-level spatial details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed PICANet outperforms state-of-the-art ISTD methods, showing satisfactory detection accuracy even against complex backgrounds. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/PICANet.

</details>


### [187] [Topologically Consistent Agricultural Parcel Vectorization with Semantic-Guided Diffusion and Topology-Aware Polygonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07520)
*Weiqin Jiao,Xiaolong Zuo,Claudio Persello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义引导的扩散框架，用于拓扑一致的农业地块矢量化。该框架将联合边-顶点潜扩散与多线索监督条件结合，生成几何规整的地块边界和顶点基元，并通过基于平面图的地块面重建方法，使相邻地块复用共享边界、避免内部侵入。实验在AI4SmallFarms和iFLYTEK数据集上取得竞争性表现，实现了零侵入率和最高的共享边召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业地块矢量化方法存在明显不足：分割方法依赖启发式栅格到矢量转换；实例和轮廓方法独立重建各地块；近期矢量导向方法虽改善了多边形规整性，但未显式地从共享拓扑结构中恢复相邻地块。实际地块图需要避免拓扑冲突并保留相邻地块之间的公共边界，这一需求尚未被解决。

Method: 提出语义引导的扩散框架，包括两部分：(1) 联合边-顶点潜扩散模型，结合监督多线索条件，生成几何规整化的地块边界和顶点基元，同时抑制误检响应；(2) 拓扑感知的地块多边形重建方法，通过从公共平面图重建地块面，将基元转换为规整多边形，使相邻预测地块复用共享边界并避免内部互相侵入。

Result: 在AI4SmallFarms和iFLYTEK数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在像素级覆盖、几何保真度、对象级正确性和拓扑一致性等方面表现强劲并具有竞争力，实现了零侵入比率和最高的共享边召回率。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在准确、规整和拓扑一致的农业地块矢量化方面展现出潜力，有效解决了现有方法无法显式恢复相邻地块共享拓扑结构的问题。

Abstract: Agricultural parcel polygons play a fundamental role in geospatial applications such as precision agriculture, land administration, and crop monitoring. Beyond regular polygon geometry and low vertex redundancy, practical parcel maps should avoid topological conflicts and preserve common boundaries between adjacent fields. Yet this requirement remains largely unresolved: segmentation-based methods mainly produce parcel masks or raster boundary cues and rely on heuristic raster-to-vector conversion, instance- and contour-based methods reconstruct parcels independently, and recent vector-oriented methods improve polygon regularity but do not explicitly recover adjacent parcels from a shared topological structure. To address this gap, we propose a semantic-guided diffusion framework for topologically consistent agricultural parcel vectorization. It couples joint edge--vertex latent diffusion with supervised multi-cue conditioning to generate geometrically regularised parcel-boundary and vertex primitives while suppressing false-positive responses. A topology-aware parcel polygon reconstruction method then converts these primitives into regular polygons by reconstructing parcel faces from a common planar graph, enabling adjacent predicted parcels to reuse shared boundaries and avoid mutual interior intrusion. Extensive experiments on the AI4SmallFarms and iFLYTEK datasets evaluate parcel vectorization in terms of pixel-level coverage, geometric fidelity, object-level correctness, and topological consistency. The results show strong and competitive performance, with zero measured intrusion ratio and the highest shared-edge recall, demonstrating the potential of the proposed framework for accurate, regular, and topologically consistent agricultural parcel vectorization.

</details>


### [188] [When Semantically Consistent Encoding Meets View-Label Heterogeneity Modeling: A Unified Framework for Incomplete Multi-View Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07525)
*Chengliang Liu,Bo Li,Bob Zhang,Yanghao Zhou,Jie Wen,Wenwu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V2L，一个统一的表示-决策框架，用于不完整多视图多标签分类。V2L通过扰动感知编码机制构建语义一致的变分后验，实现共享语义基础；同时引入主动视图-标签相关性建模策略，为每个标签自适应选择有用的视图特定证据。两种策略通过混合融合架构集成，在五个基准数据集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不完整多视图多标签学习方法存在两大局限：共享表示学习方法虽能提高对缺失视图的鲁棒性，但会压缩标签判别性的视图特有信息；决策级融合方法虽保留了各视图预测，但依赖固定或全局学习的融合权重，忽略了不同实例的不同标签可能需要不同视图的信息。

Method: V2L框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 表示侧：通过扰动感知编码机制从不完整视图中构建语义一致的变分后验，提供稳定的共享语义基础；(2) 决策侧：引入主动视图-标签相关性建模策略，估计实例级和标签级的视图贡献，使每个标签预测能自适应选择有用的视图特定证据；(3) 架构侧：通过混合融合架构将上述两种策略集成到统一框架中，同时满足跨视图语义一致性和表示互补性的需求。

Result: 在完整和不完整两种设置下，V2L在五个基准数据集上均取得了领先性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: V2L通过将共享语义表示学习与标签自适应的视图融合有机结合，有效解决了不完整多视图多标签学习中表示压缩与固定融合权重的问题，验证了表示学习与决策融合协同工作的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Incomplete multi-view multi-label learning requires not only robust semantic aggregation from partially observed views, but also label-aware exploitation of view-specific evidence. Existing approaches usually emphasize either shared representation learning or decision-level fusion. The former improves robustness against missing views, yet tends to compress label-discriminative view-specific cues into a single latent representation. The latter preserves individual view predictions, but often relies on fixed or globally learned fusion weights, ignoring that different labels of different instances may require different views. To address these limitations, this paper presents V2L, a unified representation-decision framework for incomplete multi-view multi-label classification. On the representation side, V2L constructs semantically consistent variational posteriors from incomplete views through a perturbation-aware encoding mechanism, which provides a stable shared semantic basis. On the decision side, V2L introduces an active view-label relevance modeling strategy that estimates instance-wise and label-wise view contributions, allowing each label prediction to adaptively select useful view-specific evidence. From the perspective of model architecture, these two important strategies are integrated into a unified framework through a hybrid fusion architecture, simultaneously meeting the requirements of cross-view semantic consistency and representational complementarity. Extensive experiments under both incomplete and complete settings show that V2L achieves leading performance on five benchmarks. Code is available at: https://github.com/justsmart/V2L.

</details>


### [189] [TeMo: Temperature Modulation for Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07540)
*Dhimitrios Duka,Bernt Schiele,Hilde Kuehne,Anna Kukleva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TeMo（温度调制框架），通过根据正负样本对的相似度自适应调整温度超参数τ，实现更细粒度的多模态对比学习，在零样本检索和分类任务上取得了新的最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习中的温度超参数τ控制对负样本的惩罚强度，但现有方法要么固定该超参数，要么在训练中学习一个全局值，无法针对不同样本对进行细粒度调节，限制了多模态对比学习的表达能力。

Method: 提出TeMo框架，核心思想是基于相似度自适应地为每个正负样本对调节温度。具体包括：(1) 相似度驱动的温度调制机制；(2) 将温度调制的多模态和单模态损失与标准多模态对比损失无缝集成；(3) 通过训练阶段逐步过渡，使模型在不同阶段捕获粗粒度和细粒度语义。

Result: 大量实验表明TeMo的每个组件都能在多样化的零样本检索和分类任务中持续提升性能，并建立了新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: TeMo通过细粒度的样本对级别温度调制，有效提升了多模态对比学习的性能，验证了自适应温度策略相比固定或全局温度的优势。

Abstract: Contrastive learning approaches achieve strong performance by training models to bring similar samples closer while pushing dissimilar samples apart. A crucial component of contrastive learning is the temperature hyperparameter $τ$, which controls the penalty strength applied to negative samples. However, most existing methods either fix this hyperparameter or learn a global value during training. In this paper, we introduce TeMo, Temperature Modulation framework, a similarity-based modulation approach that adaptively adjusts the temperature for each positive-negative pair according to their similarity, enabling more fine-grained multimodal contrastive learning. Our approach seamlessly integrates temperature-modulated multimodal and unimodal losses with the standard multimodal contrastive loss by gradually transitioning between them. This design allows the model to capture both coarse- and fine-grained semantics at different training stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that each component of TeMo consistently enhances performance across diverse zero-shot retrieval and classification tasks, establishing new state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [190] [Re-engineering SORT-based algorithms for low-cost small object tracking from omnidirectional footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07547)
*Xin Shu,Meegan Gower,Yvonne Buckley,Anil Kokaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OmniSORT，一个针对全景相机下飞行动物多目标跟踪的轻量级框架，通过接缝感知运动模型与关联代价解决了 equirectangular 投影中的不连续性问题，并发布了新的OmniSmall基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOT跟踪器依赖ReID和transformer外观编码器，且针对标准视场角相机设计，无法适用于低成本全景部署场景。在全景影像中，equirectangular投影产生接缝不连续性，且目标小、运动快，导致传统跟踪方法失效。

Method: 该框架在几何层面重新设计了SORT跟踪：1) 接缝感知运动模型(Seam-Aware Motion Model)保持Kalman状态跨接缝连续；2) 复合接缝感知关联代价，结合包裹欧氏距离与GIoU；3) 构建了OmniSmall基准数据集。全程不需要外观编码器，跟踪阶段仅用CPU。

Result: 在OmniSmall新数据集上，使用地面真值检测时相对OCSORT提升+8.51 HOTA、+9.41 MOTA和+10.17 IDF1；使用YOLOX检测器时HOTA提升+1.95。在JRDB上保持竞争力，同时不引入外观编码器并保持CPU-only运行。

Conclusion: 提出的OmniSORT方法有效解决了全景相机下飞行动物的多目标跟踪问题，在OmniSmall上显著优于基线，并在其他基准上保持竞争力，证明了轻量级、无外观编码器的CPU-only方案在全景跟踪场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) has advanced rapidly in urban surveillance and autonomous driving, yet many trackers rely on ReID- and transformer-based appearance encoders and are designed for standard FoV cameras. These assumptions break down for low-cost omnidirectional deployments, where equirectangular projection introduces seam discontinuities and targets appear to be small and fast-moving. We address multi-object tracking of flying animals captured in remote environments using omnidirectional cameras. We propose a lightweight framework that re-engineers SORT-based tracking for this geometry, including (i) a Seam-Aware Motion Model that keeps the Kalman state continuous across the seam, (ii) a composite seam-aware association cost that pairs a wrapped Euclidean term with GIoU, and (iii) OmniSmall, a new benchmark of omnidirectional wildlife footage. On our new dataset, with ground-truth detections, our modifications improved over OCSORT by +8.51 HOTA, +9.41 MOTA, and +10.17 IDF1; with YOLOX detections the gain narrows to +1.95 HOTA. Our proposed methods improved tracking performance on OmniSmall and remained competitive on JRDB without adding appearance encoders while keeping the tracking stage CPU-only. Our dataset and source code are available at: https://github.com/Xin-Shu/OmniSORT.git.

</details>


### [191] [Heat Kernel Textures: the Geodesic Gaussians That Do Not Splat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07557)
*Simone Foti,Caner Korkmaz,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出热核纹理（HKTex），一种基于离散黎曼几何的3D纹理表示方法，在三角网格曲面上直接使用各向异性热核作为高斯函数的测地线等价物，消除了UV映射的展开、接缝、扭曲等问题，并大幅降低内存占用，可与基于物理的渲染器集成，从现有纹理或多视图图像优化得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UV映射存在浪费UV空间、接缝、畸变、顶点重复和分辨率不一致等固有问题。受3D高斯泼溅表示启发，作者希望重新思考纹理表示，以克服UV映射的主要缺陷并显著降低内存占用。

Method: 基于离散黎曼几何，在任意三角网格曲面内部定义各向异性热核作为高斯核的测地线等价物；核的位置优化和自适应稠密化策略均在物体表面而非参数空间中执行；该表示与基于物理的渲染器完全集成，可从现有纹理或多视图图像进行优化。

Result: 该方法实现了无UV展开的纹理化过程，消除了UV空间浪费、接缝和畸变问题，同时显著降低了内存占用，并支持从多视图图像直接优化纹理。

Conclusion: HKTex提供了一种内在定义于网格曲面上的新型纹理表示，是UV映射的有效替代方案，具有更低内存开销和无缝、无畸变的优势。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently revolutionised novel view synthesis as well as many other 3D vision methods and applications. Drawing inspiration from this representation, we now rethink textures to overcome the main issues of UV mapping while considerably lowering their memory footprint. Heat Kernel Textures (HKTex) eliminate UV unwrapping as well as their persistent issues of wasted UV space, seams, distortions, vertex-duplication, and varying resolution. Grounded in discrete Riemannian geometry and intrinsically defined on any manifold surface discretised as a triangular mesh, HKTex uses anisotropic heat kernels as geodesic equivalents to Gaussians. Like our kernels, also the optimisation of their position and the adaptive densification strategies were redefined to operate on the surface of the object to be textureised. Our novel representation is also fully integrated with a physically based renderer and can be optimised either from existing textures or multi-view images. Our project page and code are available at circle-group.github.io/research/HeatKernelTextures.

</details>


### [192] [SphereSOD: Geometry-Structure Coupled Learning for 360 Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07571)
*Junsong Zhang,Zhijie Shen,Shuai Zheng,Feng Li,Runmin Cong,Yao Zhao,Chunyu Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SphereSOD，一种基于等距柱状投影（ERP）的全景显著性目标检测框架，通过将全景几何与显著性目标结构相耦合，在特征采样和上下文聚合中双向交互，在三个公开基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有360°显著性目标检测方法主要补偿ERP投影带来的空间畸变，却忽视了全景几何与显著性目标结构在特征感知和预测细化过程中的交互作用，导致检测精度受限。

Method: SphereSOD包含两个核心设计：(1) 基于球面投影几何初始化可变形采样，并使用有界、内容自适应的偏移量，使特征与全景几何更好对齐；(2) 解码器采用结构引导的上下文聚合和渐进细化，其中粗粒度显著性预测和轮廓预测影响上下文聚合，以恢复完整显著区域和精确边界。

Result: 在三个公开的360°显著性目标检测基准上达到最先进性能，同时展现良好的精度-效率权衡，验证了直接在ERP空间进行结构保持推理的可行性。

Conclusion: 直接在ERP空间推理而不依赖投影转换的轻量方案，可以作为投影型全景处理管线的一个有前景的替代路径。

Abstract: 360° salient object detection (SOD) aims to accurately segment salient regions across a full field of view. However, equirectangular projection (ERP) introduces severe spatial distortion when mapping the spherical domain onto a planar representation. Existing methods mainly focus on compensating projection distortion while overlooking the interaction between panoramic geometry and salient object structure during feature perception and prediction refinement. To this end, we propose SphereSOD, an ERP-native framework that couples panoramic geometry with evolving salient structures. Specifically, spherical geometry governs feature sampling and spatial weighting, while coarse-grained saliency and contour prediction influence context aggregation during the progressive decoding process. SphereSOD first initializes deformable sampling based on spherical projection geometry and then employs bounded, content-adaptive offsets, yielding features that are better aligned with the underlying panoramic geometry. Subsequently, the decoder performs structure-guided context aggregation and progressive refinement to recover complete salient regions and accurate boundaries. Extensive experiments on three public 360° SOD benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off, supporting structurepreserving inference directly in ERP space as a promising alternative to projection-heavy panoramic pipelines.

</details>


### [193] [Solution for UCF UrbanTwin LUMPI Track: Sim-to-Real Urban LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07590)
*Pu Luo,Cong Xu,Yumei Li,Kexin Zhang,Licheng Jiao,Wenping Ma,Lingling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于纯合成数据训练的自动驾驶LiDAR检测与点云真实性生成方案，在UCF UrbanTwin Sim2Real挑战中通过三级策略缩小Sim2Real差距，最终取得Combined Score 0.4692的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶LiDAR检测通常依赖大量真实标注数据，而真实数据获取成本高昂。本文面向Sim2Real挑战，目标是在仅使用合成数据训练的条件下，使检测器能够有效应用于真实LiDAR帧，并提升合成点云的真实感。

Method: 方法从三个层面解决Sim2Real差距：(1)数据层面——将对齐合成扫描至50k点测试密度，构建30k记录训练池，结合RangeLDM采样多样化、稀有类别复制粘贴及行人定向增强；(2)模型层面——训练互补的DSVT检测器及Car/Bus专属PointPillars专家模型；(3)融合层面——通过类别感知路由、非对称一致性融合、约束残差召回补充、类别覆盖审计及选择性框尺寸校准集成预测。真实性分支则采用径向密度匹配、弱仿射校准和校准集混合独立优化。

Result: 最终提交获得Combined Score 0.4692、Detection Score 0.1797、Realism Score 0.9035以及3D mAP@0.5达0.1258。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过多层级的数据增强、模型集成与后处理融合策略，合成数据训练的检测器能够在一定程度上跨越Sim2Real鸿沟，但检测精度仍存在较大提升空间，点云真实感方面表现相对较好。

Abstract: We present our solution to the LUMPI track of the UCF UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge at the 6th DriveX Workshop, ECCV 2026. The detector must be trained only on synthetic data and is evaluated on 50 held-out real LiDAR frames; a separate 50-frame synthetic submission is evaluated for point-cloud realism. Our method addresses the Sim2Real gap at three levels. First, we align synthetic scans to the 50k-point test density and build a 30k-record training pool using UT-LUMPI geometry, RangeLDM-based sampling diversification, rare-class copy-paste, and pedestrian-oriented augmentation. Second, complementary DSVT detectors and Car/Bus PointPillars specialists are trained under the same synthetic-only constraint. Third, predictions are integrated by class-aware routing, asymmetric agreement fusion, constrained residual-recall supplementation, class-coverage auditing, and selective box-size calibration. The realism branch is optimized independently with radial-density matching, weak affine calibration, and calibrated set mixing. The final submission obtains a Combined Score of 0.4692, a Detection Score of 0.1797, a Realism Score of 0.9035, and 3D mAP@0.5 of 0.1258.

</details>


### [194] [BarkNet-Lite: A Lightweight Texture and Colour Network with the BarkBD Benchmark for Bark-Based Tree Species Recognition in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07600)
*Aroshi Ali,Saad Ahmed,Md. Khalid Syfullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布孟加拉国树皮数据集BarkBD（20个本地树种、14,258张智能手机照片），并提出轻量级网络BarkNet-Lite（仅296万参数），从随机初始化训练，通过多尺度纹理与颜色感知双通路实现96.64%准确率，媲美九种ImageNet预训练大模型，并可迁移至公开基准，同时支持智能手机端毫秒级实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树种识别对森林清查和生物多样性监测至关重要，但依赖稀缺的分类学专家。树皮全年可见且位于地面层，是理想识别特征，但现有研究集中于温带植物群且依赖大规模ImageNet预训练模型，缺乏热带/亚热带区域的小型高效方案。

Method: 发布BarkBD数据集（14,258张未裁剪智能手机照片、20个本地树种、4个地区、3种天气条件，采用固定分层划分）。提出BarkNet-Lite，仅2.96M参数，从随机初始化训练，设计多尺度纹理通路与并行颜色感知通路的双通路架构。使用Grad-CAM可视化解释性，并通过忠实度和权重随机化检验验证。

Result: 五个随机种子下达到96.64%±0.66%准确率（严格单图推理），与九种ImageNet预训练微调模型相差仅2.3个百分点，且在最小模型的种子级标准差范围内。迁移至公开基准表现优异（BarkVN-50上95.86%，BarkNet 1.0上92.85%）。Grad-CAM确认决策基于树皮结构而非背景。导出的单精度模型在商用智能手机上单张推理仅需15.34ms。

Conclusion: BarkBD填补了热带地区树皮数据集空白，BarkNet-Lite证明了轻量网络从随机初始化即可达到接近大预训练模型的性能，兼具高精度与真实世界边缘设备部署可行性，为热带树种自动识别提供了实用的轻量化方案。

Abstract: Tree species recognition supports forest inventory and biodiversity monitoring but still depends on scarce taxonomic expertise. Bark is visible year-round at ground level, yet bark recognition has concentrated on temperate floras and on large ImageNet-pre-trained backbones. We address both gaps. First, we release BarkBD, a bark dataset for Bangladesh: 14,258 uncropped smartphone photographs of 20 native species across four districts and three weather conditions, with a fixed stratified split. Second, we propose BarkNet-Lite, a 2.96M-parameter network trained from random initialisation, pairing a multi-scale texture pathway with a parallel colour-aware pathway. Over five seeds it reaches 96.64+-0.66%accuracyunderstrict single-image inference, within 2.3 points of nine ImageNet-pre-trained backbones fine-tuned under an identical protocol and within one seed-level standard deviation of the smallest ofthem, andtransfers to public benchmarks (95.86% on BarkVN-50, 92.85% on BarkNet 1.0). Grad-CAM, validated by faithfulness and weight-randomisation checks, confirms its decisions rest on bark structure rather than background. The exported single-precision model classifies one photograph in 15.34ms on a commodity smartphone.

</details>


### [195] [Search-to-World: Evaluation of 3D World Delivery from User Request through Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07605)
*Zixiao Gu,Yabo Chen,Xunzhi Xiang,Yu He,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Yunbo Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Search-to-World，一个端到端评测任务，用于衡量智能体系统将网络检索内容转化为可用3D世界的能力，并配套提出WorldSearcher评测工具框架，包含复用-重建机制和结构化恢复控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统能解析用户请求、搜索实时网络、使用外部工具，但其将检索到的网页内容转化为可用3D世界的能力从未被系统评估，且缺乏端到端流程和基准测试。

Method: 提出Search-to-World端到端评测任务，覆盖请求理解、网络视觉内容检索和3D世界交付；定义观察检索率(ORR)和世界交付率(WDR)两个指标；提出WorldSearcher框架，采用复用-重建策略，先检索可复用的3D世界，若无则从视频重建，并由结构化恢复控制器在失败后修订时间定位、替换源视频或重新表述查询；对恢复子代理进行监督微调(SFT)。

Result: 结果表明：交付成功率依赖于底层智能体模型；观察相关内容并不能保证世界成功交付；联合训练恢复代理可提升交付成功率和行动效率。

Conclusion: Search-to-World使智能体3D世界交付能力变得可测量，WorldSearcher提供了带恢复能力的实用评估框架。

Abstract: Agentic systems can interpret user requests, search the live web, and use external tools, but their ability to transform retrieved web content into a usable 3D world has not been systematically evaluated. No established end-to-end pipeline or benchmark exists for this capability. We introduce Search-to-World, an end-to-end evaluation task covering request understanding, web visual-content retrieval, and 3D-world delivery. We define Observed Retrieval Rate (ORR) and World Delivery Rate (WDR) to distinguish observing relevant content from successfully delivering a request-aligned, perceptually acceptable world. We also present WorldSearcher, a reuse-then-reconstruction harness that connects existing search agents to world delivery: it first retrieves reusable 3D worlds and, when none are available, reconstructs a world from video. A structured recovery controller revises temporal grounding, replaces source videos, or reformulates queries after failure. Using WorldSearcher, we benchmark representative models on Search-to-World and study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) for recovery subagents. Results show that delivery depends on the underlying agentic model, and that relevant-content observation does not ensure world delivery. Jointly training recovery agents improves delivery success and action efficiency. Search-to-World makes agentic 3D-world delivery measurable, while WorldSearcher provides a practical evaluation harness with recovery capabilities.

</details>


### [196] [Solution for UCF UrbanTwin V2X-Real Track: Sim-to-Real Urban LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07608)
*Pu Luo,Cong Xu,Yumei Li,Kexin Zhang,Licheng Jiao,Wenping Ma,Lingling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多源协同训练与类感知融合框架，用于解决路边激光雷达（LiDAR）的Sim2Real三维检测问题。系统将数字孪生扫描、扩散重绘扫描、密度稳定化扫描和行人形态对齐样本统一组织为互补的训练池，在DSVT检测框架下训练来源专属专家分支，推理时通过预定义的类感知融合路径整合不同类别的最优证据。在UrbanTwin V2X-Real测试集上，综合得分为0.7421，3D mAP@0.5为0.4518，真实感得分0.8871。结果表明稳定的、可解释的数据源协作优于无约束的模型输出聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路侧LiDAR的仿真到现实（Sim2Real）转换面临多重耦合差异，包括场景几何、采样密度、回波模式和行人尺度等问题，需要系统性地弥合这些差距以提升三维检测性能。

Method: 提出多源协同训练框架：1）将数字孪生、扩散重绘、密度稳定化和行人形态对齐的扫描组织为统一训练池；2）在统一DSVT检测架构中，训练来源专属（source-specialized）专家分支，各分支保持各自数据角色同时优化同一检测目标；3）推理时采用预定义的类感知融合路径，对不同类别选择针对性分支（车辆用几何稳定和校准感知分支，卡车用采样互补分支，行人用形态一致性证据）；4）使用无标签的点云中心混合（label-free point-cloud center blend）细化几何定位。

Result: 在UrbanTwin V2X-Real隐藏测试集上，系统取得综合得分0.7421，3D mAP@0.5为0.4518，真实感评分（realism score）为0.8871。

Conclusion: 数据源之间稳定且可解释的协作机制比无约束地聚合模型输出更有价值，这为道路侧LiDAR的Sim2Real三维检测提供了一种结构化、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Bridging the simulation-to-reality gap in roadside LiDAR requires addressing several coupled discrepancies, including scene geometry, sampling density, return patterns, and pedestrian scale. This report presents a multi-source collaborative training and class-aware fusion framework for Sim2Real 3D detection. The method organizes digital-twin scans, diffusion-redrawn scans, density-stabilized scans, and pedestrian morphology-aligned samples into a unified training pool with complementary roles. Within a common DSVT detection formulation, source-specialized expert branches preserve those roles while optimizing for the same detection objective. At inference, a predefined class-aware fusion pathway integrates geometry-stable and calibration-aware branches for vehicles, sampling-complementary branches for trucks, and morphology-consistent evidence for pedestrians. A label-free point-cloud center blend then refines geometric localization. On the UrbanTwin V2X-Real hidden test set, the unified system achieves a combined score of 0.7421, with 3D mAP@0.5 of 0.4518 and a realism score of 0.8871. The results indicate that a stable, interpretable collaboration among data sources is more valuable than unconstrained aggregation of model outputs.

</details>


### [197] [Harnessing CLIP and DINO: An Uncertainty-Aware Cascaded Fusion Network for Generalizable Deepfake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07670)
*Xuechao Zou,Yi Zhou,Kai Li,Shun Zhang,Yuhui Chen,Congyan Lang,Junliang Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF-Net，一个融合CLIP语言对齐语义先验和DINO自监督视觉结构先验的不确定性感知级联融合网络，用于提升深度伪造检测的跨域泛化能力，在统一基准（约4M图像）和跨生成器评估集（8K+人脸图像）上均取得最优平均AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型的深度伪造检测器通常依赖单一预训练表征，容易对特定训练分布过拟合，难以泛化到未见过的伪造内容，需要更鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: UCF-Net通过以下步骤实现检测：1) 提取跨Transformer层级的层次化特征；2) 使用层级专家聚合机制自适应地组合CLIP和DINO各编码器的多层级线索；3) 基于熵导出的不确定性对融合后的表征进行加权融合。此外，论文还整合公共深度伪造数据集构建约4M图像的统一基准，并构建独立的跨生成器评估集。

Result: 在统一基准上，UCF-Net在域内和跨域评估中均达到最佳平均AUC；在跨生成器评估集中，UCF-Net在有限目标域数据下能有效适应，但零样本迁移仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 融合多种视觉基础模型的互补先验并引入不确定性感知的加权融合机制，能显著提升深度伪造检测的泛化能力，但在零样本跨生成器检测场景下仍有改进空间。

Abstract: The growing realism and accessibility of manipulated and generated faces threaten the trustworthiness of digital media. To detect such forgeries, deepfake detectors based on vision foundation models have shown promising performance, but they typically rely on a single pretrained representation and are prone to overfitting to particular training distributions. To improve generalization to unseen forgeries, we propose UCF-Net, an uncertainty-aware cascaded fusion network that harnesses CLIP's language-aligned semantic priors and DINO's self-supervised visual-structure priors. UCF-Net extracts hierarchical features across Transformer depths, uses layer-wise expert aggregation to adaptively combine each encoder's multi-level cues, and performs weighted fusion of the resulting representations based on entropy-derived uncertainty. We further consolidate public deepfake datasets into a unified benchmark of approximately 4M images and construct a separate cross-generator evaluation set with over 8K face images from eight recent generators. On the unified benchmark, UCF-Net achieves the best mean AUC among the evaluated methods in both in-domain and cross-domain evaluations. On the cross-generator set, it adapts effectively with limited target-domain data, although zero-shot transfer remains challenging.

</details>


### [198] [CrowdTraj: A Benchmark for Dense Crowd Trajectory Prediction in Realistic Crowded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07685)
*Antonius Bima Murti Wijaya,Paul Henderson,Marwa Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrowdTraj基准，用于自然密集人群场景下的行人轨迹预测，支持从检测到跟踪再到轨迹预测的端到端评估，包含五个场景、超过320万个标注头部边界框，揭示了现有轨迹预测方法在严重遮挡、密集人群条件下的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人轨迹预测基准存在三个局限：行人交互稀疏、假设完美的跟踪输入、依赖头顶视角以最小化遮挡和透视畸变，无法在真实的密集人群场景中评估。需要一个新的基准来支持从检测到跟踪再到轨迹预测的端到端评估。

Method: 构建CrowdTraj数据集，包含五个多样场景，每个场景平均1,146个行人，帧级最大密度为114-372人，提供超过320万注释头边界框。通过每场景单应矩阵同时提供像素坐标和真实世界坐标，支持检测-跟踪-轨迹预测的全链路评估。

Result: 在最密集场景中，跟踪准确率(IDF1)降至0.68-0.70，而较不拥挤场景约为0.90。密集场景中轨迹预测训练时间增加高达8倍。实验结果揭示了当前轨迹预测流程在跟踪噪声鲁棒性和计算可扩展性方面的局限。

Conclusion: CrowdTraj暴露了现有稀疏人群基准中隐藏的轨迹预测管道的局限性，特别是在对跟踪噪声的鲁棒性和计算可扩展性方面，为真实密集人群场景中的轨迹预测研究提供了更贴近实际的评估平台。

Abstract: In real-world applications, pedestrian trajectory prediction models rely on inputs from detection and tracking systems. Prior trajectory prediction benchmarks either contain relatively sparse pedestrian interactions, assume perfect tracking inputs, or rely on overhead viewpoints that minimize occlusion and perspective distortion, limiting evaluation in realistic dense-crowd scenarios. We present CrowdTraj, a benchmark for pedestrian trajectory prediction in natural dense crowd scenes. Unlike previous datasets, CrowdTraj supports end-to-end evaluation from detection through tracking to trajectory prediction under severe occlusion in CCTV views. It also captures diverse, natural pedestrian behaviours, including abrupt directional changes rarely observed in existing benchmarks. CrowdTraj includes five diverse scenes, with an average of 1,146 unique pedestrians per scene, maximum frame-level densities ranging from 114 to 372 pedestrians, and over 3.2 million annotated head bounding boxes. CrowdTraj provides pixel and real-world coordinates via per-scene homography matrices for physically meaningful analysis. Our experimental results show that tracking accuracy (IDF1) drops to 0.68 to 0.70 in the densest scenes, compared with approximately 0.90 in less crowded scenes. Trajectory prediction training also becomes substantially more computationally expensive in dense scenes, with training times increasing by up to 8 times. These findings show that CrowdTraj exposes limitations in current trajectory prediction pipelines that remain hidden on existing sparse-crowd benchmarks, particularly in robustness to tracking noise and computational scalability.

</details>


### [199] [Better Call CineCrew: Consistent Ultra-Long Narrative-to-Film Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07720)
*Jiaben Chen,Sixun Dong,Qinhong Zhou,Raine Ma,Zhiyang Dou,Wojciech Matusik,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FilmDSL结构化编排层，作为剧本与视频生成模型之间的中间层，通过多智能体框架实现电影级镜头控制和跨片段一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于提示词的剧本到视频工作流存在脆弱性，原因在于缺乏结构化的中间层来桥接剧本与视频模型，尤其是在剧本在关键电影决策点信息未明确时，难以同时满足镜头级可控性和跨片段视觉身份与角色行为的一致性。

Method: 该方法构建了一个多智能体框架作为结构化编排层，核心是FilmDSL领域特定语言，显式编码镜头与摄像指令、资产与连续性要求、角色线索等电影约束。生成智能体负责构建资产包和故事板关键帧来锚定构图，再进行逐片段合成；评论智能体产生结构化QA信号并触发有针对性的细化，无需重新训练基础模型。

Result: 在电视风格片段的实验中，该方法在可控性和一致性方面优于纯文本和纯参考基线。

Conclusion: 通过在剧本与视频生成器之间引入结构化编排层，以域特定语言显式表达电影约束并借助多智能体协同（生成+评论），能够在不重训底层模型的前提下有效提升长叙事视频生成的镜头级可控性与跨片段一致性。

Abstract: Long-form narrative-to-film generation requires shot-level controllability and cross-clip consistency in both visual identity and character behavior-requirements that remain difficult to satisfy with current prompt-based workflows. A core reason existing workflows remain brittle is the lack of a structured intermediate layer between scripts and video models, especially when screenplays are underspecified at key cinematic decision points. We introduce a structured orchestration layer for film-oriented script-to-video generation, implemented as a multi-agent framework that operates between scripts and off-the-shelf video generators. The layer is centered on FilmDSL, a film-oriented domain-specific language that makes cinematic constraints explicit, including shot and camera directives, asset and continuity requirements, and persona cues, so that agents coordinate through a shared structured specification for planning, generation, critique, and repair. Specifically, a generation agent constructs asset packs and storyboard keyframes that anchor composition before clip-by-clip synthesis, while a critic agent produces structured QA signals and triggers targeted refinement without retraining the base model. Experiments on TV-style segments show improved controllability and consistency over text-only and reference-only baselines.

</details>


### [200] [Zero-Shot 3D Plant Organ Segmentation with SAM3 and Semantic NeRFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07724)
*Andreas Gilson,Laura Hennig,Peter Pietrzyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标注的3D植物器官分割管线，结合文本提示的SAM3分割与语义神经辐射场（NeRF），仅需多视角RGB图像和类别名称即可生成带语义标签的3D点云。在Begonia maculata测试平台上达到92.6%的mIoU，在新数据集上平均达0.856 mIoU，验证了该方法接近监督方法的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D植物器官分割方法依赖标注训练数据或物种特定的模型配置，限制了其在自动化表型分析中的推广和应用。

Method: 提出一种零样本管线，将文本提示的SAM3分割与语义神经辐射场相结合。通过多视角NeRF融合作为隐式共识机制，将不完美的逐帧掩膜提升为精准的3D标签，无需人工标注、无需物种微调、无需领域预处理。

Result: 在Begonia maculata测试平台上SAM3管线达到92.6%的mIoU，达到使用完美真值掩膜的上限（oracle）的95.9%。在涵盖十个多样植物点云的新数据集上平均达到0.856 mIoU，所有物种的叶片和花盆IoU分别高于0.91和0.90。

Conclusion: 无需标注的3D植物器官分割现已可行，且性能正在接近监督方法的表现水平，展示了该管线在自动化植物表型分析中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate 3D plant organ segmentation is fundamental to automated phenotyping. Existing approaches rely on annotated training data or species-specific model configurations. We present an annotation-free pipeline for 3D plant organ segmentation, combining text-prompted SAM3 segmentation with semantic neural radiance fields (NeRFs). Given only multi-view RGB images and a list of class names, our zero-shot pipeline produces semantically labeled 3D point clouds without manual annotation, per-species fine-tuning, or domain-specific preprocessing. Multi-view NeRF fusion acts as effective implicit consensus mechanism that lifts imperfect per-frame masks into accurate 3D labels. On a controlled Begonia maculata testbed the SAM3 pipeline achieves 92.6% mIoU, reaching 95.9% of the oracle upper bound established with perfect ground-truth masks. The pipeline was further evaluated on a new dataset spanning ten diverse plant point clouds reaching an average 0.856 mIoU, with leaf and pot IoU above 0.91 and 0.90 for every species, respectively. These results demonstrate that annotation-free 3D plant organ segmentation is now feasible and approaching the range of supervised methods.

</details>


### [201] [TV-SGS: Gaussian Splatting with Geometric Information Propagation via Tensor Voting under sparse views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07734)
*Harish N Sathishchandra,Philippos Mordohai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TV-SGS方法，通过Tensor Voting实现3D高斯点之间的直接通信，在测试时优化过程中提供几何监督，从而提升场景几何结构的准确性，尤其在稀疏视角输入下效果显著，同时不牺牲渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）方法中，多个高斯点之间缺乏直接通信，仅通过共同投影的像素间接相互影响，导致形成的3D几何结构不够准确，尤其在输入视角稀疏、图像监督有限时几何正则化显得尤为关键。

Method: 本文借鉴了原本用于噪声数据推断结构的Tensor Voting技术，将其适配为测试时优化过程中的监督信号；引入一类不依赖渲染的新型3D损失，可直接与已有损失结合使用；该方法可轻松集成于多种骨干网络中。

Result: 在DTU和Tanks-and-Temples数据集上的实验表明，TV-SGS相比骨干方法提升了输出几何的准确性，同时保持或改善了渲染质量，尤其在稀疏视角输入场景下效果突出。

Conclusion: 通过在3D高斯点之间建立直接通信并施加基于Tensor Voting的3D几何损失，能够在不依赖渲染的情况下增强场景几何结构，为高斯泼溅技术提供了一种有效的几何正则化手段，且具备良好的通用性和可集成性。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has been effective in inferring scene representations that excel in novel view synthesis. Multiple splats cooperate seamlessly to synthesize the pixels of novel views and are jointly optimized even though they only affect each other indirectly, via pixels they project to in common. We present an approach that enables direct communication among splats to enhance the geometric structures they form in 3D. This is accomplished by Tensor Voting, which was originally designed to infer structures from noisy inputs and has been adapted here to provide supervision during test-time optimization, leading to more accurate scene geometry. We introduce a new class of 3D losses that do not rely on rendering and can be combined with essentially all losses previously reported in the literature. Our 3D losses are especially effective when the input views are sparse and geometric regularization is essential due to limited supervision from the images. Our method is easy to integrate with a diverse set of backbones, and our experiments on the DTU and Tanks-and-Temples datasets demonstrate that TV-SGS improves the geometry of the outputs compared to the backbone, while maintaining or improving rendering quality.

</details>


### [202] [TFTrack: A Template-Free Framework for Efficient 3D Point Cloud Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07738)
*Zhaofeng Hu,Sifan Zhou,Jiahao Nie,Ziyu Zhao,Weizi Li,Ci-jyun Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个无模板追踪框架TFTrack，摒弃传统孪生网络中的模板-搜索帧配对设计，仅用前一帧的边界框中心与尺寸作为引导，直接在当前帧点云中进行目标定位与追踪。实验表明该框架在KITTI和nuScenes基准上与主流基于模板的方法性能相当，同时减少约50%的FLOPs并达到约120 FPS的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR三维单目标追踪方法沿袭2D视觉的孪生追踪范式，依赖双输入设计和复杂的运动建模，导致计算开销大、效率低下。作者通过深入分析发现：模板范式和复杂运动建模本质上都是冗余的——前序边界框中心已包含足够的历史上下文信息，几何对齐即可提供充分的运动先验。因此需要一种更简洁高效的追踪范式。

Method: 提出Template-Free Tracking（TFTrack）框架，核心创新是消除模板-搜索帧配对设计，仅以先验边界框中心与尺寸为引导直接处理当前帧。框架被实例化为三个变体（TFTrack-Voxel、TFTrack-Pillar和TFTrack-Point），分别探索体素、柱状和点三种3D表示方法，以适应稀疏和稠密场景。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes基准上，TFTrack与领先的基于模板的追踪器性能相当，同时计算量（FLOPs）减少约50%，运行速度达到约120 FPS，展现出显著的效率优势。

Conclusion: 通过简化过度复杂的运动中心设计，TFTrack建立了一种高效的极简主义三维点云追踪范式，为自动驾驶等嵌入式机器人系统中的实时、资源高效部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D Single Object Tracking (3D SOT) is critical for robotic perception and navigation and aims to localize dynamic objects across frames in sparse point clouds. Existing methods, rooted in the Siamese tracking paradigm from 2D vision, rely on costly dual-input designs and excessive motion modeling guided by template priors, hindering their efficiency. Our in-depth analysis reveals: (i) the template paradigm is redundant, as the previous bounding box center encodes sufficient historical context; (ii) complex motion modeling is unnecessary, as geometric alignment provides adequate motion priors. Based on the above findings, we propose the first Template-Free Tracking framework (TFTrack). The novel framework eliminates the need for template-search pairings and operates directly on the current frame guided solely by the prior bounding box center and size. We instantiate this paradigm into three variants: TFTrack-Voxel, TFTrack-Pillar, and TFTrack-Point, to explore different 3D representations under a unified framework, ensuring flexibility across sparse and dense scenes. Extensive experiments on KITTI and nuScenes benchmarks show that TFTrack is competitive with leading template-based trackers, while reducing FLOPs by approximately 50% and running at approximately 120 FPS. By simplifying overcomplicated motion-centric designs, TFTrack establishes a new minimalist paradigm for efficient 3D point cloud tracking, paving the way for real-time and resource-efficient deployment in embedded robotic systems, such as autonomous vehicles. The code is available at https://github.com/tftrack-anonymous/TFTrack/tree/main.

</details>


### [203] [Cross-modal learning for SAR target recognition using optical vision foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07753)
*Lucas Hirsch,James R. Hopgood,Javid Khan,Yoann Altmann,Mike E. Davies*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模态EO到SAR的原型对齐框架，利用光学数据训练的DINOv3视觉基础模型构造类级光学原型，为SAR图像分类提供监督信号。在UNICORNv2数据集上，该方法优于SAR单独微调和未配对分布对齐等基线，证明光学基础模型知识可迁移至SAR分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR图像自动目标识别（ATR）面临标注数据稀缺、强斑点噪声严重以及与光学图像之间存在显著域差距等问题。相反，光学（EO）图像拥有大规模数据集、清晰视觉结构和强大的基础模型。作者希望探究能否利用光学基础模型为SAR分类提供类级监督，以缓解SAR标注不足的难题。

Method: 提出跨模态EO到SAR原型对齐框架：冻结基于DINOv3的EO编码器构造类级光学原型（无需严格EO/SAR配对）；训练SAR模型完成分类任务同时将其嵌入与对应EO类原型对齐；推理时SAR模型独立运行，不依赖光学图像。

Result: 在UNICORNv2数据集（含严重斑点噪声和类别不均衡的民用车辆EO和SAR数据）上，EO原型对齐方法在SAR分类精度上优于冻结DINOv3、仅SAR微调和未配对分布对齐等基线；t-SNE可视化显示训练后的SAR嵌入空间中各类别分离更清晰。

Conclusion: 光学视觉基础模型尽管在可见光谱图像上训练，仍能为SAR图像分类提供可迁移的监督信息，这为在不同感知模态下利用大规模预训练视觉基础模型提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) is an important modality in a wide range of imaging applications due to its versatile, long range and near all weather operating capabilities. However, Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) remains a challenging problem due to limited labelled data, the strong speckle in SAR images and the significant domain gap between SAR and more abundant optical imagery. In contrast, electro-optical (EO) imagery benefits from massive datasets, clearer visual structure and powerful foundation models. In this work, we investigate how vision foundation models trained on optical data can provide class level supervision for SAR classification.
  We propose a cross-modal EO to SAR prototype alignment framework in which a frozen EO encoder, based on a DINOv3 vision foundation model, is used to construct class level optical prototypes without requiring strict EO/SAR pairs. A SAR model is then trained to classify SAR images while aligning its embeddings to the corresponding EO class prototype. At inference time, the SAR model operates independently, without access to optical imagery. We evaluate our approach on the UNICORNv2 dataset, an EO and SAR dataset of civilian vehicles with heavily speckled images and severe class imbalance. EO prototype alignment improves SAR classification accuracy over frozen DINOv3, SAR only finetuning and unpaired distribution alignment baselines, and t-SNE visualizations provide qualitative evidence of clearer separation among classes in the trained SAR embedding space. These results suggest that optical vision foundation models, despite being trained on visible spectrum imagery, provide transferable information for SAR image classification, offering a practical method for using large scale pretrained vision foundation models across challenging sensing modalities.

</details>


### [204] [DroneGround: Open-Vocabulary Drone Payload Characterization Using Synthetic Data and Grounded Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07780)
*Ami Pandat,Rajasekhar Punna,Gopika Vinod,Rohit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DroneGround框架，用于无人机视觉载荷的开放词汇表征，结合合成数据生成与两阶段视觉-语言模型，在合成到真实分布迁移下显著优于传统闭集检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机监视系统在远距离成像条件下对载荷表征面临两大挑战：缺少标注真实数据集、部署时存在显著分布偏移。且现有方法将载荷表征视为闭集目标检测问题，无法识别未见过的载荷类别。需要一种能开放词汇识别并对抗分布偏移的鲁棒框架。

Method: 首先使用Unreal Engine 5和Cosys-AirSim生成逼真的合成无人机-载荷数据集。然后提出两阶段框架DroneGround：(1) 第一阶段使用YOLO26s检测器定位无人机并提取以无人机为中心的图像裁剪；(2) 第二阶段使用LoRA微调的PaliGemma视觉-语言模型生成检测到的无人机及其载荷的语义描述，实现超越预定义类别的开放词汇载荷表征；最后通过基于遮挡的接地模块提供可解释的载荷定位。

Result: 在合成和真实无人机图像上的广泛实验表明，DroneGround在合成到真实分布偏移下显著提升了鲁棒性：F1分数从传统闭集检测器的82.5%提升至96.3%，在未见载荷类别上的泛化能力也大幅提高（80.4%对比42.7% F1）。

Conclusion: DroneGround通过结合合成数据生成与视觉-语言基础模型，为无人机载荷表征提供了一种可处理开放词汇且对分布偏移鲁棒的有效方案，优于传统闭集方法，数据集和代码将在论文被接收后发布。

Abstract: Automated drone surveillance has become increasingly important for public safety, critical infrastructure protection,and restricted airspace monitoring. While existing vision-based systems achieve strong performance for drone detection and tracking, reliable payload characterization remains highly challenging under long-range imaging conditions due to limited availability of annotated real-world datasets, and substantial distribution shifts encountered during deployment. Existing approaches formulate payload characterization as a closed-set object detection problem, limiting their ability to recognize previously unseen payloads and generalize beyond the training distribution. To address these challenges, we generate a photorealistic synthetic drone-payload dataset using Unreal Engine 5 and Cosys-AirSim and propose DroneGround: Grounded Vision-Language Payload Characterization, a two-stage framework for robust open-vocabulary payload analysis. DroneGround first employs a YOLO26s detector to localize drones and extract drone-centric image crops, followed by a LoRA-fine-tuned PaliGemma vision-language model that generates seman- tic descriptions of the detected drones and their attached payloads, enabling open-vocabulary payload characterization beyond predefined categories. An occlusion-based grounding module further provides interpretable payload localization by identifying image regions responsible for the generated descriptions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world drone imagery demonstrate that DroneGround substantially improves robustness under synthetic-to-real distribution shifts, outperforming a conventional closed-set payload detector by improving the F1-score from 82.5% to 96.3%, while achieving significantly better generalization to previously unseen payload categories (80.4%versus 42.7% F1). Dataset and code will be released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [205] [VoT: Vision-of-Thought for Unified Multimodal Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07815)
*Jingxiang Sun,Chao Liao,Zhengxiong Luo,Chaorui Deng,Chen-lin Zhang,Junke Wang,Ceyuan Yang,Haoqi Fan,Weilin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-of-Thought (VoT)框架，在视觉语言模型（VLM）与扩散Transformer（DiTs）之间引入离散的"视觉思维"层，将VLM作为多模态规划器生成高层视觉计划（如物体和布局）的离散token，再渲染像素。通过专用的VoT tokenizer配合VLM对齐、特征重建和向量量化损失的闭环训练，实现语义可读且保留视觉信息的生成，提升语义对齐并提供可解释、可控的生成接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文生图系统采用"文本编码器+扩散解码器"范式，文本语义直接调制连续潜在噪声，缺乏显式、可解释的中间表示来有效桥接高层语言语义与低层视觉信号。

Method: 1) 在VLM与DiTs之间引入离散视觉思维层；2) 将VLM作为多模态规划器而非文本编码器，生成代表高层视觉计划（物体和布局）的离散VoT tokens；3) 在VLM语义空间中训练专用VoT tokenizer，采用融合VLM对齐、特征重建与向量量化损失的闭环目标，确保token对VLM语义可读同时保留生成所需的视觉信息。

Result: 实验表明VoT框架改善了语义对齐（semantic alignment），并提供了结构化接口实现可解释和可控的图像生成。

Conclusion: VoT通过引入显式的离散视觉思维中间层，弥合了语言语义与视觉信号之间的鸿沟，为文生图系统带来了更好的语义一致性和更强的可解释性与可控性。

Abstract: Current text-to-image systems typically employ a "text encoder plus diffusion decoder" paradigm, in which text semantics directly modulate continuous latent noise. Despite their success, these methods lack an explicit, interpretable intermediate representation that effectively bridges high-level linguistic semantics and low-level visual signals. In this paper, we propose Vision-of-Thought (VoT), a framework that introduces a discrete visual-thinking layer between vision-language models (VLMs) and diffusion transformers (DiTs).
