Result: 在SPACE基准上，实验表明尽管当前MLLMs具有强大的通用感知能力，但在将描述锚定到主体自身参考系上持续失败。所提出的框架显著提升了以人为中心的字幕质量，尤其在主体空间推理方面，实现了与强闭源模型竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 本文揭示MLLMs在以人为中心的空间理解上的系统性不足，并证明了通过专门的数据构建和对齐策略可以有效缓解该问题，为主体重心的空间推理提供了可行改进路径。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve remarkable performance on generic image captioning, they frequently suffer from structural hallucinations in human-centric scenarios. Accurately modeling human subjects is foundational for critical downstream applications, such as accurate avatar/video/image generation and fine-grained human action understanding. However, these tasks require highly precise subject-centered spatial grounding, such as distinguishing egocentric left/right laterality and maintaining correct anatomical-object bindings. Although catastrophic for structural integrity, these localized spatial inversions are often overshadowed by overall descriptive metrics in existing benchmarks. To systematically expose and quantify this bottleneck, we introduce SPACE (Subject-centric Poses, Appearance, and Characteristics Evaluation), a benchmark designed to evaluate subject-centered spatial understanding. On SPACE, we reveal that despite strong generic perception, current MLLMs consistently fail to ground descriptions in the subject's intrinsic frame of reference. To bridge this gap, we propose a specialized data construction and alignment pipeline. We first extract structured spatial hints from fine-grained body-part localization to guide a two-stage caption rewriting process, yielding highly spatially-faithful training data. Furthermore, we design a rubric-based reward for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that explicitly penalizes structurally critical spatial errors during alignment. Extensive experiments on SPACE demonstrate our framework significantly improves human-centric caption quality, particularly in subject-centered spatial reasoning, achieving performance competitive with strong closed-source models. Our benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/JHang2020/SPACE-Eval.

</details>


### [241] [TRIUNE-Net: Harmonizing Scale, Shape, and Efficiency in Pancreatic Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08303)
*Amir Hossein Saleknia,Alireza Kheyrkhah,Sanaz Karimijafarbigloo,Reza Azad,Sina Houshmand,Ulas Bagci,Dorit Merhof,Alaa Sulaiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIUNE-Net，一种轻量级3D CT胰腺肿瘤分割网络，通过多尺度上下文聚合、串行线性可变形注意力、信息保留下采样三个创新模块，仅用5.86M参数即在MSD和NVD两个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胰腺肿瘤分割面临器官和肿瘤的极端尺度差异以及高度不规则、非凸的肿瘤形态挑战，现有方法未显式解决这些问题且计算复杂度过高，难以在资源受限的临床环境中实用。

Method: 提出三个协同创新模块：(1)多尺度上下文聚合模块，采用阶段自适应空洞卷积，使模型能够推理不同解剖尺度；(2)串行线性可变形注意力机制，结合大有效感受野与形状自适应可变形卷积，捕获不规则非凸肿瘤形态；(3)信息保留下采样模块，完全取代传统最大池化，保留全部空间信息且增加参数可忽略，防止小肿瘤在识别前被丢弃。

Result: 在MSD Pancreas和NVD Pancreas数据集上，TRIUNE-Net以仅5.86M参数且无需外部预训练即达SOTA，超越所有基线。较次优模型：肿瘤Dice提升0.45%，F1提升6.0分，敏感性提升6.6分，精确率提升3.4分。

Conclusion: TRIUNE-Net通过统一尺度、形状与效率三大创新，在保持轻量化的同时实现了对不规则和微小胰腺肿瘤的有效分割，具有良好的临床实用价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Pancreatic tumor segmentation in 3D CT volumes is challenged by extreme scale variability across both the pancreas and tumor, and highly irregular tumor morphology. While recent advances have pushed segmentation performance, existing methods do not explicitly address these challenges and come at the cost of excessive computational complexity, limiting their practicality in resource-constrained clinical environments. We propose TRIUNE-Net, a lightweight unified architecture that harmonizes scale, shape, and efficiency through three synergistic innovations. A multi-scale context aggregation module with stage-adaptive dilated convolutions enables the model to reason across the broad range of anatomical scales present in both organs. A serial linear-deformable attention mechanism combines large effective receptive fields with shapeadaptive deformable convolutions to capture irregular, non-convex tumor morphologies. Finally, an information-preserving downsampling module replaces conventional max pooling entirely, retaining all spatial information while adding negligible parameters, preventing small tumors from being discarded before they can be recognized. On both the MSD Pancreas and NVD Pancreas datasets, TRIUNE-Net achieves state-of-theart results with only 5.86 M parameters and no external pre-training, outperforming all baselines across all key tumor metrics. Specifically, it surpasses the next-best model by 0.45% in tumor Dice, 6.0 points in F1 score, 6.6 points in sensitivity, and 3.4 points in precision, simultaneously reflecting its ability to suppress both missed tumors and false alarms in clinically realistic conditions. Our code is available at: https://github.com/abdora-ai/TRIUNE-Net

</details>


### [242] [FPicker: Topology-Guided Evolution for Filament Tracing in Low-SNR Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08305)
*Tingyin Zhao,Mingtao Huang,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FPicker是首个面向Cryo-EM丝状结构追踪的拓扑引导框架，通过中心-端点表示和开放曲线演化模块显式建模非循环连接，在极端噪声（-20dB）下相比基线方法mSAP相对提升超40%，拓扑断裂率降低超60%，在真实EMPIAR数据集上微调后达到82.9% mSAP的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cryo-EM丝状结构自动化追踪对3D螺旋重建至关重要，但现有方法在交叉拓扑和极低信噪比（SNR<0.1或-10dB）条件下均存在根本性缺陷：像素级分割产生拓扑断裂、框检测出现中心漂移、顺序追踪累积误差、传统活动轮廓受闭合曲线约束限制。

Method: 提出FPicker，一个拓扑引导的统一框架。核心包括两个模块：(1) 中心-端点（center-endpoint）表示法统一感知任务；(2) 开放曲线演化模块显式建模非循环连接性，避免人为闭合曲线约束。

Result: 在模拟基准上，FPicker相比顶级基线mSAP相对提升超过40%，拓扑断裂率在-20dB极端噪声下降低超60%。在真实EMPIAR数据集上零样本性能表现出强拓扑鲁棒性，微调后达到82.9% mSAP的SOTA水平。

Conclusion: FPicker通过学习内在物理几何而非局部纹理，在信号匮乏的科学成像中展现出作为弹性几何骨干的潜力。实验结果证明建模物理先验是弥合模拟-真实差距的高度鲁棒路径。

Abstract: Automating filament tracing in Cryo-Electron Microscopy (Cryo-EM) is essential for 3D helical reconstruction but challenged by intersecting topologies and extremely low Signal-to-Noise Ratios ($\text{SNR} = σ_s^2/σ_n^2$ < 0.1 or -10 dB). Existing paradigms fail: pixel-wise segmenters suffer from severe topological fracturing, box-based detectors face ghost center drift, sequential trackers derail due to error accumulation, and traditional active contours collapse under artificial closed-curve constraints. To resolve these bottlenecks, we present FPicker, the first topology-guided framework reconciling these incompatibilities. It unifies perception via a center-endpoint representation and an open-curve evolution module to explicitly model non-cyclic connectivity. On simulated benchmarks, FPicker outperforms top baselines by over $40\%$ relative gain in mean spatio-angular precision (mSAP) and reduces topological gap rates by over $60\%$ under extreme noise ($-20\text{ dB}$). By learning intrinsic physical geometry rather than local texture, FPicker demonstrates strong potential as a resilient geometric backbone. Its zero-shot performance on the real-world EMPIAR dataset exhibits robust topological resistance, achieving a state-of-the-art 82.9\% mSAP upon fine-tuning. Our results also suggest modeling physical priors is a highly robust path toward bridging the sim-to-real gap in signal-starved scientific imaging. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/tomzhaosky/FPicker.

</details>


### [243] [From Glance to Scrutiny: Progressive Distortion Reasoning for Fine-Grained Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08316)
*Aoting Zhang,Mingze Gao,Dongbao Yang,Longyi Chen,Daoxin Zhang,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-IQA框架，将图像质量评估重构为渐进式的"哪里-什么-如何"诊断过程，模拟人类从初步浏览到仔细观察的感知流程，通过两阶段强化学习范式实现，并在新构建的区域级基准Diag-Bench上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based图像质量评估方法主要关注整体质量预测，往往作为黑盒工作，在失真发生位置及其对感知质量的影响方面提供的洞察有限，阻碍了对局部化和异构退化的细粒度分析。

Method: 提出GS-IQA框架，将IQA重构为渐进式Where-What-How诊断。核心包含：1）两阶段强化学习范式——浏览阶段使用感知门控奖励建立退化位置和类型识别，仅在两者正确后激活严重程度反馈；2）审视阶段引入在线奖励条件退化生成，合成针对模型感知瓶颈的困难样本以锐化严重程度判别能力。同时构建Diag-Bench区域级基准，包含约25K样本、12种失真类型和5个序数严重度等级。

Result: 在失真定位、识别和严重程度估计方面始终优于最先进方法，且诊断表示可有效迁移到多个外部基准的传统全局质量预测任务中。

Conclusion: GS-IQA通过模拟人类感知过程的渐进式诊断框架，结合两阶段强化学习和困难样本生成，显著提升了MLLM在图像质量评估中的细粒度分析能力，其诊断表示具有良好的通用迁移性。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in image quality assessment (IQA) by bridging visual perception with descriptive evaluations. However, existing approaches mainly focus on holistic quality prediction, often functioning as black boxes that provide limited insight into where distortions occur and how they affect perceived quality, hindering fine-grained analysis of localized and heterogeneous degradations. We propose GS-IQA, a framework that reformulates IQA as a progressive Where--What--How diagnosis, emulating the human perceptual process from an initial glance to closer scrutiny. Since a severity judgment is meaningful only for a correctly localized and recognized region, we realize this progression through a two-stage reinforcement learning paradigm that respects such dependencies: the glance stage uses a perception-gated reward to establish where degradations lie and what they are, activating severity feedback only once both are correct, while the scrutiny stage introduces online reward-conditioned degradation generation to synthesize hard examples targeted at the model's perceptual bottlenecks, sharpening its discrimination of subtle severity variations. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct Diag-Bench, a region-level IQA benchmark of about 25K curated samples spanning 12 distortion types and five ordinal severity levels. Extensive experiments show that GS-IQA consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods in distortion localization, recognition, and severity estimation, and that its diagnostic representations transfer effectively to conventional global quality prediction across diverse external benchmarks. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [244] [Supervised Cross-Modal Feature Alignment for Zero-Wearable Freezing of Gait Detection in Parkinsonism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08317)
*Aryan Singh,Chandan Biswas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种监督式跨模态子空间蒸馏框架，利用IMU传感器和临床元数据作为教师信号，指导仅视觉的模型在帕金森病冻结步态（FoG）检测中达到85.5%准确率和82.4%平衡准确率，解决了视觉方法在转身任务中因自遮挡导致的精度下降问题，同时推理时仅需视觉输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴IMU传感器虽能提供精确的运动学数据用于帕金森病冻结步态检测，但强制佩戴传感器限制了临床持续部署；而无创的视觉方法在转身任务中因几何自遮挡导致骨骼关键点退化，难以捕捉FoG检测所需的高频前驱信号，两类方法各有局限。

Method: 提出监督式跨模态子空间蒸馏框架：在训练阶段，利用预训练的IMU运动学数据和临床元数据作为"oracle"（教师）指导可部署的视觉架构（学生）；引入关节速度和加速度导数作为补充特征；并设计基于置信度的门控机制，在遮挡事件中缓解骨骼跟踪误差。

Result: 实验验证了潜在空间对齐能将硬件传感器的预测保真度迁移到视觉表征中，实现85.5%的准确率和82.4%的平衡准确率，且推理阶段完全保持仅视觉模型的形式。

Conclusion: 跨模态知识蒸馏有效弥合了IMU精确性与视觉便利性之间的鸿沟，在不牺牲推理部署便利性的前提下，显著提升了纯视觉模型在转身场景下FoG检测的准确性。

Abstract: Objective assessment of Freezing of Gait (FoG) in Parkinson's disease (PD) relies predominantly on wearable Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs). While IMUs provide optimal kinematic precision, mandatory sensor attachment restricts continuous clinical deployment. Conversely, unobtrusive vision-based alternatives suffer substantial classification errors during turning-in-place tasks, where geometric self-occlusion degrades deterministic skeletal coordinates and obscures the high-frequency precursors required for FoG detection. To resolve these physical observation limits, we propose a supervised cross-modal subspace distillation framework. During optimisation, pre-trained kinematic data from IMU sensors and contextual clinical metadata act as oracles to guide a deployable visual architecture. By incorporating joint velocity and acceleration derivatives, utilising a confidence-based gating mechanism, the visual model mitigates some of the tracking errors during occlusion events. Empirical evaluations confirm this latent alignment transfers the predictive fidelity of hardware sensors directly into the visual representation, yielding $85.5\%$ accuracy, and $82.4\%$ balanced accuracy. All the while maintaining a vision only model at inference.

</details>


### [245] [Do Input-Level Defenses Transfer to Observation-Level Attacks on VideoLLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08331)
*Bangshuo Zhu,Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Yuezhong Wu,Zhiquan Liu,Yuekang Li,Jingling Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DefTEval评估框架，系统测试针对视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）观测层攻击的输入级对抗防御效果。研究发现，现有的11种输入级防御在5种攻击类型和5个VideoLLM上均无法有效抵御观测层攻击，有害内容检测率常接近零。防御失效的根本原因在于令牌压缩丢弃局部特征、模态融合弱化视觉信号，且防御效果主要由模型架构决定而非防御方法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VideoLLMs在内容审核等安全关键场景中广泛应用，其帧采样、令牌压缩和模态融合构成的观测管线易受攻击，使模型无法感知有害内容。然而目前尚无针对此类威胁设计的防御机制，亟需系统的评估来理解现有输入级防御的有效性边界。

Method: 构建DefTEval受控评估框架，覆盖5个VideoLLMs、11种代表性输入级防御方法、5种攻击类型，系统量化输入级对抗防御（作用于已采样帧像素内容）在抵御观测层攻击时的表现，并深入分析防御失效的机理。

Result: 输入级防御提供有限且不一致的保护，有害检测率频繁接近零；即使攻击信号嵌入每个采样帧，防御仍失败。令牌压缩丢弃局部特征，模态融合系统性削弱弱化视觉信号；防御有效性由模型架构主导而非防御方法本身，且检测率在不同内容类别间差异极大。

Conclusion: 仅靠输入级防御无法保障VideoLLMs安全，需要系统级鲁棒机制，包括采样感知的覆盖保障、令牌级安全相关特征保留，以及模态平衡融合策略。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications such as content moderation and video analytics. To process long videos efficiently, VideoLLMs rely on frame sampling, token compression, and modality fusion, which together form an observation pipeline that reduces the raw video to a compact internal representation. Recent observation-level attacks exploit this pipeline to prevent the model from perceiving harmful content, yet no defense has been explicitly designed for this threat. We introduce DefTEval, a controlled evaluation framework that systematically assesses whether input-level adversarial defenses, which operate on the pixel content of already-sampled frames, can mitigate observation-level attacks. Across five VideoLLMs, eleven representative defenses, and five attack types, we find that input-level defenses offer limited and inconsistent protection, with harmful detection rates frequently near zero. Critically, defenses fail even against attacks that embed harmful signals in every sampled frame, indicating that the bottleneck extends beyond sampling omission to the suppression of signals that do enter the model. Token compression discards localized features, and modality fusion systematically down-weights weakened visual signals. Furthermore, defense effectiveness is dominated by model architecture rather than by the defense method itself, and detection rates vary drastically across content categories, exposing structural weaknesses in temporal reasoning. These findings demonstrate that securing VideoLLMs requires system-level robustness mechanisms spanning sampling-aware coverage guarantees, token-level preservation of safety-relevant features, and modality-balanced fusion.

</details>


### [246] [EdMCGS: Event-Driven Markov Chain Gaussian Splatting for Extreme-Low-Frame-Rate Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08332)
*Yuzhong Wang,Wenmin Wang,Xinxing Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EdMCGS是一种端到端的动态3D场景重建方法，利用极低帧率RGB图像与事件流（event stream）相结合，建模事件驱动的马尔可夫链来重建动态场景，并可在任意中间时间戳进行渲染。该方法在推理时仍从事件中生成3D高斯的中间运动，而非仅将事件作为训练监督，在合成与真实场景中均优于RGB和基于事件的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖RGB图像重建动态3D场景时，由于连续帧之间缺乏证据，会产生大量伪影。现有方法虽然尝试引入事件流，但大多仅在训练阶段将事件作为监督信号使用，推理时无法利用事件信息生成中间运动，导致插值效果受限。

Method: 将场景运动建模为事件驱动的马尔可夫链：稀疏RGB帧作为各自时间戳处的状态锚点，区间内记录的事件驱动状态转移。状态由一组紧凑的控制点承载，每个控制点由其图像投影邻域内采样的事件驱动，并引入时间局部等距（local isometry）项保证传播运动具有局部刚性。由于转移过程在推理时仍读取当前区间的事件，中间运动直接从事件生成而非插值。

Result: 在合成和真实场景的实验表明，EdMCGS在性能上优于基于RGB和基于事件的基线方法，能以实时速度渲染，且所需的高斯数量远少于最强的事件基线方法。同时发布了源代码和新数据集。

Conclusion: EdMCGS验证了将事件作为推理阶段运动驱动源的可行性，通过在马尔可夫链框架下让事件直接驱动3D高斯运动，有效弥补了极低帧率RGB帧间的证据缺失，实现了高质量、实时的动态3D场景重建。

Abstract: We present EdMCGS (Event-driven Markov chain Gaussian Splatting), an end-to-end method for reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from extreme-low-frame-rate RGB together with an event stream, which can then be rendered at any intermediate timestamp. Methods relying solely on RGB images generate numerous artifacts due to the lack of evidence from between consecutive frames. To supply this missing evidence, we model the scene motion as an event-driven Markov chain, in which the sparse RGB frames anchor the state at their own timestamps while the events recorded within an interval drive the transition across it. Since the transition reads the events of the current interval, it remains active at inference and produces the in-between motion of the 3D Gaussians directly from the events rather than by interpolation, which sets our method apart from prior work that uses events only as training-time supervision. The state is carried by a compact set of control points, each driven by the events sampled in the neighborhood of its own image projection, and a temporal local isometry term keeps the propagated motion locally rigid. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scenes show that EdMCGS outperforms both RGB-based and event-based baselines, while rendering in real time with far fewer Gaussians than the strongest event-based baseline. We release our source code and a new dataset at https://github.com/joseclipse/EdMCGS.

</details>


### [247] [VeriScene: Reconstructing Crime Scenes from Legal Evidence via World-Model Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08342)
*Kevin Chuanpu Fu,Yongsen Zheng,Zee Kin Yeong,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriScene智能代理系统，通过世界模型从法医照片和证人证言中重建犯罪现场，确保每条证据可追溯、运动符合物理规律，在25个犯罪场景基准测试中达到0.9014的证据覆盖率与0.7217的事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多模态证据直接输入世界模型用于法医场景存在严重缺陷：会静默丢弃证据、忽略矛盾的证词、产生违反证据记录的运动。需要一种可审计、可追溯的代理来编排世界模型，确保法医重建的可靠性与可信度。

Method: VeriScene采用迭代式证据融合策略：在审计循环下将证据融合为带引用的叙述，通过世界模型中的探针回滚验证假设动态并注入纠正约束，最终从融合关键帧渲染犯罪重演视频。支持对可靠性不同的证人证言进行区分处理。

Result: 在包含25个犯罪场景、139张法医风格照片和65条含不可靠性植入的证言的基准上，VeriScene在20个测试场景上达到0.9014证据覆盖率和0.7217事实一致性，相比端到端多模态LLM基线在事实一致性上提升20.35%，时间连贯性提升34.88%，且可跨4种LLM编排后端泛化，每场成本仅1.82美元。

Conclusion: VeriScene验证了通过代理编排世界模型进行法医级动态场景重建的可行性，其证据可追溯性和物理合理性优势显著，展示了在世界模型用于法医取证场景中兼顾成本效益与可靠性的实际应用价值。

Abstract: World models take multimodal inputs like text, photos, and diagrams to generate dynamic scenes in accordance with the laws of physics, thus opening a compelling application: fusing multimodal legal evidence to re-create a crime scene and re-enact how an offence could have been committed. However, feeding the raw, unorganized evidence into a world model fails in forensic use: it silently drops evidence, glosses over contradictory testimony, and produces motion that violates the evidentiary record. This paper presents VeriScene, an agent that orchestrates the world model: it reconstructs crime scenes from forensic photographs and witness statements of varying reliability, keeping every claim traceable to evidence and every motion physically plausible. VeriScene iteratively fuses the evidence into a cited narrative under an auditing loop, verifies the hypothesized dynamics via probe rollouts in the world model with corrective constraint injection, and renders the offence as a re-enactment video from a fused keyframe. On a benchmark of 25 crime scenarios across 7 physically-driven case types (139 forensic-style photographs and 65 statements with planted unreliability), VeriScene attains 0.9014 evidence coverage and 0.7217 factual consistency (0-1 scale) on the 20 test scenes, outperforming an end-to-end multimodal-LLM baseline by 20.35% in factual consistency and 34.88% in temporal coherence, while generalizing across four LLM orchestration backends at USD 1.82 per scene.

</details>


### [248] [CoVeR: Coverage-Based Token Pruning for Multi-View 3D Reasoning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08345)
*Nhat-Tan Bui,Varshini Elangovan,Arun Reddy Anugu,Sreyas Mohan,Wei Ye,Dilin Wang,JQ Huang,Rakesh Ranjan,Aviral Chharia,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVeR，一种确定性、无需训练的视觉token选择器，仅利用token坐标在3D多视图场景中实现空间覆盖最大化，在精确预算下保留场景各区域信息，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景表示为多视图图像可利用2D VLM的先验知识进行3D推理，但会产生大量冗余视觉token。现有两类剪枝方法各有局限：学习型重要性排序会产生近重复token且忽略大部分场景；体素化方法无法精确控制token预算且在多视图重叠时饱和。因此需要一种既保证空间覆盖又能精确控制预算的token选择方法。

Method: CoVeR是一种确定性、免训练的token选择器，完全基于token的空间坐标而非任何学习信号。其核心思想是选择能够共同覆盖场景所有区域的token集合，从而精确满足每场景预算、打破体素化的饱和瓶颈，并避免学习型重要性方法产生的近重复选择。

Result: 在三个3D推理基准上均超越现有SOTA方法，可跨4种VLM作为即插即用模块泛化。仅使用约8%的视觉token即可保持93.5%的完整token性能，在基准上平均超过现有最佳方法3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 空间覆盖与3D推理性能密切相关，CoVeR通过纯几何坐标驱动的确定性选择同时解决了学习型方法和体素化方法的局限，实现了高效且精确的3D多视图视觉token压缩。

Abstract: Representing a 3D scene as multi-view images allows 2D VLMs to reason in 3D by reusing priors from pre-training, sidestepping the scarcity of annotated 3D data. However, it produces thousands of redundant visual tokens whose cost grows with every view. Existing visual token pruners fall into two families, each limited in the 3D multi-view setting. Learned importance methods rank tokens by attention or encoder features; because redundancy here is fundamentally spatial, they keep near-duplicate tokens from a few prominent regions and leave most of the scene unrepresented. Voxelization methods improve spatial coverage but cannot enforce an exact token budget and saturate as multi-view observations overlap in 3D, capping retention well below the target. We show that spatial coverage is associated with 3D reasoning performance and introduce CoVeR, a deterministic, training-free selector that uses only token coordinates, with no learned signals. CoVeR selects tokens that collectively cover every region of the scene, and solves the limitations of both families: it enforces an exact per-scene budget, breaks the voxelization saturation plateau, and avoids the near-duplicate selections of learned importance. Extensive experiments show CoVeR outperforms prior SOTAs on all three 3D reasoning benchmarks and generalizes as a plug-and-play module tested across four VLMs. Notably, with only $\approx$8% of visual tokens, it preserves 93.5% of full-token performance, surpassing SOTA by 3.9 percentage points on average across benchmarks.

</details>


### [249] [Segment Any Motion with Radar: Robust Multimodal Moving-Object Segmentation and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08346)
*Jue Wang,Xuan Wang,Hao Zhou,Ruixiang Zhou,Yixuan Zhou,Tianshuo Yuan,Jieming Ma,Jie Zhang,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RGBTR-Motion基准数据集和SAM-Radar框架，利用雷达直接测量径向速度的优势，解决恶劣光照、天气、反射和遮挡下移动目标感知与跟踪问题。SAM-Radar基于SAM 3构建，融合RGB、热成像和雷达数据，通过雷达感知检测器和轨迹跟踪实现实例分割与身份保持，在多项指标上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动目标感知方法依赖外观、光流或轨迹估计，在弱光照、恶劣天气、反射和遮挡条件下会丢失证据信息。雷达能够直接测量径向速度而非从光度对应中推断，是天然的解决方案，但现有基准缺乏同时提供雷达测量、密集移动实例掩码和时序一致身份的数据集。

Method: 1) 构建RGBTR-Motion基准：同步校准的固定相机基准，同时提供RGB、热成像和雷达流与密集实例掩码及时序一致身份。2) 开发SAM-Radar框架：基于SAM 3构建，含雷达感知检测器（融合校准的RGBT特征与投影到图像位置的雷达回波）和运动监督（对投影回波进行前景分类以拒绝杂波，无需文本提示）；跟踪器将接受的雷达回波与个体轨迹关联，作为视觉退化目标仍存在的物理证据，可跨越短暂低可见度或遮挡期并重新连接再现目标到原有身份。

Result: SAM-Radar在IoU上达到0.7027，F1-50达到0.8090；在MOTA、HOTA和IDF1上分别比最强竞争方法提高0.2977、0.1603和0.2857。

Conclusion: 融合雷达测量可有效提升恶劣条件下运动目标的分割与跟踪性能，雷达作为物理证据能够弥补视觉信息退化时的证据缺失，维持目标身份的时序一致性。

Abstract: Moving-object perception must decide which image regions correspond to real motion and keep every instance identified over time. Methods that read motion from appearance, optical flow, or estimated trajectories lose that evidence under poor illumination, adverse weather, reflections, and occlusion. Radar is a natural remedy because it measures radial velocity directly instead of inferring it from photometric correspondence. However, existing benchmarks do not jointly provide radar measurements, dense moving-instance masks, and temporally consistent identities for surveillance. We therefore introduce RGBTR-Motion, a synchronized and calibrated fixed-camera benchmark that pairs RGB, thermal, and radar streams with dense instance masks and temporally consistent identities across diverse surveillance scenes. We also develop SAM-Radar, an RGB, thermal, and radar-based segmentation and tracking framework built on SAM 3. SAM-Radar's radar-aware detector fuses calibrated RGBT features with radar returns that are grounded at their projected image locations, and motion supervision, implemented as foreground classification of those projected returns, teaches the detector to reject clutter without any text prompt. The tracker associates accepted radar returns with individual trajectories and uses them as physical evidence that a visually degraded target remains present. This allows it to bridge short periods of low visibility or occlusion and reconnect a reappearing target to its existing identity instead of starting a new track. SAM-Radar attains 0.7027 IoU and 0.8090 F1-50, and raises MOTA, HOTA, and IDF1 by 0.2977, 0.1603, and 0.2857 over the strongest competing values.

</details>


### [250] [ReMoMask-2: Latent Retrieval-Augmented Masked Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08365)
*Yiran Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Ling Shao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMoMask和ReMoMask-2两个结构感知的检索增强文本到动作生成框架。ReMoMask通过分层双向动量对比学习、语义时空注意力和拓扑结构掩码解决粗粒度检索与融合问题；ReMoMask-2通过在生成器的预量化潜在空间中重建检索数据库并利用轻量投影器对齐文本查询，消除表示鸿沟。在HumanML3D、KIT-ML和SnapMoGen上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG-T2M模型存在两大挑战：(1)粗粒度检索和融合机制忽略了人体动作的层级化、时空拓扑结构；(2)检索证据位于与生成器潜在空间分离的语义空间中，存在表示鸿沟。

Method: ReMoMask包含三个核心组件：分层双向动量(HBM)对比学习对齐全局与局部特征；语义时空注意力(SSTA)实现拓扑感知融合；拓扑结构化掩码(TSM)通过自适应掩码强制鲁棒的局部接地。ReMoMask-2则直接在生成器预量化潜在空间中重建检索数据库，并利用蒸馏的轻量投影器对齐文本查询。

Result: 在HumanML3D、KIT-ML和SnapMoGen数据集上的大量实验表明：检索器达到SOTA精度；ReMoMask-2在KIT-ML和SnapMoGen上获得最低FID，且其单一掩码Transformer阶段超越ReMoMask完整两阶段流水线，推理速度最快。

Conclusion: 结构感知的检索与融合能显著提升文本到动作生成质量，而基于生成器潜在空间的检索数据库重建有效弥合了表示鸿沟，实现了更优的性能与更快的推理效率。

Abstract: Text-to-motion (T2M) generation maps natural language to human joint movements, aiding gaming, VR, and robotics. Retrieval-Augmented Text-to-Motion (RAG-T2M) improves generation on complex descriptions by conditioning on retrieved motion-text pairs. However, existing RAG-T2M models face two challenges: coarse-grained retrieval and fusion mechanisms overlook the hierarchical, spatial-temporal topology of human motion, and a representation gap exists because retrieved evidence resides in a semantic space separate from the generator's latents. To address the first, we present ReMoMask, a structure-aware RAG framework coupling Hierarchical Bidirectional Momentum (HBM) contrastive learning to align global and part-level features with text; Semantic Spatial-Temporal Attention (SSTA) for topology-aware fusion; and Topology Structured Masking (TSM) to force robust part-level grounding via adaptive masking. To address the second, we introduce ReMoMask-2, which rebuilds the retrieval database directly within the generator's pre-quantization latent space and aligns text queries via a distilled lightweight projector, allowing the generator to directly consume the retrieved motion's semantic content. Extensive experiments on HumanML3D, KIT-ML, and SnapMoGen demonstrate our retriever achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, while ReMoMask-2 attains the lowest FID on KIT-ML and SnapMoGen; notably, its single mask-transformer stage surpasses ReMoMask's full two-stage pipeline and delivers the fastest inference.

</details>


### [251] [To Adapt or Not to Adapt? Selective Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08367)
*Siru Jiang,Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性适应（SelecTIve Adaptation）问题，通过跨增强相似度（CAS）判断测试样本是否应跳过适应性调整，避免TTA中无效或有害的适应，可跳过约85%的适应过程同时保持或提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时适应（TTA）方法存在两种失效模式：适应效果可忽略不计（预测不变）以及适应产生负面影响（将原本正确的预测翻转错误）。因此需要识别并跳过此类适应。

Method: 提出跨增强相似度（CAS）作为基线方法：仅当测试样本在多个增强视图下的预测相似度较低时才执行适应，相似度高则跳过适应。

Result: CAS不仅保持准确率，在某些情况下还提升了整体准确率，即使跳过近85%的适应过程。

Conclusion: 选择性适应是一个有价值的新研究方向，CAS作为简单基线证明了跳过不必要适应是可行且有效的，作者希望后续研究能超越该基线。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a prominent strategy for adapting vision-language models to distribution shifts during inference. We conduct a per-sample analysis of model predictions before and after adaptation, and observe two failure modes in existing TTA methods that echo previous work. Adaptations are frequently negligible, yielding no change in the model's predictions, and more severely, they can be detrimental by flipping previously correct predictions to incorrect ones. This naturally raises a question: Can we identify and skip such negligible or harmful adaptations? In this work, we introduce a new problem of selective adaptation, which aims to determine whether a given test sample should undergo adaptation or be skipped. To this end, we propose Cross-Augmentation Similarity (CAS), a simple baseline that performs adaptation only when predictions across augmented views exhibit low similarity. Notably, CAS not only preserves but in some cases improves overall accuracy, even when skipping nearly 85% of the adaptation process. We hope other researchers will explore this new direction and surpass the performance of our baseline. Our code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/selective-adaptation.

</details>


### [252] [GALoc: Gravity Aligned Wireframes for Depth-Free Monocular Floorplan Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08385)
*Jeahn Han,Minji Kim,Jeongbin Sohn,Jonghyeok Park,Matthias Wuest,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GALoc提出了一种几何优先的室内定位框架，用重力对齐线框替代深度预测，通过构建线性约束矩阵进行全局搜索找到最优相机位姿，将校正后的线框投影为鸟瞰图并与平面图匹配，在梯度噪声和真实场景中达到或超越基于深度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度网络的室内定位方法在杂乱场景中表现脆弱，而平面图本身是紧凑且外观不变的理想定位地图。作者希望摆脱对深度预测的依赖，通过几何约束构建对杂乱场景更鲁棒的定位框架。

Method: GALoc采用几何优先策略：(1) 用满足垂直性和共面性约束的重力对齐线框替代深度预测；(2) 利用单目RGB、相机内参、相对位姿和IMU方向构建线性约束矩阵，通过全局搜索最小化最小奇异值来确定相机位姿；(3) 通过闭式FOV一致变换将校正线框投影为鸟瞰图布局；(4) 通过无度量SE(2)搜索与平面图匹配。

Result: 在Structured3D、Gibson和真实采集序列上评估，GALoc在可见足够墙体几何时达到或超越深度基线——在Gibson数据集上100步序列中0.1m精度的顺序定位成功率88%，而基线仅68%。在结构盲场景中GALoc会主动放弃定位。

Conclusion: GALoc证明了利用几何先验（垂直性、共面性）即可以替代深度网络实现鲁棒的室内定位，尤其在结构可见的场景中表现优异，并且能够通过主动弃权机制避免在结构不可见的场景中产生错误定位。

Abstract: Floorplans are compact, appearance-invariant maps ideal for indoor localization, yet existing methods rely on depth networks that are brittle in cluttered scenes. We propose GALoc, a geometry-first framework that replaces depth prediction with gravity-aligned wireframes that satisfy verticality and coplanarity by construction. Given monocular RGB, camera intrinsics, relative poses, and IMU orientation, GALoc constructs a linear constraint matrix encoding verticality and coplanarity, and finds the camera gauge minimizing its smallest singular value via global search. The rectified wireframes are projected into bird's-eye-view layouts through a closed-form, FOV-consistent transformation and matched against the floorplan via metric-free SE(2) search. We evaluate end-to-end on Structured3D, with calibrated noise on Gibson, and on real-world author-collected sequences. When sufficient wall geometry is visible, GALoc matches or outperforms depth-based baselines -- achieving 88% sequential localization success at 0.1m over 100-step sequences on Gibson vs the baseline's 68% -- while abstaining in structure-blind scenes.

</details>


### [253] [From Coordinates to Candidate Regions: Temporal Change Localization via Region Selection in Remote Sensing Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08391)
*Juwan Chung,Sungjune Park,Yeongyun Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向遥感场景的区域选择范式（RS-RegionSelect），将自然图像MLLM中的区域选择方法扩展到遥感领域，并延伸至多时相变化定位任务。该方法通过文本条件化的区域提议模块生成候选区域，将其编码为携带空间和时间线索的特殊标记，使LLM通过选择区域标记来完成目标定位，显著优于传统的坐标生成基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感多模态大语言模型在场景理解和视觉问答方面已有进展，但在定位小尺寸、密集排列的地物目标或变化区域方面仍具挑战。现有方法依赖将边界框坐标生成为token序列，这对遥感中常见的小目标、密集目标较为脆弱，且当需要同时定位多个目标时错误率更高。因此亟需一种更适合遥感场景的定位范式。

Method: 提出RS特定的区域选择范式，核心包括：(1) 文本条件化的区域提议模块生成候选区域；(2) 将每个候选区域编码为特殊token，携带逐帧视觉特征并融合空间和时间线索；(3) LLM通过在其回复中选择区域token来完成目标定位。同时构建了涵盖单图像和多时相设置下的多任务训练与评估套件（包括定位、指代表达、视觉接地和理解任务）。

Result: 实验表明该方法在时间变化定位任务上大幅超越坐标生成基线，在单图像视觉接地任务上也有提升，同时保持了有竞争力的理解性能。Oracle分析还分别解构了区域提议器和LLM选择器的贡献，提供了该框架独有的诊断性洞察。

Conclusion: 区域选择范式相比坐标生成方法在遥感目标定位（尤其是时间变化定位）上具有显著优势，代码已开源，为遥感MLLM定位任务提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS-MLLMs) have advanced scene understanding and visual question answering over satellite imagery, yet localizing specific objects or changed regions remains challenging. Existing approaches rely on generating bounding box coordinates as token sequences, which is fragile for the small, densely packed objects common in remote sensing and increasingly error-prone when multiple targets must be localized simultaneously. In this work, we present an RS-specific formulation of the region selection paradigm, previously explored in natural-image MLLMs, and extend it to temporal change localization over multi-image sequences. Our framework employs a text-conditioned region proposal module, encodes each candidate as special tokens carrying per-frame visual features enriched with spatial and temporal cues, and lets the LLM localize targets by selecting region tokens in its response. We construct a multi-task training and evaluation suite spanning localization, referring expression, visual grounding, and understanding tasks across single-image and multi-temporal settings. Experiments show that our approach substantially outperforms coordinate-generation baselines on temporal change localization, while improving single-image visual grounding and maintaining competitive understanding performance. Oracle analysis decomposes the contributions of the region proposer and the LLM selector, providing diagnostic insight unique to this framework. Our code will be available at https://github.com/juwan-kr/RS-RegionSelect.

</details>


### [254] [Towards Embodied Air-Ground Cooperative Object Search: Benchmark, Dataset and Agentic Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08402)
*Boao Yu,Zimo Chen,Junreng Rao,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Yong Zhao,Rusheng Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGOS-Bench，首个用于评估通用视觉语言模型(VLM)在城市环境中通过无人机(UAV)与地面车辆(UGV)协同完成空中-地面目标搜索(AGOS)任务的基准，并配套提供了包含7.7k个跨越三个难度等级episode的AGOS-Dataset。作者同时提出AGOS-Agent，一种无需训练的工具增强方法，通过"搜索-交接-验证"协作协议来缓解VLM在复杂动态协调上的负担。实验表明，在九个VLM骨干上，AGOS-Agent在八个模型上提升了成功率，且在所有九个模型上减少了决策步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中的空中-地面目标搜索(AGOS)是一项具有挑战性的具身任务，需要UAV和UGV从多视角视觉参考中联合搜索并验证指定目标车辆。然而这一领域此前缺乏系统性研究，没有专门的基准来评估VLM在此类多智能体协同场景中的能力。

Method: 作者构建了AGOS-Bench基准框架和由自动管线生成的AGOS-Dataset（7.7k episode，覆盖多种类目与属性，分三个难度级别），并提出AGOS-Agent——一种无需训练、工具增强的智能体方法。该方法通过"搜索-交接-验证"的刻意协作协议，仅要求VLM负责场景理解与决策，从而降低其对复杂动态协同的依赖。

Result: 在九个VLM上的广泛实验显示：AGOS-Agent在九个评估骨干中的八个上提高了整体成功率，并在所有九个模型上减少了决策步骤。在困难子集上，Gemini-3.6-Flash的成功率从8.6%提升至55.7%，SPL从7.6%提升至44.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了AGOS任务缺乏专用基准的空白，验证了通过精心设计的协作协议配合工具增强，VLM能够在无需额外训练的情况下有效完成UAV-UGV协同的目标搜索与验证任务，为该领域后续研究提供了基准、数据集和方法论基础。

Abstract: Air-Ground Object Search (AGOS) in urban environments is a challenging embodied task, which requires an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) and an Unmanned Ground Vehicle (UGV) to jointly search for and verify a specified target vehicle from multi-view visual references. To study this underexplored problem, we introduce AGOS-Bench, the first dedicated benchmark for evaluating whether general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can integrate aerial discoveries and ground-level verification through UAV-UGV cooperation. We further provide AGOS-Dataset as the companion resource of exemplary trajectories constructed by an automatic pipeline. It consists of 7.7k episodes for searching objects of diverse categories and attributes, spanning three difficulty levels. To address the AGOS task, we propose AGOS-Agent, a training-free and tool-augmented approach. The agentic method relieves VLMs from complex and dynamic coordination via a deliberate search-handoff-verify cooperation protocol, only demanding VLMs for scene understanding and decision-making. Extensive experiments on nine VLMs show that AGOS-Agent improves overall success rate for eight of the nine evaluated backbones while reducing decision steps for all nine. On the hard split, the SR and SPL of Gemini-3.6-Flash increase from 8.6% to 55.7% and from 7.6% to 44.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [255] [CAR-MIL: Counterfactual Attention Regularization for Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08419)
*Imane Chraki,Pierre Marza,Stergios Christodoulidis,Maria Vakalopoulou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAR-MIL框架，通过反事实注意力正则化目标来引导注意力学习，提高多实例学习（MIL）中注意力权重的可靠性和可解释性，在合成基准和五个数字病理数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL方法通过注意力机制聚合实例特征，但注意力权重并不总能真实反映实例的重要性，可能关注到虚假相关的区域，导致注意力不可靠。

Method: 在标准基于注意力的MIL架构上，引入一个轻量级反事实注意力分支，该分支产生替代预测的同时保持与事实注意力分布接近，从而鼓励预测变化源于最小化、结构化的注意力重分布。最终得到的事实和反事实注意力图捕捉互补证据：事实图突出支持预测的区域，反事实图揭示需要重新加权才能挑战预测的区域。

Result: 在合成MIL基准和五个数字病理数据集（涵盖四个任务）上评估，CAR-MIL保持竞争性的分类性能，在更具挑战性的任务上取得最大提升，同时改善了注意力可靠性。

Conclusion: 将反事实可解释性推理整合到注意力学习中是有益的，CAR-MIL能有效提升MIL注意力分配的可靠性并保持分类性能。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) is widely used for weakly supervised learning, particularly in digital pathology, where fine-grained annotations are costly. Most MIL methods aggregate instance features via attention mechanisms. However, attention weights do not always faithfully reflect instance importance and may focus on spuriously correlated regions. In this work, we propose CAR-MIL, a framework that explicitly guides attention learning through a counterfactual attention regularization objective inspired by counterfactual explanations. Built on a standard attention-based MIL architecture, our approach introduces a lightweight counterfactual attention branch trained to produce an alternative prediction while remaining close to the factual attention distribution. This encourages prediction changes to arise from minimal, structured redistributions of attention, leading to more informative evidence allocation. The resulting factual and counterfactual attention maps capture complementary evidence: the former highlights regions supporting the prediction, while the latter reveals regions whose reweighting would challenge it. We evaluate our method on synthetic MIL benchmarks with instance-level ground truth enabling controlled analysis of attention behavior and on five digital pathology datasets across four tasks. CAR-MIL maintains competitive classification performance, with the largest gains observed on more challenging tasks, while improving attention reliability, demonstrating the benefits of integrating counterfactual explainability reasoning into attention learning. Code is available at: https://github.com/ImaneCR/CAR-MIL/.

</details>


### [256] [AirAnchor: Bridging Local and Global Spatial Information for Zero-Shot Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08442)
*Shanwei Fan,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu,Yingxuan Teng,Siqi Dai,Lin Cheng,Guoliang Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AirAnchor是一种面向航空视觉语言导航（AerialVLN）的新范式，通过空间锚点连接局部和全局空间信息并将其整合到统一的导航框架中，在两个方面的零样本方法中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本航空视觉语言导航方法通常只在单一空间尺度上操作，要么依赖在线构建的局部表示，要么依赖离线建立的全局记忆，缺乏对局部和全局空间信息的综合理解，限制了导航决策的准确性。

Method: AirAnchor包含三个核心组件：(1) 查询驱动的空间锚点定位——从视觉观测中识别决策相关的锚点并组织成局部空间表示；(2) 持久对象空间记忆——增量维护对象知识库作为全局空间记忆并检索地标相关的空间先验；(3) 空间感知导航代理——将局部和全局空间信息显式整合到智能代理框架中进行决策。

Result: 在AerialVLN数据集上的大量实验表明，AirAnchor在性能上大幅超越现有零样本基线方法，验证了该范式的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 通过空间锚点桥接局部与全局空间信息是实现全面空间感知导航的关键，AirAnchor提出的统一框架为航空视觉语言导航的零样本决策提供了一种有效且高效的新思路。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation requires drones to follow natural-language instructions and navigate through complex urban environments. Accurate navigation relies on both local and global spatial information, which support immediate action grounding and long-horizon path planning, respectively. However, existing zero-shot methods typically operate at a single spatial scale, relying either on local representations constructed online from current observations or on global memories built offline from historical experience. To address this limitation, we propose AirAnchor, a new paradigm that bridges local and global spatial information through spatial anchors and integrates both into a shared navigation framework, enabling comprehensive spatial grounding for decision-making. AirAnchor consists of three core components: (1) Query-Driven Spatial Anchor Grounding, which identifies decision-relevant anchors from visual observations and organizes them into local spatial representations; (2) Persistent Object Spatial Memory, which incrementally maintains an object knowledge base as persistent global spatial memory and retrieves landmark-related spatial priors; and (3) a Spatially-Informed Navigation Agent, which explicitly integrates both local and global spatial information into an agentic framework for decision-making. Extensive experiments on AerialVLN demonstrate that AirAnchor substantially outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, validating the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed paradigm.

</details>


### [257] [GSComplete: Gaussian Splat Completion with 2D Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08449)
*Elias Brugger,Philipp Erler,Stefan Ohrhallinger,Paul Guerrero*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSComplete提出了一种基于分数蒸馏采样(Score Distillation Sampling)与新颖保存损失相结合的高斯溅射物体补全方法，仅利用2D扩散先验即可补全缺失区域，同时完整保留原始溅射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯溅射在实际中常因输入数据不完整而存在缺失区域，现有补全方法要么无法保留原始溅射，要么需要稀缺的3D训练数据。

Method: 结合基于Score Distillation Sampling的3D生成，并引入新颖的保存损失，鼓励在原本应可见的区域保留原始溅射，仅在缺失区域生成新溅射，不会遮挡输入。

Result: 引入部分高斯溅射物体新数据集，实验表明GSComplete在输入保留准确性上显著优于现有方法，且补全结果具有相当的合理性。

Conclusion: GSComplete仅使用2D扩散先验即可高效完成高斯溅射补全，同时完全保留现有溅射，解决了数据依赖与原始信息丢失的问题。

Abstract: Gaussian splats provide a fast, high-fidelity representation for 3D objects but are often constructed from incomplete input data in practice, leaving missing regions. Existing completion methods either do not preserve the original splats or require scarcely available 3D training data. We propose GSComplete, which combines 3D generation based on Score Distillation Sampling with a novel preservation loss that encourages the original splats to be preserved where they should be visible. This effectively completes the Gaussian splat object using only 2D diffusion priors while fully preserving existing splats and generating new splats only in missing regions, without occluding the input. To evaluate our approach, we introduce a new dataset of partial Gaussian splat objects and show that GSComplete achieves significantly more accurate preservation of the input than existing methods with comparable plausibility of the completed result. Our code and dataset will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [258] [SignRefine: Adapting Foundational Video Models for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08496)
*Anton Pelykh,Edward Fish,Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignRefine是一种手语视频生成模型，通过局部适配器和空间接地技术从2D关键点条件生成清晰可懂的手语视频，配合新构建的NVSign大型手语数据集，在手部姿态精度上较最强基线提升30%，超过80%的对比中更受手语用户青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散模型主要基于口语视频训练，生成手语视频时在手部和面部关节表达上存在伪影，导致手语内容难以理解。作者旨在解决这一关键缺陷。

Method: 基于预训练的视频扩散Transformer，引入带空间接地（spatial grounding）的局部适配器，选择性精炼手部和面部区域的生成；同时构建了NVSign大规模原生手语视频数据集用于训练。

Result: 手部姿态精度指标较最强基线提升最多30%；在视觉质量与可理解性方面，超过80%的对比中更受手语用户偏好。

Conclusion: SignRefine通过局部精细优化和适配器设计，能够仅从2D关键点条件生成可理解的手语视频，在不同外观和视觉条件下均具有良好泛化能力，为手语视频生成研究提供了新方法和大规模数据集支撑。

Abstract: Sign language video generation demands precise hand and facial articulation, yet modern video diffusion models, trained predominantly on spoken-language video, produce artifacts that render signing unintelligible. We propose SignRefine, a sign language video generation model that produces comprehensible signing from 2D keypoint conditioning alone, generalizing across appearances and visual conditions. Our approach builds on a pretrained video diffusion transformer and introduces local adapters with spatial grounding to selectively refine hand and face regions, steering the strong base model's prior toward accurate articulation. To enable this work and support broader sign language research, we present NVSign, a large-scale dataset of video content natively produced in sign language, offering diverse signer appearances, environments, and natural conversational settings. Trained on this data, our model shows up to 30% improvement in hand pose precision metrics over the strongest baseline and is preferred by sign language users for visual quality and comprehensibility in more than 80% of comparisons.

</details>


### [259] [Temporal State Transport in Video Generation: Diagnosing and Correcting Spectral Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08505)
*Luyao Tang,Bingjun Luo,Dong Yi,Jialin Guo,Haoning Xi,Cheng Chen,Yizhou Yu,Chaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从"时间状态传输"的新视角研究视频生成中的时序一致性问题，提出光谱张力（Spectral Tension）诊断指标识别时间交互的"碎片化传输"与"过度混合热点"两种失效模式，并据此设计训练无关的正则化器，在不微调的情况下提升视频生成的时序一致性与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的视频生成不仅需要高质量帧，还需要跨时间的连贯叙事（保持身份、场景布局、运动等视觉属性的持久状态）。现有免训练方法主要关注增强跨帧注意力或分析局部注意力熵，但这些视角无法揭示时间交互是否处于健康的传输状态。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) Spectral Tension——一种有符号的诊断指标，比较局部注意力扩散度与全局光谱多样性；(2) Spectral Transport Homeostasis——基于该诊断的训练无关调节器，对病态时间状态进行软性校正，同时尽可能保留平衡状态。

Result: 在预训练视频生成模型上的实验表明，原始模型常处于不平衡的时间状态域，而该方法能对最差的时间热点施加较大校正，在不微调的情况下提升时序一致性和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过光谱张力的诊断框架与光谱传输稳态调节器，成功识别并纠正了视频生成中隐含的时间状态病变，为免训练的视频时序一致性改进提供了新的诊断-校正方法论。

Abstract: Reliable video generation requires more than high-quality frames to form a coherent story: a model must maintain a persistent state, transporting visual attributes such as identity, scene layout, motion, and fine details across time. Existing training-free methods mainly strengthen cross-frame attention or analyze local attention entropy, but these views do not reveal whether temporal interactions stay in a healthy transport regime. In this work, we study video generation through the perspective of Temporal State Transport. We introduce Spectral Tension, a signed diagnostic that compares local attention diffuseness with global spectral diversity, and use it to identify two opposite temporal failures: fragmented transport and over-mixing hotspots. Based on this diagnosis, we propose Spectral Transport Homeostasis, a training-free regulator that softly corrects pathological temporal states while largely preserving balanced ones. Experiments on pretrained video generation models show that the original model often occupies imbalanced temporal regimes, whereas our method selectively applies larger corrections to the worst temporal hotspots and improves temporal consistency and visual quality without finetuning. Code: https://github.com/lytang63/temporal-state-transport

</details>


### [260] [Layer Selection in VLMs for Zero-Shot OOD Detection via Multi-Resolution Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08524)
*Shyam Nandan Rai,Francesco Di Salvo,Sebastian Doerrich,Christian Ledig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多分辨率熵估计的中间层选择策略，用于医学影像中的零样本分布外（OOD）检测。研究发现VLM的中间层能提供互补的OOD信号，且最优深度因影像模态而异，而现有的单分辨率熵估计对分箱选择高度敏感（AUROC波动可达19.3%）。所提方法通过聚合多种离散化尺度的直方图统计，实现了稳健的中间层选择，在MIDOG和OASIS两个医学OOD基准上一致优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的医学OOD检测方法几乎只使用最终层嵌入，隐含假设最深表示普遍最优，但这一假设在医学影像中并不成立——中间层提供互补的OOD信号，且最优表示深度因影像模态而异。同时，已有的基于熵最小化的层选择方法受限于单分辨率熵估计的数值不稳定性，亟需更稳健的解决方案。

Method: 提出多分辨率熵估计策略，在多个离散化尺度上聚合直方图统计，替代对分箱选择敏感的单分辨率熵估计，用于稳健地选择最优中间层组合进行零样本OOD检测。

Result: 在涵盖不同影像模态、不同偏移类型和不同VLM骨干网络的MIDOG和OASIS两个医学OOD基准上，该方法一致优于现有最先进方法，同时保持轻量级和稳定性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示医学影像中VLM中间层嵌入对OOD检测的重要价值，并证明多分辨率熵估计能有效克服单分辨率方法的敏感性缺陷，为医学AI的安全部署提供了轻量且稳定的零样本OOD检测解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for safe deployment of medical AI systems, where domain shifts arise across institutions, acquisition protocols, and patient populations. VLMs enable zero-shot OOD detection by embedding images into a language-aligned latent space, where cross-modal similarity serves as a non-parametric confidence signal for identifying in-distribution samples. Yet existing methods rely almost exclusively on final-layer embeddings, implicitly assuming that the deepest representations are universally optimal. We first show that this assumption does not hold in medical imaging: intermediate layers provide complementary OOD signals, and the optimal representational depth depends on the respective image modality. While prior work selects layer combinations via entropy minimization of normalized histograms, we demonstrate that single-resolution entropy estimation is highly sensitive to binning choices, leading to performance variations of up to 19.3% AUROC. To address this instability, we propose a multi-resolution entropy estimation strategy that aggregates histogram statistics across multiple discretization scales, enabling robust and stable intermediate-layer selection. Across two medical OOD benchmarks, namely MIDOG and OASIS, covering distinct imaging modalities, diverse shift types, and different VLM backbones, our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, offering a lightweight and stable solution for zero-shot OOD detection.

</details>


### [261] [STSG-VQA: Evidence-Grounded Temporal Question Answering from Surgical Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08543)
*Jing Li,Duygu Sarikaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STSG-VQA基准，通过多层级时空场景图（STSGs）监督增强手术视觉语言模型的时间推理能力，在Qwen3-VL-4B和Hulu-Med-4B上分别比零样本基线提升24.39和19.56个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉语言模型主要依赖帧级场景图进行监督，缺乏对手术工作流动态过程的显式建模，导致时间推理能力受限。

Method: 提出多层级结构化时间监督方法，在帧级手术场景图基础上增强对象级连续性、事件级交互连续性和程序级连通性，构建时空场景图（STSG）；通过执行时间查询生成带证据溯源的问答对，形成STSG-VQA基准，共18,458个问答对覆盖七类时间类别。

Result: STSG-VQA包含18,458个问答对；微调Qwen3-VL-4B和Hulu-Med-4B后，问题级微准确率比零样本基线分别提升24.39和19.56个百分点，比静态场景图监督分别提升16.50和14.25个百分点，提升覆盖所有时间类别。

Conclusion: STSG衍生的监督信号能有效帮助手术VLM对时间上相互关联的交互进行推理，而非仅依赖孤立的帧，表明结构化时空监督是提升手术场景时间理解能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in surgical vision-language models (VLMs), temporal reasoning remains limited because existing supervision is largely frame-centric. Frame-level scene graphs (SGs) have proven effective in providing structured representations of surgical environments but do not explicitly model the dynamics of surgical workflows. To explicitly model how surgical states evolve across time, we introduce a multi-level structured temporal supervision methodology that augments frame-level surgical SGs with object-level continuity, event-level interaction continuity, and procedure-level connectivity. We then execute temporal queries over the resulting spatio-temporal scene graphs (STSGs) to generate evidence-grounded question-answer pairs, which together form the STSG-VQA benchmark. Each question is linked to the temporal interval and STSG evidence used to derive its reference answer, enabling traceable verification. The benchmark contains 18,458 question-answer pairs across seven temporal categories. Fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-4B and Hulu-Med-4B with STSG-derived supervision improves question-level micro accuracy by 24.39 and 19.56 percentage points over their zero-shot baselines and by 16.50 and 14.25 points over static scene-graph supervision, respectively. These gains span all temporal categories, indicating that STSG-derived supervision helps surgical VLMs reason over temporally grounded interactions rather than isolated frames. The code and dataset will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [262] [Effects of model architecture and learning strategies on deep learning-based recognition of activated sludge microscopic images and comparison with quantitative image analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08570)
*Suguru Hakoshima,Tomohiro Tobino,Fumiyuki Nakajima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了深度学习在活性污泥显微图像分类中的表现，发现Transformer架构与其他预训练策略优于传统CNN与ImageNet预训练；过小图像会降低精度但过大无益；保持视野而非分辨率的降采样策略更有效；深度学习在精度上优于传统定量图像分析（QIA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性污泥显微图像分析中，以往研究多依赖CNN和有监督ImageNet预训练，鲜有探索Transformer或自监督基础模型；图像降采样的影响及与QIA的定量对比也缺乏系统研究。

Method: 制备三类活性污泥样本并进行显微图像分类，系统比较不同模型架构（含Transformer）与预训练策略的性能，分析不同降采样策略（保持视野vs保持分辨率）的影响，并与传统定量图像分析（QIA）进行精度对比。

Result: Transformer架构及替代预训练方法在分类精度上表现更优；过小的图像会降低精度，但超过一定大小后继续增大图像无益；保持视野比保持分辨率更有利于实现高分类精度；深度学习在精度上优于QIA。

Conclusion: 深度学习（尤其是Transformer架构与适当的预训练及降采样策略）可有效提升活性污泥显微图像分类精度，且确实优于传统QIA方法，为活性污泥监测提供了更优的技术路径。

Abstract: Microscopic image analysis has long been recognized as a promising approach for monitoring activated sludge. In recent years, deep learning-based image analysis has been increasingly adopted in this field because of its high performance. However, previous studies on microscopic image analysis of activated sludge have rarely explored transformer-based models or self-supervised foundation models and have instead relied on CNNs and supervised ImageNet pretraining. In addition, previous studies often downsampled image sizes, but the effects of downsampling have not been sufficiently investigated, and the relationship between downsampling strategies and image analysis performance remains unclear. Furthermore, no study has quantitatively compared deep learning performance with quantitative image analysis (QIA), which was widely used before the emergence of deep learning. In this study, to examine how model architecture and learning strategies affect performance in microscopic image analysis of activated sludge and to quantitatively determine whether deep learning outperforms QIA, we prepared three types of activated sludge samples, classified their microscopic images, and evaluated classification accuracy. Our results showed that transformer-based architectures and alternative pretraining methods were effective in terms of classification accuracy. Our downsampling analysis showed that using overly small images reduced accuracy, but increasing image size beyond a certain point did not improve it further. In addition, the analysis indicated that, to achieve high classification accuracy, maintaining the field of view was a more effective downsampling strategy than maintaining resolution. Finally, our comparison between deep learning and QIA showed that deep learning outperformed QIA in terms of accuracy.

</details>


### [263] [GOLF: Global Observation with Local Focus for Calibration-Aware Stereo Interaction Field Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08607)
*Minqiang Zou,Riqiang Jin,Zhi Lv,Dong Luo,Lianghai Tian,Zhenyu Zhao,Qi Xu,Tong Wu,Mochen Yu,Yao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOLF是SHOW3D交互场估计挑战赛的第一名解决方案。该方法利用同步的第一人称立体视图，预测21个手部关节到被操作物体最近点的3D向量。系统结合了密集全局上下文、局部采样的手/物体证据以及共同帧Plücker射线的几何信息，基于DINOv3 ViT-H+/16骨干网络，采用LoRA和可训练的LayerNorm参数进行联合解码。主模型在隐藏测试集上达到27.61的官方分数（平均ADE为27.96mm），等权集成互补的微调变体后进一步提升至27.47的官方分数（平均ADE为27.82mm），最终获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手-物交互场估计是理解人手与物体交互的关键问题，对VR/AR、机器人抓取等领域具有重要意义。该挑战利用第一人称（egocentric）立体视觉来预测每个手关节指向被操作物体的3D向量，需要有效融合全局场景上下文与局部交互证据。

Method: GOLF方法由三部分组成：（1）使用DINOv3 ViT-H+/16作为骨干网络，采用LoRA轻量微调和可训练的LayerNorm参数，以提取密集的全局上下文特征；（2）基于预测的3D手部信息，在局部对每个关节采样手/物体交互证据；（3）利用共同帧（common-frame）的Plücker射线几何对ego中心立体输入进行编码，使网络能利用射线几何约束。两类交互场通过统一的解码头进行联合预测。

Result: 主模型在SHOW3D挑战赛隐藏测试集上达到27.61的官方分数和27.96mm的平均ADE；与一个互补的直接微调变体进行等权集成后，官方分数提升至27.47，平均ADE降至27.82mm，最终获得比赛第一名。

Conclusion: GOLF验证了全局视觉上下文、局部手/物证据与几何约束相结合的架构在交互场估计任务上的有效性，并展示了不同训练策略的模型集成可带来稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: We present GOLF, the first-place solution to the SHOW3D Interaction Field Estimation Challenge at HANDS@ECCV 2026. Given synchronized egocentric stereo views, the task is to predict a 3D vector from each of 21 hand joints to the closest point on the manipulated object. GOLF combines dense global context, locally sampled hand/object evidence, and common-frame Plücker-ray geometry. We adapt DINOv3 ViT-H+/16 with LoRA and trainable LayerNorm parameters, then jointly decode both interaction fields. Our primary model achieves an official score of 27.61 and a mean ADE of 27.96 mm on the hidden test set. An equal-weight ensemble with a complementary directly fine-tuned variant improves these results to an official score of 27.47 and a mean ADE of 27.82 mm, securing first place.

</details>


### [264] [SynthRCT: Scalable Conditional Deformation Synthesis for Synthetic Repeat CT Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08627)
*Tomas Guija-Valiente,Blanca Rodriguez-Gonzalez,Norberto Malpica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynthRCT，一个基于条件变分自编码器的可扩展条件生成框架，用于合成3D解剖变形，通过学习潜在变形空间并在给定输入解剖条件下解码为局部静态速度场，再组装成完整的全容积变换，从而在质子治疗中生成患者特异性、更真实的解剖变化场景以改进鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子治疗计划通常在单一计划CT上优化，因此解剖变化下的鲁棒性评估至关重要。然而现有场景常依赖简化的扰动，无法捕捉复杂且患者特异的变异，需要更真实的解剖变形合成方法。

Method: 基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）学习潜在变形空间；将潜变量解码为以输入解剖为条件的局部静态速度场（SVF）；将局部场组装为连贯的全容积变换，实现对大规模视野CT数据的内存可扩展生成；在呼吸4DCT数据上（每位受试者含多个呼吸相位解剖）进行验证。

Result: SynthRCT能够在超出预定义鲁棒性场景之外，实现患者特异的、合理的解剖变换采样；在呼吸多相位4DCT数据上成功验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SynthRCT提供了一种可扩展且患者特异的解剖变形合成框架，有望替代简化的扰动场景，提升质子治疗鲁棒性评估的真实性与准确性。代码已开源。

Abstract: In proton therapy, plans are typically optimized on a single planning CT, making robustness evaluation essential under anatomical changes. However, current scenarios often rely on simplified perturbations that poorly capture complex, patient-specific variability. We propose SynthRCT, a scalable conditional generative framework for 3D anatomical deformation synthesis. Based on a conditional variational autoencoder, SynthRCT learns a latent deformation space and decodes sampled latent codes into local stationary velocity fields conditioned on an input anatomy. Local fields are assembled into coherent full-volume transformations, enabling memory-scalable generation for large field-of-view CT data. We validate the approach on respiratory 4DCT data with multiple breathing-phase anatomies per subject. SynthRCT enables patient-specific sampling of plausible anatomical transformations beyond predefined robustness scenarios. Code available at: https://github.com/TomasGuija/SynthRCT.

</details>


### [265] [From Where to How: Continuous 4D Interaction Forecasting from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08636)
*Qiaohui Chu,Haoyu Zhang,Meng Liu,Haoxiang Shi,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Coherent4D大规模第一人称4D交互预测数据集（约23.3万个样本，覆盖三个领域），以及HIGFlow框架，通过"where-to-how"级联方式将未来3D交互位置预测与全身姿态预测统一在共享坐标系中，实现连续空间的位置预测与兼具多样性和结构一致性的姿态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将语义理解转化为精确的连续3D定位，且姿态预测在运动多样性与结构一致性之间难以平衡；更根本的问题是交互位置和身体运动通常被分开建模，缺乏连续的几何与时间对应关系。

Method: 提出HIGFlow（Hand Interaction Guided Residual Flow）框架，将预测建模为级联的"where-to-how"过程：首先结合语义接地与短时视觉动态预测连续的未来交互位置，然后用预测的位置序列条件化一个确定性运动锚点并配以残差Flow Matching，实现多样化且结构一致的全身运动预测。

Result: 在三个领域上的大量实验表明，HIGFlow在位置预测和姿态预测方面均优于代表性基线方法，消融实验验证了各提出组件的有效性。

Conclusion: Coherent4D数据集和HIGFlow框架有效地填补了交互位置与身体运动之间连续几何-时间对应关系建模的空白，为第一人称4D交互预测提供了新基准和可靠方法。

Abstract: Egocentric 4D interaction forecasting aims to anticipate both where future interactions will occur in 3D and how the human body will move to realize them, providing an important capability for assistive robotics and human-computer interaction. Existing methods struggle to translate semantic understanding into precise continuous 3D localization and to balance motion diversity with structural consistency in pose forecasting. More fundamentally, these tasks are often modeled separately, leaving the continuous geometric and temporal correspondence between interaction locations and body motion insufficiently captured. To address these challenges, we introduce Coherent4D, a large-scale egocentric dataset for continuous 4D interaction forecasting, comprising approximately 233K samples across three domains. Each sample pairs a sequence of future 3D interaction locations with corresponding full-body poses, aligned in time and expressed in a shared coordinate system. We also provide evaluation metrics in continuous space. Building on this formulation, we propose HIGFlow, a Hand Interaction Guided Residual Flow framework that models forecasting as a cascaded where-to-how process. HIGFlow first forecasts continuous future interaction locations by combining semantic grounding with short-horizon visual dynamics, and then uses the predicted location sequence to condition a deterministic motion anchor and residual Flow Matching for diverse yet structurally consistent full-body motion forecasting. Extensive experiments across all three domains demonstrate consistent improvements over representative baselines on both location and pose forecasting, while ablations validate the contributions of the proposed components. The project page is available at https://corrineqiu.github.io/from-where-to-how/.

</details>


### [266] [Charts Are Beyond Pixels: Probing for Layer-Wise Chart Understanding and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08657)
*Xiaochuan Zhong,Yifan Hou,Chenxi Pang,Shaobo Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerWiseBench基准，专注于图表的图层级理解与编辑能力评测。基于可执行图表程序生成2,800个源图表，派生出7,329个理解问题和53,791个编辑变体，从图层归因、图层绑定和可见性排序三个概念维度评估视觉语言模型与图像编辑器的图层级行为。结果显示模型在图层归因和绑定上表现良好，但在可见性排序上存在明显短板，编辑任务中可见性约束编辑的mIoU最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表基准主要评估最终输出的正确性或保真度，没有直接评估模型在图层级别的理解与操控能力。图表本质上是结构化视觉组合，其元素具有不同的功能角色、语义对应关系和可见性关系，因此需要一种能够直接评估模型图层级行为的基准。

Method: 基于可执行图表程序生成LayerWiseBench，将渲染图表与空间对齐的逐层RGBA资源及构造派生的标注配对。围绕图层归因、图层绑定和可见性排序三个核心概念组织理解与编辑评估，从中派生出受控的理解问题、编辑目标、参考图像和评估区域，构建包含2,800个源图表、14种图表范式的基准。

Result: Qwen3.5-27B在QA宏平均中最高，图层归因准确率93.04%，图层绑定97.46%，但可见性排序仅61.46%。四个图像编辑器的整体mIoU从1.49%到4.93%不等，可见性约束编辑的mIoU在所有编辑器中最低（0.37%-2.00%）。

Conclusion: 重叠组件间前后关系的任务（可见性排序）是理解和编辑任务中反复出现的挑战性环节，表明需要更显式地建模组件身份与可见性关系。

Abstract: Charts are structured visual compositions whose elements have distinct functional roles, semantic correspondences, and visibility relations. This structural view motivates evaluating whether models can understand and manipulate charts at the layer level. Existing chart benchmarks, however, primarily assess the correctness or fidelity of final outputs and do not directly evaluate these layer-wise behaviors. We present LayerWiseBench, a benchmark organized around three core concepts, layer attribution, layer binding, and visibility ordering, that structure its chart-understanding and chart-editing evaluations. Generated from executable chart programs, LayerWiseBench pairs each rendered chart with spatially aligned per-layer RGBA assets and construction-derived labels for functional roles, semantic bindings, and visibility relations. From this layer-wise representation, we derive controlled understanding questions, editing targets, reference images, and evaluation regions. It contains 2,800 source charts across 14 chart paradigms, from which we derive 7,329 layer-wise understanding questions and 53,791 instruction-guided editing variants. Among the evaluated VLMs, Qwen3.5-27B, which achieves the highest QA macro-average, obtains 93.04% accuracy on layer attribution and 97.46% on layer binding, but only 61.46% on visibility ordering. Across the four evaluated image editors, overall mIoU ranges from 1.49% to 4.93%, and visibility-constrained edits have the lowest mIoU for every editor, ranging from 0.37% to 2.00%. Taken together, these results identify tasks involving front-to-back relations between overlapping components as a recurring challenge across understanding and editing, motivating more explicit modeling of component identity and visibility relations.

</details>


### [267] [TriCCOT: Tri-part Convolutional Conformal Transformer for Onboard Space Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08659)
*Adrien Dorise,Marjorie Bellizzi,Julia Cohen,Stéphane May*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriCCOT是一种面向星载嵌入式平台的端到端可部署目标检测架构，结合卷积区域提议网络、共形预测校准和硬件友好的注意力分类器（Aper-GATES），在DIOR和VDVRaw数据集上展现出与FPGA兼容架构相当的检测性能，并成功在Xilinx Versal VCK190 FPGA上完成完整部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测中的星载目标检测面临计算资源受限和缺少完全校正影像的挑战。卷积检测器在硬件上高效但难以从原始含噪数据中提取稳健表征，而Transformer模型虽具有更强的全局推理能力，但因二次注意力复杂度和不兼容操作难以在FPGA加速器上部署。因此需要在保持Transformer全局推理优势的同时实现硬件友好的部署。

Method: TriCCOT采用三部分架构：(1) 卷积区域提议网络用于生成候选边界框；(2) 共形预测阶段对边界框进行放大，提供无分布假设的概率覆盖保证；(3) Aper-GATES硬件友好的注意力分类器，通过卷积投影、全局通道统计和硬件友好门控操作重构自注意力，避免标准Transformer操作对CNN导向加速器的不兼容问题。

Result: 在DIOR和VDVRaw数据集上的实验表明，TriCCOT在检测性能上与FPGA兼容架构相当，并且在空间模糊和信号相关噪声条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性。此外，该架构已在Xilinx Versal VCK190 FPGA上无需修改底层DPU架构即可完成完整部署，实现了CNN-Transformer统一推理。

Conclusion: TriCCOT有效弥合了Transformer全局推理能力与FPGA硬件部署之间的鸿沟，为星载嵌入式场景提供了既稳健又可直接部署的目标检测解决方案，证明了在资源受限的轨上环境中实现统一CNN-Transformer推理的可行性。

Abstract: Onboard object detection in Earth observation is constrained by limited computational resources and the absence of fully corrected imagery. While convolutional detectors are hardware-efficient, they often struggle to extract robust representations from raw and noisy data. Conversely, transformer-based models provide stronger global reasoning capabilities but remain difficult to deploy on FPGA accelerators due to quadratic attention complexity and non-compatible operations.
  We introduce TriCCOT, a tri-part architecture for robust and deployable onboard object detection. TriCCOT combines a convolutional region proposal network, a conformal prediction stage, and Aper-GATES, our hardware-friendly attention-based classifier. The region proposal network generates candidate bounding boxes, which are subsequently enlarged via conformal prediction, providing a distribution-free probabilistic coverage guarantee. The resulting crops are processed by Aper-GATES, which reformulates self-attention through convolutional projections, global channel statistics, and hardware-friendly gating operations, avoiding standard transformer operations that are poorly suited to CNN-oriented accelerators.
  Experiments on the DIOR and VDVRaw datasets demonstrate competitive detection performance and improved robustness to spatial blur and signal-dependent noise when compared to FPGA-compatible architectures. Finally, we report full deployment on a Xilinx Versal VCK190 FPGA without modifying the underlying DPU architecture, enabling unified CNN-Transformer inference for spaceborne embedded applications.

</details>


### [268] [CoordFormer: Give Me Any Coordinates and I Will Give You Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08660)
*Iacopo Curti,Pierluigi Zama Ramirez,Alioscia Petrelli,Luigi Di Stefano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoordFormer是一种基于坐标架构的新型语义分割方法，通过坐标解码器和局部交叉注意力机制，在超高清图像上实现任意空间位置的标签预测，兼顾高分辨率细节与低计算成本，在MaSS13K等数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清图像的语义分割面临两大挑战：高计算成本和难以捕捉细粒度细节。现有方法难以在保持像素级精度的同时控制内存和延迟开销。

Method: 提出CoordFormer架构，包含：(1) Coordinate Decoder，通过坐标嵌入结合高分辨率局部补丁特征；(2) Localized Cross-Attention机制，与ViT基础编码器提取的全局token交互；(3) 支持任意分辨率推理并保持低内存；提出语义边缘聚焦策略，集中计算在边界区域以降低延迟和计算成本。

Result: CoordFormer在MaSS13K上达到最先进的性能，并在DIS5K和KPIs上优于同规模及参数量更大的方法。

Conclusion: CoordFormer通过坐标解码与局部交叉注意力机制有效解决了超高清语义分割中精度与效率的矛盾，为高质量超高清分割提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation on very-high-resolution images remains challenging due to the high computational cost and the difficulty of capturing fine-grained details. We propose CoordFormer, a novel coordinate-based architecture for semantic segmentation that predicts labels at arbitrary spatial locations through a Coordinate Decoder equipped with a Localized Cross-Attention mechanism. The decoder combines coordinate embeddings with high-resolution local patch features and interacts with global tokens extracted from a downsampled image processed by a ViT foundation encoder, enabling rich semantic context while preserving pixel-level precision. This design enables flexible inference at arbitrary resolutions while keeping memory low on very-high-resolution inputs, and supports an efficient semantic-edge-focused strategy that concentrates computation along boundaries, maintaining fine-grained accuracy while reducing latency and computational cost. CoordFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance on MaSS13K and outperforms comparably sized and higher-parameter methods on DIS5K and KPIs, demonstrating its effectiveness for high-quality, very-high-resolution semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [269] [CausalChapter: Improving Long-Video Chaptering with Interventional Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08686)
*Xinran Duan,Guozhang Li,Yaoyao Zhong,Mei Wang,Lizhi Wang,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalChapter框架，通过轻量级掩蔽和移除干预来估计预测层面的因果影响，解决长视频章节化中段边界错误传播和跨片段上下文碎片化两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长格式教学视频需要自动章节化以支持浏览和知识访问。现有方法中，长上下文语言模型对密集讲稿视频成本高且不稳定，而"先分段后描述"的可扩展范式虽然降低开销，却引入了边界错误传播和跨章上下文碎片化两个新问题。

Method: 提出CausalChapter框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Local Dependency Shift模块用于边界定位，通过检测相邻时间窗口间预测依赖性的下降来判断边界；(2) Cross-Segment Support Selection模块用于章节描述生成，根据历史上下文对当前预测的支持程度进行重排序。

Result: 在长视频章节化基准上的实验表明，CausalChapter在边界定位、章节描述质量和跨章节连贯性方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: CausalChapter通过干预启发的因果分析框架有效缓解了分段-描述范式中的错误传播问题，提升了长视频自动章节化的整体性能。

Abstract: Long-form instructional videos require automatic chaptering to support browsing, navigation, and knowledge access. Recent long-context language models can perform chaptering from textualized video inputs, but they remain costly and brittle for content-dense lecture videos with long transcripts, smooth topic transitions, and detailed chapter outputs. A scalable segment-then-caption paradigm reduces this cost, but introduces two new challenges: boundary error propagation and fragmented cross-chapter context. We propose \textbf{CausalChapter}, an intervention-inspired framework for long-video chaptering that estimates prediction-level influence through lightweight masking and removal interventions. For boundary localization, our Local Dependency Shift module detects drops in predictive dependency between adjacent temporal windows; for chapter description generation, our Cross-Segment Support Selection module reranks historical contexts according to their support for the current prediction. Experiments on long-video chaptering benchmarks show that CausalChapter improves boundary localization, chapter description quality, and cross-chapter coherence.

</details>


### [270] [Enhancing Table Structure Recognition via Bounding Box Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08705)
*Lei Hu,Shuangping Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BGTR框架，先预测单元格边界框，再用边界框信息引导HTML序列生成，解决表格结构识别中图像到序列方法忽略边界框信息的问题，并引入合成自然场景表格数据集SNSTab，在五个基准数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像到序列方法在预测HTML序列时没有显式利用单元格边界框信息，导致在复杂场景下产生错误预测。此外，自然场景表格数据量过小，不足以充分训练边界框引导的HTML生成任务。

Method: 提出BGTR框架，采用两阶段流程：首先预测单元格边界框，然后利用该边界框信息引导HTML序列的生成。针对自然场景表格数据不足的问题，采用渐进式训练方法，并引入合成的自然场景表格数据集SNSTab。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，BGTR达到了最先进的性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 显式利用边界框信息引导HTML序列生成可以有效提升表格结构识别在复杂场景下的准确率，合成数据与渐进式训练策略为数据稀缺问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) aims to extract the bounding boxes of cells and table structure (e.g., HTML) from table images. Although current approaches have made significant progress, the latest image-to-sequence methods overlook the explicit utilization of the bounding box information when predicting HTML sequences, leading to error predictions in complex scenes. In this paper, we introduce a novel framework BGTR (Bounding Box-Guided Table Recognizer). To more effectively utilize bounding box information, we first predict the bounding boxes of cells and then use this information to guide the generation of HTML sequences. While utilizing bounding box information can enhance the accuracy of HTML sequences, for natural scene tables, the data volume is too small to allow for sufficient training of bbox-guided HTML generation. In response, we adopt a progressive training method for natural scene tables and introduce SNSTab, a synthetically generated natural scene table dataset. Our experiments on five benchmark datasets demonstrate SOTA performance.

</details>


### [271] [CVT-GS: Learning to Simplify 3D Gaussian Splatting with Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08730)
*Bingxian Li,Yilong Li,Jingliang Peng,Peng-Shuai Wang,Fei Zhu,Guozheng Li,Chi Harold Liu,Guoping Wang,Bo Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVT-GS，一种免优化的即插型3DGS简化框架，通过几何感知的质心Voronoi划分对高斯中心进行空间聚类，再用轻量神经网络将每个单元内的多个高斯原语合并为一个代表性高斯，实现100倍压缩率下仍保持视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽在实时新视角合成方面表现优异，但高保真渲染需要大量高斯原语，带来存储和计算开销。现有简化方法多具侵入性，需要训练期剪枝、架构修改或昂贵的逐场景微调，限制了其在现成预训练模型上的部署。

Method: CVT-GS首先通过几何感知的质心Voronoi划分（CVT）在高斯中心上构造空间连续单元，随后利用轻量级神经单元合并器，在可微渲染监督下预测每个单元中单个高代表性高斯原语的几何与外观。将简化建模为渲染感知的多对一合并过程，而非朴素原语剪枝。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，在实现100倍高斯点减少时，该方法比最先进方法快12倍，同时PSNR提升1.3 dB。

Conclusion: CVT-GS输出标准3DGS场景，与现有渲染器无缝兼容，是一种高效、非侵入性的后训练简化方案，能大幅压缩3DGS模型而不损失视觉质量。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful representation for real-time novel view synthesis, rendering high-fidelity scenes often relies on a massive number of Gaussian primitives, incurring substantial storage and computational overhead. Existing simplification techniques are largely intrusive, requiring training-time pruning, architectural modifications, or computationally expensive per-scene fine-tuning. These drawbacks limit their deployment on off-the-shelf pretrained models. In this paper, we propose CVT-GS, a novel optimization-free post-hoc simplification framework that directly compresses trained 3DGS scenes without sacrificing visual fidelity. Our approach first constructs spatially coherent cells over Gaussian centers via a geometry-aware Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation (CVT). Subsequently, a lightweight neural cell merger predicts the geometry and appearance of a single, highly representative Gaussian primitive for each cell under differentiable rendering supervision. By formulating simplification as a rendering-aware many-to-one merging process rather than naive primitive pruning, CVT-GS outputs a standard 3DGS scene that is seamlessly compatible with existing renderers. Experiments on various datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method. Notably, when achieving a 100-fold reduction in Gaussian points, our method operates 12 times faster than state-of-the-art methods while improving the PSNR by 1.3 dB.

</details>


### [272] [Kairos: A Dataset for Fine-Grained Video-Language Modeling over Space, Time, and Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08755)
*Ruibo Ming,Lei Sun,Deheng Zhang,He Zhang,Jialu Li,Jian Wang,Zhendong Li,Mengshun Hu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kairos，一个时间分辨率级别的视频语言建模数据集，包含10分钟至半小时的长时长视频，并配有细粒度的时间对齐标注，用于支持长时视频理解、推理、指令学习、表征学习和视频生成等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据集大多依赖粗略或稀疏对齐的监督信号，压缩了时间动态变化，限制了模型学习连续视觉动态的可复用表征能力。随着视频语言建模任务向长时间跨度发展，需要超越片段级抽象的新数据集。

Method: 构建了Kairos数据集，包含时长从10分钟到半小时的长视频，配备细粒度的时间对齐注释，标注内容包括持续动作、实体出现及属性、交互关系以及随时间演化的上下文线索，形成时间分辨率的结构化信息。

Result: 该时间分辨率结构支持细粒度评测、长时建模与推理、指令数据构建、表征学习以及视频生成等多种下游应用，为长时间视觉体验建模提供了通用基础。

Conclusion: Kairos作为通用型长时视频语言数据集，填补了现有数据在时间细粒度与长时间跨度上的空白，为未来视频语言模型在连续视觉动态建模方面奠定了基础。

Abstract: Many emerging video language modeling tasks require systems to move beyond clip-level abstraction and model visual content as it unfolds over extended time horizons. However, most existing video datasets rely on coarse or sparsely aligned supervision, which compresses temporal variation and limits the ability of models to learn reusable representations of continuous visual dynamics. We introduce Kairos, a video dataset for video-language modeling with time-resolved annotations. Kairos consists of long-duration videos, ranging from ten minutes to half an hour, annotated with fine-grained temporal alignment. The annotations capture ongoing actions, entity appearances and attributes, interactions, and evolving contextual cues along the video timeline. This time-resolved structure supports fine-grained evaluation, long-range modeling and reasoning, instruction data construction, representation learning, and video generation. Kairos provides a general-purpose foundation for modeling visual experiences over time.

</details>


### [273] [Compensating for Scarce Historical Images in Cross-Domain Cultural Heritage Retrieval Using Synthetic Aging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08766)
*Marcin Iwanowski,Adam Mazgaj,Ferdynand Gorski,Sabina Szymoniak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨合成老化当代图像能否替代或补充稀缺的历史图像训练数据，用于双向实例级检索任务。结果显示完全替代会降低性能（mR@1从86.56%降至81.27%），但在真实历史数据稀缺（25%-75%覆盖率）时，合成补充可显著提升检索性能（最高提升3.69个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产收藏中常同时包含同一物理对象的当代与历史视觉记录，但由于视角、采集条件、色彩、分辨率及老化程度差异，将两类记录相互关联十分困难；同时真实历史图像往往稀缺，限制了依赖大量标注数据的检索模型训练。

Method: 使用面向退化的变换生成合成老化当代图像；采用EfficientNetV2-M模型，在身份不相交的训练/验证/测试集上评估；比较真实-合成混合训练与仅真实训练基线，采用等比缩放和固定300批次/epoch两种调度方案；通过多个数据集分区和训练种子进行统计验证。

Result: 完全用合成图像替代真实历史图像会使mR@1从86.56%降至81.27%；独立生成更多合成变体无一致改进。但在真实历史覆盖率25%时，合成补充提升3.69个百分点，50%时提升2.92，75%时提升2.00。固定调度对照组未复现此改进。

Conclusion: 真实与合成观测具有互补性。合成补充主要通过扩展跨域身份覆盖而非增加训练曝光来提升检索效果，其贡献随真实历史覆盖率增加而逐渐减小。建议在真实历史数据稀缺场景下将合成补充作为可行策略，而非完全替代。

Abstract: Cultural heritage collections often contain contemporary and historical visual records of the same physical object. Linking these records is difficult because corresponding images may differ in viewpoint, acquisition conditions, color reproduction, framing, resolution, and degradation, while genuine historical images are frequently scarce. This study investigates whether synthetically aged contemporary images can replace or complement missing historical training data in bidirectional instance-level retrieval. Synthetic old-domain images are generated using degradation-oriented transformations. An EfficientNetV2-M model is evaluated on identity-disjoint training, validation, and test sets across three dataset partitions and three training seeds. Mixed real-synthetic training is compared with real-only baselines using proportionally scaled and fixed 300-batch-per-epoch schedules. Complete replacement of genuine historical images reduced bidirectional mean R@1 from 86.56% to 81.27%, showing that synthetic aging does not reproduce the full genuine old-domain variability. Increasing the number of independently generated synthetic variants provided no consistent improvement. Under controlled scarcity, however, synthetic completion improved mean R@1 by 3.69 percentage points at 25% genuine historical coverage and by 2.92 points at 50%, relative to the proportionally scaled real-only baselines. At 75%, the gain decreased to 2.00 points, while performance remained comparable to the complete-real-data reference. Fixed-schedule real-only controls did not reproduce these improvements. The results indicate that genuine and synthetic observations are complementary. Synthetic completion primarily benefits retrieval by extending cross-domain identity coverage rather than by increasing training exposure, with its contribution gradually decreasing as genuine historical coverage increases.

</details>


### [274] [AXS-Net: Interpretable Deep Unfolding for Hyperspectral Image Denoising via Spectral Basis Unmixing and Structured Noise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08777)
*Ziyi Guan,Jianping Zhang,Zheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AXS-Net，一种将高光谱图像去噪建模为低秩光谱子空间重构加结构化稀疏噪声加残差高斯噪声的交替近端展开网络，通过K阶段展开实现可解释的噪声分离与去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像常受混合噪声（带相关高斯扰动、条纹、死线和脉冲噪声）影响，现有深度去噪器直接回归干净图像，将信号与结构化噪声纠缠在一起，缺乏对噪声结构的显式建模与可解释性。

Method: 将去噪问题形式化为Y=AX+S+N的优化模型，其中AX为低秩光谱子空间重构，S为结构化稀疏噪声，N为残差高斯噪声。将正则化优化问题展开为K阶段交替近端点框架AXS-Net，每阶段包含解析谱基梯度步、用于丰度系数的SSX-Block近端算子、以及用于结构化残差的SBlock近端算子（含列一致性和稀疏先验）。

Result: 在ICVL、CAVE和Harvard数据集及五种噪声配置下，AXS-Net取得强域内精度和具有竞争力的零样本迁移性能，在ICVL和Harvard上所有五种噪声设置均实现一致增益。恢复的结构化噪声与合成参考高度一致，恢复的谱基平滑且按波段有序排列。

Conclusion: AXS-Net通过优化与网络的深度耦合，实现了可解释的高光谱去噪，能够分离出有意义的端元、丰度图和结构化噪声估计，验证了基于模型的展开方法在高光谱去噪任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) are often degraded by mixed noise, including band-dependent Gaussian perturbations and structured artifacts such as stripes, dead-lines, and impulse noise. Most deep denoisers regress the clean image directly, entangling signal and structured noise. We instead model HSI denoising as $\Y=\A\X+\Snoise+\Nnoise$, where $\A\X$ is a low-rank spectral-subspace (unmixing) reconstruction, $\Snoise$ is structured sparse noise and $\Nnoise$ is residual Gaussian noise. The resulting regularized optimization problem is unrolled into AXS-Net, a $K$-stage alternating proximal-point framework. Each stage combines an analytic spectral-basis gradient step, an SSX-Block proximal operator for abundance coefficients, and an SBlock proximal operator for the structured residual with column-consistent and sparse priors.
