This optimization correspondence exposes interpretable endmembers, abundance maps, and structured-noise estimates. Across ICVL, CAVE, and Harvard datasets and five noise configurations, the proposed AXS-Net achieves strong in-domain accuracy and competitive zero-shot transfer, with consistent gains across all five noise regimes on ICVL and Harvard. The recovered structured-noise closely follows the synthetic reference, and the recovered spectral basis is smooth and band-ordered rather than an arbitrary set of latent channels.

</details>


### [275] [Interpretable Hyperspectral Unmixing Framework with Fixed Endmember Prior and Structured Residual Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08786)
*Ziyi Guan,Jianping Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的分阶段高光谱解混框架（I-HyperSU），在固定端元先验下，将解混显式分解为固定端元、丰度估计和结构残差精化三个模块，通过软性丰度松弛和深度图像先验来处理模型失配，实验验证了该方法在不完美端元条件下能显著降低重构误差且不损害丰度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱解混流水线先估计端元再固定丰度估计，但当固定的端元先验不准确时，由光照变化、传感器伪影或物质边界引起的结构性失配会被错误归入丰度变量，导致分解不稳定。现有方法缺乏对这种交互关系的透明处理，需要一种可解释且鲁棒的框架来解决固定且不完美端元下的解混问题。

Method: 提出I-HyperSU框架，显式分解为三个模块：固定端元矩阵A、丰度块X和结构残差精化块S。X块采用FISTA算法估计丰度，同时施加非负性约束、稀疏增强以及近似满足和为一约束的软惩罚；S块联合使用低秩SVD结构正则化和轻量级深度图像先验（DIP）来精化结构化残差。这种分阶段设计使丰度和残差之间的交互透明可解释。

Result: 在Samson、Urban和Jasper Ridge数据集上的实验表明：在固定且不完美的端元先验下，软性丰度松弛一致优于硬单纯形投影；在默认N-FINDR端元先验下，该框架相比固定A的UCLS基线将联合重构误差降低61.7%-69.5%，同时丰度RMSE几乎不变。例如Urban数据集上，重构SAM从仅X模型的5.99度降至完整模型的1.92度。

Conclusion: 残差精化分支能够在固定端元先验下有效吸收结构化模型失配，而不削弱丰度估计的精度，验证了分阶段可解释框架在处理不完美端元先验时的高效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral unmixing decomposes mixed pixels into material endmembers and their abundances from contiguous spectral observations. In modular sensing pipelines, endmembers are often first identified and then treated as fixed during abundance estimation. When this fixed endmember prior is inaccurate, spatially structured mismatch arising from illumination changes, sensor artifacts, or material boundaries may be incorrectly captured by the abundance variables, leading to unstable decompositions. This study presents an interpretable stage-wise hyperspectral unmixing framework (I-HyperSU) under fixed endmember priors, which is explicitly decomposed into a fixed endmember matrix $\mathbf{A}$, an abundance block $\mathbf{X}$, and a structural residual refinement block $\mathbf{S}$. The X-block estimates abundances using FISTA with nonnegativity and sparsity enhancement, and a soft penalty that approximately enforces sum-to-one constraints. The S-block jointly applies low-rank SVD structural regularization and a lightweight deep image prior (DIP) to refine structured residuals. This staged design makes the interaction between abundance and residual components transparent and interpretable. Experiments on Samson, Urban, and Jasper Ridge datasets demonstrate that, under fixed and imperfect endmember priors, soft abundance relaxation consistently outperforms hard simplex projection. Under the default N-FINDR endmember prior, the proposed framework reduces the joint reconstruction error by 61.7\%--69.5\% compared with a fixed-$\mathbf{A}$ UCLS baseline, while keeping the abundance RMSE nearly unchanged, indicating that the residual refinement branch accounts for structured model mismatch without degrading the abundance estimates. For example, on Urban, the reconstruction SAM decreases from $5.99^\circ$ for the X-only model to $1.92^\circ$ for the full model.

</details>


### [276] [Hi-FLoop: Hierarchical State-Feedback Loops for Multi-Timescale World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08796)
*Rx Fan,Zhan H*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HI-FLOOP提出一种基于分支一致性的多时间尺度状态反馈框架，用于多智能体交通仿真长时程闭环生成，通过场景级World分支共享、分层时间尺度（Goal/Preview/Control）决策以及前缀冻结的A-to-B级联恢复机制，在H-D公开验证集上获得Overall分数0.689987，oracle-minADE@8达1.196636米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通仿真方法通常从初始场景直接展开长时程未来，并将意图、交互和运动控制进行整体式的单一化求解，导致跨时间尺度的一致性不足，且难以在生成状态演化的闭环中实现自适应调整。

Method: HI-FLOOP采用分支一致的多时间尺度状态反馈框架：场景级八分支World表示联合假设并在8秒内共享分支身份；分层决策包括8秒Goal（意图）、2秒Preview（交互）和1秒Control（运动）；每0.5秒commit仅将已执行前缀作为事实反馈，未执行假设不进入事实记忆。JPI（联合预览交互）模块通过稀疏有向未来图和冲突概率及到达时间差门控交互细化。对生成状态恢复采用前缀冻结的A-to-B级联，Model A生成0-1秒后仅传递物理状态、上下文和分支索引（不含潜在状态）给独立的Model B完成1-2秒恢复。

Result: 在H-D公开验证集955个场景上，单次S1运行的Overall分数为0.689987（官方评估器）；在智能体中心Oracle评估下，HI-FLOOP在8秒范围内达到oracle-minADE@8为1.196636米，6秒范围为0.526米。

Conclusion: HI-FLOOP通过分支一致性和多时间尺度分层状态反馈，有效解决了长时程闭环交通仿真中的跨尺度一致性问题，同时其前缀冻结的级联生成策略避免了潜在状态泄露，保障了生成状态恢复的物理合理性与适应能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent traffic simulation seeks diverse, coordinated, and physically realistic futures from maps and observed history. Long-horizon closed-loop generation must reconcile multiple decision time scales while its context evolves with generated states. Existing methods often unfold long futures from the initial scene and resolve intent, interaction, and motion monolithically, weakening cross-scale consistency and adaptation. We present HI-FLOOP, a branch-consistent multi-timescale state-feedback framework. Eight scene-level Worlds represent joint hypotheses, and all agents share the selected World identity throughout an 8-second rollout. Within the branch, an 8-second Goal anchors intent, a 2-second Preview coordinates interactions, and 1-second Control produces physical motion. Every 0.5-second commit feeds back only its executed prefix as new facts, while unexecuted hypotheses never enter factual memory. Joint Preview Interaction (JPI) induces a sparse directed future graph from Preview and uses conflict probabilities and signed arrival-time differences to gate interaction refinement. For generated-state recovery, a prefix-frozen A-to-B cascade lets frozen Model A generate 0-1 seconds, then transfers typed physical state, admissible context, and the branch index, but no latent state, to an independent Model B for re-encoding and 1-2-second recovery. On the full H-D public-validation split of 955 scenarios, one complete S1 run yields an Overall score of 0.689987 with the official evaluator. Under agent-centric oracle evaluation, HI-FLOOP achieves oracle-minADE@8 of 1.196636 m over the 8-second horizon and 0.526 m over the 6-second horizon.

</details>


### [277] [Evaluation Principles for MRI-MRA Registration in Trigeminal Neuralgia: An ROI-Centered Neurovascular Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08805)
*Xupeng Zhang,Xihang Wang,Michael Xie,Haoyuan Liang,Hau Ern Lien,Oishika Das,James Feghali,Risheng Xu,Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对三叉神经痛术前评估中MRI-MRA影像融合任务，提出了以ROI为中心、血管感知、对比度敏感且FOV感知的配准评估框架。基于149例患者构建基准数据集，系统评估了6种代表性配准流程，发现传统全脑配准评估指标存在误导性，局部图像相似度、血管分割可分性和下游血管定位不能一致地排序方法；可变形配准仅带来微小且依赖FOV的优势。研究建议将TN的MRI-MRA配准视为局部、血管感知的可视化任务而非通用多模态脑配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三叉神经痛的术前评估需要联合解读结构性MRI和TOF-MRA，两者融合对于可视化神经血管压迫具有临床价值。然而，由于临床关注区域仅为小范围的三叉神经ROI、血管标注部分且聚焦临床、局部TOF-MRA对比度变化大以及FOV不匹配等问题，传统全脑配准评估方法无法有效衡量这一临床任务的配准质量，亟需针对性的评估框架。

Method: 将TN MRI-MRA融合问题建模为ROI中心的神经血管配准-评估问题，利用149例具有临床医师标注双侧三叉神经ROI的患者构建基准数据集。采用局部图像指标、分割导出的血管定位指标、预测体积分析以及对比度/FOV分层比较，对6种代表性配准流程进行系统评估。

Result: 研究发现：传统评估摘要常具误导性——局部图像相似度、血管-背景可分性与下游血管定位无法一致地对方法排序；单侧血管距离在部分标注下受预测血管范围强烈影响；局部MRA对比度决定了血管可分性指标何时具有信息量。可变形精化相对仿射对齐仅提供微小且依赖FOV的改善，读者评审显示局部血管距离有利的情况可能与整体不合理的配准并存。

Conclusion: TN MRI-MRA配准应被评估为局部、血管感知、对比度敏感且FOV感知的可视化任务，而非通用的多模态脑部配准问题。该研究提供了公开可用的基准和代码，为临床影像融合评估方法学提供了重要的新视角。

Abstract: Preoperative evaluation of trigeminal neuralgia (TN) often requires joint interpretation of structural MRI, which depicts the trigeminal nerve and surrounding cisternal anatomy, and time-of-flight MRA, which highlights vascular structures. Although MRI-MRA fusion is clinically attractive for visualizing neurovascular compression, this task is poorly captured by conventional whole-brain registration evaluation because the clinically relevant target is a small trigeminal ROI, vessel annotations are partial and clinically focused, local TOF-MRA contrast is variable, and field-of-view mismatch can limit deformable alignment. We formulate TN MRI-MRA fusion as an ROI-centered neurovascular registration-evaluation problem and construct a benchmark from 149 patients with clinician-annotated bilateral trigeminal ROIs. Six representative registration pipelines were evaluated using local image-based metrics, segmentation-derived vessel-localization metrics, prediction-volume analysis, and contrast- and FOV-stratified comparisons. Conventional evaluation summaries were often misleading: local image similarity, vessel-background separability, and downstream vessel localization did not co-rank methods; one-sided vessel distances were strongly affected by predicted vessel extent under partial annotations; and local MRA contrast determined when vessel-separability metrics were informative. Deformable refinement provided only a small, FOV-dependent benefit over affine alignment, while reader review showed that locally favorable vessel distances could coexist with globally implausible registrations. These findings indicate that TN MRI-MRA registration should be evaluated as a local, vessel-aware, contrast-sensitive, and FOV-aware visualization task rather than as generic multimodal brain registration. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jhuldr/TN-Reg-Benchmark.

</details>


### [278] [ArmPoser: Real-Time, Calibration-Free Arm Pose Estimation from Smartwatch IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08806)
*Bishnu Dev,Vasco Xu,Xi-Aan Loh,Chenfeng Gao,Henry Hoffmann,Karan Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ArmPoser系统，利用单个智能手表IMU实现无需校准的手臂姿态估计。其核心创新是在消费级智能手表固有的参考坐标系中直接训练模型，消除了传统方法所需的坐标变换、显式对齐和骨骼偏移校准等预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能手表的手臂姿态估计方法依赖校准姿势和预处理流程，将原始IMU测量转换为标准化训练格式。这些步骤阻碍了日常场景中的部署，并因校准不完善和传感器漂移而引入误差。作者希望消除这些限制，实现真正免校准的日常部署。

Method: 1) 在设备本征参考坐标系中直接训练模型，使学习过程与部署设备的IMU数据生产方式对齐；2) 通过物理有根据的佩戴位置和手臂形态变化增强训练数据，以适应个体差异；3) 设计佩戴配置模块，推断前臂/后臂放置位置和表冠方向

Result: 在公共基准测试和包含10名参与者、30项活动的watchOS和Android智能手表研究中，ArmPoser在无需任何用户校准的情况下达到了与校准基线相当或更优的姿态估计性能。

Conclusion: ArmPoser证明了在设备固有坐标系中训练模型可以完全消除校准需求，同时保持甚至超越传统校准方法的性能，为智能手表在日常场景中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Arm pose estimation enables applications in fitness, extended reality input, rehabilitation, and life logging. Prior smartwatch-based approaches rely on calibration poses and preprocessing pipelines that transform raw IMU measurements into standardized training formats. These steps hinder deployment in everyday settings and introduce errors due to imperfect calibration and sensor drift. We present ArmPoser, a calibration-free arm pose estimation system using a single smartwatch IMU. Our central contribution is training models directly in the reference frame native to consumer smartwatches, aligning learning with how IMU data is produced by deployed devices. By operating on device-native axes, ArmPoser removes the need for coordinate transformations, explicit alignment, and bone-offset calibration used in prior work. We further augment training with physically grounded variations in watch placement and arm morphology to account for user-specific variability. ArmPoser also includes a wear-configuration module that infers anterior or posterior forearm placement and crown orientation. We evaluate pose estimation on public benchmarks and on a 10-participant, 30-activity study using watchOS and Android smartwatches, where ArmPoser matches or exceeds calibrated baselines without any user calibration.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Gait: Person Identification from Millimeter-Wave Point Clouds Across Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08818)
*Xilai Wang,Zixiong Han,Saad Rhanmouni,Chenzhe Zhao,Yunze Lu,Miodrag Bolic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用毫米波点云数据进行人物识别的问题，提出了一种活动条件化的混合专家框架，将人类活动识别作为上下文来辅助学习身份表征。实验表明，与仅依赖步态相比，利用七种日常生活活动（ADLs）进行身份识别是可行的，活动条件化框架在闭集识别和重识别任务上均显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于毫米波点云的人物识别主要依赖步态，但室内行走往往短暂且频繁被打断，而其他日常活动可能提供互补的身份信息。作者希望探索超越步态的日常活动是否可用于人物识别，并研究活动上下文是否能帮助学习更好的身份表征。

Method: 提出了一个活动条件化的混合专家（MoE）框架：首先通过一个人体活动识别路由器将每个点云片段分配给对应的活动专属身份专家；每个专家使用双流静态-动态PointNet（DS-SDPNet），结合时间聚合的空间结构和帧间动态信息。

Result: 在闭集识别任务中，硬路由将精度从62.1%提升至68.0%；在双人重识别设置中，硬路由将mAP从57.2%提升至75.4%，Rank-1准确率从59.1%提升至82.1%。在匹配的图库划分下，活动专属专家也优于共享嵌入。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在受控室内条件下，利用步态之外的日常生活活动进行身份识别是可行的，且活动条件化能有效提升识别性能，其收益不仅限于限制候选图库规模。

Abstract: Person identification from millimeter-wave (mmWave) point clouds has mainly relied on gait. Indoor walking, however, is often brief and interrupted, while other activities of daily living (ADLs) may provide complementary identity information. We investigate identification across seven ADLs using mm-ADL, a new point-cloud dataset collected from 11 subjects under a controlled protocol. This extension introduces heterogeneous states and transitions whose spatial and temporal characteristics vary with activity. We therefore study whether activity can provide useful context for learning identity representations. We propose an activity-conditioned framework in which a human activity recognition router dispatches each clip to an activity-specific identity expert. The framework is implemented as a supervised mixture of experts, using a dual-stream static-dynamic PointNet (DS-SDPNet) to combine time-aggregated spatial structure with frame-to-frame information. We evaluate closed-set identification (ID) and subject-disjoint re-identification (ReID). With learned hard routing, ID accuracy increases from 62.1% to 68.0%. In a two-occupant ReID setting, hard routing increases mAP from 57.2% to 75.4% and Rank-1 accuracy from 59.1% to 82.1%. Under a matched gallery partition, activity-specific experts also outperform a shared embedding, showing that the gain extends beyond restricting the gallery. These results support the feasibility of using ADLs beyond gait for identification and the value of activity conditioning under controlled indoor conditions.

</details>


### [280] [Leveraging Visual and Geometric Priors for Metric-scale and Complete Vehicle Gaussian Reconstruction from Limited Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08841)
*Jinyu Miao,Jiusi Li,Yifei He,Miao Long,Kun Jiang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种前馈式车辆资产重建方法，利用视觉基础模型和对称性先验，从车载相机拍摄的单侧稀疏观测中重建具有度量尺度且完整的三维高斯车辆表示，在公开数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量车辆资产对可控交通场景生成至关重要，尤其用于合成罕见和安全性关键的长尾场景。然而，从车内真实图像中重建可复用的车辆表示存在两大挑战：一是现有的图像到3D生成方法缺乏可靠的度量尺度；二是车载相机通常只能观察到目标车辆的单侧，导致常规多视角重建在未观察区域不完整。

Method: 提出一种前馈式车辆资产重建方法，利用两个互补先验进行3D高斯重建：(1) 视觉基础模型作为视觉先验用于3D高斯初始化，实现度量尺度重建；(2) 基于车辆左右对称结构的对称感知克隆策略，直接在高斯空间中补全未观测侧，利用几何先验完成车辆重建。高斯属性由可学习的编码器-解码器模块估计。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在车辆资产完整性和几何精度方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过结合视觉先验与对称性几何先验，所提出的方法有效解决了单侧观测下的度量尺度和完整性两个核心问题，为交通场景生成提供了高质量、可复用的车辆资产表示。

Abstract: High-fidelity vehicle assets are essential for controllable traffic scene generation, particularly for synthesizing rare and safety-critical long-tail scenarios. However, reconstructing a reusable vehicle representation from in-the-wild onboard images remains challenging for two reasons. First, image-to-3D generation methods generally produce models without reliable metric scale. Second, onboard cameras usually observe only one side of a target vehicle, making conventional multi-view reconstruction incomplete on unobserved regions. To solve these problems, we propose a feed-forward vehicle asset reconstruction method, which leverages two complementary priors to reconstruct 3D Gaussian representations for vehicles using sparse one-sided observations. To achieve metric-scale reconstruction, a visual foundation model is first utilized to serve as a visual prior for Gaussian initialization. The Gaussian attributes are then estimated by a learnable encoder-decoder module. A symmetry-aware cloning strategy is presented to complete the unobserved side directly in Gaussian space, which exploits the bilateral structure of vehicles as a geometric prior. Experiments on the public dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing approaches in both vehicle asset completeness and geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [281] [FIRE3D: Feed-forward Interactive 3D Scene Reconstruction Within A Minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08848)
*Hongchi Xia,Tianhang Cheng,Wei-Chiu Ma,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIRE3D是一个统一框架，可从单张RGB图像或RGB视频在不到一分钟内将场景转换为仿真就绪的3D资产生成结果，采用前馈端到端网络直接预测每个物体的位姿、包围盒、网格和纹理，无需测试时优化，速度比以往方法快数个数量级，同时具备物体级完整性和竞争性/最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D场景重建方法要么速度慢（需要测试时优化），要么缺乏交互就绪性和物体级完整性，难以满足游戏和交互式应用对实时、仿真就绪3D场景资产生成的需求。

Method: 采用前馈端到端网络，从RGB图像/视频估计RGB-D观测，预测包含6自由度位姿、包围盒、网格和纹理的组件化场景表示；将场景建模为离散实体集合，实现物体物理解耦且具备模态完整性，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上的姿态精度、几何完整性和纹理质量方面达到竞争性或最先进的性能，运行速度比现有交互就绪方法快数个数量级，能在不到一分钟内完成场景资产生成。

Conclusion: FIRE3D为游戏和交互应用提供了一种兼顾速度、完整性与物理可用性的场景资产生成方案，弥合了前馈3D重建方法与高效仿真就绪场景生成之间的差距。

Abstract: We present FIRE3D, a unified framework that takes a single RGB image or casual RGB video and transforms it into simulation-ready 3D scene assets for games and interactive applications in under a minute. At the core of FIRE3D is a feed-forward, end-to-end network that predicts a compositional scene representation from posed RGB-D observations estimated from the RGB capture, including the 6-DoF pose, bounding box, mesh, and texture for every object. By modeling the scene as a collection of discrete entities, FIRE3D produces amodally complete and simulation-ready environments where objects are physically decoupled and ready for interaction. Our framework requires no test-time optimization, runs orders of magnitude faster than prior interaction-ready methods, and provides object-level completeness beyond existing feed-forward 3D approaches. We demonstrate competitive or state-of-the-art results across pose accuracy, geometry completeness, and texture quality across various datasets while being orders of magnitudes faster. Project page: https://xiahongchi.github.io/Fire3D/

</details>


### [282] [DSE-VTG: Dual-Side Enhancement for Training-Free Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08850)
*Zhuo Cao,Bingqing Zhang,Sen Wang,Xue Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSE-VTG是一个无需任务训练的视频时序定位框架，通过双向增强（视觉端的多尺度相似度融合和文本端的查询级测试时适应）来解决训练免费VTG方法中的两个信息瓶颈问题，在多个标准基准和OOD基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费VTG方法虽然避免了密集标注和任务特定模型的昂贵成本，但仍面临两大信息瓶颈：帧级视觉编码忽视了时序动态信息，固定的查询嵌入无法解决查询歧义问题。

Method: 提出DSE-VTG双端增强框架：(1)视觉端采用多尺度相似度融合(MSF)，将帧级和片段级相似度结合为统一的时序感知相似度；(2)文本端采用查询级测试时适应(Q-TTA)，在测试时优化轻量级加性偏移来调整查询嵌入，无需微调骨干网络或调用外部大语言模型。

Result: 在三个标准基准和两个OOD基准上的广泛实验表明，DSE-VTG在训练免费方法中达到最先进性能。在Charades-STA上较最强先前训练免费方法mIoU提升5.61个百分点；在分布偏移下，Charades-CG Novel-Word上达到50.86 mIoU，超过最强监督基线2.76 mIoU。

Conclusion: DSE-VTG通过视觉和文本双端的针对性增强，有效解决了训练免费VTG的核心瓶颈，在标准场景和分布偏移场景下均展现出强大的鲁棒性和性能优势。

Abstract: Text-guided Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) aims to localize the relevant segments in an untrimmed video based on text queries, yet collecting dense temporal annotations and training task-specific models remain costly and brittle under distribution shift. Recent training-free VTG approaches mitigate this issue by directly matching pretrained vision-language representations, but they still face two fundamental information bottlenecks: frame-wise visual encoding overlooks temporal dynamics, while fixed query embeddings cannot resolve query ambiguity. To address these issues, we propose DSE-VTG, a \underline{D}ual-\underline{S}ide \underline{E}nhancement framework that addresses both without any task-specific training. On the visual side, Multi-scale Similarity Fusion (MSF) combines frame- and clip-level similarities into a unified, temporally aware similarity profile. On the textual side, Query-level Test-Time Adaptation (Q-TTA) optimizes a lightweight additive offset to adapt the query embedding to the video at test time, without finetuning the backbone or calling external large language models. Extensive experiments on three standard and two OOD benchmarks show that DSE-VTG achieves state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods. On Charades-STA, it improves mIoU over the strongest prior training-free method by 5.61 points. Under distribution shift, DSE-VTG reaches 50.86 mIoU on Charades-CG Novel-Word, surpassing the strongest supervised baseline by 2.76 mIoU. Our code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [283] [SeGDeP: Semantic- and Geometric-Aware Decoupled Prompts for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08867)
*Linnan Zhao,Xu Liu,Lingling Li,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu,Wenping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeGDeP，一种显式的"what-where"解耦接口，用于推理分割任务。它通过语义提示分支和独立的几何投影路径，分别将MLLM状态转化为语义特征和DETR预测的边界框，共同调节SAM 3掩码解码器。训练采用先对齐接口再用GDPO平衡三种反馈的策略，仅微调0.38%参数即达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM分割器将语义识别和空间定位两种信号耦合在单一上下文中，但二者接受不同监督且失败模式不同，导致无法判断失败源于目标理解还是定位问题。

Method: 提出SeGDeP框架：1）语义提示分支和独立几何投影路径分别处理语义特征与DETR预测框；2）联合调节SAM 3掩码解码器；3）两阶段训练：先对齐可执行接口，再用组奖励解耦策略优化(GDPO)平衡格式、框IoU和掩码IoU三种反馈。

Result: SeGDeP-4B在RefCOCO系列的8个分割上达到82.7平均cIoU；在ReasonSeg验证/测试集上分别达到66.0/59.6 gIoU，仅通过LoRA适配了Qwen3-VL参数的0.38%。

Conclusion: 通过阶段式消融、梯度诊断和提示干预实验证明，两个路径发展出互补的语义和几何专门化能力，而非重复编码相同证据，验证了what-where解耦设计的有效性。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation converts an implicit linguistic conclusion into a precise mask, requiring both semantic identification and spatial grounding. Existing MLLM-segmenter interfaces either use a special trigger or compress both signals into one context, although they receive different supervision and fail differently. This coupling obscures whether a failure arises from target interpretation or from localization. We present SeGDeP, an explicit what-where interface. A semantic prompt branch and an independent geometric projection path transform resolved MLLM states into semantic features and a DETR-predicted box, which jointly condition a SAM 3 mask decoder. Training first aligns this executable interface, then uses group reward-decoupled policy optimization (GDPO) to balance format, box-IoU, and mask-IoU feedback. SeGDeP-4B reaches 82.7 average cIoU over eight RefCOCO-family splits and 66.0/59.6 gIoU on ReasonSeg val/test while adapting only 0.38% of Qwen3-VL parameters through LoRA. Controlled stage-wise ablations, gradient diagnostics, and prompt interventions further show that the two paths develop complementary semantic and geometric specialization rather than duplicating the same evidence.

</details>


### [284] [Medical AI Encodes a "Feeling of Error": Verifying Cancer Segmentation via Internal Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08879)
*Mengmeng Ma,Yunxiang Peng,Tang Li,Lu Lin,Binsheng Zhao,Oguz Akin,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用机械可解释性工具（稀疏自编码器）从癌症分割模型的内部激活中捕捉其"错误感"（FOE），通过分解内部神经激活为可解释概念并训练分类器，实现对分割失败的准确检测，同时提供失败原因的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症分割模型可能静默失败，生成看似合理但错误的掩码，导致漏诊或不必要活检。传统输出级信号（如置信度或不确定性）无法解释失败原因，且存在灵敏度-质量权衡问题。类比人类的"错误感"（FOE），作者希望探究AI模型是否具有类似的内部错误信号，并利用该信号预测自身失败。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将模型的内部神经激活分解为人类可解释概念的字典，对比成功与失败分割案例中活跃概念的数量和激活幅度，发现失败案例具有更少活跃概念且激活幅度更低（形成独特的潜在特征）。在此基础上训练一个基于概念激活的分类器来实现失败检测。

Result: 在前列腺、胰腺和脑癌分割实验中，该方法在失败检测方面优于基于输出的方法（如置信度和不确定性），同时保持原有的分割质量不受影响。

Conclusion: 通过深入模型内部机制而非仅依赖输出信号，可以有效检测癌症分割模型的失败，并附带可解释的失败原因，为临床AI系统的可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cancer segmentation models can fail silently, generating plausible but incorrect masks that risk missed findings or unnecessary biopsies. A critical question arises: Do AI models "know" when they are wrong, and if so, can we use the signal to predict their own failures? Humans do have a "Feeling of Error" (FOE): a spontaneous sense of unease that flags a potential error during thinking. We investigate whether cancer segmentation models exhibit an analogous internal signal. Unlike output-level cues (e.g., prediction confidence or uncertainty), which offer no insight into why a failure occurs and suffer from a sensitivity-quality tradeoff where high detection sensitivity could degrade overall segmentation quality. We instead propose to capture the model's FOE from its inner workings. Using mechanistic interpretability tools, specifically Sparse Autoencoders, we decompose internal neural activations into a dictionary of human-interpretable concepts and show that failure cases exhibit a distinct latent signature: fewer active concepts with lower activation magnitudes compared to successful segmentation. By training a classifier on these concept activations, we achieve accurate failure detection along with explanations for the model's mistakes. Experiments on prostate, pancreatic, and brain cancer segmentation demonstrate that our approach outperforms output-based methods in failure detection while preserving segmentation quality.

</details>


### [285] [FRAME: Factored Retrieval via Attribute Readouts for Object-Centric Scene Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08886)
*Woosang Jeon,Sanghyeok Choi,Minwoo Kim,Taehyun Jung,Taehyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了属性组合检索问题，即通过自然语言查询固定场景记忆中具有多种持久属性（如类别、材质、尺寸、外观）的对象。作者提出了FRAME方法，将语言转化为属性权重，从对象嵌入中估计每个属性的证据，并通过聚合证据来排序对象，在隐藏场景和对象资产上优于代表性基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的机器人需要持久的场景记忆来遵循指令、重访对象和解析跨时间的对象引用。以往的场景记忆检索多侧重于空间或关系引用，而许多日常对象引用通过多种持久属性（如类别、材质、尺寸、表面外观）来指定对象，这一问题尚未被系统性形式化和研究。

Method: FRAME方法包含三个关键步骤：(1) 将自然语言查询转化为与查询相关的属性权重；(2) 使用学习到的读出器从对象嵌入中估计每个属性的证据；(3) 根据查询的权重聚合各属性证据并对对象进行排序。此外，作者设计了固定场景记忆和属性定义目标的受控评估协议，将检索与感知和标注歧义分离。

Result: 在保留的场景和对象资产上，FRAME优于代表性的场景记忆检索基线方法，同时将分解后的对象评分简化为轻量级的矩阵-向量计算，在准确性和效率上均表现出优势。

Conclusion: 属性组合检索可作为语言引导机器人场景记忆的补充能力，持久对象属性可被表示为可组合的证据，支持准确且高效的多属性检索。

Abstract: Language-guided robots need persistent scene memories to follow instructions, revisit objects, and resolve references to objects encountered over time. While much of language-guided scene-memory retrieval has emphasized spatial or relational references, many everyday object references specify objects by multiple persistent attributes, such as category, material, size, or surface appearance. We formalize this problem as attribute-compositional retrieval, where a fixed object-centric scene memory is queried with natural language to retrieve the object satisfying the requested attributes. To investigate this capability directly, we introduce a controlled evaluation protocol with fixed scene memories and attribute-defined targets, separating retrieval from perception and annotation ambiguities. We then propose FRAME, which turns language into query-relevant attribute weights, uses learned readouts to estimate per-attribute evidence from object embeddings, and ranks objects by aggregating this evidence according to the query. Across held-out scenes and object assets, FRAME outperforms representative scene-memory retrieval baselines while reducing post-decomposition object scoring to lightweight matrix-vector computation. These results position attribute-compositional retrieval as a complementary scene-memory capability for language-guided robots, showing that persistent object attributes can be exposed as composable evidence for accurate and efficient multi-attribute retrieval.

</details>


### [286] [CoSA: Correlation-Guided Change A ttention with Learnable Residual Gating for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08914)
*Abdirashid Omar,Jonghyuk Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了交通摄像头图像中人行横道分割的跨域迁移问题，使用241张人工标注图像和5,926张无标注CCTV帧进行数据高效训练。源域U-Net达到93.05% IoU（仅作基线），在CCTV目标域上训练的DeepLabV3模型达到88.91% IoU。通过置信度和几何先验筛选的伪标签方法达到0.988组合分数，但审计发现报告的第二阶段98.52% IoU实际上是伪标签一致性而非人工真值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定交通摄像头图像的像素级标注成本高昂，而基于街景图像训练的人行横道模型在应用到高架CCTV视角时存在显著的视角和外观差异，需要探索数据高效的目标域迁移方案。

Method: 使用241张人工标注CCTV图像和5,926张无标注帧进行训练。源域使用31M参数U-Net（FPP真实场景），目标域使用42M参数DeepLabV3-ResNet50。采用置信度排序和最大连通分量面积先验筛选无标注帧中的高质量伪标签，用于自训练。

Result: 源域基线为93.05% IoU；在201张人工标注图像上训练并在40张标注图像上验证，目标域结果为88.91% IoU。伪标签筛选的top 1,000候选达到平均置信度0.976和组合分数0.988。批处理FP32推理速度77.03 FPS。审计发现98.52%的IoU报告实为伪标签一致性而非人工真值精度。

Conclusion: 研究支持置信度-几何过滤策略在跨域目标域训练中的实用性，同时揭示了伪标签评估必须与自训练所用标签完全隔离，否则会产生误导性的性能指标。可辩护的目标域结果应视为88.91% IoU。

Abstract: Pixel-level annotation of fixed traffic-camera imagery is expensive, while crosswalk models trained from street-level imagery face a substantial viewpoint and appearance shift when applied to elevated CCTV. We investigate a data-efficient target-domain pipeline using 241 manually annotated CCTV images and 5,926 unlabeled CCTV frames. A source-domain experiment trains a 31.0M-parameter custom U-Net on 3,300 first-person-view (FPV) images and obtains 93.05% IoU on its 330-image FPV test split. This result is a source baseline, not transferred performance: the released CCTV notebook instantiates a 42.0M-parameter DeepLabV3-ResNet50 from torchvision weights, and no compatible mapping from the U-Net checkpoint is implemented. Training on 201 manual CCTV images and selecting on 40 held-out manual masks yields 88.91% IoU. The model then predicts all unlabeled frames; image-level certainty and a largest-component area prior rank the candidates, and the top 1,000 attain mean certainty 0.976 and mean combined score 0.988. A repository audit shows that the reported second-stage 98.52% IoU was measured on a 150-image split containing only teacher-generated pseudo-masks. Because of a directory-layout mismatch, the executed combined-data loader found zero manual samples and split 1,000 pseudo-labeled samples into 850 training and 150 evaluation samples. We therefore report 98.52% as internal pseudo-label agreement rather than human-ground-truth accuracy. The defensible target-domain result is 88.91% IoU on the 40 manual validation images. Batch-one FP32 inference at 512 x 512 requires 12.98 ms, corresponding to 77.03 FPS, on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 48 GB GPU. These findings support the practicality of confidence-and-geometry filtering while also showing why pseudo-label evaluation must remain isolated from the labels used for self-training.

</details>


### [287] [EgoSIS: From Factorized Visual Ego-Transitions to Motion-Canonical Spatial Evidence for UAV Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08938)
*Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Mingxuan Cui,Yilin Sun,Hang Zhang,Jianhua Zhu,Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoSIS，一个无需位姿的适配器，通过三阶段处理将RGB派生的双向光流转化为运动规范化的视觉证据，用于无人机视频问答，显著提升感知和记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视频问答需要分离相机运动与场景变化，但仅基于RGB的多模态模型缺乏明确、稳定的参考来实现这种分离。

Method: EgoSIS通过三个阶段工作：(1) FVET拟合鲁棒的图像平面转换并暴露运动、残差支撑和可靠性因子；(2) ReTEM利用可靠性加权的更新维护有界历史，并在剪辑或持续不确定时重新锚定；(3) EASE将支撑视觉特征扭曲到各段局部锚点，通过零初始化残差注入四个空间证据令牌，且不改变Qwen的视觉令牌数量。

Result: 在SIS-Bench基准上，EgoSIS-8B达到89.9%的感知准确率、82.5%的感知加记忆准确率、76.2%的整体准确率，最大的提升集中在自我意识感知和记忆方面。

Conclusion: EgoSIS为光流与空间推理之间提供了一个可解释的接口，有效解决了无人机视频问答中相机运动与场景变化分离的难题。

Abstract: UAV video question answering requires separating camera motion from changes in the scene, but RGB-only multimodal models receive no explicit, stable reference for that separation. We present EgoSIS, a pose-free adapter that converts RGB-derived bidirectional flow into motion-canonical visual evidence in three stages. Factorized Visual Ego-Transitions (FVET) fits a robust image-plane transition and exposes motion, residual-support, and reliability factors. Reliability-Gated Ego-Transition Memory (ReTEM) uses reliability-weighted updates for a bounded history and re-anchors it at cuts or sustained uncertainty. Ego-Aligned Spatial Evidence (EASE) warps supported visual features into each segment's local anchor and injects four spatial evidence tokens per visual slice through zero-initialized residuals, without changing Qwen's visual-token count. On SIS-Bench, EgoSIS-8B obtains 89.9\% perception, 82.5\% perception-plus-memory, and 76.2\% overall accuracy, with the largest gains concentrated in self-awareness perception and memory. The adapter thus provides an interpretable interface between optical flow and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [288] [Prior-free relative 6D pose estimation of multiple object instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08949)
*Behdad Khodabandehloo,Andrea Caraffa,Davide Boscaini,Fabio Poiesi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无先验的相对6D位姿估计方法PROSE，无需CAD模型、模板或参考图像即可估计同一图像中未知物体的多个实例之间的相对位姿，并构建了相应的基准数据集PRENCH用于系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体6D位姿估计方法逐渐减少对物体特定先验的依赖（从3D模型到多视图捕获再到单张参考图像），本文希望将这一趋势推向极致——消除"知道场景中是什么物体"的假设，实现完全无先验的相对位姿估计。

Method: PROSE方法利用多模态基础特征在物体实例间寻找粗略对应关系（无需训练），通过跨实例元组的循环一致性约束来精炼这些对应关系，并利用全局一致的对应关系估计任意实例对之间的相对6D位姿。同时设计了基于三个多实例BOP数据集构建的PRENCH基准。

Result: PROSE在PRENCH基准上持续优于通过改编最新单图像方法得到的基线模型，且不需要任务特定的监督或额外学习组件。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，完全无先验的相对6D位姿估计是可行的，PROSE在此新设定下展现了优越性能，为该研究方向奠定了基准与基线。

Abstract: Object 6D pose estimation formulations have progressively reduced reliance on object-specific priors, evolving from explicit 3D models to multi-view object captures to single reference images. We take this progression to its extreme by introducing prior-free relative 6D pose estimation, which lifts the assumption of knowing which object is to be posed within the scene. This novel setting aims to estimate the relative poses of multiple instances of an unknown object within the same image, without requiring CAD models, templates, or reference images. We solve this by formulating a novel method (PROSE) that finds coarse correspondences between object instances using multimodal foundation features, thus requiring no training. We refine these correspondences by imposing cycle consistency across tuples of instances, and leverage the resulting globally consistent correspondences to estimate the relative 6D pose between any pair of instances. To enable systematic evaluation, we design a novel benchmark (PRENCH) built from three multi-instance BOP datasets and enriched with task-specific metadata. PROSE consistently outperforms baselines obtained by adapting state-of-the-art single-image methods to the proposed setting, while requiring neither task-specific supervision nor additional learned components. Project website: https://tev-fbk.github.io/PROSE/

</details>


### [289] [Concentrate After Imagination: Text-Conditioned Evidence Grounding for Partially Relevant Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08999)
*Shuaiqi Cheng,Siyu You,Yanbi Wu,Yuxi Chen,Jiahao Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种用于部分相关视频检索（PRVR）的分数级证据验证算子。TRACE通过激活查询相关的全局视频寄存器、将支持路由到帧级证据，并在局部时间选择前平滑边缘化查询到寄存器再到帧的替代路径，以查询条件残差校准方式修正原始局部分数，解决PRVR中的查询无关浓度瓶颈问题。在三个基准上均取得最佳SumR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRVR方法虽在局部表征、不确定性建模和全局上下文上有所改进，但最终排名往往过度信任最强的局部响应，导致偶然相似的片段会产生无依据的高分峰值。作者将这认定为"查询无关浓度瓶颈"问题。

Method: 提出TRACE分数级证据验证算子：给定查询和全局视频寄存器，激活查询相关的寄存器，将其支持路由至帧级证据，平滑边缘化查询-寄存器-帧的替代路径，再进行局部时间选择。与表示级特征融合不同，TRACE仅将证据作为查询条件残差来校准原始局部分数。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions、Charades-STA和TVR三个基准上均取得最佳SumR，相比DreamPRVR骨干分别提升1.2、1.1和1.5个百分点。消融、路由损坏、难负样本和跨骨干迁移分析均支持增益来源于查询条件证据验证而非通用分数偏移。

Conclusion: TRACE通过分数级（而非表示级）的查询条件证据验证，有效缓解了PRVR中查询无关峰值的误判问题，且该方法具有良好的跨骨干可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Partially Relevant Video Retrieval (PRVR) retrieves untrimmed videos when queries describe only short moments. Although recent methods improve local representations, uncertainty modeling, and global context, final ranking often still trusts the strongest local response; a coincidentally similar fragment can therefore produce an unsupported peak. We identify this failure as the query-agnostic concentration bottleneck and propose TRACE, a score-level evidence verification operator for PRVR. Given a query and global video registers, TRACE activates query-relevant registers, routes their support to frame-level evidence, and smoothly marginalizes alternative query-to-register-to-frame paths before localized temporal selection. Unlike representation-level feature fusion, TRACE uses this evidence only as a query-conditioned residual calibration of the original local score. On ActivityNet Captions, Charades-STA, and TVR, TRACE achieves the best SumR on all three benchmarks and improves the DreamPRVR backbone by 1.2, 1.1, and 1.5 points, respectively. Ablation, routing-corruption, hard-negative, and cross-backbone transfer analyses support the interpretation that the gains arise from query-conditioned evidence verification rather than a generic score offset.

</details>


### [290] [DXPR: Depth-Based Vision-LiDAR Cross-Modal Place Recognition Using Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09005)
*Yungsoo Han,Youngseok Jang,Seungwon Roh,Jeongyeon Seo,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DXPR框架，利用视觉基础模型（VFMs）实现深度跨模态位置识别，将单目相机图像与LiDAR地图进行匹配，无需特定模态编码器。核心思想是将图像和LiDAR扫描统一转换为深度图像表示，并使用单个VFM骨干网络学习模态不变全局描述符，同时引入几何感知重叠挖掘器优化度量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨模态位置识别方法通常依赖特定模态的编码器，在季节、天气和光照剧烈变化下鲁棒性不足。作者希望实现仅使用相机在预先构建的LiDAR地图中进行鲁棒定位，从而降低机器人系统对LiDAR传感器的依赖。

Method: DXPR将相机图像和LiDAR扫描统一表示为深度图像，使用视觉基础模型(VFM)骨干网络和聚合头学习模态不变描述符。引入几何感知重叠挖掘器，通过跨模态尺度对齐后前向扭曲计算像素级重叠分数，重新标注模糊样本对，并自适应调整多相似性损失的正边距以避免弱重叠视图过拟合。

Result: 在KITTI和Boreas数据集上进行广泛实验。KITTI上多数序列达到接近完美的Recall@1并超越此前CMPR基线；Boreas上内部序列性能与强单模态基线DINOv2-SALAD相当，跨序列设置中表现更优；相比RangeBEV，在内部和跨序列评估中均表现更好。

Conclusion: DXPR通过统一的深度图像表示和VFM骨干网络有效实现了跨模态位置识别，在严重季节、天气和光照变化条件下表现出强鲁棒性，证明了无需特定模态编码器的CMPR框架的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: We present DXPR, a depth-based cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) framework that uses vision foundation models (VFMs) to match monocular camera queries against a LiDAR map without modality-specific encoders. This enables robots and autonomous vehicles to robustly localize using only cameras within pre-built LiDAR maps, even under severe seasonal, weather, and illumination changes. The key idea is to convert both camera images and LiDAR scans into a unified depth image representation so that a single VFM backbone with an aggregation head can learn modality-invariant global descriptors. To make pairwise metric learning faithful to scene geometry, we introduce a geometry-aware overlap miner: after cross-modal scale alignment of camera and LiDAR depth, we forward-warp measurements between views to compute a pixel-level overlap score. This score relabels ambiguous pairs and adaptively modulates the positive margin in a multi-similarity loss to avoid overfitting on weakly overlapping views. Extensive experiments on KITTI odometry and Boreas demonstrate strong performance and robustness across seasons, weather, and day/night. On KITTI, DXPR achieves near-perfect Recall@1 on most sequences and outperforms prior CMPR baselines. On Boreas, DXPR achieves intra-sequence performance on par with a strong single-modal baseline (DINOv2-SALAD), while showing clear improvements in the more challenging inter-sequence setting. Compared with RangeBEV, our method consistently performs better in both intra- and inter-sequence evaluations, demonstrating robustness under diverse seasonal and illumination changes.

</details>


### [291] [A Joint 2D-3D Statistical Shape Model for Orthopedic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09010)
*Florence Dell'Aniello Picard,Pranav Poudel,Nairouz Shehata,Frédéric Lavoie,Herve Lombaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合2D-3D统计形状模型（SSM），通过在共享潜空间中显式建模2D与3D分割之间的共变关系，直接从X光图像恢复三维股骨形状，无需迭代的3D-to-2D投影匹配，推理速度约4倍于基线方法，单例推理时间低于3秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D SSM的三维股骨重建方法依赖迭代式3D-to-2D投影匹配，计算开销大，且SSM被限制在单一维度，未能充分挖掘2D观测与3D几何之间的统计关联。

Method: 提出联合2D-3D SSM，将训练时的2D和3D分割注册到共同3D模板及对应2D投影上，再将所得平稳速度场通过PCA联合分解，在共享潜空间中建模2D与3D的共变关系。推理时通过将2D潜坐标提升至3D PCA子空间直接恢复3D形状，绕过迭代投影匹配。

Result: 在NMDID数据集上，联合2D-3D SSM优于广泛使用的3D-only SSM基线，且推理速度约为基线的4倍，单例推理时间低于3秒。

Conclusion: 联合2D-3D统计分析框架能够直接从数据学习2D到3D的映射，在提升重建精度的同时显著降低推理耗时，为基于X光片的三维形态重建提供了高效且可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional femoral reconstruction from radiographs supports surgical planning, implant sizing, and post-operative follow-up, but remains ill-posed as X-ray projections discard depth information. Existing methods often incorporate a 3D statistical shape model (SSM) as a shape prior to guide reconstructions toward anatomically plausible shapes, relying on iterative 3D-to-2D projection matching. Yet, these approaches are computationally expensive and constrain their SSM to a single dimensionality, leaving the statistical relationship between 2D observations and 3D geometry largely unexploited and unexplored. We instead propose a joint 2D-3D SSM that explicitly captures the co-variation between 2D and 3D segmentations in a shared latent space. During training, 2D and 3D segmentations are registered to a common 3D template and its corresponding 2D projections, and the resulting stationary velocity fields are jointly decomposed using principal component analysis (PCA). This joint modeling allows the 2D-to-3D mapping to be learned directly from data rather than computing correspondences at inference time. For unseen subjects, the 3D shape is recovered directly by lifting the 2D latent coordinates to the 3D PCA subspace, thereby eliminating the need for iterative 3D-to-2D projection. Experiments on NMDID demonstrate that the proposed joint 2D-3D SSM outperforms a widely-used 3D-only SSM baseline while achieving inference approximately 4 times faster, at under 3 seconds per subject. The code is available at: https://github.com/florence-dellaniello-picard/joint2d3d-ssm.

</details>


### [292] [Spheriverse: 3D Scene Understanding from Spherical Observations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09012)
*Fei Teng,Sheng Wu,Mengfei Duan,Guoqiang Zhao,Junhui Ma,Kai Luo,Siyu Li,Hao Shi,Zhiyong Li,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Spheriverse数据集（64,400对球面图像-LiDAR对）以及SphereOcc占用预测框架，通过球面几何建模与语义证据检索解决球面图像与笛卡尔世界之间的跨空间表征鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面观测图像编码在角度域，而物理世界以笛卡尔坐标表示，这种跨空间表征差异导致几何对应和语义证据聚合困难。

Method: 1) 构建Spheriverse大规模数据集；2) 提出SphereOcc框架，包含CSRR模块将球面range-azimuth几何融入笛卡尔体素特征，以及SER模块基于体素内容和range-height-azimuth几何自适应检索球面图像中的语义证据。

Result: SphereOcc在语义占用预测上达到13.91% mIoU和24.65% GeoIoU，较TPVFormer和SurroundOcc分别提升1.70和2.10个百分点，并在所有五个场景中均排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过Spheriverse基准和SphereOcc方法，验证了球面几何建模与语义证据检索能有效弥合球面观测与笛卡尔3D场景理解之间的表征差距。

Abstract: Spherical observations provide global visual context for 3D scene understanding. However, visual information is encoded in an angular domain, whereas the physical world is represented in Cartesian coordinates. This cross-space representation gap complicates geometric correspondence and semantic evidence aggregation. To delve into this challenge, we introduce Spheriverse, comprising $64,400$ temporally aligned spherical image-LiDAR pairs organized into 644 sequences. The dataset spans diverse scenes, illumination, and weather conditions, with fine-grained semantic classes. We further establish benchmarks for semantic occupancy prediction, semantic mapping, and 3D object detection, evaluating 30+ methods through overall and scene-wise comparisons. For dense prediction, we propose SphereOcc, an occupancy framework that couples spherical geometry modeling with semantic evidence retrieval. Cartesian-Spherical Representation Remodeling (CSRR) incorporates spherical range-azimuth geometry into Cartesian voxel features through region-wise modulation. Spherical Evidence Re-querying (SER) then conditions queries on voxel content and range-height-azimuth geometry to adaptively retrieve relevant semantic evidence from source spherical image features. SphereOcc achieves 13.91% mIoU and 24.65% GeoIoU, outperforming the respective best-performing methods, TPVFormer and SurroundOcc, by 1.70 and 2.10 percentage points. It also ranks first in both metrics across all five scenes, with consistent advantages across the evaluated spatial partitions and reduced fields of view. The established benchmark and source code will be available at https://feit-feiteng.github.io/Spheriverse.

</details>


### [293] [PIC: Revisiting INR for Image Coding with Fast Encoding and Sub-Millisecond Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09020)
*Xiang Liu,Jinxiang Wang,Bin Chen,Zimo Liu,Mingyao Hong,Jiawei Li,Yaowei Wang,Shu-tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种前馈式隐式神经表示（INR）图像编码架构PIC，在单次前向传播中计算INR网络所需的全部信息，实现20 FPS的编码速度和2000 FPS的解码速度，在率失真性能和速度上均优于或媲美JPEG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR图像编码在解码复杂度上优于传统端到端编码器，但受限于编码速度慢和解码效率未充分利用，阻碍了实际应用。

Method: 提出前馈式INR图像编码架构PIC，通过单次前向传播计算INR网络所需全部信息，并实现高度优化的解码器，兼顾编码速度与解码效率。

Result: 编码速度达20 FPS，解码速度达2000 FPS，在相当的率失真性能下解码速度显著超越JPEG；这是首个在RD性能与解码速度上同时优于或媲美JPEG且具备实用编码速度的学习型图像编码器。

Conclusion: PIC验证了INR图像编码在实用化方面的可行性，为平衡编码速度、解码速度与压缩性能提供了新思路，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) has achieved remarkable progress in novel view synthesis and image/video coding in recent years.Compared to conventional end-to-end image codecs, INR-based compressors demonstrate significant advantages in decoding complexity. However, their practical application has been hindered by the inferior encoding speed and underutilized decoding efficiency.In this work, we propose a feedforward INR image coding architecture, Practical INR Image Codec (PIC), that computes all the necessary information for INR network in a single forward pass, achieving an encoding speed of 20 FPS. Additionally, we implement a highly optimized decoder that reaches 2000 FPS decoding speed, significantly surpassing JPEG's performance at comparable rate-distortion (RD) performance. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first learning-based image codec that simultaneously outperforms or is comparable with JPEG in both RD performance and decoding speed while maintaining practical encoding speed. Code is available at https://github.com/actcwlf/PIC.

</details>


### [294] [Task-driven Processing with Coarse-to-Fine Glimpse-based Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09025)
*Oleh Kolner,Thomas Ortner,Stanisław Woźniak,Angeliki Pantazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CF-GAP（Coarse-to-Fine Glimpse-based Active Perception），一种任务驱动的视觉前端模块，通过选择性引导一系列有限视角的"瞥视"（glimpse）在高分辨率场景中迭代聚焦相关区域，再由下游实例检测器对这些局部区域进行高分辨率处理，从而避免全图处理并消除无关干扰信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型通常处理整幅图像，缺乏选择性放大相关区域的能力。在面向具体任务的场景（如实例检测）中，高分辨率、杂乱场景下的关键细节往往会因图像缩放而丢失，严重影响了检测性能。

Method: CF-GAP作为一个任务驱动的前端模块，利用任务信息引导一系列有限的"瞥视"序列，在场景中逐步精炼聚焦于最相关的区域，然后将这些高分辨率局部区域交由下游实例检测器处理。整个流程避免了全图处理，同时消除了无关的干扰信息。

Result: 在HR-InsDet和Robotools基准测试中，CF-GAP在多种最先进的实例检测器上平均精度（AP）最高提升了20%，并且能使轻量级检测器超越其更大规模的对应版本。

Conclusion: CF-GAP通过粗到细的主动感知策略，有效提升了现有实例检测器在高分辨率杂乱场景下的处理能力，在显著提升检测精度的同时降低了计算负担，验证了选择性视觉感知在任务驱动场景中的有效性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision models process images in their entirety, lacking the ability to selectively zoom in on relevant regions. This limitation is particularly acute in scenarios where processing must be conditioned on a specific task - such as instance detection, which requires localizing a specific object in a high-resolution, cluttered scene. In such settings, critical details are easily lost as images are often resized to match the model dimensions and computational constraints. We introduce Coarse-to-Fine Glimpse-based Active Perception (CF-GAP), a task-driven front-end that enhances high-resolution processing of existing instance detectors. CF-GAP selectively directs a sequence of limited view glimpses across the scene, utilizing task information to iteratively refine focus on the most relevant regions. These localized regions are then processed at high resolution by a downstream instance detector. By avoiding full-image processing and eliminating irrelevant confounding information, CF-GAP improves Average Precision (AP) by up to 20% across various state-of-the-art instance detectors on the HR-InsDet and Robotools benchmarks, while further enabling lightweight detectors to outperform their larger counterparts.

</details>


### [295] ["World Knowledge" in the Weights: Reading Concept Circuits of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09055)
*Yanlin Chen,Tang Li,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用跨层转码器（CLTs）从视觉Transformer（ViTs）中读取"概念电路"，即将模型内部表示解析为由稀疏、可解释概念构成的图形结构，并提供全局（输入不变）和实例（输入依赖）两种互补视图，用于发现和移除虚假相关、比较不同基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer在各种视觉任务上取得了显著性能，但人们对其内部如何表示世界结构知之甚少。缺乏对模型内部概念表征及其跨层交互的深入理解，限制了模型的可解释性、可控性和可靠性。

Method: 利用跨层转码器（CLTs）从ViTs中读取概念电路。全局概念电路可从跨层权重直接恢复，不依赖具体输入，揭示模型编码的可复用"世界知识"；实例概念电路则依赖输入，识别特定预测实际使用的概念和路径。文章还展示了基于全局电路统计信息的自动虚假相关发现方法，以及通过干预实例电路实现虚假相关移除的技术。

Result: 在Waterbird数据集上，该方法相比现有方法在虚假相关移除方面提升了11.0%的性能。此外，通过概念电路对比不同基础模型（如CLIP与DINO）的表示结构，揭示了不同监督范式如何塑造模型内部表征。

Conclusion: 概念电路提供了一种有效的全局与实例级双视角可解释框架，能够帮助研究人员理解ViTs的内部世界模型，并在虚假相关识别与修正、模型结构对比等任务中展现实用性。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have achieved remarkable generalization across visual domains, yet little is known about how they internally represent the structure of the world. To address this gap, we use Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) to read concept circuits from ViTs: directed graphs whose nodes correspond to sparse, interpretable concepts and edges capture concept interactions across layers. Our method yields two complementary views of model behavior. The global concept circuit is input-invariant and can be recovered directly from learned cross-layer weights, exposing the reusable "world knowledge" encoded in the model. The instance concept circuit is input-dependent and identifies the concepts and pathways actually used for a specific prediction, enabling faithful example-level explanations. We demonstrate the utility of concept circuits in three ways: (1) Automatic spurious correlation discovery: leveraging the statistics of our global concept circuits to identify shortcut dependencies within the model. (2) Spurious correlation removal: intervening on the instance concept circuit to steer the model towards correct predictions. Empirical results show that our method outperforms existing counterparts by 11.0% on the Waterbird dataset. (3) Model comparison: contrasting the global concept circuits of different foundation models (e.g., CLIP vs. DINO) to reveal how supervision paradigms shape representational structure. Our code is available at https://github.com/deep-real/VisionCLT

</details>


### [296] [GoDeep: Annotation-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding via Language-Space Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09082)
*Thodoris Betsas,Anastasios Doulamis,Andreas Georgopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种开放词汇3D语义分割方法，利用视觉语言模型仅作为翻译器，将每张图像转换为结构化实体级描述，直接投影并聚合到通用语言嵌入空间中，无需3D训练语料库或专用3D编码器。该方法在ScanNet++上与强基线竞争，在文化遗产基准上通过词汇修正可超越CLIP方法，并能更好地分离和定位词汇外（OOV）物体，且预测在点级别可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放词汇3D语义分割方法通常将CLIP特征提升到3D空间，但CLIP嵌入在组合任务上表现如词袋模型，且免标注变体往往需要大规模3D训练语料和专用3D编码器，限制了泛化性和可解释性。

Method: 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为翻译器，为每个位姿图像生成结构化实体级描述；将描述进行grounding、投影并在纯语言嵌入空间中聚合。整个流程无需3D训练语料库或专门的3D编码器。同时提出一个基于启发式加权和可解释性的聚合策略，偏好精确而非频繁的观测。

Result: 在ScanNet++上与在ScanNet上训练的强免标注基线竞争性相当；在5栋建筑文化遗产基准上，通过单次系统性词汇修正即可逆转CLIP变体优劣排名，且该效应被第二个独立类的修正所证实；语言空间嵌入对OOV物体的存在与缺失区分比CLIP嵌入更显著，并能定位场景中的OOV物体。

Conclusion: 语言空间嵌入比CLIP嵌入更能忠实反映物理内容，且由于所有表示保持离散文本形式，预测在点级别具有可解释性；提出的聚合策略可作为概念验证，有利于更精细的元素定位。

Abstract: Open vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation methods typically lift CLIP features into 3D. This embeds points in a joint vision-language space known to behave like a bag-of-words on compositional tasks. Furthermore, even annotation free variants often require a large 3D training corpus and a dedicated 3D encoder per domain. Instead we use a vision-language model purely as a translator. It produces structured, entity-level descriptions of each posed image. These descriptions are grounded, projected, and aggregated directly in a general-purpose, language-only embedding space, with no 3D training corpus or encoder required. On ScanNet++, our pipeline is competitive with strong annotation free baselines trained on ScanNet. On a 5-building cultural heritage benchmark, raw scores initially favor a CLIP-based variant, but a single systematic vocabulary correction reverses this ranking. An effect confirmed by a second, independent correction on a different class, indicating that language-space embeddings track physical content more faithfully. This fidelity extends to genuinely out-of-vocabulary (OOV) objects on ScanNet++ proving that language-space embeddings separate presence from absence objects far more sharply than CLIP-based embeddings do. GoDeep also localize these OOV objects within the scene, all without any 2D-3D annotation. Because every representation remains discrete text, predictions are also explainable at the point level. Finally, exploiting both a heuristic weighting, that favors precise over merely frequent observations and GoDeep's explainability property, we propose an aggregation strategy, as a proof of concept, that favors finer elements localization.

</details>


### [297] [Mask Forcing: Improving Autoregressive Video Diffusion Distillation via Dual-Noise Masking Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09123)
*Zhuoran Zhao,Shengju Qian,Tongtong Liang,Xianghao Kong,Songchun Zhang,Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Xin Wang,Pan Hui,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mask Forcing策略，通过双噪声掩码回滚机制扰动自回归视频扩散模型的自回滚过程，缓解逆向KL散度导致的模式坍缩问题，在不使用真实视频数据的前提下提升生成视频的视觉质量和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在实时视频生成中很有潜力，但现有基于分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）的方法存在过饱和和过平滑问题，根源在于逆向KL目标的模式寻求行为会导致学生分布坍缩到教师分布的少数模式上。

Method: 提出Mask Forcing双噪声掩码回滚策略：在AR学生自回滚过程中，沿空间和时间轴随机掩码注入更干净的信号到带噪回滚输入中。这种扰动促使学生回滚探索教师分布的更多区域，使DMD能提供超越学生已覆盖模式的监督信号；同时更干净的token为其他更噪声的token提供去噪指导，减少误差累积。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法能够改善多种AR视频扩散蒸馏方法，在不引入真实视频数据或额外后训练阶段的情况下，以较高效率显著提升视觉质量。

Conclusion: Mask Forcing通过扰动策略有效缓解了逆向KL模式寻求导致的模式坍缩问题，为AR视频扩散蒸馏提供了一种无需额外数据即可提升生成质量的通用改进方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models have shown great potential in real-time video generation. Recent methods distill pretrained bidirectional video diffusion models into causal AR students through Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), but the generated videos often suffer from over-saturation and over-smoothing issues, resulting in limited visual quality and realism. The key contributing factor is the mode-seeking behavior of the reverse KL objective in DMD, which can cause the student distribution to collapse onto only a few modes of the teacher distribution. To address this, we propose Mask Forcing, a Dual-Noise Masking Rollout strategy that perturbs the AR student self-rollout to mitigate mode collapse induced by reverse-KL mode seeking. The core idea is to inject cleaner signals into noisy rollout inputs via random masks along spatial and temporal axes during the self-rollout process of AR diffusion distillation. Such perturbations encourage the student rollouts to explore more regions of the teacher distribution, allowing DMD to provide learning signals beyond the modes already covered by the student. Moreover, the cleaner tokens act as denoising guidance for other noisier tokens, improving the intermediate rollout predictions and reducing error accumulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method improves multiple AR video diffusion distillation methods with higher visual quality efficiently, without incorporating real video data or additional post-training stages.

</details>


### [298] [Canonical Color as a Lens into Concept Decodability in Vision Encoders and VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09124)
*Xiaofu Chen,Stella Frank,Yova Kementchedjhieva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文以典型颜色为受控测试案例，研究了视觉编码器是否在线性可访问层面编码概念性（而非仅视觉可见的）信息。作者构造了典型颜色物体的数据集，通过探测灰度图像下编码器的颜色与物体身份可解码性，发现典型颜色仍可从灰度图像中被解码且与物体身份预测相关联，说明存在概念链接。此外，VLM的后训练会显著影响视觉编码器中颜色的可解码性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究视觉编码器所构建的图像表示中，除了输入中立即可见的视觉信息外，是否还包含概念层面的信息。典型颜色（如香蕉是黄色）作为受控测试案例，可用于检验编码器是否存储了超出直接视觉输入的概念性语义知识。

Method: 构建具有典型颜色的物体数据集，对视觉编码器施加线性探针（probing），分别使用彩色和灰度图像测试颜色和物体身份的可解码性；扩展分析至完整的视觉-语言模型（VLM），考察后训练对视觉编码器中颜色可解码性的影响。

Result: 典型颜色可从灰度图像中线性解码，且与预测的物体身份相关，表明编码器存在概念性颜色信息；VLM后训练对视觉编码器中的颜色可解码性有显著影响。

Conclusion: 典型颜色可作为可控制的视角透镜，用于在视觉编码器和VLM中追踪物体级别的概念性语义信息，揭示了视觉表示中概念信息的存在及其与后训练的交互。

Abstract: Visual encoders construct a representation of the image input for Vision-Language models. How much conceptual, as opposed to immediately visible, information does this representation contain? We use canonical color as a controlled test case to ask whether vision encoders make canonical-color information linearly accessible, even when color is removed from the input image. We construct a dataset of objects with canonical colors, and probe vision encoders for both color and object identity using color and grayscale images. We find that canonical color remains decodable from grayscale images, and is tied to predicted object identity, indicating a conceptual link. Extending this analysis to full VLMs, we find that VLM post-training can have a surprisingly large effect on color decodability in the vision encoder. Overall, canonical color provides a usefully controllable lens for tracing object-level conceptual semantic information in vision encoders and VLMs.

</details>


### [299] [Studying Image Tokenizers as Visual Languages in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09143)
*Siting Li,Zhengyang Wang,Simon Shaolei Du,Xi Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个纯自回归受控测试平台，通过跟踪联合多模态预训练中多种任务（文本、图像、T2I和I2T）的验证损失，系统研究了图像分词器作为"视觉语言"在与文本联合建模时的行为、损失与下游性能的关系，以及分词器设计对未来训练的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分词器的评估通常只关注孤立指标或仅从生成/理解单一角度出发，无法完整反映视觉token在与文本联合建模时的实际表现。作者希望建立一个统一的视角来理解图像分词器如何影响多模态联合训练。

Method: 构建受控的纯自回归测试平台，在文本、图像、T2I和I2T四种预测任务上跟踪多模态持续预训练过程中的任务特定验证损失，分析损失的缩放行为及其与下游性能的关系，并以此研究多模态可学习性和分词器设计的影响。

Result: (1)不同任务的损失呈现不同的缩放行为并对分词器产生不同排序；(2)损失与性能的关系取决于预测的token空间，I2T损失（共享文本词表）比T2I损失（依赖图像token空间）提供更一致的信号；(3)更好的重建并不必然带来更低的任务损失或更强的下游性能；(4)图像分词器的选择会影响联合优化下的文本建模能力。

Conclusion: 任务特定损失为理解和评估图像分词器作为视觉语言提供了互补视角，揭示了图像分词器与文本在多模态联合训练中的相互作用，并通过判别器、语义监督和词表大小三个设计轴的案例研究深化了这一认识。

Abstract: Image tokenizers define the ``visual language'' of unified multimodal models, yet are commonly studied through isolated metrics or generation-/understanding-only evaluations. These evaluations do not fully capture how visual tokens behave when modeled jointly with text. We build a controlled pure-autoregressive testbed and track task-specific validation losses during multimodal continual pretraining across text, image, text-to-image (T2I), and image-to-text (I2T) prediction. We examine how these losses scale and relate to downstream performance, then use them to study multimodal learnability---how well image and text tokens are jointly modeled---and tokenizer design. We find that (1) losses should be analyzed by task, since they exhibit distinct scaling behavior and rank tokenizers differently. (2) The loss--performance relationship depends on the predicted token space: for a fixed tokenizer, T2I and I2T losses correlate with generation quality, but across tokenizers, the T2I loss--performance relationship shifts with the image-token space, whereas I2T loss, computed over a shared text vocabulary, provides a more consistent signal. I2T loss also correlates with both generation and visual understanding performance after supervised finetuning. Using losses as a lens, we show that (3) better reconstruction does not necessarily yield lower task-specific losses or stronger downstream performance, and that (4) image tokenizer choice can affect text modeling under joint optimization. As case studies, we revisit three tokenizer design axes---the discriminator, semantic supervision, and vocabulary size---to examine their effects on joint modeling and downstream performance. Together, our testbed offers a complementary perspective on image tokenizers as visual languages, highlighting their interplay with text in joint multimodal training.

</details>


### [300] [Point4D: Long-range 4D Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09145)
*Minsik Jeon,Jay Karhade,Deva Ramanan,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Point4D，一种用于长视频序列4D重建的前馈模型，能够在数百帧视频中可靠推断稠密逐点3D轨迹，优于现有仅支持短输入窗口的4D方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D重建方法受限于短输入窗口（最多几十帧），无法处理长视频序列（数百帧）中的稠密逐点3D轨迹推理，亟需一种能处理长范围视频的4D方法。

Method: 核心创新是基于3D查询的运动解码器，将轨迹预测与图像平面可见性解耦；预测的3D端点直接在下个块中重新查询，无需重投影或匹配；同时从任意可见帧提取并重用视觉描述符以提升性能。

Result: Point4D在多个覆盖超过200帧的长视频跟踪基准上取得了最先进的性能，大幅超越了此前的前馈4D方法。

Conclusion: Point4D通过解耦轨迹预测与图像平面可见性，并结合3D端点直接重新查询和跨帧视觉描述符重用，成功实现了长视频序列的可靠4D重建。

Abstract: We introduce Point4D, a feed-forward model for 4D reconstruction of long-range video sequences. Point4D is able to reliably infer dense per-point 3D trajectories across multi-hundred-frame videos, unlike existing 4D methods that are limited to short input windows of at most a few dozen frames. A key innovation that enables this is our flexible 3D query-based motion decoder that decouples trajectory prediction from image-plane visibility. The predicted 3D endpoints are then directly re-queried in the next chunk without re-projection or matching. Furthermore, we show that extracting and reusing a visual descriptor from an arbitrary frame where the point is visible leads to better performance than relying solely on the source patch. Overall, Point4D achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-video tracking benchmarks spanning over 200 frames and largely outperforms previous feed-forward 4D method. Project page: https://point-4d.github.io

</details>


### [301] [SyncWorld: Visual Calibration Enables World Models as Zero-Shot Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09155)
*Yuncong Yang,Zhengtao Han,Furkan Ozyurt,Zeyuan Yang,Han Yang,Junyi Cao,Haoyu Zhen,Yilun Du,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SyncWorld，一种动作条件的世界模型，可作为零样本模拟器在新环境中无需额外训练即可使用。它通过视觉校准片段（配对帧和动作）在上下文中指定设置特定的"动作-视觉映射"，实现了对未见环境的精准模拟，并支持测试时策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人领域作为策略循环中的想象环境日益重要，但可扩展性受限——动作在像素空间中不是通用语言：视觉环境、相机视角、机器人位置或具身形态的变化都会改变相同数值动作的视觉表现，导致混合训练下的冲突监督和部署时的脆弱泛化。

Method: SyncWorld利用视觉校准片段（展示所有可控自由度的配对帧和动作）在上下文中指定特定设置的动作-视觉映射。训练时采用视觉校准上下文，教会模型通过视觉证据解释动作，并在缺乏显式校准时利用交互历史。以此实现零样本跨环境模拟。

Result: 实验表明SyncWorld能够在未见过的环境中准确模拟动作结果，且其 rollout 模拟能力可在无需训练的情况下实现测试时策略改进。

Conclusion: 通过上下文学习动作-视觉映射，SyncWorld有效解决了动作在不同视觉设置下不一致的问题，实现了零样本、免训练的世界模型模拟与策略优化能力。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used as policy-in-the-loop imagination environments, where reliable rollouts require fine-grained controllability with respect to low-level robot actions. A key obstacle to scaling such models in robotics is that actions are not a universal language in pixel space: changes in visual environment, camera view, robot placement, or embodiment alter how the same numerical action manifests visually, leading to conflicting supervision under mixed training and brittle generalization at deployment. We introduce SyncWorld, an action-conditioned world model that serves as a zero-shot simulator across unseen environments without any additional training. SyncWorld leverages a visual calibration episode---paired frames and actions that showcase all the controllable degrees of freedom---to specify the setup-specific Action--Visual Mapping in context. Training with visual calibration contexts teaches the model to interpret actions through visual evidence and to leverage interaction history when explicit calibration is unavailable. Experiments show that SyncWorld can accurately simulate action outcomes in previously unseen settings, and that its capability of simulating rollouts enables test-time policy improvement without training.

</details>
